## Scope rulings

Each ruling is `Ruling: <decision> — <why> — <cost if wrong>`; the ids in parentheses are the requirement-map items and questions it settles. Task writers follow them; a task that finds a ruling impossible stops and reports instead of improvising. Every Plan 2 ruling (R-01…R-39, ledger rulings) and Plan 3 ruling (R3-01…R3-70) not amended here still binds.

**Sequencing and ownership:**
- **R5-01 Base gate.** Ruling: Plan 5 runs on feat/p5-claude rebased onto main after Plans 2 and 3 have merged with their exit evidence, and after Plan 8a Tasks 2, 3 and 11 (`src/text/sanitize.ts`, `src/i18n/render.ts`, the `cli.js` dispatch line) — 8a Task 3 has merged (reconciliation, Plan 5 item 24), so Plan 5 uses 8a's `sanitize.ts`/`render.ts` and creates neither (the former "Task 1 creates both when 8a has not" branch is moot); Task 1's re-baseline test imports every Plan 1/2/3/8a name this contract cites (and F04-a's `src/since/report.ts` names when landed) and stops for a ruling on any divergence; Task 16 additionally waits for F04-a (R5-61); Plans 4 and 5 overlap (roadmap "Execution order"), so Tasks 1–17 call Plan 4 only through `RegionsPort` bound to `noRegions` (no foreign claim exists before Plan 4, so G2 is the correct answer, not a fail-open: "a locate failure never becomes a whole-file claim or a deny", 06 §3.5.3), and Task 18 starts only after Plan 4 has merged, binding `RegionsPort` through `plan4Regions(core)` (Plan 4's own `plan4Ports(host)` line binds `PublishGatePort`, `EditCheckPort`, `T0RegionsPort` and `SymbolPort`, X-33) and re-running the claim-dependent tests of Tasks 10–14 — roadmap row 5 depends on 2, 3 and 4 and lets 4 and 5 overlap after 3 — cost if wrong: Task 18 waits for Plan 4. (BL2-00, R1-001, P4X-01 stub, Q-15 base)
- **Plan 4 ‖ Plan 5 merge order (verify pass V-11; the same paragraph stands in Plan 4's header).** Plans 4 and 5 may run side by side (roadmap "Execution order") and both edit about 22 shared files; the rules: (1) `src/engine/deps.ts` (`MutantId`) and `src/ports/trace.ts` (trace variants): Plan 4 Task 5 merges first, Plan 5 Task 1 rebases onto it (both only append union members); (2) `src/engine/{core,loop,publish,csStep}.ts`: each plan edits only its own named lines (Plan 4: Tasks 5, 8, 11–16 as listed in their Files; Plan 5: Task 8's wiring, Task 11's `Engine.freshFetch`, Tasks 9, 13, 16 as listed) and whichever task merges later rebases and re-runs its tests — no task rewrites the other plan's lines; (3) `src/daemon/run.ts`, `test/sim/engineAdapter.ts`, `test/helpers/{coreFixture,engineFixture}.ts`, `tools/real/two-clone.ts`: one wiring line each (X-111, X-33), the later merge rebases; (4) `test/sim/{oracles,generators}.ts`, `tools/sim/{run-seeds,run-shard}.ts`, `tools/lint/rules.ts`: append-only, the later merge rebases; (5) `tools/build/build.mjs` and `test/integration/build.test.ts`: X-107's order and explicit list, each plan appending its entries; (6) `.github/workflows/{ci,sim-exit}.yml` and `test/unit/ci-config.test.ts`: only X-113's table rows and each plan's own `sim-exit` shard. A conflict outside these rules stops the later task for a controller ruling.
- **R5-02 Plan 2/3 files extended in place.** Ruling: Plan 5 makes only additive edits to Plan 2/3 files, each named in §File Structure with its owning task: `EngineDeps.claude: ClaudeEnginePort` (default `noClaude`) consumed by `tickSkip`, the TXN skip of detection and materialization, the apply admission and the writer's P1 re-check; `Engine.setFreezeReason`; `Engine.freshFetch` (beside the landed `requestFreshFetch(): void`, X-77); `Engine.markSince` (the writer of F04-a's `meta.since`, X-75); `Engine.onFoldStep`; `Engine.onCandidateSettled`; `CsSignal.expect`; `ChangeSets.signal(s, aNow?)`, `verifyCandidate` and `ChangeSets.onHumanCsOpened` (after Plan 3 merges, Plan 3 reviewer, X-18); `DaemonArgs` spawn fields; Plan 5 `TraceEvent` variants and `MutantId` members, appended in place (X-114, X-115) — and never `Meta.since`/`SinceMarks` (F04-a's, X-75) or `src/holds/lastCheck.ts` (Plan 3's, X-05) — Plan 2's ledger (Task 10: "01-engine §3.11 P1 're-check D' is Plan 5"), R-11, R-25 and Plan 3's R3-05, R3-08, R3-41 hand these to Plan 5; the pattern is Plan 3's — cost if wrong: a later plan finds a Plan 2/3 file it reads changed (each edit has its own test). (X-2.1, X-2.4, XP-01…XP-05, XP-09, X-3.1, X-3.4, BL2-03…BL2-05, BL2-11, BL2-18)
- **R5-03 Tiers, import direction and lint exemptions.** Ruling (as amended by X-61, X-63, X-110): `src/claude/wire` and `src/claude/text` import only `src/ports` (types), `src/text`, `src/i18n`, the two node-only leaves `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts` and `src/data/canonRoot.ts`, and `node:*`; `src/claude/client` imports only those plus `src/sec/{ipcKey,winAcl,endpoint,handshake,sddl}.ts` (`sddl.ts` per X-62), `src/ports/{ipc,inbox,fs,clock,random}.ts` and, as the one named exception, Plan 1's `src/git/env.ts` (`buildDaemonEnv` for `spawnDaemon`, CCI-51; Task 2 pins that file, `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts` and `src/data/canonRoot.ts` to `node:*` imports only) — never any other `src/git` or `src/data` file, `src/engine`, `src/holds`, `src/board`, `src/regions`, `src/claude/daemon` (L-HOOK-THIN, checked on the transitive import graph of `src/entry/{core,mcp}.ts`, type-only imports included); where client code needs an engine-side type it uses a wire-local structural copy pinned by an X5-45 test (`WireJson`, `StatusHeartbeat`, `ClientMutantId`, `SinceMarks`); `src/claude/daemon` never imports `src/claude/client`; `src/entry/hook.ts` imports only `node:*` and loads `./core.js` at run time with `await import('./core.js')` (A2-1) (L-LOADER: ≤ 150 lines in `dist/hook.js`, the only file exempt from L-FS-PORT and L-CLOCK); `child_process` is added to L-PORTS' allow-list for exactly `src/claude/client/spawn.ts` and `src/sec/winAcl.ts`; only `src/claude/client/spawn.ts` spawns a daemon and the MCP tier spawns nothing (L-SPAWN); only `src/claude/client/mcp/doorbell.ts` names `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN` (L-TOKEN, test files included); `canonRoot` (in `src/data/canonRoot.ts`, X-63) and the data-directory rule (in `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts`, X-61) each have one pure implementation plus a loader copy kept equal by a parity test; X-110's directory order (below, "Import direction") places `src/claude` after `src/engine` and before Plan 6's `src/coord`, so a later plan implements a Plan 5 port type in its own file and the wiring lives in `src/daemon/run.ts` (X-111) — 06 §3.2 build lints ("`dist/hook.js` over 150 lines or requiring anything other than Node built-ins and `./core.js`", "any daemon spawn outside `spawnDaemon()`", "any spawn call in `dist/mcp.js`"), 11 §3.10 L-PORTS ("the daemon spawner"), the 1.5 s deadline and ≤ 100 ms p95 need a thin `core.js` — cost if wrong: a lint exemption list grows by one file. (Q-10, C10, OQ-14 (r2), R1-103, R1-203)
- **R5-04 F04-a, 8a and Plan 3 additions Plan 5 consumes.** Ruling (as ruled by X-05, X-75, X-76): F04-a (D5; the Plan 2 controller's follow-up F-P2-1) declares `SinceMarks`, the `meta.since` parse (no `meta.v` bump) and `src/since/report.ts`; Plan 3 (Task 10 amendment, F-P3-1) owns `src/holds/lastCheck.ts` and `ChangeSets.lastCheck`. Plan 5 uses them at their landed signatures if they equal §Interfaces and otherwise stops for a ruling; Plan 5 never adds any of them itself (the former "add it when absent" branches are deleted) and writes no stand-in report — F04-a is a hard prerequisite of Task 16 (R5-61, A16-2) and Plan 3's store of Task 13; Plan 5 is the writer of `meta.since` (`markSince`, the drain's meta-only CAS) and of the skip records 3–6, and keeps a structural wire copy of `SinceMarks` in `frames.ts` (X5-03, X5-45) — D5 and roadmap rows 2–3 put the definitions earlier and the writers in Plan 5 — cost if wrong: none (the owners are ruled). (X-2.4, X-P2-01, X-3.1, XP-07, DEC-05)
- **R5-05 The wake-turn split (S5).** Ruling (confirmed by X-41: `WakeScopePort` is the one port — Plan 6's `WakeTurnPort` and `wakeCheckShell` are deleted names — and `backstop`'s `changes[]` gain `h1` and `localOnly`, which Plan 5 hashes with `hashRouteBStdin(…, true)`; Plan 6 creates the PIN inside its `backstop` binding, R3-08): Plan 5 owns the single turn-origin rule (R5-36), the gate row-3 call, the shell wake rule (every file-writing command in a wake-originated turn — class (b), and every class (c) writer or redirection whatever its target — is G1 `WAKE_TURN` in both `wake.turnEdits` modes; this needs no membership), and the three backstop call sites (edit-class PostToolUse, TXN close, `cowork_resolve` restore write) behind `WakeScopePort`; Plan 6 binds `WakeScopePort` with the wake work set (07 §2 part (a), Plan 4's `ownContained`/`notOwnContained` for part (b)), the `wake-scope` PIN through Plan 3's `pinCs('wake-scope', …, 'wake', …)` and `T-WAKE_RECAP`; Plan 5's binding `plan5WakeScope` answers `allow` (in `wake.turnEdits = deny` mode `{deny: 'deny'}`, whose wake work set is empty by 06 §3.8.2a and needs no membership, A10-5) and creates no PIN — safe because only a rung doorbell starts a wake turn and Plan 5's production `WakePolicy` is `neverRing` (R5-37), so no production wake turn exists before Plan 6 binds both ports; the backstop is awaited before the hook replies (record first) and its `OWN_CHANGE_HELD` rides the K2 carrier after a K2 block reason — Plan 3 R3-08 ("Plan 6 — `wake-scope` creation and the wake work set"), roadmap row 6 (wake turns S5, exit O-WAKETURN) and Plan 6's own drafts agree; 06 §3.8.2a/CCI-38 name the hook sites, which are Plan 5's — cost if wrong: Plan 6 moves the `pinCs` call behind the same port (one call site). (C3, Q-5(a), OQ-07 (r3), OQ-20 (r2), R2-C9, BL3-11, R1-603, R1-702, R1-906)
- **R5-06 SUSPECT.** Ruling (accepted by the reconciliation, Plan 5 item 16; Plan 2's header line is amended by F-P2-4; the fetch wait uses `Engine.freshFetch()`, X-77; X-40: the SUSPECT gate half is this, so Plan 6's `suspectCheck` entry point is dropped and Plan 6's `SuspectApi` binds Plan 4's `SuspectPort`, while `Coordinator.markOwnClaimsSuspect()` is Plan 6's, X-85): Plan 5 implements gate row 12 (the `extend{7000}` frame within 100 ms, start or join a fetch, wait ≤ 5 s for a fetch completed after the request and its fold, then row 13 on the fresh fold or G1 `SUSPECT_LOST`) and the PRO-37 self-guard predicate `suspectNow()` over Plan 2's own fetch/push anchors (`ownStaleness` > 60 s or a suspend gap > 5 s), applied to the `ownTouches` Plan 4's `predict` returns; with `noRegions` no own claim exists, so row 12 is vacuous until Plan 4; Plan 6 keeps PRO-37's other consumers (presume_absent, liveness) — 06 test 13 and TC-CCI-04 are Plan 5 tests and the predicate has no Plan 6 input; Plan 2's "Out of Plan 2: Plan 6 … SUSPECT self-guard" line is amended (SE5-11) — cost if wrong: Plan 6 imports the predicate from Plan 5. (C5, Q-5(b), R1-406)
- **R5-07 A CS with a SHADOW takes no edit checkpoint.** Ruling: 06 §3.8.4 skip 2 wins (no candidate, no check, no record); Plan 3's HLD-67 readiness test stays as the second guard and HLD-67's last paragraph is aligned in the spec batch — 06 owns the trigger ("this chapter owns the trigger", rev 2.4 notes call skip 2 a conservative default) — cost if wrong: a CS with a SHADOW publishes only at its boundary, as before rev 2.4. (C6, Q-5(c))
- **R5-08 F05 prerequisites only.** Ruling: Plan 5 ships the PostToolUse(Read) payload `read{offset, limit, full, enoent, size}`, the per-(seat, path) view blob, READ leases and ranged-read records, the 600-char attribution slot (`ATTRIBUTION` first, then a `SlotExtraPort` item when both fit), delivery confirmation on `printed{batch_id}` with an `onDelivered` callback, the context-reset events (SessionStart `compact|clear|resume|fork`, SubagentStart/SubagentStop of an agent), the 5,000-char claim-ahead share of the meter, the ≤ 5 ms main-thread rule for PostToolUse(Read) and a late-result queue; `cowork_show(id: string)` with ids resolved first (a non-id string → `UNKNOWN_ID`) and a parametric schema builder; Plan 6 (roadmap row 6, QUEUE Q8) ships CCI-57…CCI-66 (selection, dedupe, templates, the `string | string[]` widening, the path view); Plan 5's L-MCP-SCHEMA measures the eight-tool schema with an 80-char reserve for F05's clause from day one — QUEUE's design files win over a chapter's owner prefix for feature slices — cost if wrong: Plan 6 re-measures the schema (one test). (R2-D6, R2-H7, OQ-19 (r2), OQ-08 (r3), OQ-09 (r2), F05-01…F05-11)
- **R5-09 Engine events to seats.** Ruling (accepted by the reconciliation, Plan 5 item 17: Plan 6 emits no transition line; X-38: Plan 6's content enters Plan 5's `EventStore` as `SeatEvent`s with their `slot`, and its digest and slot content through `DigestPort`/`SlotExtraPort`): Plan 5 owns the carrier mechanics and the CCI-44 transition lines (once per seat per transition of `status.json` `state`, `freeze_reasons` or `publish_pause`, a `systemMessage` plus an additionalContext line rendered from codes and typed params only; a stale heartbeat renders `DAEMON_DOWN`), and delivers Plan 3's four status-only codes on the PostToolUse carrier (`CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE` once per CS per 06 §3.12.1 after 300 s of uncovered edits, `{n}` = files changed since the newest passing capture; `INCOMING_WAITING`, `CS_GROUP_WAITING`, `CS_READY_CANCELLED` once per raise to the seats whose CS or read set the code names); Plan 6 owns the `EV.*` templates and the routing of every other engine event (COO-31) through `DigestPort` — Plan 2's "Out of Plan 2" ("Plan 6: … engine-event delivery to seats") and R-27 ("delivery to seats and OS notifications are Plans 5–7") with 06 §3.14.6 — cost if wrong: Plan 6 moves the transition lines into its router (one module). (R2-E7, OQ-06 (r3), OQ-21 (r2), BL2-09, BL3-07, R2-C4)
- **R5-10 Coordination ops before Plan 6.** Ruling: the MCP handlers validate and refuse exactly as 06 §3.16.3 (`NOT_OWN_RECORD`, `TERMINAL_RECORD`, `AMBIGUOUS_ID`, `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY`, `MULTI_HOLDER` for both modes, `NOT_ADDRESSED_TO_SEAT`, `NOT_OWN_CLAIM`, `RELEASE_UNLANDED`) and apply the `queued` rule (an op not allowed under the current FREEZE or pause is kept in the outbox and returns `{ok: true, queued: <reason code>}`); the op itself is produced by `CoordToolsPort`, whose Plan 5 binding `outboxCoord` drafts exactly the glossary §7 op (`request` with `origin: claude|wake`, `answer` with `by: claude` and `verb: grant` for an accepted go-first, `note` with its target per glossary §24 default 10) and nothing else (no go-first limits, no doorbell, no offer transition); Plan 2 folds `request`, `answer` and `note` as no-ops (R-12), so no peer's state changes; `cowork_release` goes through `CoordToolsPort.release` to `RegionsPort.releaseClaims`, i.e. Plan 4's one release emitter (`noRegions`: every id `why: 'not_own'` → `NOT_OWN_CLAIM`; X-22); Stop's due asks come from `DueRequestsPort` (`noDueRequests` → `[]`); Plan 6 binds `CoordToolsPort` (its `CoordTools` members `request`, `respond`, `note`, `release`, `show`, `status` → `Promise<ToolResult>`, X-39), `CoordHooksPort` (X-40) and `DueRequestsPort` through its one `bindClaude(coordinator, ctx)` file, spread into `ClaudeBindings` in `run.ts` (X-38, X-111) — roadmap row 6 owns requests/answers/go-first; 06 owns the tool surface — cost if wrong: Plan 6 replaces one binding spread. (R2-H9…R2-H12, R2-F1, X-P6-06, X-P6-11)
- **R5-11 Claude-facing text before Plan 6.** Ruling (as amended by X-54, X-72): Plan 5 creates the one Claude template registry `src/i18n/claude.ts` (`renderClaude(templateId, params, lang, channel?)`, `registerClaudeCatalog`; row modules `src/i18n/claude/{en,ko}/*.ts`; `dist/i18n/claude.{en,ko}.json` written only by `tools/build/claudeCatalog.ts`), which Plan 6 Task 3 extends in place (no takeover, move or deletion), while `claudeTemplateOf`, `loaderLang`, `capLines` and `LOADER_TEXTS` stay in `src/claude/text/claudeText.ts`; the registry is seeded with exactly the rows Plan 5 asserts verbatim, copied from 07 in `en` and `ko`: `G4`, `PAUSED`, `T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL` (the three loader texts, generated into the loader at build time), `T-SINCE` and `T-SINCE.touch`; it registers `UI_SINCE_RECAP_HEAD`, `UI_SINCE_RECAP_TAIL` and the §3.9a section-2 row codes in `en` through 8a's `registerCatalog` for the `system` recap until Plan 7 Task 2 moves those rows into its catalogs and deletes Plan 5's `registerCatalog` calls in the same commit (X-72: a human-facing code row is registered by exactly one module at a time); every other code renders as the deterministic code line `[cowork-sync] <CODE> k=v …` (code plus sanitized typed params, no prose); Plan 6 completes every 07 template in place with its G-templates tests, Plan 7 adds the five other human languages — CCI-45 ("no free-form Claude-facing text outside the three loader texts"), T-F04-return (a Plan 5 exit test) asserts `T-SINCE`; Plan 6's drafts expect Plan 5's first version — cost if wrong: duplicated rows Plan 6 reconciles (a deep-equal test against 07 catches drift). (OQ-09 (r3), X-6.1, R1-211, F04-05, F04-06)
- **R5-12 `user.json` before Plan 7.** Ruling (as ruled by X-52, X-53): Plan 5 reads `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA/user.json` leniently (missing file, bad JSON or a mistyped key → the default) into the one daemon-side type `UserPrefs` (Plan 6's `PrefsPort.user()` returns it): `language` (L_h's first source; the loader's L_c through `loaderLang`, the daemon's through `claudeLang(resolveHumanLang({user, team, osLocale}).lang)`; a value outside the six languages reads as absent, I18N-02), `wake.turnEdits` (`allow`, read by the row-3 call for Plan 6's binding), `wake.enabled` (true), `wake.maxPerSeatPerHour` (4), `notifications.os` (true), `since.recap` and `since.claudePointer` (default `true`, glossary §29.5); Plan 7 adds the strict schema (`UserJson`, loaded by its `loadUserJson(fs, dataDir)`), validation and the setting verbs — roadmap row 7 item 3 gives Plan 7 the keys, while Plan 5's recap and gate must honour them now — cost if wrong: a mistyped key behaves as the default until Plan 7. (OQ-23 (r3), X-7.2, F04-12)
- **R5-13 Acting verbs for O-HUMANONLY.** Ruling: the daemon side of SEC-25 is complete in Plan 5 (evidence store, mint refusals, ≤ 60 s Δmono via `mono_ns`, single use, HMAC-tagged `evidence.jsonl` ingestion, the `acting{verb, args_sha256, tty, ancestry_has_claude, mode, params}` frame, the ≤ 3 s wait done daemon-side by `EvidenceStore.await`, `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN`, the 60-min human lease renewal) behind `ActingVerbRegistry`; Plan 5 registers `done` (Plan 2 `Engine.done()`) and `resolve <id> --publish|--discard|--park|--restore` (Plan 3 `Engine.resolve`, `humanEvidence: true`) and adds their two `cli.js` dispatch lines; Plan 7 registers the other acting verbs on the same registry and uses this `acting` frame (X-58: structured `params`, the arguments' identity only as `args_sha256`; the handler result widens with `data?: WireJson` for previews, phases and single-use tokens; `handle` renews the human lease once per accepted request, Plan 7 calls `grantHuman(minutes)` only for `start --human`), and Plan 7 Task 11 deletes Plan 5's two `done`/`resolve` registrations when it registers its own handlers (X-59); `ancestry_has_claude` = env `CLAUDECODE` present, or a claude ancestor found by a `/proc` walk ≤ 16 levels on Linux; on win32 and darwin the process walk is Plan 7's (it needs a process runner; accepted by the reconciliation, Plan 5 item 18) and until then only the env test runs — safe because route (a) also requires `stdin` and `stdout` to be TTYs, which no Claude tool call has — cost if wrong: on win32/darwin a pty-wrapping trick passes route (a) until Plan 7 (refused by evidence otherwise). (Q-9, C9, R2-J2, X-P7-10, R1-206)
- **R5-14 D1 (owner decision, final): who sees `last_check`.** Ruling (as ruled by X-01, 2026-10-08; replaces the grant-gated draft): **no gate**. The daemon puts `last_check` (one slot per open seat CS, glossary §29.8 shape, with `params.last_code`/`params.last_params` for `--json`, read from Plan 3's `ChangeSets.lastCheck`, X-05) into every reply to an all-paths `flow` request with `detail: 'flow'` (`/cowork status --flow`) and into every `wrap` reply, whoever asked — `tty`, `pipe` or `slash` mode, a human's `!` line or Claude's own Bash call (a pull Claude made, not a push); it never tests evidence, a grant, a route or the caller's ancestry for them, so the `flow` and `wrap` frames carry no `tty` or `ancestry_has_claude` field (09 §3.11 as final in rev 2.5). A path-scoped `flow` reply (`show <path>`) and an all-paths `flow` with `detail: 'section'` (plain `status`'s section 6, R5-57) carry `last_check: null`; there the CLI (Plan 7) prints an O10 entry's first line and then the pointer `UI_FLOW_LAST_WITHHELD` once (10 §3.5.1, O10 row), and Plan 5's `last_withheld` is defined as "the reply holds an O10 entry and no `last_check`". Nothing about `last_check` ever reaches a §3.14.1 carrier, a hook output, a human mirror, an OS notification, a teammate, the board, `status.json`, a log or a file; slash output is shown on the budget line and not metered (CCI-46; the CLI reports its size with `slash_shown`, R5-58). The read-only grant survives only for FEAT-F04-18's `since_ran` (X-02): `EvidenceStore.grant(seat, 'since', argsSha256, promptId)` / `awaitGrant('since', …)`, single use, `READ_ONLY_GRANT_VERBS = ['since']`; the UserPromptExpansion frame still carries `args_sha256` for every `/cowork` verb (SE5-02, narrowed). — glossary §29.1 D1 and 09 §3.11 are final ("the daemon does not test who asked"; X-01) — cost if wrong: none; it is spec text. (DEC-01, F-D1, R2-K7, Q-2, OQ-02 (r3), OQ-16 (r2), R1-305, F14-02)
- **R5-15 Exit gate.** Ruling: the exit criterion is roadmap row 5's — O-LEASE, O-SEAT, O-TURN-ORIGIN and O-HUMANONLY on 1,000 `seats` seeds per OS, hook p95 ≤ 100 ms on windows-latest (06 test 8, TC-CCI-03), T-F04-return, -restart and -since-first — plus O-EDITCHECK with its mutants M37 and M47, which Plan 3 deferred to Plan 5 (R3-49, R3-50) and which no other plan's exit names (roadmap edit SE5-11); every `seats` run also asserts O-NONLIVE, O-TOKEN, O-NO-ORPHAN, O-RECORD-FIRST, O-ASK-ONCE, O-IPC (their 11 §3.4 rows) and Plans 2/3's oracles (TST-07 "every SIM run MUST assert the oracles of §3.4"), so a failure of any of them fails the exit run; claim-dependent rows (O-RECORD-FIRST's K1 half, TC-CCI-01's fake peer holding `foo()`) run with Plan 4's binding (R5-01) — roadmap row 5, 11 §3.4, Plan 3 R3-49/R3-50 — cost if wrong: the exit run is stricter than the roadmap line by one oracle Plan 5 builds anyway. (R1-953, R2-L3, OQ-18 (r2), OQ-20 (r3), BL3-09, R2-L4)
- **R5-16 SIM with fake seats.** Ruling (X-119: the in-process SIM seat module is `test/sim/fakeSeats.ts`; Plan 4 keeps `test/sim/seats.ts` for its `ClaimsModel`/scripted seats): the seeded `seats` SIM runs fake seats in process: each fake seat feeds the hook JSON of a scripted tool call to the same `runCore` (minus process exit and stdio), speaks to the real `ClaudeDaemon` over the real `Ipc` port with the real handshake, and runs the MCP server's `handleTool` path for tool calls; seat ids, doorbell nonces and handshake nonces come from the peer's `seededRandom` (11 §3.2: "seat ids are seeded"); timing is never a SIM decision input (deadlines are asserted only in FakeHost and latency tests); the real-process FakeHost suite of 11 §3.3 step 5 (real `dist/hook.js` and `dist/mcp.js` children, the Inbox stub, measured host behaviours: exit 2 honoured, exit 1/other/timeout/ENOENT fail open) is a separate, unseeded per-OS leg that asserts the same four exit oracles over its own trace and carries the p95 gate — 11 §3.3 step 5 asks for real processes and TST-03 for byte-identical seeded traces, which real 1.5 s deadlines on a loaded CI runner cannot give; the deviation is listed (SE5-10) — cost if wrong: a process-boundary defect is found by the FakeHost leg instead of the seeded run. (Q-13, OQ-19 (r3), R1-954, R2-L2, BL2-16)
- **R5-17 SIM and FakeHost start.** Ruling: peers start through Plan 2's `Engine.start` (as Plans 2 and 3 SIM do) plus Plan 5's `setupLiveRepo` test helper (plugin data dir, `createIpcKey`, the `live` marker); the 11 §3.3 step 4 start "through the real CLI entry (`dist/cli.js start`) with a test TTY" joins when Plan 7's `start` exists — the verb is Plan 7's (roadmap row 7) — cost if wrong: none (the same engine entry). (OQ-17 (r2))
- **R5-18 The `live` marker before Plan 7.** Ruling: only in the IPC mode (`daemon.js run --data-dir …`, R5-19; Plan 2/3 tests and SIM runs without `--data-dir` write no marker), Plan 2's `daemon.js run` writes `<gitdir>/cowork/live` (content: `canonRoot(main worktree)` + LF from `src/data/canonRoot.ts` — the same string Plans 7 and 8b write, X-63) after a successful `startDispatch` and removes it at a clean stop or drain, flagged as a stopgap that Plan 7's `start`/`stop` replace (X-69: Plan 7 Task 8 deletes this writer); `paused` stays written only by the loader and the human (R5-38); the marker backstop (Task 9) treats this removal as a stop sequence — glossary §6 (`live` written by `/cowork start`), Plan 5's SIM and FakeHost need live repos, Plans 2/3 already ship `daemon.js` stopgaps (R-28, R3-02) — cost if wrong: one more stopgap Plan 7 deletes. (Q-15, X-2.8)
- **R5-19 Daemon process modes.** Ruling: a spawned daemon runs `daemon.js run --repo <canonical root> --branch <b> --data-dir <CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA> --plugin-root <CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT> --spawned`; it binds the endpoint (the single-instance authority, SEC-21) before taking `daemon.lock` (advisory, R-26); a second instance gets `EADDRINUSE`, runs the handshake as a client and exits 0 quietly on success (`reuse`) or refuses `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER` (security event, one notification); `recover|resume|resolve` keep refusing while a daemon holds the lock; the branch comes from `<gitdir>/HEAD` read as a file (no git process) and a detached HEAD spawns nothing (gating events take G4 until Plan 7's HEAD table decides); `daemon.js run` without `--data-dir` keeps Plan 2/3 behaviour (no IPC) for their tests — 06 §3.17.2 (one spawn routine, single instance by the endpoint bind, plugin paths as arguments), Plan 2 R-26 — cost if wrong: a branch switch while Claude runs is handled only by Plan 7. (BL2-12, OQ-22 (r3), R2-I2, X-2.8)

**Capture, signals and edit checkpoints:**
- **R5-20 Capture identity at the trigger.** Ruling: before replying to a hook that triggers a capture — an edit checkpoint (trigger (a) or (b)), a closing signal, a green or tool checkpoint — Plan 5 computes within 150 ms the content identity of the CS's paths, `expect: Record<path, OidOrAbsent>` (the edited path's post-image oid from the bytes already read for confirmation; every other CS path stat-checked against its newest candidate or ready pair and re-hashed by one short read only when stat-dirty; for a TXN close, the close snapshot's oids), hashing by route (b) without `-w` (no object), and sends the signal with `expect` without awaiting `Engine.signal`; Plan 3's out-of-cycle iteration keeps the 300 ms quiet period (01 §3.5 (c), never waived for hook-named paths) and materializes; an edit candidate whose materialized `S_disk` differs from `expect` on any CS path is dropped (`last_check{skip: slow_read}`, never taken late) and a closing, green or tool capture that differs is taken from the materialized content and marked late (`cowork_checkpoint` returns `captured: 'late'`); identity not computed in 150 ms → edit: skip `slow_read`; closing/green/tool: sent without `expect` and late. Objects: no Plan 5 hook path writes one; the blobs Plan 3's iteration writes when it materializes a candidate are 01 §3.8 (a)'s occasion ("`merge-tree --merge-base=A R TIP` needs R as a tree"), so 06 test 46's loose-object clause is asserted for hook paths and the growth of failing candidates (bounded by ≤ 1 running + 1 waiting per CS) is Plan 7's soak measurement (Plan 3 R3-08's Q14 carry); every spec sentence that says a candidate writes no object is aligned by SE5-03 (the alternative, a scratch object directory for candidates, is rejected: Plan 3's materializing iteration writes through the one object store, and a second store would need alternates and its own cleanup) — 06 §3.8.4 ("content at that edit", "dropped, never taken late") and 01 §3.5/§3.8 both hold; Plan 3 R3-41 pre-authorizes "Plan 5 swaps the candidate's content source behind the same `Candidate` shape"; whether the check reads trees or bytes stays Plan 3's `EditCheckPort` (tree oids) bound by Plan 4 (X5-23) — cost if wrong: failing candidates leave unreferenced loose blobs until gc, and a capture taken 300 ms after a closing hook can include an edit made in those 300 ms (labelled late). (C7, Q-1, OQ-01 (r3), OQ-06 (r2), OQ-15 (r3), R2-C2, R1-605, SE5-03)
- **R5-21 The per-CS edit-check queue.** Ruling: Plan 5 keeps per CS at most one candidate in flight (its `checkpoint{source: 'edit'}` sent, not yet settled) and one waiting (a newer trigger replaces the waiting one, never the one in flight); it sends the waiting candidate when `Engine.onCandidateSettled` reports the previous one; Plan 3's iteration coalescing stays underneath; the check's scheduling is Plan 4's pool, whose classes are `fg`, `save`, `bg` (X-21: Plan 5 maps gate parses, confirmations, edit checks and T0 to `fg` and undo views to `bg`; Plan 5 adds no class and edits no pool) — 06 §3.8.4 step 3 ("at most one check runs and one waits per CS"), Plan 3's `Engine.signal` ("at most one in flight") — cost if wrong: one extra check per burst. (R1-605, BL3-03, OQ-29 (r3))
- **R5-22 `last_check` skips and `edit_n`.** Ruling: Plan 5 numbers every edit-checkpoint trigger of a CS with `edit_n` (1-based, skipped triggers counted) and records `last_check{kind: 'skipped', skip}` for skips 3–6 (`shrink`, `slow_read`, `base_moved`, `batch`); skips 1–2 (CS held by a PIN, CS with a SHADOW) record nothing; `fail`/`conflict`/`incomplete` come from Plan 3's HLD-67 step 4 and `stale` from Plan 3's T0 drop, with `check`/`path`/`line` from the verdict's `entries?.[0]` (else `failed[0]`) and `findings[0]` (X-06, X-07, X-08; Plan 5 reads `entries`, never a re-typed `failed`); the store and those writes are Plan 3's (X-05: `src/holds/lastCheck.ts`, one instance `ChangeSets.lastCheck`, Plan 3 Task 10); Task 13 creates nothing and stops for a ruling if it is absent; it is cleared when the CS closes or a passing capture with a higher `edit_n` lands, never persisted — glossary §29.8, 10 §3.5.1 — cost if wrong: none (ruled). (BL3-04, X-3.1, XP-07, F14-06)
- **R5-23 `checks_run` and green checkpoints.** Ruling: a PostToolUse of a `Bash|PowerShell` call whose command starts with one of `checks.checkpoint` (read through Plan 3's lenient `TeamConfig.checks.checkpoint`) emits `checks_run{ok: exit_status === 0}` when `tool_response` exposes a status and `checks_run{ok: true}` when it does not (06 §8 Q6 default), and an ok call also emits `checkpoint{source: 'green'}`; a PostToolUseFailure of such a call emits `checks_run{ok: false}` and no checkpoint; `cowork_checkpoint` emits `checkpoint{source: 'tool'}` and no `checks_run` — Plan 3 R3-43 ("a 05/06 gap: Plan 5 feeds it"), HLD-37, 06 §3.12.1 — cost if wrong: a `checks=` trailer value (SE5-06). (BL3-02, OQ-05 (r2), OQ-13 (r3), R2-C3)
- **R5-24 Signals and replies.** Ruling: hook handlers hand CS signals to a per-seat `SignalPump` that calls `Engine.signal` in order and never awaits it on the reply path (it resolves only after a 300 ms quiet period) *(narrowed by ruling R-F2-5, F2 #32 #62: the one awaited send is Task 12's `post.edit` effect, which resolves once Plan 3's `queueSignal` has run — right away on an idle engine, after the iteration in flight otherwise, never after a quiet period, since an effect asks for no out-of-cycle iteration)*; a capture-at-trigger's 150 ms bound applies to the identity of R5-20, not to Plan 3's capture; a nonce-carrying UserPromptSubmit emits no `prompt` signal (CCI-37) — 06 §3.4.4 ("no reply waits for" the edit check), Plan 3's `Engine.signal` contract — cost if wrong: none. (OQ-15 (r3), BL3-01, R2-C1)

**Leases, reads, defer set and attribution:**
- **R5-25 Apply coalescing and DEGRADED sets in the defer set.** Ruling: a path held back by apply coalescing (fully read by a seat that is `MID_TURN` by activity and sent a hook in the last 60 s: at most one apply into it per 2 min; for a seat in `PULL_MODE`: only at its Stop or UserPromptSubmit) and a path in a DEGRADED seat's 30-min read or edit set enter ENG-31's defer set with the existing reason `read_lease` (Plan 2's `DeferReason` union, `meta.pending` and `parseMeta` unchanged; HLD-54's 60 s INCOMING record applies as to every deferral); Plan 5 tags each such path in memory with its source (`coalesce_mid_turn`, `coalesce_pull_mode`, `degraded`), the seat number and the Δmono of the last apply into that seat's read set, and the F14 flow inputs classify a coalescing-only path I4a/I4b before I3 — CCI-23 ("`deferredPaths` … apply coalescing including PULL_MODE as ENG-31 defer-set reasons", which the writer needs to defer the whole group, INV-GROUP), 10 §3.5.1 ("whatever reason value the engine stores … never I3 `READ_LEASE`"), 06 §3.11.2a vs §3.7.5's two `MID_TURN` readings combined — cost if wrong: a status label (SE5-05). (C8, C20, Q-4, OQ-08 (r2), OQ-05 (r3), OQ-04 (r3), R1-506, R2-G4, F14-04)
- **R5-26 Plan 5's own short read.** Ruling: hook reads use Plan 5's `shortRead(fs, abs)` (bytes in memory, `lstat` before and after, one re-read, an `unstable` result carrying the second read's bytes, 06 §3.7.6 "taken from the second read"), not Plan 2's `stagedRead` (which copies into `tmp/stage` for git); oids come from Plan 2's route (b) `hash-object --stdin --path=<p>` without `-w`; `DiskSnapshots` is 04 §2's `diskSnapshotId` table (in memory, 64 MB total, beyond it spilled under `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/stage/` and deleted at release, never serialized) — 01 §3.8 "Hook requests", REG-35, glossary §26.1 W1/W3/W6 — cost if wrong: two read helpers with one rule set (a shared conformance test). (C21, X-2.2, BL2-08, XP-02, R1-507)
- **R5-27 Expiry-guard granularity.** Ruling: when an INFLIGHT lease expires before its Post on a path the verifying writer wrote after acquisition, `observedEdit(path)` answers false for every delta on that path until the seat's next confirmed edit-class Post on it — stricter than "that tool call's hunks", never weaker (the taint only holds bytes; B1 and HLD act on content) — cost if wrong: an unrelated edit to that path in the same window is also taint-checked (held as a PATCH, never lost). (C19, Q-11, R1-505)
- **R5-28 Attribution binding and D2.** Ruling: `hookAttribution` answers `removalAttributed(p)` true inside an INFLIGHT or TXN window of a live seat or the 3 s formatter grace after the seat's edit-class Post on p; keeps Plan 2's R-25 answer for `shrinkAttributed` (true: the human two-tick rule stays in the snapshot); `observedEdit(p)` true only for edit-class windows and their grace (minus R5-27 taints; shell windows are taint-checked as UNOBSERVED); `hookAttributed(p)` true for any seat window; no port signature changes — D2's content-keyed exemption stays inside Plan 3's `t0Shrink` caller (Plan 3/F10 kernel) — 06 §3.8.3, HLD-26, R-25 — cost if wrong: none for D2 (Plan 3 owns it). (BL2-02, OQ-21 (r3), R1-604, DEC-02)
- **R5-29 Seat tags of late writes.** Ruling: a delta first seen after a TXN closed by its cap, or written by a `run_in_background` process after its tool returned, is human-class and carries a display-only seat tag until that seat's next Stop/StopFailure or its seat end; F04's "every path of its first delta is seat-tagged" reads this tag — HLD-35, 06 §3.12.2, F04 §9 Q1 default — cost if wrong: one extra or one missing F04 mark. (OQ-28 (r3), BL3-13)
- **R5-30 `pathState` at the gate.** Ruling: `'busy'` (the write group did not finish within 250 ms) → G1 `PATH_BUSY`; `'pending'`'s reason is read from memory (`meta.pending[p]` or the lease table); `frozen` and `incident` are not gate outcomes (the gate keeps evaluating under FREEZE, new claims stay TENTATIVE) — ENG-38, glossary §11, 06 §3.5.2 — cost if wrong: none. (C17, OQ-27 (r3), BL2-06)
- **R5-31 Held ranges after a PostToolUse.** Ruling: after every PostToolUse on p Plan 5 refreshes p's held ranges in memory by mapping the last `maskStep` ranges from the materialized `S_disk[p]` to the post-image `diskSnapshotId` with Plan 4's `mapRanges(…, 'local')` (`TOMBSTONE`/`WHOLE_FILE` → the whole file held); with `noRegions` the last `maskStep` ranges stay (Plan 3 R3-62) — HLD-21 ("refreshed … after every PostToolUse on that path") — cost if wrong: before Plan 4 a held range can be stale by one edit until the next materialization. (OQ-14 (r3), BL3-05, R1-402 row 11)
- **R5-32 Event-loop budget.** Ruling: Task 1 measures the daemon's longest main-thread slice (`monitorEventLoopDelay`) while Plan 2/3's loop applies six 1.3 MB BOM+CRLF files and records it in the ledger; Task 10's 06 test 8 enforces ≤ 20 ms; any Plan 2/3 step over 20 ms is sliced by Plan 5 (yielding through the `Scheduler` port between chunks) as an additive edit, never by changing its result — CCI-12 — cost if wrong: the roadmap's p95 gate fails late. (OQ-18 (r3), BL2-21)

**Seats and turns:**
- **R5-33 Seat numbers ("session N").** Ruling: N is assigned when the seat record is created, from a per-daemon counter starting at 1, kept for the seat's life (so `/clear`, `/compact`, in-process `/resume` and `/fork`, which keep the seat, keep N), never reused while the daemon runs and restarted at 1 by a daemon restart; status section 9 and F14's `{s}` list seats by N; N never leaves this PC (no board op) — 06 §3.11.1 ("numbered per daemon"), INV-CCI-SEAT; a never-reused number names one seat in every text it appeared in, while a reused one could make an older "your other session (session 2)" note point at a new window — cost if wrong: numbers grow within one long daemon run. (Q-3, OQ-03 (r2), OQ-03 (r3), R1-802, R2-B3, F14-03)
- **R5-34 Seat health events.** Ruling (X-104: these health codes, `LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED` included, also enter 8a's closed code set `codes.ts` + `en.ts` with their fold effect, in the commit that posts them or in Plan 5's viewer follow-up, X-91): each change of a seat's wiring or flags queues one `event{kind: 'health', reason_code}` for the next governed push (codes only): `HOOK_ONLY`, `NOHOOKS`, `DEGRADED`, `PAUSED` (existing codes), `SEAT_FULL`, `SEAT_NO_MCP`, `SEAT_PULL_MODE` (new); entering lease idle queues `PEER_IDLE` (new); `seat_start` waits in the outbox under FREEZE (not allowed while frozen), `seat_end` and health events go board-only — 06 §3.11.2 ("published as a `health` event (codes only)"), §3.17.1 ("the board shows the peer idle"), SEC-24, glossary §8 — cost if wrong: four registry codes (SE5-08). (OQ-04 (r2), OQ-12 (r3), R2-B5, BL2-14)
- **R5-35 Leases (CCI-50).** Ruling: a seat lease is valid while the seat is not `ENDED` and either its MCP heartbeat is ≤ 45 s old (3 × 15 s) or, for `HOOK_ONLY`/`NO_MCP` wiring, its claude pid is alive by the 5 s check; any hook renews it; the human lease is 60 min from each accepted acting verb; `SeatLeasePort.grantHuman(minutes)` (1–480) is exposed for Plan 7's `start --human`; `idleMs()` is 0 while any lease is valid, else Δmono since the last ended — 06 §3.17.1, glossary §15, R-11 — cost if wrong: an idle Claude window keeps its daemon alive (by design while the process lives). (BL2-01, OQ-12 (r3), R2-I1, X-P7-06)
- **R5-36 The single turn-origin rule.** Ruling: implement 06 §3.12.2 exactly — a UserPromptSubmit with non-empty `nonces[]` is a doorbell (confirms its doorbell's events, clears that pending wake, no `prompt` signal, refreshes nothing, releases no lease and closes no TXN mid-turn); a turn is wake-originated iff its `prompt_id` differs from the one recorded at the `sent` post and (a) its UserPromptSubmit carries the posted nonce or (b) its first hook after the post is not a UserPromptSubmit under the 60 s quiet condition, with no nonce-less UserPromptSubmit since the post; (b) can only classify as wake; origin clears at that turn's Stop/StopFailure; a pending wake expires at its nonce or 60 s; a `/cowork` expansion is wake-classified while a wake is pending with a different `prompt_id`, when it carries a `cws-` nonce, or when the seat's current turn is wake — 06 owns the rule ("the single turn-origin rule … COO- uses it"); 07 §3.15's "removed" wording is aligned (SE5-09); FakeHost always fires UserPromptSubmit, so O-TURN-ORIGIN exercises (a) and a unit test covers (b) — cost if wrong: (b) classifies a rare human turn as wake (the restricted side). (OQ-01 (r2), R1-901…R1-905, R2-C5, R2-C6)
- **R5-37 Doorbells in Plan 5.** Ruling (confirmed by X-42: Plan 5 posts, Plan 6 decides; `WakePolicy` gains the optional `onRingResult?(seatId, eventIds, result: PostResult | 'not_rung', nonce)` (the nonce per verify pass V-10); Plan 6 deletes its own poster, `RingResult`, `mintNonce` and `NONCE_RE`): Plan 5 ships the mechanism — `mintNonce` (`cws-` + 16 lowercase hex from the `Random` port; the RingDriver mints it before asking the policy and passes it to `shouldRing`, `ring` and `onRingResult` — verify pass V-10, Plan 6's O-2), `Ringer.ring(seatId, eventIds, text, nonce)` (no ring to a `MID_TURN` seat, text ≤ 1,500 never starting with `/` and carrying the nonce exactly once), the `ring`/`ring_result` frames with a 5 s internal wait for `ring_result` (missing → `error`), the pending wake on `sent` (registered in flight at the ring frame's send, 06 §3.12.2 as amended, ruling R-F2-17; F2 #61), delivery only by a nonce-carrying UserPromptSubmit — and a `WakePolicy` port bound to `neverRing` in production and to a scripted policy in SIM and FakeHost, so 06 test 30, TC-COO-14's Plan 5 half and O-TURN-ORIGIN run before Plan 6 binds the policy and its text — 06 §3.16.5 ("COO- decides whether to ring and builds the text"); the 5 s bound is the IPC liveness bound, the host-side test is the 100 ms close — cost if wrong: none. (Q-8, OQ-02 (r2), R2-H16, R1-901)
- **R5-38 UserPromptExpansion frame and evidence fields.** Ruling: the loader checks `expansion_type = slash_command` and `command_name ∈ {cowork, cowork-sync:cowork}` and stores neither; the frame payload carries `command_name`, `verb`, `command_source`, `args_sha256` (every verb, SE5-02), `nonces[]` (every `cws-[0-9a-f]{16}` token of the expansion's prompt, SE5-02) and `evidence?`; the evidence record is 09 §2's `{seat_key, prompt_id, verb, args_sha256, mono_ns, used:false}`; the loader writes no evidence when it sees a nonce; `pause` and the marker step of `resume` stay native in the loader and work without `core.js` — 09 owns the encoding (SEC-26 "only as `{prompt_id, verb, args_sha256}`"), SEC-25 step 2 needs the nonce test — cost if wrong: one payload field. (C11, C16, Q-7, R1-206, R2-J1)
- **R5-39 Turn items and `unacked`.** Ruling: Plan 5's `TurnItems` records, per seat and agent, the denied, held, recorded and not-shared items of the current turn (SHADOW ids at K1, PIN ids at K2/backstop, `NOT_SHARED` paths) and is the HLD-23 `unacked(seat)` source for Stop and digests; X-13: it is the one source — Plan 3 Task 12a drops its `Engine.unacked` stub (F-P3-7) — HLD-23, 06 §3.15 — cost if wrong: Plan 6 reads `TurnItems` instead of `Engine.unacked` (one import). (BL3-06, R2-F1, H-7)

**Texts, codes and ports:**
- **R5-40 Fixed texts.** Ruling: the loader renders glossary §11's G4 text verbatim with no cause slot for every G4 (daemon down or hook error), 07's `T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL` for a load failure and 07's `PAUSED` line; `commands/cowork.md` carries 10-product §3.3's three-sentence block verbatim; `DAEMON_DOWN` rides additionalContext and `systemMessage` — 07 owns Claude-facing text, UX-05 the command block, 06 §3.14.4's code table and CCI-44 are the more specific carrier rows — cost if wrong: a cause word missing from G4 (SE5-07). (C12, C13, C15, Q-12, R1-210, R1-224)
- **R5-41 MCP refusal codes Plan 3 left open.** Ruling (recorded as ruled by X-14; Plan 3's ledger records the same mapping, F-P3-9): `claudeRestoreRefusal` → `not_owner` `NOT_OWN_RECORD`, `binary` `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY`, `quarantine` `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY`, `state` `TERMINAL_RECORD` when the record is terminal else the new `RESTORE_NOT_ALLOWED`; `ResolveResult.locked` → `PATH_BUSY`; an undo-view request for an unknown cs id → `UNKNOWN_ID`, for an ambiguous one `AMBIGUOUS_ID` with ≤ 5 qualified candidates, for an own CS that is open, not shared or older than the 14-day window → the CS summary without an undo section — 06 §3.16.3 ("anything else is refused with HLD-'s code"), Plan 3 R3-54 ("a recorded gap") — cost if wrong: one registry code (SE5-08). (OQ-12 (r2), OQ-16 (r3), OQ-17 (r3), BL3-08)
- **R5-42 Secret scanning before Plan 7.** Ruling (as ruled by X-51): notes and undo-view diff lines go through Plan 5's `NoteScanPort` (renamed from `SecretScanPort`, which stays Plan 7's engine port name; async `scan(text, kind): Promise<{available, hit}>`); the scanner itself (`scanSecrets`, `scanHit`, `scanD2`) is Plan 7's alone and Plan 7 binds this port to `scanHit`; Plan 6 uses it and creates no scanner; Plan 5's production binding `unavailableScan` fails closed: `cowork_note` (and a `note` on `cowork_request`) returns `{ok: false, code: 'NOTE_SCAN_UNAVAILABLE'}` (new code) and `cowork_show(cs_id)` returns the CS summary with no diff lines; test builds bind `canaryScan` (detects `COWORK_CANARY_[A-Za-z0-9_]+`) so TC-F10-12, 09 test 10 and the canary tests run; Plan 7 binds SEC-07's scanner (`scanHit`); a redact-nothing stub is never bound — CCI-68 ("Every rendered diff line MUST pass `scanSecrets`"), CCI-48 note rule, Plan 2 R-06 (scanner in Plan 7), D4 (nothing released before Plan 8b) — cost if wrong: notes are unavailable in a pre-Plan-7 build. (OQ-11 (r2), OQ-10 (r3), F10-01)
- **R5-43 Metering.** Ruling: the meter counts every character cowork causes to be injected (additionalContext, reasons, Stop blocks, doorbells) and every `systemMessage`, the F04 recap in full (CCI-46) and the other mirrors too; tool results Claude requested and slash-mode CLI output (its size reported by the CLI's `slash_shown` request, R5-58) are shown on the budget line and not counted — 06 §3.16.1 ("a `systemMessage` can reach the model's context"; "This chapter does not decide whether other `systemMessage` mirrors are metered") — cost if wrong: a seat reaches `PULL_MODE` slightly earlier (SE5-13). (OQ-07 (r2), F04-07, R2-G1)
- **R5-44 OSC 9.** Ruling: Plan 5 emits no `terminalSequence`; INV-NOSILENT is met by the `systemMessage` and status lines; Plan 7 adds OSC 9 with its notification work once a host field identifies an interactive session — no measured hook input marks a session interactive — cost if wrong: one interactive cue missing until Plan 7. (CCI-44, R1-321)

**IPC and security:**
- **R5-45 Phases of the IPC schema.** Ruling: the schema is keyed by (phase, direction, `t`): before the handshake completes only `hello{v,n1}`/`srv`/`cli` are legal; after it only session frames, where `hello` is the MCP hello; an unknown `t`, a schema failure or a frame over 1 MiB closes the connection; Plans 6/7 add kinds through `registerFrameKind` — SEC-22 — cost if wrong: none. (OQ-15 (r2), R2-K5)
- **R5-46 Security log.** Ruling: `securityLog` writes `<gitdir>/cowork/log/security.jsonl` (codes and typed params only) and mirrors each line to the daemon log at level `security`; it is the daemon's one security Log, which `run.ts` also hands to Plan 4 as `RegionDeps.securityLog` and Plan 7 wraps (`secureLog`, rotation) (X-34); Plan 7's rotation covers both — ENG-03, 09 §3.11, §3.13 — cost if wrong: none. (OQ-25 (r3), BL2-19)
- **R5-47 Plugin data directory.** Ruling (as amended by R5-56 and X-61): one module `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts` (`pluginDataDir`, `resolveDataDir`, `pluginIdOf`; imports only `node:*`) for core, MCP server, CLI, `cli.js view` and Plans 7/8b, with the loader's inlined copy kept equal by `loader.parity.test.ts`: `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` when set and absolute, else `<CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR when set and absolute, else <home>/.claude>/plugins/data/<plugin id>` derived from the plugin root (`CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`, else the running bundle's `dist/..`); the spawner passes the resolved directory as `--data-dir`; SIM and FakeHost run with and without the variable; a host check (Task 4, recorded in the ledger) pins the derived directory name — 06 §8 Q10, 09 §8 Q2 defaults — cost if wrong: one path rule. (OQ-10 (r2), R1-211)
- **R5-48 Windows ACL runner.** Ruling: `src/sec/winAcl.ts` is the one SEC process runner (fixed argv, `shell: false`, `windowsHide: true`, no user input in argv except the key path): `whoami /user /fo csv /nh`, `icacls <file> /inheritance:r /grant:r *<SID>:F`, PowerShell `(Get-Acl -LiteralPath <file>).Sddl`; SIDs are compared, never localized names — SEC-20 — cost if wrong: none. (OQ-14 (r2), R2-K1)
- **R5-49 Install and refusal checks.** Ruling (confirmed by X-65: Plan 5's names; Plan 7 calls them and keeps no fallback; X-66: Plan 7 calls `spawnDaemon(o, d)` with two arguments, and its `ipcSelfTest`/`lastDaemonExit` are doctor code over Plan 5's `clientHandshake`/`cliRequest` and `status.json`/logs): Plan 5 provides the pure CCI-04 checks (`checkInstallScope`, `hostFloorRefusal`, `checkSecondCheckout` over `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA/repos.json` plus each listed gitdir's `live` marker, comparing repo identity and branch, `checkWorktreeShape`) and `staticPolicyCheck`; the G1 `CC_TOO_OLD` path and the SessionStart `static_policy_hits[]` are Plan 5's; Plan 7's `start`/`doctor` call the rest — CCI-04 owns the checks, LIF the procedure — cost if wrong: none. (Q-14, R1-104, R1-111, R2-B12, X-7.1)
- **R5-50 The Read payload host check.** Ruling: Task 12 includes a SPIKE-0-style host check that records the host's default row count for an unranged Read and whether `offset` is 0- or 1-based into the payload schema (`read.offset` normalized to 1-based rows); until it is recorded, Plan 5 uses `read.full` only (READ lease, full-read set) and records ranged reads by their raw `offset`/`limit`, and F05 (Plan 6) uses neither — 06 §8 rev 2.5 notes — cost if wrong: F05 waits for the check. (Q-6, F05-01, R1-301)
- **R5-51 Removal shutdown before Plan 7.** Ruling: Plan 5 implements `removalShutdown()` with LIF-36's steps (remove its own `live` markers first, no further working-tree write or snapshot, one push of captured ready trees through every gate plus `release{reason: stop}`, `seat_end` and `leave` when online — board-only when frozen —, exit, one `DAEMON_REMOVED` notification; `meta.stop_mode := removed` is the one LIF-36 step left out, because Plan 2's `meta` has no `stop_mode` field and adding one changes `meta` (LIF-37), A9-3); Plan 7 owns the notification text, adds `stop_mode` with its `stop`/`uninstall` verbs and its surfaces, and (X-69: Plan 7 owns LIF-36) its Task 11 replaces the body at the same signature with `stopSequence(…, mode 'removed')`, while its Task 8 deletes the R5-18 marker-writer stopgap — CCI-53 and INV-CCI-NO-ORPHAN are Plan 5 tests — cost if wrong: Plan 7 rewrites one function. (OQ-13 (r2), R2-I4, X-P7-08)

**F04 and F14 shares:**
- **R5-52 `meta.since` values.** Ruling: `meta.since` is `{seen_h, return_h, run_h}` with each a non-negative integer or `null`; absent = no marks (no `meta.v` change, LIF-37); the first human activity ever (no `seen_h`) sets `seen_h` and makes no recap pending (nothing was seen before); a daemon restart within 30 min is a return; the marks are held in memory and persisted only inside the next state transition or as the drain's single `meta`-only CAS (ENG-03, ENG-05) — 01 §3.2, FEAT-F04-03, glossary §29.7, 10 §3.9a (`RF_SINCE_ANCHOR` when `run_h` is null) — cost if wrong: one fewer recap on a brand-new clone. (OQ-11 (r3), BL2-11, F04-02, F04-08)
- **R5-53 F14 inputs.** Ruling (as ruled by X-11, X-74): Plan 5 provides the `flow` frame, its schema, the ≤ 2,000-entry cap with the omitted count, the `last_check` fill (R5-14: no gate, X-01) and its own `ClaudeFlowInputs` (seat numbers, activity, `PULL_MODE`, full-read sets with the last-apply instants, per-path defer reasons with their sources and since-instants, the TXN-open instant, the edit-check queue per CS); F14's engine inputs (`Engine.flowInputs(): EngineFlowInputs` — dirty, incoming with writer and display name, push-intent, exclusion and not-applicable paths, seat-CS rows, hold-registry rows, first-seen anchors — and `ChangeSets.quietLeftMs()`, `Governor.waitDetail()`, `hasShadow(path)`) are Plan 3's round-2 Task 18 (F-P3-3); the stage table and rendering are Plan 7's `flowEntries({engine: engine.flowInputs(), claude}, selector, {max})` behind `FlowPort.entries(claude, selector, max): {entries; omitted}` (its body keeps the event-loop block ≤ 20 ms), bound by Plan 5 to `noFlowEntries`; `withLastCheck` and `admitsLastCheck` are deleted names; Plan 5's tests use a synthetic `FlowPort` — cost if wrong: one signature (X-74). (OQ-24 (r3), BL2-17, X-2.9, F14-01, R1-305)
- **R5-54 Release (D4).** Ruling: no Plan 5 task publishes a package, a marketplace entry or a README; Plan 5's `README`/`SHA256SUMS`/marketplace parts of the package list are Plan 7's preparation and Plan 8b's release — owner decision D4 — cost if wrong: none. (DEC-04, OQ-26 (r3))

**Review round 1 (2026-10-08):**
- **R5-55 The composition root is pre-wired by Task 5 (A5-12).** Ruling: Task 5's `createClaudeDaemon` makes every cross-module binding and `attach` call whose owner shares a wave with another editor of `claudeDaemon.ts`, each through a lazy `ctx` delegate: `SeatRegistry.notice` → `ctx.events.queueSeatContext(seatId, code, params)`; `new BudgetMeter(clock, (seatId, on) => ctx.seats.setFlag(<seat of seatId>, 'PULL_MODE', on))`; the status observer (every `status.json` write) → `ctx.transitions.observe(status, nowMono)`; `attach(engine, core)` runs `await ctx.shadows.convertLeftovers()` as its first statement, then `ctx.checkpoints.attachEngine(engine)`, before it resolves (the server answers no hook frame before that, A5-6); every stub method these paths or an earlier-landed module's hot path reach returns the neutral value of its type (A5-5's list as extended in the Tasks preamble), so no engine iteration, gate request or digest reaches a throwing stub from Task 8 on; a test-only `ClaudeDaemonDeps.ctxOverrides` (and `seatHarness`'s `ctx` option) replaces `events`, `leases`, `health`, `readSets`, `views`, `turns`, `signals` or `reads` with doubles, so wave-mates' tests use doubles through the harness; Tasks 7, 11 and 13 then edit no composition file, while Tasks 6, 8, 9, 12, 14, 15 and 16 keep their one-line bindings because no wave-mate edits `claudeDaemon.ts` — "tasks in one wave touch disjoint files" and A5-5's "no engine iteration reaches a throwing stub" — cost if wrong: about ten lines Task 5 writes that a later task would have written.
- **R5-56 One plugin data directory for Plans 5 and 8a.** Ruling (as amended by X-61): the shared derivation that 8a's R-V11 names as its own cost if wrong ("Plans 5/7 replace `pluginDataDir` with the shared derivation (same signature)") lives in one module, `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts`: `pluginDataDir(env, home, platform, pluginRoot?)` (derived directory) and `resolveDataDir(env, home, platform, pluginRoot?): string | null` — `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` when set and absolute (a relative value is ignored, as in 8a); else `<CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR when set and absolute, else <home>/.claude>/plugins/data/<pluginIdOf(pluginRoot)>`; a plugin root of another shape gives `null` (fail closed, SEC-20: no other location is tried); the plugin root is `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` when set, else the running bundle's `dist/..`. The bodies move from 8a's landed `src/viz/cli/dataDir.ts`, which re-exports both names unchanged (8a's F-8a-2, run by the 8a controller before Plan 5 starts — verify pass V-12; Plan 5 never makes the move); `resolvePluginDataDir`, `PluginDataUnresolved`, Task 15's body replacement in 8a's file and the 8a-vs-Plan 5 parity test are deleted (one module, so `cli.js view` and `done`/`resolve` resolve one directory by construction); the dependency-free loader keeps its inlined copy, checked by `loader.parity.test.ts` against `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts` (the one place a copy is unavoidable) — both resolvers ship in one `dist/cli.js`, and `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` moves Claude Code's whole config root — cost if wrong: the R5-47 host check changes one path rule in one module and the loader copy.
- **R5-57 The `flow` request says what it shows; only `since_ran` awaits a grant.** Ruling (as ruled by X-01, X-02, X-57): `FlowSelector` is `{all: true; detail: 'section' | 'flow'} | {path: string}` (`detail` is the one part of the selector SE5-20 still adds to 09 §3.11, X-04); plain `status` (section 6's ≤ 5 entries plus the see-all count) sends `detail: 'section'` together with Plan 7's own `status_detail` request, `status --flow` sends `detail: 'flow'`, `show <path>` sends `{path}` (Plan 7's CLI, X5-36); only `detail: 'flow'` and `wrap` replies carry `last_check`, with no evidence or grant check (R5-14); `handleFlow` and `handleWrap` never wait. In `slash` mode, when route (a) fails, only `HumanActivity.sinceRan` calls `EvidenceStore.awaitGrant('since', argsSha256, {maxAgeMs: C.GRANT_MAX_AGE_MS, waitMs: C.CLI_EVIDENCE_WAIT_MS})`, which re-checks every 50 ms like `EvidenceStore.await` — 10 §3.5.1 and T-F14-claude-fail require plain `status` to carry no `params.last_code`, owner D1 (final) names `/cowork status --flow` and `/cowork wrap`, and 09 §3.13 step 4 / 06 §3.18 let the `!` line start before the `since` expansion's frame lands — cost if wrong: a slash `since_ran` without a grant is applied up to 3 s later (as an acting verb without evidence is).
- **R5-58 Slash output is reported to the daemon.** Ruling: the CLI reports the size of every slash-mode output it prints with a one-way session request `slash_shown{seat_key, chars}` (10 §3.3: "the CLI reports its size to the daemon, which shows it on the seat's budget line"); the CLI computes `seat_key = seatKeyOf(env, process.ppid)` exactly as hooks do (the SHA-256 of `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET` when the `!` process inherits it); the daemon charges `BudgetMeter.charge(seat, 'slash', chars)` on the seat with that key (`slashShown` grows, `injected` unchanged, CCI-46) and ignores an unknown key without a refusal; one-way requests (`since_ran`, `slash_shown`) get no reply frame (`ClaudeServer.onRequest` handlers may resolve `null`); Plan 5's `done`/`resolve` dispatch sends it after printing in slash mode and Plan 7's verbs (all of them, `status`, `wrap` and `since` included) call the same `reportSlashShown(chars, seatKeyOf(env, ppid), repo, d)` (X5-36, X-64); the kind is Plan 5's (X-57) and a 09 §3.11 addition (SE5-20, which now holds only this frame and `FlowSelector.detail`, X-04) — CCI-46 is Plan 5's rule and the budget line needs the number — cost if wrong: a slash output whose `!` shell lacks the socket variable reaches no budget line (nothing is over-counted).
- **R5-59 The undo view's description clause before the kernel lands.** Ruling (as ruled by X-09): `cowork_show` carries F10's description clause from Task 14 on; X5-14's `revertView(core, csId): Promise<RevertView>` is owned by Plan 3's new round-2 Task 17 "F10 revert kernel" (F-P3-2, `src/holds/revertCs.ts`, which also declares the `RevertView` type Plan 5 imports); until that task has merged and `plan3RevertView` delegates to it, `plan3RevertView` answers `summary_only` for every own CS, CCI-68 and TC-F10-12 are met only through `scriptedRevertView`, and Task 18's exit evidence lists this as a known gap — the schema is built in the client tier, which cannot see the daemon's binding without a new frame, L-MCP-SCHEMA must measure the clause, and nothing is released before Plan 8b (D4) — cost if wrong: a pre-release build advertises an undo diff that answers with the summary only.
- **R5-60 Codes from other registries, template-only overflow, two refusal codes.** Ruling: `ROUTED_CODES` in `codes.ts` lists the registry codes of other chapters that Plan 5 selects or delivers — `HOLD_PIN_CONTEXT`, `INCOMING_WAITING`, `CS_GROUP_WAITING`, `CS_READY_CANCELLED` (05), `FROZEN`, `UNFROZEN`, `OFFLINE`, `ONLINE`, `NONRETRYABLE`, `VERSION_PAUSED`, `INCIDENT` (glossary §8 and the 01/02 status rows), `STANDING_RULES` (07 §3.16.4) and `RF_UNKNOWN_VERB` (10 §1.2; declared in Plan 7's registry, X-79) — each pinned by a test against its chapter's text *(F2 #143, ruling R-F2-4: plus `CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE`, `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED`, `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY`, `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY` and `RESTORE_MARKERS`, whose one declaration is Plan 3's `HOLD_CODES` in `src/holds/codes.ts`)*; `ROUTED_CODES` is a reference list, never a declaration (X-117); `codeOnlyDigest`'s rules item is `STANDING_RULES`; an overflow line is a template-only `CarrierItem` (`code: ''`, `template: 'T-MORE' | 'T-STOP.more' | 'T-AHEAD.more'`; `T-MORE` is Plan 5's id for 07's composition sentence "and k more: cowork_status", whose `{k, where}` catalog row in en/ko Plan 6 Task 3 adds, X-49, X5-22); an acting verb with valid evidence and no registered handler returns `{ok: false, code: 'RF_UNKNOWN_VERB'}` in every build (the evidence is consumed; accepted by X-79, the 09 §3.13 sentence goes in SE5-08); a `flow` path selector that fails `validPathSyntax` returns `refused{code: 'FLOW_PATH_INVALID'}` (new, declared in Plan 5's `NEW_CODES`, SE5-08, X-79) — glossary §19's single registry (L-CODES) must find every code a Plan 5 module emits — cost if wrong: one template id renamed by Plan 6.
- **R5-61 F04-a is a hard prerequisite of Task 16.** Ruling (as ruled by X-75, X-76): Task 16 starts only after F04-a (the Plan 2 controller's follow-up F-P2-1, D5; gate P and 8a Tasks 2, 4, 11; it also gates Plan 6 Task 16 and Plan 7 Task 17) has merged; its first step runs `test/unit/p5-baseline.f04a.test.ts` (creating it when Task 1 could not) and stops for a controller ruling when `src/since/report.ts`, `src/data/zone.ts`, `SinceMarks` or the `meta.since` parse is absent or differs from X5-38 — T-F04-return and -restart, both in roadmap row 5's exit, need `sinceReport`'s recap counts and `T-SINCE` touches — cost if wrong: Wave J waits for F04-a.
- **R5-62 Seat-harness defaults.** Ruling: `seatHarness` grants every peer a human lease at start (`humanLeaseMin`, default 480 min, `SeatLeasePort.grantHuman`), so a long virtual-time scenario stays leased like a human at the keyboard; lease and lifetime tests (Task 5's lease test, Task 9's lifetime test "kill every MCP server and stop hooks…", Task 18's `seats-x-orphan-*` through `seatsSetup`'s own A17-2 rule) pass `humanLeaseMin: 0`; each harness daemon's `pluginVersion` and `hookProto` are options (defaults `PLUGIN_VERSION`, `HOOK_PROTO`) — without a human lease a FULL fake seat with no heartbeat is idle after 45 s (R5-35), its daemon stops syncing at 2 min and exits at 10 min, so a 31-min F04 scenario would test a respawn instead of the return rule; CCI-52's version table needs a daemon of proto 3 — cost if wrong: a lifetime test that forgets `humanLeaseMin: 0` fails loudly (it asserts the drain).
- **R5-63 Reproducing a failure.** Ruling: every seeded `seats` failure prints `tsx tools/sim/run-seeds.ts --profile seats --seed <s>` and keeps under `test-results/sim/<seed>/` the trace, the `recordingIpc` frames, each peer's `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` and `<gitdir>/cowork/log`; a rerun of that command reproduces the same failing step (a test asserts it); every FakeHost cell reruns alone with `npm run test:fakehost -- --scenario <name> --cell <i>` and keeps under `test-results/fakehost/<scenario>/<cell>/` the hook stdin JSON, the loader's stdout, stderr and exit code, the daemon log, `status.json` and the per-call latency samples; the exit run allows no retry and its evidence lists every failing seed with its rerun command — seeded legs replay byte-identically (TST-03, R5-16) while the FakeHost leg is unseeded, so its kept artifacts are what makes a red cell diagnosable — cost if wrong: CI artifacts grow, on failure only.
- **R5-64 The M45 site.** Ruling (confirmed by X-43: M41, M44 and M45 have one site each, Plan 5's; Plan 6 appends only M29 and places none of them): M45's one switch is the frame type of `createInbox` in `src/ports/inbox.ts` (`{"type":"message",…}` instead of `{"type":"user",…}`), selected in a test build by `src/entry/mcp.ts` from `COWORK_TEST_MUTANT` (A4-11's pattern, behind `__COWORK_TEST_BUILD__`); Task 14's inbox test and Task 17's FakeHost doorbell scenario kill it (the Inbox stub drops a `message` frame, so no nonce UserPromptSubmit arrives and TC-COO-14's wake turn never opens); the other half of 11 §3.9's M45 ("a post counted as delivered without a nonce-carrying UserPromptSubmit") is `EventStore`'s INV-CCI-DOORBELL rule, pinned by Task 7's test — TST-11 requires every M01–M48 switch in test builds and both halves are Plan 5 code; the parallel Plan 6 draft's `src/coord/inbox/post.ts` does not exist in Plan 5's layout (X-42 deletes it) — cost if wrong: Plan 6 moves the switch with the poster (one line).

O-17: a batch without `printed` is re-queued at the seat's next hook and re-sent in full (07 COO-20, 11 TC-COO-11), never marked delivered by the hook alone (SE5-22). O-18: the engine-event split is by source (`transitionItems`' note). O-14: the T-PRO-15 single-flight gate wait is Plan 5's test. The other items (O-1, O-3, O-8…O-13, O-15) change no Plan 5 text.

**Spec conflicts and gaps recorded above:** (D1's who-sees-`last_check` question is settled by rev 2.5's final text and X-01, not by a Plan 5 edit; R5-14); 06 §3.8.4's hook-time candidate with no object vs 01 §3.5 (c)/§3.8 (a)'s materialized candidate after the quiet period, and 06 test 46's loose-object clause (R5-20); 06 skip 2 vs HLD-67's last paragraph (R5-07); the UserPromptExpansion payload without `nonces[]` or `args_sha256` for read-only verbs (R5-38, R5-14); 06 §3.4.1 step 4's evidence fields vs 09 §2 (R5-38); the G4 `{cause}` slot absent from 07 and glossary §11 (R5-40); `DAEMON_DOWN`'s carrier in §3.4.2 step 6 vs §3.14.4 (R5-40); 06 §3.12.2's fallback (b) vs 07 §3.15 (R5-36); the apply-coalescing reason left open in 06 §3.7.5 (R5-25); two `MID_TURN` readings (R5-25); `checks_run` absent from 06 §3.12.1 (R5-23); the seat-number rule (R5-33); health codes for wiring (R5-34); the seat-lease validity window (R5-35); metering of other mirrors (R5-43); R3-54's `state` code and `locked` (R5-41); 11 §3.3 step 5's real-process SIM vs TST-03 (R5-16); the SUSPECT owner line in Plan 2 (R5-06); `wake-scope` ownership between 06 and Plan 3 R3-08 (R5-05); D4 vs roadmap rows 7/8 and glossary §29.1 (R5-54; rev 2.5 already carries the final text, X-03); SEC-25's `args_norm` over quoted arguments vs the unquoted `$ARGUMENTS` of the command file (SE5-15, closed by X-60: shell-word `normArgs` on both sides); plain `status` and `status --flow` sharing one undistinguished `flow` request (R5-57, SE5-20's `detail`), and the `since` read-only grant racing the `!` line (R5-57); the CLI's slash-size report with no 09 §3.11 request kind (R5-58, SE5-20); 8a's fixed `cowork-sync` data directory vs Plan 5's `<plugin>-<marketplace>` and `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` (R5-56, SE5-21 per X-61's one module); the spec's one `tmp/deny-<id>.bin` vs one file per deny (SE5-19); the no-object sentences for edit candidates beyond those SE5-03 first listed (R5-20, SE5-03).

The original flag, for the record: `commands/cowork.md` runs `node "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/dist/cli.js" $ARGUMENTS` unquoted, so for an argument the human quoted (`/cowork save "x"`) the loader's `args_norm` (from `command_args`) keeps the quotes while the CLI's argv-joined `args_norm` does not, and `args_sha256` never matches; it does not affect the verbs Plan 5 exercises (`status --flow`, `wrap`, `since`, `done`, `resolve <id> --publish|…`), but 06 test 45 and 09 test 8 use `save "x"`. (The two options were stripping shell quoting on both sides or quoting `$ARGUMENTS`; quoting would turn every multi-argument verb into one argument, so X-60 chose the first. A18-3's `depends: SE5-15` skip is removed.)

- **SE5-18 (flag, A4-2).** 06 §3.4.1 step 3's `.git`-file branch emits `WT_NOTE`/`WT_SUBMODULE` on SessionStart, edit-class PreToolUse **and SubagentStart** (06 §3.10's SubagentStart-with-a-worktree-`cwd` sentence wins over the step's two-event list).

**Readings flagged for reconciliation.** The task writers' contract additions (labelled `A<task>-<n>` and folded into §Interfaces, where the label marks each member) include these readings of the spec. Reconciled 2026-10-08 (Plan 5 item 11): they are internal and bind as read, except where a ruling settled them — A2-4 by X-71 (the root `package.json`), A9-3 and the Task 9 marker note by X-69 (Plan 7 owns LIF-36), A10-5 by X-41, A15-1 by X-99, A16-2 by X-75/X-76:
- **A2-1** — the bundles stay ESM, so the loader's step 8 is `await import('./core.js')` (SE5-16); **A2-4** — the shipped plugin root therefore keeps a `package.json` with `"type": "module"` and no dependencies or install scripts (X5-44 as ruled by X-71).
- **A3-6** — on win32 a hook accepts `ipc.key` through the `ipc.key.acl` stamp of the last passing full verification instead of spawning `whoami`/PowerShell on every hook (SE5-17).
- **A4-13** — the loader resolves the data directory and reads `user.json`'s `language` right after step 1, before step 2's stdin parse, so every failure after step 1 (malformed stdin included) renders in `L_c` (06 §3.4.1 orders the language read inside step 3).
- **A4-1** — the loader's "gating event" test is `argv[2] === 'PreToolUse'` (holds for malformed stdin); **A4-2** — `WT_NOTE`/`WT_SUBMODULE` also on SubagentStart (SE5-18); **A4-6** — `DAEMON_DOWN` prints only on an event that can carry text and stays pending otherwise; **A4-12** — Agent definitions are read from `repo`, `user`, then enabled plugins' `agents/` (06 §8 Q11).
- **A5-2** — a seat exists with `seatId: null` for ≤ 3 s between its first hook and its binding; the `seat.register` step awaits the binding within the loader deadline.
- **A6-1** — a prompt whose nonces were never posted to this seat is still a doorbell (never a human prompt) and opens no turn; **A6-2** — the posted nonces outlive the 60 s pending-wake expiry, so rule (a) still classifies a later nonce-carrying turn as wake.
- **A7-8** — the effective text cap of the SessionStart/SubagentStart carriers is the host's 2,000-char preview, not the 2,500 row of 06 §3.14.1.
- **A9-3** — `removalShutdown` leaves out `meta.stop_mode := removed` (Plan 2's `meta` has no `stop_mode`; Plan 7 adds it, X5-32).
- **A9-5** — NO_HOOKS trigger (2) is lifted only by that seat's next edit-class PreToolUse, not by "any hook" (06 §3.11.6), because the unmatched Posts are hooks themselves.
- **Task 9 Notes** — the `live` marker stopgap is removed only by the SIGTERM/SIGINT stop of `daemon.js run` and by `removalShutdown`; an `IDLE_NO_SESSION` drain and a version drain keep it (a reading of R5-18's "clean stop or drain").
- **A10-5** — `plan5WakeScope.gateEdit` denies in `wake.turnEdits = deny` mode (the deny needs no membership); R5-05 is worded accordingly.
- **A10-6** — the G2/G3 note never carries `OFFLINE` in Plan 5; both OFFLINE notices of an episode are transition lines (06 §3.6.2 makes the repeat a seat obligation).
- **A12-8** — a full Read takes a READ lease, a view blob and a full-read-set entry only for a `LIVE` path.
- **A13-9** — a Bash/PowerShell TXN closed by its own PostToolUseFailure is checkpoint trigger (b), so `STEP_ORDER.PostToolUseFailure` lists `checkpoint.trigger` (shipped in Task 5's table, registered by Task 13; PermissionDenied's order unchanged).
- **A14-1 / A9-2** — the one `node:fs.watch` binding is the exported function `realWatch` of `src/ports/fs.ts` (Task 9); Task 14's draft wording "`Fs.watch?` port method" is replaced by it (one name; the `Fs` interface gains no member).
- **Task 12 Notes** — `OWN_CHANGE_HELD` (06 §3.14.4) is selected for "a record created from this seat's own change", which 05 HLD-23 names `HOLD_NOT_SHARED` (declared for Plan 5 by Plan 3 R3-65); Plan 5 never emits `HOLD_NOT_SHARED`. `HOLD_IN_FILE` rides as the PostToolUse coordination event (≤ 1,000), not in the 600 attribution slot.
- **A15-1** — Plan 8a's L-VIZ-IMPORT clause (X5-40; settled by X-99: the landed rule already admits `cli.ts → src/claude/client/cliClient.ts`).
- **A16-2** — `src/since/report.ts` is F04-a's only; Task 16 stops for a ruling rather than writing a stand-in (R5-04 as amended; X-75, X-76: F04-a also owns `SinceMarks` and the `meta.since` parse).

Import direction (X-110, the one order of every plan): `src/ports` → `src/text` → `src/i18n` → `src/git` → `src/sec` → `src/data` → `src/regions` (Plan 4) → `src/board` → `src/since` (F04-a) → `src/holds` → `src/claims` (Plan 4) → `src/engine` → `src/claude` → `src/coord` (Plan 6) → `src/lifecycle` → `src/cli` (Plan 7) → `src/daemon`, `src/entry`; each imports only from those before it; `src/product` is pure (types plus `src/text`, `src/i18n`) and `src/viz` keeps L-VIZ-IMPORT; an earlier-plan module never imports a later-plan module (a later plan implements a Plan 5 port type in its own file; the wiring lives in `src/daemon/run.ts`, X-111). Plan 5 imports `src/text` and `src/i18n` only from `src/claude` and later — `src/ports` and `src/sec` never import them (they declare local structural types instead: `NotifyParams` A1-5, `SecParams` A3-7) and never import `src/claude/wire` (the `ipc.key.acl` stamp is encoded locally, A3-6). Inside `src/claude`: `wire` and `text` import only `src/ports` types, `src/text`, `src/i18n`, the node-only leaves `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts` and `src/data/canonRoot.ts` (X-61, X-63) and `node:*`; `client` imports `wire`, `text`, `src/sec/{ipcKey,winAcl,endpoint,handshake,sddl}.ts`, `src/ports/{ipc,inbox,fs,clock,random}.ts`, `src/text`, `src/i18n`, those two `src/data` leaves, Plan 1's `src/git/env.ts` (the one named `src/git` exception, `buildDaemonEnv`, R5-03) and nothing else (L-HOOK-THIN on the transitive graph of `src/entry/core.ts` and `src/entry/mcp.ts`, type-only imports included; client code reads engine-side shapes through the wire copies `StatusHeartbeat`, `ClientMutantId`, `SinceMarks`, X5-45); `src/claude/client/cliClient.ts` imports nothing from `src/viz/**` (`src/entry/cli.ts` computes the output mode with 8a's `outputMode` and passes it in, A15-6); `daemon` imports `wire`, `text` and everything before `src/claude`, never `client`; `src/claude/daemon/regions.ts` is the only file that imports `src/regions/**` or `src/claims/**` (Task 18 binding; Plan 4's real directories, R4-02); `src/entry/hook.ts` imports only `node:*` (L-LOADER). `src/engine` never imports `src/claude` (Plan 5 facts reach it through `EngineDeps` ports). Every module has one responsibility, as listed. Unit tests live in `test/unit/`, real-git and real-IPC tests in `test/integration/`, SIM tests in `test/integration/sim.*`, real-process host tests in `test/fakehost/`, each named in its task.

## Interfaces (the contract every task implements exactly)

Plan 1, 2, 3 and 8a names are used as their contracts state them (`GitRepo`, `Oid`, `OidOrAbsent`, `ABSENT`, `TreeMap`, `Clock`, `Anchor`, `anchorNow`, `elapsedMono`, `ownStaleness`, `suspendGapMs`, `machineMonoMs`, `Random`, `randomHex`, `seededRandom`, `ulid`, `Fs`, `FsLstat`, `sameStat`, `realFs`, `FaultFs`, `Scheduler`, `Trace`, `TraceEvent`, `Log`, `fileLog`, `crashpoint`, `KilledAt`, `buildDaemonEnv`, `createGitRunner`, `hashRouteB`, `StageDir`, `ScopeClass`, `NotSharedReason`, `scopeOfTracked`, `admitNew`, `portable`, `validPathSyntax`, `validId`, `wildmatch`, `base32c`, `DeferReason`, `Meta`, `parseMeta`, `serializeMeta`, `StateStore`, `Transition`, `FoldState`, `EventOp`, `SeatStartOp`, `SeatEndOp`, `OpDraft`, `Outbox`, `PushState`, `PeerId`, `Json`, `FreezeReason`, `PublishPause`, `StatusJson`, `computeStatus`, `LeasePort`, `alwaysLeased`, `LEASE_STOP_SYNC_MS`, `LEASE_EXIT_MS`, `AttributionPort`, `humanOnlyAttribution`, `EngineDeps`, `EngineCore`, `IterationCtx`, `tickSkip`, `TickInput`, `Writer`, `WriterDeps`, `GroupResult`, `PATH_BUSY_WAIT_MS`, `Fetcher`, `Engine`, `EngineInspection`, `runLoop`, `runDaemon`, `DaemonArgs`, `DaemonLock`, `DAEMON_LOCK_FILE`, `Folder`, `mergeFile`, `MutantId`, `PLUGIN_VERSION`, `realEngine`, `runSim`, `SimContext`, `Oracle`, `exitOracles`, `TraceRecorder`, `VirtualClock`, `makeCores`, `makeEngines`, `makeTempRoot`, `createBareRemote`, `clonePeer`; Plan 3's `CsSignal`, `SeatActivity`, `EditCheckPort`, `EditCheckVerdict`, `grammarlessEditCheck`, `PublishGatePort`, `openGate`, `plan3Ports`, `pinCs`, `shadowRecord`, `NewRecord`, `HoldRegistry`, `HoldRecord`, `isTerminal`, `HOLD_CODES`, `heldPins`, `ChangeSets`, `Candidate`, `CsTable`, `CsTableEntry`, `queueSignal`, `captureStep`, `ResolveVerb`, `ResolveOptions`, `ResolveResult`, `ClaudeRestoreReason`, `claudeRestoreRefusal`, `Engine.signal`, `Engine.heldRanges`, `Engine.resolve`, `EngineFixture.signal`, `TeamConfig.checks.checkpoint`, and from Plan 3's round 2 (X-05, X-09): `src/holds/lastCheck.ts`'s `LastCheckKind`, `SkipReason`, `LastCheck`, `LastCheckStore` with the one instance `ChangeSets.lastCheck` (Task 10, F-P3-1), `T0Verdict.findings?`, `EditCheckVerdict.entries?` (X-07, X-08), and `src/holds/revertCs.ts`'s `revertView`, `RevertView` (Task 17, F-P3-2); Plan 8a's `sanitize`, `SanitizeChannel`, `cap`, `render`, `registerCatalog`, `Lang`, `Params`, `CatalogEntry`, `CHANNEL_CAPS`, `OutputMode`, `outputMode`, `OpVerdict`, `BoardCommitData`, `verifyDataDir`, and (moved by F-8a-2) `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts`, `src/sec/sddl.ts`; F04-a's `SinceMarks`, `Meta.since`, `SinceStep`, `SinceReport`, `MyRecord`, `sinceInit`, `sincePush`, `sinceFinish`, `zone` (X5-38, X-75, X-76); Plan 4's names through `RegionsPort` only (X-20)). Everything below is new or changed. A comment after a member is part of the contract; a `/** … */` line names the spec ids it implements.

### Wire: constants, codes, frames, schema (Task 1)

```ts
// src/claude/wire/constants.ts — 06 §2 "Local numbers" and glossary §20 seat, fence, MCP, IPC, budget rows; one module, pinned by "C is the spec values"
export const C: {

  readonly EVIDENCE_MAX_AGE_MS: 60_000; readonly CLI_EVIDENCE_WAIT_MS: 3_000; readonly GRANT_MAX_AGE_MS: 60_000;                    // SEC-25, R5-14

  readonly FRAME_MAX_BYTES: 1_048_576; readonly FLOW_MAX_ENTRIES: 2_000; readonly HANDSHAKE_NONCE_BYTES: 16; readonly IPC_KEY_BYTES: 32;
  readonly HANDSHAKE_FAIL_NOTIFY_AT: 5; readonly HANDSHAKE_FAIL_WINDOW_MS: 60_000; readonly SECURITY_NOTIFY_SPACING_MS: 1_800_000; readonly SOCKET_PATH_MAX_BYTES: 100;
```
