```ts
/** R5-50 host check (A12-6; Task 12 reads them): null until the ledger records the host's row count for an unranged Read and its offset base; while null only read.full is used. */
export const READ_HOST_DEFAULT_ROWS: number | null;
export const READ_OFFSET_BASE: 0 | 1 | null;

// src/claude/wire/codes.ts — 06 §3.14.4 and §1.2 (selection only; templates are 07's/10's). Every entry matches ^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,47}$.
/** Exactly the codes of 06 §3.14.4's table, §1.2's refusal table and 09 §3.11/§3.13's SEC_ codes, copied by Task 1 (the test pins each name against the spec text), plus NEW_CODES,
 *  minus the five that Plan 3's HOLD_CODES declares (CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE, HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED, QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY, RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY, RESTORE_MARKERS),
 *  which ROUTED_CODES references instead (X-117; F2 #143, ruling R-F2-4). */
export const CCI_CODES: readonly string[];
export const NEW_CODES: readonly ['SEAT_FULL', 'SEAT_NO_MCP', 'SEAT_PULL_MODE', 'PEER_IDLE', 'RESTORE_NOT_ALLOWED', 'NOTE_SCAN_UNAVAILABLE', 'FLOW_PATH_INVALID'];   // R5-34, R5-41, R5-42, R5-60 (SE5-08)
/** R5-60: other chapters' registry codes Plan 5 selects or delivers, each pinned by a test against its chapter's text (05, glossary §8 / 01 / 02, 07, 10);
 *  a status message_code passed through by a transition line is Plan 2/3's and is not listed. */
export const ROUTED_CODES: readonly ['HOLD_PIN_CONTEXT', 'INCOMING_WAITING', 'CS_GROUP_WAITING', 'CS_READY_CANCELLED',
  'CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE', 'HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED', 'QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY', 'RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY', 'RESTORE_MARKERS',   // F2 #143, ruling R-F2-4 (declared in Plan 3's HOLD_CODES)
  'FROZEN', 'UNFROZEN', 'OFFLINE', 'ONLINE', 'NONRETRYABLE', 'VERSION_PAUSED', 'INCIDENT', 'STANDING_RULES', 'RF_UNKNOWN_VERB'];
/** 06 §3.16.1: shortened (T1R) but never suppressed in PULL_MODE: every G1 reason, the K2 block, OWN_CHANGE_HELD for a wake-scope PIN, Stop truthfulness. */
export const MANDATORY_FAMILIES: readonly ['g1', 'k2', 'own_change_held_wake', 'stop_truth'];
/** 06 §3.16.1, F05/F04 additions: suppressed in PULL_MODE. Plan 6 tags its bystander lines with family 'bystander'. */
export const PULL_MODE_SUPPRESSED: readonly ['ATTRIBUTION', 'CLAIM_AHEAD', 'CLAIM_AHEAD_ENDED', 'SINCE_POINTER', 'CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE', 'bystander'];
/** A6-9 (Task 6): codes written only to the daemon log — never a carrier, the board or status.json; each matches REASON_CODE_RE. X-30: every Log.write code is a registry
 *  code with surfaces ['log'], so SESSION_END and SEAT_HOOK (written by Plan 5's daemon log) are declared here too. */
export const LOG_ONLY_CODES: readonly ['CS_SIGNAL_REJECTED', 'SESSION_END', 'SEAT_HOOK'];
// X-117: CCI_CODES, NEW_CODES and LOG_ONLY_CODES are declarations (each code declared in exactly one plan's codes module; HELD_CS, OWN_CHANGE_HELD, STOP_SUMMARY and
//   DIGEST_SUPERSEDES are declared here and nowhere else, X-48); ROUTED_CODES is a reference list whose codes must exist in their owners' modules (RF_UNKNOWN_VERB is
//   declared in Plan 7's registry, X-79); Plan 7's carried.ts imports these modules instead of retyping them, and its L-CODES fails a duplicate declaration.

// src/claude/wire/frames.ts — 06 §3.4.3, glossary §13 (as superseded by §25 F1/F5), 09 §3.11 incl. rev 2.5 CLI requests; R5-38, R5-45
export type HookEvent = 'SessionStart' | 'UserPromptSubmit' | 'UserPromptExpansion' | 'PreToolUse' | 'PostToolUse' | 'PostToolUseFailure'
  | 'PostToolBatch' | 'PermissionDenied' | 'Stop' | 'StopFailure' | 'SubagentStart' | 'SubagentStop' | 'SessionEnd' | 'Notification';
export type EditTool = 'Edit' | 'Write' | 'MultiEdit' | 'NotebookEdit';
export type ShellTool = 'Bash' | 'PowerShell' | 'Monitor';
export type GatingClass = 'edit' | 'shell' | 'agent' | null;   // 06 §2 "gating event"; agent = Agent|EnterWorktree (decided locally)
/** A1-1: the wire tier's JSON type, used wherever a wire or client-tier type carries arbitrary JSON (tool.args, acting.params, tool_result.structured, wrap_result.rows,
 *  HookInput.tool_input/tool_response, McpToolSchema.inputSchema, worktreeDenied's toolInput); Plan 2's Json lives in src/board/canonicalJson.ts, which the client tier must
 *  not reach (L-HOOK-THIN); a type-level test pins WireJson and Json mutually assignable (X5-45). Daemon-tier types keep Plan 2's Json. */
export type WireJson = null | boolean | number | string | WireJson[] | { [k: string]: WireJson };
/** A1-2: CliCaller.mode; Plan 8a declares OutputMode in src/viz/cli/view.ts, outside the wire tier's import set; a type-level test pins CliMode and OutputMode mutually assignable. */
export type CliMode = 'tty' | 'pipe' | 'slash';
/** A1-7: the 14 events of glossary §13 in its row order. */
export const HOOK_EVENTS: readonly HookEvent[];
/** A1-7: glossary §13 as superseded by §25 F5 — SessionStart `startup|resume|clear|compact|fork`; UserPromptSubmit null; UserPromptExpansion `cowork|cowork-sync:cowork`;
 *  PreToolUse three rows `^(Edit|Write|MultiEdit|NotebookEdit)$`, `^(Bash|PowerShell|Monitor)$`, `^(Agent|EnterWorktree)$`; PostToolUse `.*`; PostToolUseFailure `.*`;
 *  PostToolBatch, Stop, StopFailure, SubagentStart, SubagentStop null; SessionEnd null with timeout 2; PermissionDenied `.*`; Notification `idle_prompt|permission_prompt`;
 *  every other row timeout 10 (Task 2's hooks/hooks.json test and L-HOOKS-JSON consume it). */
export const HOOK_REGISTRATIONS: ReadonlyArray<{ event: HookEvent; matcher: string | null; timeout: 10 | 2 }>;
export const HOOK_PROTO: 1;                                     // CCI-52: this build's hook protocol (A1-7: the first); a daemon serves its current and previous (HOOK_PROTO − 1) value
export interface HookHeader { seat_key: string; claude_pid: number; session_id: string; prompt_id: string | null; agent_id: string | null; agent_type: string | null;
  permission_mode: string; repo_root: string; plugin_version: string; hook_proto: number; }
export interface EditInput { old_string?: string; new_string?: string; replace_all?: boolean; content?: string;
  edits?: ReadonlyArray<{ old_string: string; new_string: string; replace_all?: boolean }>;
  notebook?: { cell_id?: string; new_source?: string; cell_type?: string; edit_mode?: string }; }   // daemon memory only (CCI-09)
export interface ReadInfo { offset: number | null; limit: number | null; full: boolean; enoent: boolean; size: number | null; }   // F05 prerequisite; offset base per R5-50
export interface EvidenceRecord { seat_key: string; prompt_id: string; verb: string; args_sha256: string; mono_ns: string; used: boolean; }   // 09 §2 (SE5-02)
export interface PolicyHit { setting: string; file: string; scope: 'managed' | 'user' | 'project' | 'local'; value: string; }        // SEC-28/29: name and file; value = the JSON scalar or 'set'
export type HookPayload =
  | { event: 'SessionStart'; source: 'startup' | 'resume' | 'clear' | 'compact' | 'fork'; transcript_path: string | null; messaging_present: boolean; static_policy_hits: PolicyHit[] }
  | { event: 'UserPromptSubmit'; nonces: string[] }                                                               // no other prompt text (CCI-54)
  | { event: 'UserPromptExpansion'; command_name: 'cowork' | 'cowork-sync:cowork'; verb: string; command_source: string | null;
      args_sha256: string; nonces: string[]; evidence: EvidenceRecord | null }                                    // R5-38 (args_sha256 and nonces: SE5-02)
  | { event: 'PreToolUse'; gating: 'edit'; tool_name: EditTool; tool_use_id: string; path: string; tool_input: EditInput }   // path repo-relative POSIX NFC
  | { event: 'PreToolUse'; gating: 'shell'; tool_name: ShellTool; tool_use_id: string; command: string; run_in_background: boolean }
  | { event: 'PostToolUse'; tool_name: string; tool_use_id: string; path: string | null; read: ReadInfo | null; exit_status: number | null; command: string | null }   // command: Bash|PowerShell only, for R5-23
  | { event: 'PostToolUseFailure' | 'PermissionDenied'; tool_name: string; tool_use_id: string; command: string | null }
  | { event: 'PostToolBatch'; tool_use_ids: string[] }
  | { event: 'Stop' | 'SubagentStop'; stop_hook_active: boolean; background_tasks_running: number; ids_in_last_message: string[] | null }   // null: host gave no last message (§8 Q7)
  | { event: 'StopFailure' | 'SessionEnd'; reason: string | null }
  | { event: 'SubagentStart'; agent_id: string; agent_type: string | null; cwd: string }
  | { event: 'Notification'; type: 'idle_prompt' | 'permission_prompt' };
export interface HookFrame { t: 'hook'; event: HookEvent; h: HookHeader; payload: HookPayload; }
export type CarrierDecision = 'deny' | 'block' | 'context' | 'none';
export interface ReplyFrame { t: 'reply'; batch_id: string; decision: CarrierDecision; text: string; system: string | null; events: string[]; cursor: number; }
export interface ExtendFrame { t: 'extend'; deadline_ms: number; }             // SUSPECT only, ≤ 7,000, at most once per request (CCI-17)
export interface PrintedFrame { t: 'printed'; batch_id: string; }
export interface McpHelloFrame { t: 'hello'; seat_id: string; seat_key: string; claude_pid: number; cc_version: string | null; plugin_version: string; hook_proto: number; messaging_present: boolean; }
export type ToolName = 'cowork_status' | 'cowork_show' | 'cowork_request' | 'cowork_respond' | 'cowork_note' | 'cowork_release' | 'cowork_checkpoint' | 'cowork_resolve';   // glossary §15
export type ActingVerb = 'start' | 'stop' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'save' | 'done' | 'resolve' | 'review' | 'revert' | 'release' | 'recover' | 'uninstall';   // glossary §15 (rev 2.5: revert)
export const ACTING_VERBS: readonly ActingVerb[];
export const READ_ONLY_GRANT_VERBS: readonly ['since'];   // X-02: the grant survives only for FEAT-F04-18's since_ran (R5-14 as ruled by X-01)
export interface CliCaller { mode: CliMode; tty: boolean; ancestry_has_claude: boolean; verb: string; args_sha256: string; }   // SEC-25 step 3, 10 §2
/** 09 §3.11; R5-57: detail 'section' = plain status's section 6 (its reply carries last_check: null), 'flow' = status --flow (its reply always carries last_check, X-01).
 *  The schema checks the path is a non-empty string without NUL; the daemon applies validPathSyntax and refuses FLOW_PATH_INVALID (R5-60). */
export type FlowSelector = { all: true; detail: 'section' | 'flow' } | { path: string };
export interface FlowEntryWire { path: string; dir: 'out' | 'in'; stage: string; code: string; params: Params; }   // 10 §3.5.1 --json shape; produced by FlowPort (Plan 7)
export interface LastCheckWire { cs: string; edit_n: number; kind: string; check?: number; path?: string; line?: number; skip?: string; more: number; last_code?: string; last_params?: Params; }
/** X-01: last_check is an array (one slot per open seat CS that has one; possibly empty) for {all: true, detail: 'flow'} whoever asked, and null for detail 'section' and
 *  for a {path} selector; last_withheld = the reply holds an O10 entry and last_check is null (the CLI then prints UI_FLOW_LAST_WITHHELD once, Plan 7). */
export interface FlowResultFrame { t: 'flow_result'; tip_height: number; fetched_age_ms: number | null; entries: FlowEntryWire[]; omitted: number; last_check: LastCheckWire[] | null; last_withheld: boolean; }
/** X-01: every wrap reply carries last_check (never null, never withheld), so the frame has no last_withheld field. */
export interface WrapResultFrame { t: 'wrap_result'; rows: WireJson; last_check: LastCheckWire[]; }
/** 01 §3.2 meta.since (R5-52). The wire copy: src/engine/state.ts declares the engine's SinceMarks beside Meta (X5-03); a type-level test pins the two mutually assignable (X5-45). */
export interface SinceMarks { seen_h: number | null; return_h: number | null; run_h: number | null; }
/** A4-14: what core reads from status.json (06 §3.4.2 step 1), a wire-local shape so the client tier never imports Plan 2's StatusJson (L-HOOK-THIN); StatusJson is
 *  pinned assignable to it (X5-45). */
export interface StatusHeartbeat { heartbeat_mono_ms: number; loop_period_ms: number; state: string; }
/** A4-14, R5-64: the mutants whose switch lives in a client-tier process (loader, core, MCP server); a subset of MutantId pinned by a type-level test (X5-45). */
export type ClientMutantId = 'M08' | 'M26' | 'M27' | 'M45';
export interface SinceResultFrame { t: 'since_result'; marks: SinceMarks | null; records: Array<{ id: string; paths: string[]; state: string; offer: string }>; labels: Record<string, string>; }
export type SessionClientFrame =
  | HookFrame | PrintedFrame | McpHelloFrame | { t: 'heartbeat' } | { t: 'seat_end' }
  | { t: 'ring_result'; nonce: string; result: 'sent' | 'refused' | 'error' }
  | { t: 'tool'; call_id: string; name: ToolName; args: WireJson }
  | ({ t: 'acting'; params: WireJson } & CliCaller)                               // R5-13
  | { t: 'flow'; selector: FlowSelector } | { t: 'wrap' }                         // X-01: no tty/ancestry fields — the daemon does not test who asked (09 §3.11)
  | { t: 'since_query' } | ({ t: 'since_ran'; to: number } & CliCaller)         // since_ran keeps the route-(a) fields and args_sha256 for the 'since' grant (X-02)
  | { t: 'slash_shown'; seat_key: string; chars: number };                       // R5-58: one-way, like since_ran (no reply frame); chars a non-negative integer
export type SessionServerFrame =
  | ReplyFrame | ExtendFrame
  | { t: 'ring'; event_ids: string[]; text: string; nonce: string }               // ≤ 1,500, never starts with '/'
  | { t: 'tool_result'; call_id: string; text: string; structured: WireJson }    // never isError (CCI-48)
  | { t: 'acting_result'; ok: boolean; code: string; params: Params; data?: WireJson }   // X-58: data carries Plan 7's previews, phases and single-use tokens
  | FlowResultFrame | WrapResultFrame | SinceResultFrame | { t: 'refused'; code: string };
export type HandshakeFrame = { t: 'hello'; v: 1; n1: string } | { t: 'srv'; n2: string; p: string } | { t: 'cli'; p: string };   // SEC-22 (base64 fields)

// src/claude/wire/schema.ts — SEC-22, R5-45
export type Phase = 'handshake' | 'session';
/** One decoded frame against the schema for (phase, direction, t); unknown t or a shape failure → null and the caller closes the connection. Codes and typed params only (I18N-01). */
export function validateFrame(phase: Phase, dir: 'c2s' | 's2c', v: unknown): HandshakeFrame | SessionClientFrame | SessionServerFrame | null;
/** Plans 6/7 add request kinds (09 §3.11 "others unchanged"); throws on a duplicate (dir, t). A1-11: every schema entry is a closed shape — an unknown key at any level
 *  is a schema failure (privacy: a prompt or command_args key can never ride a frame, 09 test 8). */
export function registerFrameKind(dir: 'c2s' | 's2c', t: string, check: (v: unknown) => boolean): void;
export function registeredKinds(): ReadonlyArray<{ phase: Phase; dir: 'c2s' | 's2c'; t: string }>;   // A1-11: tests and Plans 6/7 read the registry

// src/claude/wire/ndjson.ts
export class FrameTooLarge extends Error {}
/** Newline-delimited JSON, ≤ 1 MiB per frame (SEC-22); push() returns complete lines and throws FrameTooLarge past the cap. */
export class LineReader { push(chunk: Buffer): string[]; }
/** A1-3: object keys sorted by UTF-16 code unit at every depth, no insignificant whitespace, strings escaped as JSON.stringify does, -0 written 0, non-finite numbers and
 *  undefined throw TypeError; byte-equal to Plan 2's canonicalBytes on every value both accept (parity test, X5-45); the encoding the evidence tag signs (evidenceTag). */
export function canonicalJsonLine(v: WireJson): string;
export function encodeFrame(f: object): Buffer;   // = Buffer.from(canonicalJsonLine(f) + '\n', 'utf8') (A1-3)
```

### Wire: roots, hook input, shell tokens, plugin data; text (Tasks 1, 10)

```ts
// src/data/canonRoot.ts — 06 §3.4.1 step 3, CCI-07, SEC-21, glossary §24 default 12; pure over injected probes (the loader carries a copy, parity-tested).
//   X-63: the one canonRoot of every plan (not under src/claude/wire); verify pass V-12: 8a Task 7 creates the file with canonRoot and RootProbe/realRootProbe at exactly
//   these signatures (8a runs long before Plan 5 Task 1 can start, R5-01), and Task 1 adds sameRoot, endpointRoot, findRepo, liveState in place; a node-only leaf (imports only the RootProbe type and node:*), so the wire and
//   client tiers may import it (R5-03 as amended by X-61); Plan 8a Task 7 imports it instead of its own canonicalRoot; Plan 7 uses it in place (no move); the `live` marker
//   holds canonRoot(main worktree) + LF for Plans 5, 7 and 8b alike.
export type { RootProbe } from '../ports/fs.js';   // declared in src/ports/fs.ts beside realRootProbe (the bottom layer may not import src/data or src/claude, X5-46)
/** realpathSync.native of the nearest existing ancestor + the remaining components; lower-case drive letter; '\' → '/'; NFC. */
export function canonRoot(p: string, probe: RootProbe, platform: NodeJS.Platform): string;
/** DAT-34 rule: case-insensitive on win32 and darwin, exact elsewhere. */
export function sameRoot(a: string, b: string, platform: NodeJS.Platform): boolean;
/** SEC-21 endpoint input: canonRoot(common gitdir), lower-cased (locale-independent) on win32/darwin. */
export function endpointRoot(commonGitdir: string, probe: RootProbe, platform: NodeJS.Platform): string;
export type RepoLocation =
  | { kind: 'dir'; root: string; gitdir: string }
  | { kind: 'file'; root: string; commonGitdir: string | null; submodule: boolean };   // linked worktree / .claude/worktrees/** / submodule: never live
/** Walk up from start to the first `.git` entry; parses `gitdir:` and `commondir` without git; null when none (exit 0 silently). */
export function findRepo(start: string, probe: RootProbe, platform: NodeJS.Platform): RepoLocation | null;
/** live iff <gitdir>/cowork/live exists and names this worktree's canonical root (sameRoot); paused iff <gitdir>/cowork/paused also exists. */
export function liveState(loc: Extract<RepoLocation, { kind: 'dir' }>, probe: RootProbe, platform: NodeJS.Platform): 'nonlive' | 'live' | 'paused';
export interface LiveRepo { root: string; gitdir: string; coworkDir: string; dataDir: string; endpointRoot: string; }

// src/claude/wire/hookInput.ts — 06 §3.4.1 steps 2–4, §3.4.3; SEC-26
export class HookInputError extends Error {}
export interface HookInput { event: HookEvent; session_id: string; prompt_id: string | null; transcript_path: string | null; cwd: string; permission_mode: string;
  agent_id: string | null; agent_type: string | null; tool_name: string | null; tool_use_id: string | null; tool_input: WireJson | null; tool_response: WireJson | null; fields: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>; }
/** Typed view of the host's hook JSON; throws HookInputError on a malformed shape (loader step 2 → internal failure). */
export function parseHookInput(event: HookEvent, json: unknown): HookInput;
export function gatingClass(event: HookEvent, toolName: string | null): GatingClass;
export const NONCE_RE: RegExp;                    // /cws-[0-9a-f]{16}/g
export function nonces(text: string): string[];   // every cws- token, deduplicated, in order
/** SEC-25 as ruled by X-60 (Plan 7's spec edit 13, the one implementation for loader, core and CLI; SE5-15 closed): the words are the shell words of the input — a string
 *  (the loader's command_args) is split with POSIX quoting as the `!` line's shell splits it; an array (the CLI's argv after the verb's own process start) is taken as
 *  those words; verb = the first word; args_norm = every further word with its whitespace runs collapsed to one space, each JSON-quoted (JSON.stringify), joined by one
 *  space ('' when there is none); args_sha256 = hex SHA-256(args_norm). So `/cowork save "fix  the bug" --sign` and argv ['save', 'fix  the bug', '--sign'] give one
 *  args_sha256 ('"fix the bug" "--sign"'); `$ARGUMENTS` stays unquoted in commands/cowork.md. */
export function normArgs(input: string | readonly string[]): { verb: string; argsNorm: string; argsSha256: string };
/** 06 §3.4.3: the frame payload; never copies prompt text, command_args, tool_response content or transcript content beyond the listed fields; path repo-relative POSIX NFC.
 *  A1-8: extra.target = the canonical absolute target path of an edit-class or Read call (core computes it with canonRoot + realRootProbe); path = target relative to
 *  repo.root (prefix compared by sameRoot's case rule, remainder kept in the target's on-disk spelling, '/' separators, NFC), null when outside the root;
 *  ids_in_last_message carries only idsInText tokens, never the message (CCI-09). */
export function buildPayload(i: HookInput, repo: { root: string }, extra: { messaging_present: boolean; static_policy_hits: PolicyHit[]; evidence: EvidenceRecord | null; target: string | null }): HookPayload;
/** A1-8: the host hook-input field names Plan 5 reads that 06 §3.4.3 does not spell, in one table so the SPIKE-0/R5-50 host checks (Task 12) correct a name in one place:
 *  exitStatus ['exit_code', 'exitCode'], readTruncated 'truncated', readEnoent 'enoent', readSize 'file.numLines' (dotted paths inside tool_response); backgroundTasks
 *  'background_tasks_running', lastMessage 'last_assistant_message', notificationType 'notification_type' (top-level). An absent field gives exit_status null,
 *  background_tasks_running 0, ids_in_last_message null (06 §8 Q7), read.enoent false, read.size null; read.full = offset === null && limit === null && truncated !== true. */
export const HOST_FIELDS: { readonly exitStatus: readonly string[]; readonly backgroundTasks: string; readonly lastMessage: string; readonly notificationType: string;
  readonly readTruncated: string; readonly readEnoent: string; readonly readSize: string };
/** A1-8: hold ULIDs, request display ids, claim ids, cs ids — /\b(?:[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}|R[0-9a-z]{8}|C[0-9a-z]{16}|[0-9a-f]{32}\/[1-9][0-9]{0,9})\b/g. */
export const COWORK_ID_RE: RegExp;
/** A1-8: every COWORK_ID_RE match plus every token (split on whitespace and ` ' " ( ) [ ] , ;, trailing . and : trimmed) that contains '/' or '.', has ≤ 200 UTF-16 units
 *  and no control character (the not-shared item ids of Stop (a) are paths); deduplicated in order. */
export function idsInText(text: string): string[];
/** A1-8: lowercase hex SHA-256 of CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET when set and non-empty, else String(ppid) (glossary §2; the loader carries a copy, Task 4 parity test). */
export function seatKeyOf(env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>, ppid: number): string;
/** A1-8: non-null exactly for a UserPromptExpansion with expansion_type slash_command, command_name ∈ {cowork, cowork-sync:cowork}, verb ∈ ACTING_VERBS and no cws- nonce in
 *  its prompt: {seat_key, prompt_id, verb, args_sha256, mono_ns: monoNs.toString(), used: false} (09 §2, R5-38). */
export function evidenceFor(i: HookInput, seatKey: string, monoNs: bigint): EvidenceRecord | null;
export function evidenceTag(key: Buffer, r: EvidenceRecord): string;   // A1-8: hex HMAC-SHA256(key, "evidence" ‖ canonicalJsonLine(r)) (09 §3.13 step 1)

// src/claude/wire/shellTokens.ts — CCI-28 (Task 1 tokenize; Task 10 classify, writesFiles)
export interface ShellSegment { argv: string[]; redirects: Array<{ op: '>' | '>>'; target: string }>; cwd: string | null; gitC: string | null; }
/** Split on ; && || | and newlines; strip a leading `cd <dir> &&` and environment-variable prefixes; honour `git -C <dir>`; normalize PowerShell aliases. Pure. */
export function tokenize(command: string, tool: ShellTool): ShellSegment[];
export type ShellHit =
  | { cls: 'git'; verb: string } | { cls: 'formatter'; tool: string } | { cls: 'inplace'; targets: string[] }
  | { cls: 'cowork_cli'; why: 'acting_verb' | 'state_path' | 'ipc_key' } | { cls: 'worktree' };
/** Classes (a)–(e) of 06 §3.9; (c) lists the literal targets (the daemon checks them against the claim view); read-only CLI verbs and `cat .git/cowork/status.json` are no hit.
 *  Class (d)'s cowork-CLI token table (`SHELL_COWORK_CLI`, Task 10) admits named exception rows `{cli, hint}`; Plan 8b adds `{cli: 'view', hint: '/cowork view'}` and its
 *  06 §7 test-22 row as an 8b edit with the Plan 5 reviewer (8b CPA-5.4, accepted by the reconciliation). */
export function classify(segs: readonly ShellSegment[], ctx: { gitdir: string; root: string; dataDir: string; platform: NodeJS.Platform }): ShellHit[];
/** 06 §3.9 last paragraph: class (b), or any class (c) writer or redirection whatever its target (the wake-turn rule, R5-05). */
export function writesFiles(segs: readonly ShellSegment[]): boolean;

// src/data/pluginDataDir.ts — R5-47, R5-56, X-61: the one plugin-data-directory module of Plans 5, 7, 8a and 8b; imports only node:* (a leaf the wire and client tiers
//   may import, R5-03). The bodies move from 8a's landed src/viz/cli/dataDir.ts, which re-exports pluginDataDir and resolveDataDir unchanged (F-8a-2, run by the 8a
//   controller; Task 1 starts after that move merged and only adds pluginRoot? and pluginIdOf — verify pass V-12). Plan 7 computes its `source` itself; 8b re-exports from here. No resolvePluginDataDir, no
//   PluginDataUnresolved (deleted names, X-61).
/** R5-56 rule (derived form): <CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR when set and absolute, else <home>/.claude>/plugins/data/<pluginIdOf(pluginRoot ?? the running bundle's plugin root)>;
 *  the plugin root is CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT when set, else the bundle's dist/.. (06 §8 Q10, 09 §8 Q2, SE5-21). Signature: 8a's landed one plus the optional pluginRoot. */
export function pluginDataDir(env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>, home: string, platform: NodeJS.Platform, pluginRoot?: string): string;
/** CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA only when absolute (a relative value is ignored), else the derived directory; null = fail closed (no plugin id derivable from the root, SEC-20: no
 *  other location is ever tried). Every Plan 5 caller (core, MCP server, CLI, spawner, tests) uses this one; null takes the outcomes the deleted PluginDataUnresolved had. */
export function resolveDataDir(env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>, home: string, platform: NodeJS.Platform, pluginRoot?: string): string | null;
/** A1-12: for …/plugins/cache/<marketplace>/<plugin>/<version> (either separator) → `<plugin>-<marketplace>` with every character outside [A-Za-z0-9_-] replaced by '-'
 *  (the plugin id <plugin>@<marketplace> in Claude Code's data-directory form); any other shape → null (resolveDataDir then answers null). Task 4's host check (R5-47,
 *  A4-10) records the real directory name; a ledger ruling amends this rule if they differ. */
export function pluginIdOf(pluginRoot: string): string | null;

// src/claude/wire/pluginData.ts — R5-12; X-52 (the directory functions moved to src/data/pluginDataDir.ts, X-61)
/** A1-10: envPassthrough = the user.json env.passthrough names, each matching ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$ (others dropped; default []) — the passthrough argument of
 *  buildDaemonEnv for spawnDaemon (Task 4). X-52: the one daemon-side user.json type of Plans 5, 6 and 7 (Plan 6's PrefsPort.user() returns it; Plan 7's strict file
 *  schema type stays UserJson, loaded by its loadUserJson(fs, dataDir)); gains the glossary §17 keys wakeEnabled (wake.enabled, default true), wakeMaxPerSeatPerHour
 *  (wake.maxPerSeatPerHour, default 4) and notificationsOs (notifications.os, default true). A language outside 8a's six Lang values reads as null (I18N-02). */
export interface UserPrefs { language: string | null; wakeTurnEdits: 'allow' | 'deny'; wakeEnabled: boolean; wakeMaxPerSeatPerHour: number; notificationsOs: boolean;
  sinceRecap: boolean; sinceClaudePointer: boolean; envPassthrough: readonly string[]; }
/** Lenient: a missing file, bad JSON or a mistyped key → the default (null, 'allow', true, 4, true, true, true, []). A1-10: each key in nested ({"wake": {"turnEdits":
 *  "deny"}}) or dotted ({"wake.turnEdits": "deny"}) form, nested winning. The name stays Plan 5's (Plan 7's strict loader is loadUserJson, X-52). */
export function readUserJson(text: string | null): UserPrefs;

// src/i18n/locale.ts — glossary §17, X-53: created by Task 1 with Plan 7's signatures; Plan 7 Task 2 extends it in place (langOfLocale, …)
/** L_h: user.json → team config → OS locale → en; a user or team value outside the six languages counts as absent and is named in `invalid` (I18N-02).
 *  One declaration with Plan 7 (verify pass V-4): `invalid` lists the sources skipped, in order; `osLocale` is null when the OS gives none (the daemon and Plan 6 pass null). */
export function resolveHumanLang(i: { user: string | null; team: string | null; osLocale: string | null }): { lang: Lang; invalid: ReadonlyArray<'user' | 'team'> };
/** L_c = L_h when 'en' or 'ko', else 'en' (glossary §17). The daemon renders Claude-facing text in claudeLang(resolveHumanLang({user, team, osLocale}).lang). */
export function claudeLang(lh: Lang): ClaudeLang;

// src/i18n/claude.ts — CCI-45, glossary §19, R5-11, X-54: the one Claude template registry of every plan (+ the row modules src/i18n/claude/{en,ko}/*.ts); created here
//   by Task 1 with its seed rows; Plan 6 Task 3 extends it in place (renderClaudeChecked, TEMPLATE_IDS, templateCap, claudeCatalog, CODE_TEMPLATE_MAP and the full 07
//   catalogs; no move, no deletion); Plan 7 uses renderClaude, TEMPLATE_IDS and CODE_TEMPLATE_MAP. src/i18n is a leaf every tier may import.
export type ClaudeLang = 'en' | 'ko';
/** Template text from the catalogs; params sanitized (8a sanitize, 'deny' for G1/K2 reasons, 'claude' elsewhere) and capped by type;
 *  a template id with no catalog entry renders the code line `[cowork-sync] <CODE> k=v …` (code and sanitized params only).
 *  A1-13: {k} with a number → String(n) (no grouping); a string → sanitize(value, channel) then cap(value, kind) for ParamType name/path/label/note (8a CapKind of the
 *  same name), uncapped otherwise; null → empty; a missing param → empty and, in a test build, TypeError. The code line appends, for each param in UTF-16 key order,
 *  ` k=v` with v substituted as above and every space in v replaced by U+00A0, so the line stays parseable. */
/** Verify pass V-5 (X-54): this behaviour is the contract — Plan 6 never turns an unregistered id or a missing param into a throw here (throws live only in its
 *  renderClaudeChecked) and never redeclares renderClaude or ClaudeLang. Plan 6 Task 1 widens `params` in place from `Params` to its `TemplateParams` (a supertype adding
 *  EnumRef/ActorRef/DurRef values; every Plan 5 call compiles unchanged), with this plan's reviewer. */
export function renderClaude(templateId: string, params: Params, lang: ClaudeLang, channel?: 'claude' | 'deny'): string;
export function registerClaudeCatalog(lang: ClaudeLang, entries: Readonly<Record<string, CatalogEntry>>): void;   // identical re-registration no-op, different entry throws
/** A1-4 (as amended by X-54): the seed rows live in src/i18n/claude/{en,ko}/*.ts (one TypeScript row module per language written by Task 1; Plan 6 adds its modules
 *  beside them), each exactly the five rows G4, PAUSED, T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL ({v: 'text'}), T-SINCE ({from: 'n', s: 'n', c: 'n', k: 'n', touch: 'text'}) and T-SINCE.touch ({path: 'path',
 *  name: 'name'}) as 8a CatalogEntry rows, texts copied from 07; claude.ts imports both and registers them at module load. dist/i18n/claude.{en,ko}.json has one writer,
 *  tools/build/claudeCatalog.ts (created by Task 2, extended by Plan 6), run by `npm run build` (A2-1). */

// src/claude/text/claudeText.ts — the Plan 5-only helpers that stay outside the registry (X-54): claudeTemplateOf, loaderLang, capLines, LOADER_TEXTS
/** claudeTemplateOf maps the 07 §3.16.4 rows Plan 5 seeds — G4_DOWN → G4, DAEMON_DOWN → G4, HOOK_LOAD_FAIL → T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL, PAUSED_LINE → PAUSED,
 *  SINCE_POINTER → T-SINCE — and every other code to itself (rendered as its code line until Plan 6 adds the template; Plan 6's CODE_TEMPLATE_MAP then decides). */
export function claudeTemplateOf(code: string): string;
/** X-53: the two-argument function formerly named claudeLang, renamed loaderLang — the L_c of the dependency-free loader (and of core's loader-text paths, which run
 *  before any daemon answers), which cannot read team config (glossary §17 "user.json → OS locale → en"; A1-10): a non-null userLanguage decides alone ('ko' → 'ko',
 *  anything else → 'en'); only a null one falls to the OS locale (ko… → 'ko', else 'en') (06 §3.4.1). Daemon-rendered text uses src/i18n/locale.ts's claudeLang(lh). */
export function loaderLang(userLanguage: string | null, osLocale: string | null): ClaudeLang;
/** Cuts at line boundaries to ≤ max chars, keeping the last line when keepTail (F04 recap); never cuts inside a line except the first. */
export function capLines(text: string, max: number, keepTail: boolean): string;
/** Generated at build time from the catalogs: the glossary §11 G4 text verbatim, 07 PAUSED and T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL ({v}), en and ko (R5-40). */
export const LOADER_TEXTS: { readonly G4: Readonly<Record<ClaudeLang, string>>; readonly PAUSED: Readonly<Record<ClaudeLang, string>>; readonly HOOK_LOAD_FAIL: Readonly<Record<ClaudeLang, string>> };
```

### Ports, trace, engine deps (Tasks 1, 3, 8, 14)

```ts
// src/ports/ipc.ts — 11 §3.2 Ipc seam ("the same, real" in SIM); raw bytes, no auth (src/sec/handshake.ts authenticates)
export interface IpcConn { write(b: Buffer): void; onData(cb: (b: Buffer) => void): void; onClose(cb: () => void): void; end(): void; destroy(): void; }
export interface IpcServer { close(): Promise<void>; }
export interface Ipc {
  /** Binds a named pipe (win32) or Unix socket; rejects with an error whose code is 'EADDRINUSE' when taken (never unlinks a socket file: the squatter check decides). */
  listen(endpoint: string, onConn: (c: IpcConn) => void): Promise<IpcServer>;
  connect(endpoint: string, timeoutMs: number): Promise<IpcConn>;
  /** A3-5, POSIX only: true after unlinking endpoint when a connect to it fails with ECONNREFUSED and lstat shows a socket owned by this uid; false otherwise and always
   *  on win32 (a pipe disappears with its process). ClaudeServer.listen calls it once on EADDRINUSE before the squatter handshake; a live listener is never removed. */
  removeStale(endpoint: string): Promise<boolean>;
}
export const realIpc: Ipc;

// src/ports/fs.ts (changed; X5-46) — L-FS-PORT: the only node:fs importer (the loader excepted)
/** A1-6 (Task 1): the canonical-root probe, declared here because src/ports may not import src/data or src/claude; src/data/canonRoot.ts imports and re-exports it (X-63). */
export interface RootProbe { realpathNative(p: string): string; exists(p: string): boolean; readText(p: string): string | null; isDir(p: string): boolean; }
/** A1-6 (Task 1): realpathNative = fs.realpathSync.native; exists = existsSync without following a final symlink (lstatSync, throwIfNoEntry: false); readText = one
 *  readFileSync(p, 'utf8') or null on any error; isDir = lstatSync(p).isDirectory(). Synchronous: the loader's copy and core's hot path need no promise hop. */
export const realRootProbe: RootProbe;
// A3-1 (Task 3): FsLstat gains uid?: number (lstat({bigint: true}).uid; realFs fills it on every platform, Windows reports 0) and ctimeNs?: bigint (status-change time);
//   sameStat ignores both; optional so Plan 2/3 FsLstat literals keep compiling.
// A3-2 (Task 3; X-73): Plan 2's landed Fs.writeFileExclusive(p, data) gains only the optional third parameter — writeFileExclusive(p: string, data: Buffer, mode?: number):
//   Promise<void> — mode passed to the exclusive open (POSIX; the umask still applies and createIpcKey verifies the result); default unchanged; realFs and Plan 1's FaultFs
//   pass it through. Order of the additive Fs edits: Plan 5 (this, RootProbe, realRootProbe, FsLstat.uid?/ctimeNs?, realWatch), then Plan 7 (openExclusive + FaultOp, volumeKind).
/** A9-2 (Task 9 creates it, Task 14 reuses it — A14-1): the one node:fs.watch binding; node:fs.watch with errors swallowed, the returned function closes the watcher; the
 *  production binding of MarkerBackstop's `watch` and of McpDeps.watch; tests pass their own watch function. The Fs interface gains no watch member. */
export function realWatch(path: string, cb: () => void): () => void;

// src/ports/notifier.ts — 11 §3.2 Notifier seam (OS notification, status write, trace event). X-67: the one Notifier interface of every plan, created here by Task 1;
//   Plan 6 appends its OsClass to NtClassKey (and deletes its NotifierPort), Plan 7 appends its classes to NtClass (24 in all) and its NotificationDispatcher extends Notifier.
export type NtClass = 'NT_NOHOOKS' | 'NT_SECURITY' | 'NT_LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED' | 'NT_PLUGIN_REMOVED';   // Plan 5's four (X-67); Plan 7 appends in place
export type NtClassKey = NtClass;                                                                        // X-67: Plan 6 widens it to NtClass | OsClass in place
export type NotifyParams = Readonly<Record<string, string | number | null>>;   // A1-5: structurally 8a's Params (src/ports may not import src/i18n types)
/** X-67: key — the per-subject rate key (e.g. NT_REQUEST_AGE's per-request rule); absent = the class's own rate. */
export interface Notifier { raise(cls: NtClassKey, code: string, params: NotifyParams, key?: string): void; }
export function logNotifier(log: Log): Notifier;   // Plan 5 production binding until Plan 7's OS adapter (X5-31); A1-5: each call is one log.write('warn', code, { ...params, cls }) line
/** X-67: a factory (no class); A1-5: 11 §3.2 "recorder (counts per reason class)". */
export function recordingNotifier(): Notifier & { readonly calls: ReadonlyArray<{ cls: NtClassKey; code: string; params: NotifyParams; key: string | null }>; countOf(cls: NtClassKey): number };

// src/ports/inbox.ts — 11 §3.2 InboxPoster seam; used only by dist/mcp.js (Task 14)
export type PostResult = 'sent' | 'refused' | 'error';
/** 06 §3.16.5: connect to the socket, write {"type":"auth","token":T} first, then exactly one {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":text}} line with no session_id,
 *  observe 100 ms on the caller's monotonic clock: still open → sent; closed → refused; connect/write failure → error. */
export interface InboxPoster { post(socket: string, token: string, text: string): Promise<PostResult>; }
/** R5-64: the M45 site — frame 'message' writes {"type":"message",…} instead of the user line; only src/entry/mcp.ts selects it, in a test build under
 *  COWORK_TEST_MUTANT=M45 (behind __COWORK_TEST_BUILD__, A4-11's pattern). */
export function createInbox(o: { frame: 'user' | 'message' }): InboxPoster;
export const realInbox: InboxPoster;   // = createInbox({frame: 'user'})

// src/ports/trace.ts — Plan 5 variants (Task 1 declares all; each owning task emits its own). No durations, no wall times (TST-03). X-115: one TraceEvent union, members
//   appended in place by each plan (no alias union); Plan 5's kinds are hook lease seat turn ring evidence acting deny_record edit_trigger ipc carrier capture_late, disjoint
//   from every other plan's (Plan 6 deletes its coord_carrier and second CarrierKind and reads this carrier trace, X-44).
/** A1-5: the same 14-member string union as HookEvent (src/ports may not import src/claude); a type-level test pins the two unions equal. */
export type TraceHookEvent = 'SessionStart' | 'UserPromptSubmit' | 'UserPromptExpansion' | 'PreToolUse' | 'PostToolUse' | 'PostToolUseFailure'
  | 'PostToolBatch' | 'PermissionDenied' | 'Stop' | 'StopFailure' | 'SubagentStart' | 'SubagentStop' | 'SessionEnd' | 'Notification';
export type TraceEvent =
  /* …Plan 2/3 variants unchanged… */
  | { kind: 'hook'; peer: string; seat: string | null; event: TraceHookEvent; promptId: string | null; toolUseId: string | null;
      outcome: 'g1' | 'g2' | 'g3' | 'g4' | 'block' | 'context' | 'none' | 'nonlive' | 'paused'; code: string | null }                 // Tasks 4, 5
  | { kind: 'lease'; peer: string; seat: string; lease: 'INFLIGHT' | 'READ' | 'TXN'; key: string; path: string | null;
      op: 'acquire' | 'renew' | 'release'; why: string | null }                                                                         // O-LEASE input (Task 8)
  | { kind: 'seat'; peer: string; seat: string; op: 'register' | 'alias' | 'wiring' | 'flag' | 'end'; sessionId: string | null;
      wiring: string | null; flags: readonly string[]; why: string | null }                                                             // O-SEAT input (Tasks 5, 9)
  | { kind: 'turn'; peer: string; seat: string; promptId: string | null; op: 'human' | 'wake' | 'doorbell' | 'stop'; nonce: string | null;
      humanPromptRefreshed: boolean; csReset: boolean; released: number }                                                              // O-TURN-ORIGIN input (Task 6)
  | { kind: 'ring'; peer: string; seat: string; nonce: string; result: 'sent' | 'refused' | 'error' | 'not_rung' }                      // Task 6
  | { kind: 'evidence'; peer: string; seat: string | null; verb: string; promptId: string | null;
      op: 'mint' | 'grant' | 'consume' | 'refuse' | 'expire'; why: string | null }                                                      // O-HUMANONLY input (Task 15)
  | { kind: 'acting'; peer: string; verb: string; accepted: boolean; route: 'tty' | 'evidence' | null }                                 // Task 15
  | { kind: 'deny_record'; peer: string; seat: string; id: string; phase: 'prerecord' | 'converted' | 'local_only' }                    // O-RECORD-FIRST input (Task 11)
  | { kind: 'edit_trigger'; peer: string; seat: string; cs: string; editN: number; candSeq: number | null;
      outcome: 'candidate' | 'shrink' | 'slow_read' | 'base_moved' | 'batch' | 'pinned' | 'shadow' | 'paused' }                          // O-EDITCHECK input (Task 13)
  | { kind: 'ipc'; peer: string; op: 'accept' | 'auth_ok' | 'auth_fail' | 'close'; bytesToUnauthenticated: number }                     // O-IPC input (Task 3)
  | { kind: 'carrier'; peer: string; seat: string; event: TraceHookEvent; chars: number; metered: number; codes: readonly string[]; eventIds: readonly string[] }   // Task 7
  | { kind: 'capture_late'; peer: string; cs: string; candSeq: number; source: 'green' | 'tool' | 'closing' };   // Task 13 (A13-8): a closing/green/tool capture taken late (R5-20)
//   edit_trigger is emitted once per trigger, with candSeq null for every skip (A13-8)

// src/engine/deps.ts (changed; R5-02)
export type MutantId = /* …Plan 2/3 members… */ | 'M08' | 'M26' | 'M27' | 'M37' | 'M40' | 'M41' | 'M44' | 'M45' | 'M47';   // X-114: one union, appended in place, no alias types; M41/M44/M45 sites are Plan 5's alone (X-43)
//   11 §3.9; one site each, test builds only (R-29): M08 loader (Task 4), M26 output encoding (Task 4), M27 spawn env (Task 4), M40/M41 hook reads (Task 8),
//   M44 turn origin (Task 6), M45 the inbox frame type (Task 14, R5-64), M37/M47 Plan 3's captureStep/settleCandidate (Task 13); the loader, core and the MCP
//   server read their mutant from env COWORK_TEST_MUTANT only in a test build (L-DIST strips it) and type it as the wire's ClientMutantId (A4-14), never as MutantId
//   A4-11 (Task 4) test-build seams, all behind __COWORK_TEST_BUILD__ (removed by --release): COWORK_TEST_MUTANT — M08 in the loader's step-1 handler (exit 0 instead of 2),
//   M26 in encodeOutput (a G2 PreToolUse output gains a permission-decision "allow" key composed at run time, so no source or bundle contains the literal L-PERMISSION forbids),
//   M27 in spawnDaemon (the child gets the caller's whole environment instead of buildDaemonEnv's); COWORK_TEST_KILL_BEFORE_PRINTED=1 (core exits right after flushing its
//   output, before sending printed, for 06 test 5). A6-11 (Task 6): M44 makes onPromptSubmit treat a nonce-carrying prompt as a human prompt (refreshes
//   lastHumanPromptMono, opens a human turn, so signal.emit sends prompt).
/** The engine-facing Claude port (06 §3.7.3, §3.7.5; ENG-23, ENG-31; 01 §3.11 P1). */
export interface ClaudeEnginePort {
  txnOpen(): boolean;                                                         // any Bash|PowerShell|Monitor TXN open on this worktree → no detection tick, no materialization, no apply
  deferred(): ReadonlyMap<string, DeferReason>;                               // deferredPaths(worktree): fence | read_lease | txn | conflicted_leased (R5-25)
  recheck(paths: readonly string[]): ReadonlyMap<string, DeferReason>;        // P1 re-check of one write group (a fence or TXN opened after J)
  fenced(): ReadonlySet<string>;                                              // INFLIGHT-fenced paths: never re-read by a tick (TickInput.skip, ENG-23)
  /** After every written group, once, with exactly the written paths (A8-5): read-set apply instants, READ_REPLACED, apply journal. detail[i].from/.to = the pre-apply A
   *  and the written blob; writer = the incoming CS's writer when Plan 2's apply has it, else null (X5-06's IncomingCs.writer is Plan 7's seam). */
  applied(paths: readonly string[], detail?: ReadonlyArray<AppliedPath>): void;
}
export interface AppliedPath { path: string; from: OidOrAbsent; to: OidOrAbsent; writer: PeerId | null; }   // A8-5
export const noClaude: ClaudeEnginePort;                                      // Plan 2/3 behaviour: nothing open, nothing deferred; applied() ignores its arguments
export interface EngineDeps { /* …Plan 2/3 fields… */ claude: ClaudeEnginePort; }   // default noClaude in every Plan 2/3 fixture and in tools/real/two-clone.ts
// src/engine/writer.ts (changed; Task 8): WriterDeps gains `recheck?: (paths: readonly string[]) => ReadonlyMap<string, DeferReason>` — called once per group immediately before P1;
//   any hit defers the whole group with that reason (GroupResult.outcome 'deferred'), nothing of it written (01 §3.11 writer protocol P1).
// src/engine/core.ts (changed; Task 8): tickSkip(core) ∪ deps.claude.fenced(); prepareIteration skips the detection tick and materialization while deps.claude.txnOpen();
//   applyStep's admission and D take deps.claude.deferred() (each entry with its reason, INV-GROUP closures unchanged; a conflict take-TIP write on a deferred path records
//   conflicted_leased — the engine's admission chooses it, not DeferSet); deps.claude.applied(written, detail) after every written group;
//   EngineCore.onHoldCode(cb: (r: HoldCodeRaise) => void): () => void — listeners notified by Plan 3's raiseHoldCodes after its status and log writes (A8-9; additive edit
//   of Plan 3's code in this file, X5-41); Task 8 binds it to routeHoldCode.
// src/data/blobRoutes.ts (changed; Task 8, A8-7): the one new hash-object argv (L-OBJ-WRITE) —
```

### Security (Task 3)

```ts
// src/sec/ipcKey.ts — SEC-20
export interface KeyDeps { fs: Fs; random: Random; platform: NodeJS.Platform; uid: number | null; acl: WinAcl; }
export type KeyCheck = { ok: true; key: Buffer } | { ok: false; code: 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL' | null };   // null: no key file (the caller never connects; MCP → NO_MCP, hooks → G4)
/** <dataDir>/ipc.key: 32 bytes; POSIX mode 0600, owner = uid, parent not group/world-writable; win32 SDDL ACEs ⊆ {user SID, S-1-5-18, S-1-5-32-544}; never another location.
 *  A3-6 (flag, SE5-17): on win32 a verifier that passed (the daemon at start and at every 30 s check, Task 9; the MCP server at start; a hook that had to verify) writes
 *  <dataDir>/ipc.key.acl (user-only) holding the one-line JSON {"ctimeNs":…,"dev":…,"ino":…,"mtimeNs":…,"size":…} (decimal strings, keys in that fixed order, encoded
 *  locally with JSON.stringify — src/sec may not import src/claude/wire; a test-side parity check pins it byte-equal to canonicalJsonLine, X5-45) of the key file it verified; stamp 'use' (hooks) accepts
 *  the key without spawning when that stamp parses and equals the key's current lstat identity, else runs the full check and rewrites the stamp; 'write' (what the daemon
 *  passes, A5-1) = full verification, the stamp written on a pass; 'ignore' = full verification, the stamp neither read nor written (both readings inferred from A3-6, which
 *  defines only 'use'); a failing verifier deletes the stamp. POSIX verification is an lstat and needs no stamp. */
export function readIpcKey(dataDir: string, d: KeyDeps, o?: { stamp: 'use' | 'write' | 'ignore' }): Promise<KeyCheck>;
/** 32 random bytes created user-only (POSIX 0600 via the Fs port; win32 icacls /inheritance:r /grant:r *<SID>:F); called by Plan 7's start (X5-28) and setupLiveRepo. */
export function createIpcKey(dataDir: string, d: KeyDeps): Promise<Buffer>;
export function keyHash(key: Buffer): string;   // SEC-23 "exists with the same hash" (the 30 s check, Task 9)

// src/sec/winAcl.ts — SEC-20; the one SEC process runner (fixed argv, shell false, windowsHide; L-PORTS allow-listed; R5-48)
export interface WinAcl { currentUserSid(): Promise<string>; restrictToUser(file: string, sid: string): Promise<void>; readSddl(file: string): Promise<string>; }
/** A3-4: realWinAcl = createWinAcl(execFileRunner), the file's one child_process.execFile call (shell: false, windowsHide: true, 10 s timeout, environment of only SystemRoot,
 *  PATH and COWORK_ACL_PATH); the three fixed argv: `whoami /user /fo csv /nh`; `icacls <file> /inheritance:r /grant:r *<SID>:F`; `powershell.exe -NoProfile -NonInteractive
 *  -Command (Get-Acl -LiteralPath $env:COWORK_ACL_PATH).Sddl` with COWORK_ACL_PATH=<file> (the path never enters a PowerShell command string; R5-48). */
export function createWinAcl(run: (file: string, args: readonly string[], env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>) => Promise<{ code: number; stdout: Buffer }>): WinAcl;
export const realWinAcl: WinAcl;
// X-62: no SDDL parser here — sddlAllowSids and ALLOWED_SYSTEM_SIDS are deleted names. The SDDL parsing and the user-only verdict are src/sec/sddl.ts's (moved from 8a's
//   src/viz/cli/dataDir.ts, which re-exports them; F-8a-2, run by the 8a controller before Task 1 — verify pass V-12), with its one allowed-SID set (the user, S-1-5-18,
//   S-1-5-32-544): `interface Sddl { owner: string | null; allow: string[]; deny: string[] }`, `parseSddl(sddl: string): Sddl | null`, `sddlUserOnly(s: Sddl, userSid:
//   string): boolean`, `sddlOwnerOk(owner: string | null, userSid: string): boolean` (8a's landed signatures); readIpcKey's win32 check is sddlUserOnly(parseSddl(readSddl(key)),
//   currentUserSid()) — SIDs compared, never localized names. User-only directory creation stays 8a's verifyDataDir(dir, {create: true}) (no createUserOnlyDir).

// src/sec/endpoint.ts — SEC-21
/** k = HMAC-SHA256(key, "endpoint:" ‖ endpointRoot); win32 \\.\pipe\cowork-<hex(k[0..16])>; POSIX <dir>/<hex(k[0..12])>.sock. */
export function endpointName(key: Buffer, endpointRoot: string, platform: NodeJS.Platform, socketDir: string | null): string;
/** First of $XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/cowork-sync, /tmp/cws-<uid> whose full socket path is ≤ 100 bytes; created 0700, lstat-verified (directory, owner uid, not a symlink, 0700). */
export function socketDir(env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>, uid: number, fs: Fs, sockName: string): Promise<{ ok: true; dir: string } | { ok: false; code: 'SEC_REFUSE_SOCKET_PATH' }>;

// src/sec/handshake.ts — SEC-22, INV-IPC, R5-45
/** A3-3: src/sec comes before src/claude in the import order, so the session codec is injected; callers bind it as wireCodec = {reader: () => new LineReader(),
 *  encode: encodeFrame, validate: (d, v) => validateFrame('session', d, v)} (Task 4's openSession, Task 5's ClaudeServer). The handshake frames (hello, srv, cli) are
 *  validated by handshake.ts itself (fixed shapes; base64 of exactly 16 bytes for nonces and 32 for MACs). */
export interface FrameCodec { reader(): { push(b: Buffer): string[] }; encode(f: object): Buffer; validate(dir: 'c2s' | 's2c', v: unknown): object | null; }
export interface SecureConn<F extends object = object> { send(f: F): void; onFrame(cb: (f: F) => void): void; onClose(cb: () => void): void; close(): void; }   // A3-3: callers instantiate it with the wire unions
export class HandshakeFailed extends Error { readonly code: 'SEC_IPC_HANDSHAKE_FAILED'; }
/** Client: sends {t:'hello',v:1,n1}; verifies srv.p = HMAC(key, "srv" ‖ n1 ‖ n2 ‖ endpoint) with timingSafeEqual; sends cli.p = HMAC(key, "cli" ‖ n2 ‖ n1 ‖ endpoint); sends no session byte before srv verifies. */
export function clientHandshake(c: IpcConn, key: Buffer, endpoint: string, random: Random, timeoutMs: number, codec: FrameCodec, o?: { clock?: Clock }): Promise<SecureConn>;
/** Server: sends nothing before hello; verifies cli; then frames are NDJSON ≤ 1 MiB validated by codec.validate (validateFrame('session', 'c2s')); unknown t / schema failure /
 *  oversize → close. A mismatch closes at once and calls onFail. A3-3: a handshake not completed within o.timeoutMs (Task 5 binds 3,000, the SessionStart deadline) closes with
 *  onFail('timeout'); onEvent is the O-IPC trace input (Task 5 maps it to TraceEvent ipc with its peer). */
export function serverHandshake(c: IpcConn, key: Buffer, endpoint: string, random: Random, onFail: (why: 'mac' | 'shape' | 'timeout') => void, codec: FrameCodec,
  o: { clock: Clock; timeoutMs: number; onEvent?: (op: 'accept' | 'auth_ok' | 'auth_fail' | 'close', bytesToUnauthenticated: number) => void }): Promise<SecureConn>;

// src/sec/securityLog.ts — ENG-03, SEC-38, R5-46
/** A3-7: a local structural copy of 8a's Params (src/sec does not import src/i18n); X5-45 pins it. */
export type SecParams = Readonly<Record<string, string | number | null>>;
export interface SecurityLog { event(code: string, params: SecParams): void; }
/** <coworkDir>/log/security.jsonl (codes and typed params only), each line mirrored to the daemon log at level 'security'. X-34: the one security Log per daemon —
 *  src/daemon/run.ts builds it once and passes it to Plan 4's productionRegionDeps as RegionDeps.securityLog (Plan 4 uses a plain fileLog behind that parameter only
 *  before Plan 5); Plan 7 later wraps it with secureLog/rotatingFileLog. No second writer of log/security.jsonl exists. */
export function securityLog(fs: Fs, clock: Clock, coworkDir: string, daemonLog: Log): SecurityLog;
/** SEC-22: ≥ 5 failures within 60 s → at most one NT_SECURITY notification per 30 min. */
export class HandshakeFailureRate { constructor(clock: Clock, notifier: Notifier, security: SecurityLog); fail(why: string): void; }
```

```ts
// src/entry/hook.ts — the loader (06 §3.4.1, CCI-06…CCI-08, CCI-45, CCI-54); bundled alone as dist/hook.js (≤ 150 lines, node:* only; L-LOADER). Not a module API: its steps are the contract.

//    and `declare const __COWORK_TEST_BUILD__: boolean` (both defined by the build, A2-1); Task 2's stub exports LOADER_TEXTS_EMBEDDED so the strings survive

//  A4-2: the loader (node:* only) carries private copies of canonRoot + findRepo + liveState (synchronous node:fs), seatKeyOf, evidenceFor, evidenceTag, canonicalJsonLine,
//    pluginIdOf/resolveDataDir (X-61: the loader is the one place a copy of src/data/pluginDataDir.ts is unavoidable), readUserJson's language, loaderLang (X-53) and
//    normArgs (X-60); loader.parity.test.ts keeps each equal to the shared module it copies (src/data/pluginDataDir.ts, src/data/canonRoot.ts, Task 1's wire and text).
//  1 process.on('uncaughtException'|'unhandledRejection') → gating event: LOADER_TEXTS.G4[L_c] to stderr, exit 2; other events: exit 0, no output. Exit 1 never.
//    A4-1: "gating event" = argv[2] === 'PreToolUse' (all three PreToolUse matchers are gating events, 06 §2), so the test needs no parsed input and holds for malformed
//    stdin (06 test 2); the Agent|EnterWorktree class is decided in core without IPC (A4-7) and never takes G4.
//  1a A4-13: data dir (copy of resolveDataDir with CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT or the loader's own ../) and user.json `language` → L_c (loaderLang), before stdin is read, so every
//    failure after step 1 (malformed stdin included) renders in L_c; neither needs stdin.
//  2 read stdin fully, JSON.parse (failure → step 1 mapping).
//  3 root: edit-class PreToolUse from tool_input.file_path|notebook_path (relative → against cwd), else cwd; canonical root form (loader copy of canonRoot);
//    walk to the first `.git`: none → exit 0; a file → never live: when its common gitdir is live, WT_NOTE (WT_SUBMODULE under modules/) as additionalContext on
//    SessionStart, edit-class PreToolUse and SubagentStart (A4-2: 06 §3.10 wins over step 3's two-event list, SE5-18), emitted as the R5-11 code lines
//    `[cowork-sync] WT_NOTE` / `[cowork-sync] WT_SUBMODULE`, exit 0; a directory → live/paused per liveState.
//  4 UserPromptExpansion with expansion_type 'slash_command' and command_name ∈ {cowork, cowork-sync:cowork}: verb `pause` → create <gitdir>/cowork/paused (idempotent),
//    `resume` → remove it (both without core.js); every verb → {verb, args_sha256} (normArgs); acting verb and no cws- nonce in the prompt → evidence record
//    {seat_key, prompt_id, verb, args_sha256, mono_ns, used:false}; never blocks (exit 0, no output); neither prompt text nor command_args leaves the process.
//  5 not live → exit 0, no IPC, no output (except step 4's marker and the evidence line, appended to <gitdir>/cowork/tmp/evidence.jsonl, HMAC-tagged when ipc.key exists).
//  6 paused → no IPC; SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit print the 07 PAUSED line as additionalContext; other events exit 0 (plus step 4).
//  7 UserPromptSubmit sends only nonces[] (via core); nothing waits on it.
//  8 try { await import('./core.js') } catch → gating event: T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL {v} to stderr, exit 2; other events exit 0. Then core.main(...);   // A2-1: the ESM form of 06's require (SE5-16)
//    an evidence record core did not deliver (no reply in the deadline) is appended to evidence.jsonl by the loader.
//  seat_key = seatKeyOf(env, process.ppid) (SHA-256(CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET) when set, else String(process.ppid)); the token variable is never read (L-TOKEN).

// src/claude/client/hookCore.ts — dist/core.js (06 §3.4.2, CCI-09…CCI-11)
/** A4-3: startNs is the loader's process.hrtime.bigint() at its first statement; Deadline starts at Number(startNs / 1_000_000n) on the same base as machineClock. */
export interface CoreEnv { env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>; ppid: number; pluginRoot: string; startNs: bigint; platform: NodeJS.Platform; home: string; osLocale: string | null; }
export interface CoreIo { stdout(s: string): void; stderr(s: string): void; flush(): Promise<void>; }
export interface CoreDeps { fs: Fs; ipc: Ipc; clock: Clock; random: Random; spawner: DaemonSpawner;
  acl: WinAcl; uid: number | null; probe: RootProbe; }   // A4-3 (socketDir's input comes through CoreEnv.env)
// A4-14: core parses <gitdir>/cowork/status.json as the wire's StatusHeartbeat and its test-build mutant as ClientMutantId (L-HOOK-THIN: no src/engine type, R5-03).
/** A4-3: core's production clock = realClock with monoMs = machineMonoMs (Plan 2, process.hrtime.bigint() / 1e6, the only hrtime reader). */
export const machineClock: Clock;
export interface CoreOutcome { exit: 0 | 2; evidenceDelivered: boolean; }
/** The loader's entry into core.js after steps 1–8 for a live, unpaused repo. Never rejects for a loader-controlled failure: deadline expiry or connection failure →
 *  gating event G4 (exit 2), other events exit 0.
 *  A4-6 DAEMON_DOWN: printed by the first non-gating hook of a seat after the daemon became unreachable whose event can carry text — SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit,
 *  SubagentStart and PostToolUse (additionalContext and systemMessage, both the G4 text, C15), PostToolUseFailure (additionalContext only); an event that cannot carry text
 *  prints nothing and leaves the notice pending. <gitdir>/cowork/tmp/seen-<first 8 hex of seat_key> holds the outage's episode id (the stale status.json heartbeat_mono_ms as
 *  a decimal string, or `missing`); the notice prints iff the file is absent or holds another episode id (nothing is deleted on the hot path); a pid seat key uses the first
 *  8 hex of sha256(seat_key).
 *  A4-7: Agent|EnterWorktree PreToolUse never opens a connection, live or not (06 §3.10): worktreeDenied true → exit 2 with renderClaude('WORKTREE_DENY', {}, L_c, 'deny')
 *  on stderr, else exit 0 with no output.
 *  A4-8: with the daemon down and <gitdir>/HEAD not `ref: refs/heads/<b>`, core spawns nothing (R5-19): a gating event takes G4 at once, a non-gating one exits 0 (with A4-6).
 *  A4-9: at SessionStart core starts copyRuntime concurrently with the hook request and awaits it after printed for at most the remaining deadline. */
export function main(event: HookEvent, json: unknown, repo: { root: string; gitdir: string }, evidence: EvidenceRecord | null, env: CoreEnv): Promise<CoreOutcome>;
/** Steps 1–6 with injected io and ports (the SIM fake seat calls this in process, R5-16): status.json liveness (heartbeat older than 3 loop periods or missing → spawnDaemon first);
 *  connect + handshake (retry every 50 ms until the deadline); exactly one hook frame; frames until the first complete reply line (an extend frame raises the deadline once, ≤ 7 s);
 *  write the event's output (encodeOutput), flush stdout and stderr, then send printed{batch_id} on the same connection; never waits for the pipe's end event. */
export function runCore(event: HookEvent, input: HookInput, repo: LiveRepo, evidence: EvidenceRecord | null, env: CoreEnv, io: CoreIo, deps: CoreDeps): Promise<CoreOutcome>;

// src/claude/client/ipcClient.ts
export class Deadline { constructor(clock: Clock, startMono: number, ms: number); remainingMs(): number; expired(): boolean; extendTo(ms: number): boolean; }   // extendTo: once, ≤ 7,000
/** Verified key (readIpcKey with {stamp: 'use'}, A3-6), endpoint, connect + clientHandshake with wireCodec; null when no verified key, no endpoint or no connection before
 *  the deadline. A4-5: a key refusal or a missing key returns null at once (no retry, no connect, S3 → G4); a connect or handshake failure is retried every
 *  C.RETRY_SPACING_MS (50) until the deadline (06 §3.4.2 step 2); env feeds socketDir. */
export function openSession(repo: LiveRepo, d: { fs: Fs; ipc: Ipc; random: Random; platform: NodeJS.Platform; uid: number | null; acl: WinAcl;
  env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>> }, deadline: Deadline): Promise<SecureConn | null>;
/** A4-5: {reader: () => new LineReader(), encode: encodeFrame, validate: (dir, v) => validateFrame('session', dir, v)} — the one FrameCodec binding (A3-3). */
export const wireCodec: FrameCodec;
/** Sends one frame and resolves with the first reply-kind frame; an ExtendFrame calls deadline.extendTo and keeps waiting; 'timeout' at expiry. */
export function request(conn: SecureConn, f: SessionClientFrame, deadline: Deadline): Promise<SessionServerFrame | 'timeout'>;

// src/claude/client/output.ts — CCI-11, INV-CCI-NODECISION, R5-44
/** Per-event encoding: PreToolUse G1/G4 → exit 2 + UTF-8 stderr reason; G2/G3 → exit 0 with empty stdout or {"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"PreToolUse","additionalContext":…}} (+ top-level systemMessage);
 *  PostToolUse → exit 0, additionalContext, or {"decision":"block","reason":…} (K2 only), optional systemMessage; PostToolUseFailure → exit 0, optional additionalContext;
 *  SessionStart/SubagentStart/UserPromptSubmit → exit 0, optional additionalContext and systemMessage, never decision:block; Stop → exit 0 empty or decision:block;
 *  SubagentStop, StopFailure, SessionEnd, PostToolBatch, PermissionDenied, Notification, UserPromptExpansion → exit 0, no stdout. Never 'permissionDecision'; no terminalSequence. */
export function encodeOutput(event: HookEvent, r: ReplyFrame | { g4: true; lang: ClaudeLang }): { exit: 0 | 2; stdout: string; stderr: string };

// src/claude/client/spawn.ts — CCI-51 (L-PORTS allow-listed; the only daemon spawn, L-SPAWN)
export interface DaemonSpawner { spawn(argv: readonly string[], env: Record<string, string>): void; }   // process.execPath, detached, stdio 'ignore', windowsHide, unref()
/** A4-4: realSpawner = createSpawner(childProcess.spawn); options {detached: true, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true, env}, then child.unref(); never waits for the child
 *  and never attaches the hook's stdio. */
export function createSpawner(spawn: typeof import('node:child_process').spawn): DaemonSpawner;
export const realSpawner: DaemonSpawner;
export interface SpawnDeps { fs: Fs; spawner: DaemonSpawner; env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>; pluginRoot: string; dataDir: string; version: string; passthrough: readonly string[]; }
/** Runs CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA/runtime/<version>/daemon.js, else ${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/dist/daemon.js, with `run --repo --branch --data-dir --plugin-root --spawned` (R5-19);
 *  environment exactly buildDaemonEnv (09 §3.4; never a CLAUDE_* value, M27), imported from Plan 1's src/git/env.ts — the client tier's one src/git import (R5-03; Task 2
 *  pins that file to node:* imports). Never called from the MCP tier. (P7 A5-4 name.) */
export function spawnDaemon(o: { repoRoot: string; branch: string }, d: SpawnDeps): Promise<void>;
/** SessionStart: copies daemon.js, worker.js and dist/grammars into CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA/runtime/<version>/ (temp dir + rename) within the 3 s deadline; 'present' when already there.
 *  A4-9: copies into <dataDir>/runtime/.tmp-<version>-<16 hex from Random>/, renames it to runtime/<version>/ and, before starting, removes every runtime/.tmp-* left by an
 *  interrupted copy; a copy cut by the deadline leaves only a .tmp-* directory (never a partial runtime/<version>/). */
export function copyRuntime(pluginRoot: string, dataDir: string, version: string, fs: Fs): Promise<'copied' | 'present' | 'failed'>;
/** <gitdir>/HEAD read as a file (no git process); `ref: refs/heads/<b>` → b; detached → null (no spawn; gating events take G4, R5-19). */
export function headBranch(gitdir: string, fs: Fs): Promise<string | null>;

// src/claude/client/worktree.ts — CCI-29 (local, no IPC; never G4)
export interface AgentDefRoots { user: string; repo: string; plugins: readonly string[]; }   // ~/.claude/agents, <repo>/.claude/agents, enabled plugins' agents/
/** A4-12: user = <home>/.claude/agents, repo = <repoRoot>/.claude/agents, plugins = <home>/.claude/plugins/cache/<marketplace>/<plugin>/<version>/agents for every
 *  <plugin>@<marketplace> set true in enabledPlugins of the user, project or local settings (newest <version> directory by semver); 06 §8 Q11 default: core reads definitions. */
export function agentDefRoots(repoRoot: string, home: string, fs: Fs): Promise<AgentDefRoots>;
/** EnterWorktree; Agent with tool_input.isolation === 'worktree'; Agent whose subagent_type names a definition with frontmatter `isolation: worktree` → true (WORKTREE_DENY, exit 2).
 *  A4-12: a definition is the first <subagent_type>.md found in repo, user, then plugins order; isolation is read from its YAML frontmatter key `isolation`. */
export function worktreeDenied(toolName: string, toolInput: WireJson, roots: AgentDefRoots, fs: Fs): Promise<boolean>;

// src/claude/client/policy.ts — CCI-04, SEC-28, SEC-29, 09 §3.14 item 2 (R5-49)
/** Reads only the documented managed-settings files (C:\Program Files\ClaudeCode\managed-settings.json, /Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json,
 *  /etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json and their managed-settings.d/) and the user, project and local settings; reports disableAllHooks, allowManagedHooksOnly,
 *  deniedMcpServers, allowManagedMcpServersOnly, crossSessionInbound, strict marketplace keys, enabledPlugins entries disabling cowork-sync and a managed statusLine. */
export function staticPolicyCheck(repoRoot: string, d: { fs: Fs; platform: NodeJS.Platform; home: string }): Promise<PolicyHit[]>;
export function checkInstallScope(repoRoot: string, d: { fs: Fs; home: string }): Promise<'ok' | 'REFUSE_PROJECT_SCOPE'>;
export function hostFloorRefusal(i: { ccVersion: string | null; nodeMajor: number; fromTerminal: boolean }): { code: 'REFUSE_CC_VERSION' | 'REFUSE_NODE_VERSION'; params: Params } | null;
/** CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA/repos.json + each listed gitdir's live marker; same repo identity (sorted root oids of the fetched branch, glossary §18) and branch → the first checkout's realpath. */
export function checkSecondCheckout(repoRoot: string, branch: string, d: { fs: Fs; dataDir: string; repoIdentity: (gitdir: string) => Promise<string | null> }): Promise<{ code: 'REFUSE_SECOND_CHECKOUT'; params: { first: string } } | null>;
export function checkWorktreeShape(loc: RepoLocation): 'ok' | 'REFUSE_WORKTREE';

// tools/build/build.mjs (changed; Task 2) — A2-1 (flag, SE5-16): the bundles stay ESM (Plan 1 format 'esm', package.json "type": "module"), so the loader's step 8 is
```
