### Client tier: MCP server and CLI client (Tasks 14, 15, 16)

```ts
// src/claude/client/mcp/server.ts — CCI-47, CCI-48 (no gating; never spawns the daemon, L-SPAWN)
export interface McpEnv { env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>; ppid: number; cwd: string; pluginRoot: string; home: string; platform: NodeJS.Platform; }
export interface McpDeps { fs: Fs; ipc: Ipc; clock: Clock; random: Random; inbox: InboxPoster;
  watch: (path: string, cb: () => void) => () => void;   // A14-1: bound in src/entry/mcp.ts to realWatch (A9-2); tests pass a scripted function (fire on demand, or a no-op for the 30 s re-check)
  acl: WinAcl; uid: number | null; }
/** One stdio JSON-RPC 2.0 server per Claude Code process: mint seat_id (128 random bits, 32 lowercase hex), seat_key, Doorbell from its own env; initialize → clientInfo.version
 *  (< 2.1.270 → only cowork_status, result carrying CC_TOO_OLD, and hello.cc_version tells the daemon); resolve the repo from cwd (canonRoot); not live → only cowork_status
 *  (≤ 400 chars); watch <gitdir>/cowork/live (fs.watch + 30 s re-check) → notifications/tools/list_changed; live → openSession with backoff (HOOK_ONLY while no daemon),
 *  hello, heartbeat every 15 s, reconnect on loss; tools/call → a `tool` frame; ring frames → Doorbell.post → ring_result; stdin EOF → seat_end, exit within 1 s.
 *  A14-5 JSON-RPC mapping: tools/call {name, arguments} → one tool{call_id, name, args} frame; tool_result{text, structured} → {content: [{type: 'text', text}],
 *  structuredContent: structured, isError: false}; isError is never true (CCI-48). A14-3: with no daemon answering, the server itself answers cowork_status and cowork_show
 *  with {daemon: 'down', cause: 'DAEMON_DOWN'} (cause 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL' when the key check failed) and every other tool with {ok: false, code: 'DAEMON_DOWN'}. */
export function runMcpServer(io: { stdin: NodeJS.ReadableStream; stdout: NodeJS.WritableStream }, env: McpEnv, d: McpDeps): Promise<void>;

// src/claude/client/mcp/tools.ts — CCI-47, CCI-67, glossary §15, R5-08
export const TOOL_NAMES: readonly ToolName[];   // exactly the eight of glossary §15
export interface McpToolSchema { name: ToolName; description: string; inputSchema: WireJson; }
/** Every description includes "files a record on the shared cowork board; this is not a message to another session."; cowork_show carries F10's clause
 *  ("a change-set id of this PC adds an undo diff") and accepts `id: string` (Plan 6 widens through ext.showIdSchema); 'live' → eight tools ≤ 3,000 chars measured
 *  with CAPS.F05_CLAUSE_RESERVE reserved; 'nonlive' and 'too_old' → only cowork_status, ≤ 400. */
export function toolSchemas(mode: 'live' | 'nonlive' | 'too_old', ext?: { showClause?: string; showIdSchema?: WireJson }): McpToolSchema[];
export function schemaChars(s: readonly McpToolSchema[]): number;   // L-MCP-SCHEMA measure

// src/claude/client/mcp/doorbell.ts — CCI-49, SEC-24, INV-TOKEN (the only file naming CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN; L-TOKEN)
/** A2-3 (Task 2): the only spelling of the name in the repository (09 test 22); every test that plants a canary token sets env[TOKEN_ENV]. */
export const TOKEN_ENV: 'CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN';
export class Doorbell {
  constructor(env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>, inbox: InboxPoster);   // reads socket and token once into memory
  readonly messagingPresent: boolean;
  /** Posts only text a daemon `ring` frame carried; text beginning with '/' → 'error', nothing posted; only to its own session's socket. */
  post(text: string): Promise<PostResult>;
}

// src/claude/client/cliClient.ts — SEC-25 step 3, 09 §3.11 rev 2.5 (Task 15; sinceQuery/sinceRan Task 16)
export interface ProcWalker { claudeAncestor(pid: number, maxLevels: number): Promise<boolean>; }   // /proc walk on linux; win32/darwin: always false until Plan 7 (R5-13, X5-35)
/** A15-5: reads /proc/<pid>/stat (parent pid) and /proc/<pid>/cmdline through the Fs port, ≤ C.ANCESTRY_MAX_LEVELS levels; src/entry/cli.ts picks it on linux. */
export function linuxProcWalker(fs: Fs): ProcWalker;
export const noProcWalker: ProcWalker;   // A15-5: claudeAncestor → false; the win32/darwin binding until Plan 7 (X5-35)
/** args_sha256 is computed over the arguments after the verb in normArgs form (status --flow → sha256('"--flow"'), done → sha256('')), the same form the loader uses for the
 *  expansion (A15-4; normArgs(argv) is the shell-word rule of X-60, so quoted arguments match too). A15-6: mode is computed by src/entry/cli.ts with 8a's outputMode(process.stdout.isTTY, env) and passed in —
 *  cliClient.ts imports nothing from src/viz/** (R5-03, 8a L-VIZ-IMPORT); tty = isTTYin && mode === 'tty'. */
export function cliCaller(mode: CliMode, isTTYin: boolean, env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>, procs: ProcWalker, argv: readonly string[]): Promise<CliCaller>;   // verb + args_sha256 from argv (normArgs form)
/** R5-58: one-way slash_shown{seat_key, chars} after a slash-mode output is printed; the caller passes seatKeyOf(process.env, process.ppid); never awaited past the write,
 *  never refused. Plan 5's done/resolve dispatch and every Plan 7 verb call it (X5-36; Plan 7's reportSlashOutput is this function, X-64). */
export function reportSlashShown(chars: number, seatKey: string, repo: LiveRepo, d: CliDeps): Promise<void>;
/** A15-7: plain code-line en rows for every code runActing can print (SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN {cmd: 'text'}, RF_UNKNOWN_VERB, DAEMON_DOWN, SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL, Plan 2's
 *  Engine.done and Plan 3's Engine.resolve result and refusal codes); src/entry/cli.ts registers them with 8a's registerCatalog('en', …) before rendering — until Plan 7
 *  Task 2 moves these rows (and Task 16's en rows) into its six-language catalogs and deletes these registerCatalog calls in the same commit (X-72; a human-facing code row
 *  is registered by exactly one module at a time); Plan 7's L-CODES then runs over CCI_CODES ∪ ROUTED_CODES. */
export const CLI_EN_ROWS: Readonly<Record<string, CatalogEntry>>;
/** X-56: the one generic CLI → daemon call (Plan 7's ipcRequest is this function): verified key, endpoint, handshake (openSession), one frame {t: kind, ...body}, the first
 *  reply-kind frame or {refused: code}; a one-way kind (since_ran, slash_shown) resolves null once its frame is written; the per-kind helpers below are thin wrappers over it. */
export function cliRequest(kind: string, body: WireJson, repo: LiveRepo, d: CliDeps): Promise<WireJson | { refused: string }>;
/** Sends `acting`; the daemon waits ≤ 3 s for evidence and runs the verb or refuses SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN (printed with the command to retype). X-58: data passes Plan 7's
 *  previews, phases and single-use tokens through unchanged. */
export function runActing(verb: ActingVerb, params: WireJson, caller: CliCaller, repo: LiveRepo, d: CliDeps): Promise<{ ok: boolean; code: string; params: Params; data?: WireJson }>;
/** X-01: no caller fields — the daemon does not test who asked for flow or wrap. */
export function queryFlow(selector: FlowSelector, repo: LiveRepo, d: CliDeps): Promise<FlowResultFrame | { refused: string }>;
export function queryWrap(repo: LiveRepo, d: CliDeps): Promise<WrapResultFrame | { refused: string }>;
export function sinceQuery(repo: LiveRepo, d: CliDeps): Promise<SinceResultFrame | { refused: string }>;
export function sinceRan(to: number, caller: CliCaller, repo: LiveRepo, d: CliDeps): Promise<void>;   // applied or ignored by the daemon, never refused (FEAT-F04-18)
export interface CliDeps { fs: Fs; ipc: Ipc; clock: Clock; random: Random; platform: NodeJS.Platform; uid: number | null; acl: WinAcl; }
```

### Daemon composition, server, versions (Task 5)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/ctx.ts
export interface DaemonPorts { clock: Clock; random: Random; fs: Fs; scheduler: Scheduler; git: GitRepo; ipc: Ipc; notifier: Notifier; }
export interface HookRequest {
  frame: HookFrame; seat: Seat; startedMono: number;
  extend(ms: number): boolean;                                                       // SUSPECT only, once (CCI-17)
  turn: { doorbell: boolean; newTurn: boolean; origin: 'human' | 'wake' } | null;    // set by step turn.prompt (UserPromptSubmit) for the steps after it
  trigger: EditTrigger | null;                                                       // A12-1: set by post.edit and txn.close, read by checkpoint.trigger (Task 13)
}
export interface ReplyDraft {
  carrier: CarrierKind; items: CarrierItem[];
  deny: { code: string; params: Params; form: 'T1' | 'T1R' | 'other' } | null;   // G1: exit 2 reason (first deny wins; later steps skip)
  block: { code: string; params: Params } | null;                               // K2 (PostToolUse) or STOP_SUMMARY (Stop)
  done: boolean;                                                                 // a step ended the pipeline for this event
}
export type HookStep = (r: HookRequest, d: ReplyDraft, ctx: DaemonCtx) => Promise<void>;
export interface DaemonCtx {
  readonly repo: LiveRepo; readonly ports: DaemonPorts; readonly log: Log; readonly security: SecurityLog; readonly trace: Trace; readonly engine: EngineView;
  readonly bindings: ClaudeBindings; readonly prefs: () => UserPrefs; readonly versions: VersionPolicy; readonly seats: SeatRegistry; readonly seatLease: SeatLeasePort;
  readonly turns: TurnOrigin; readonly items: TurnItems; readonly signals: SignalPump; readonly ring: Ringer;
  readonly events: EventStore; readonly meter: BudgetMeter; readonly transitions: Transitions;
  readonly reads: DiskSnapshots; readonly leases: LeaseTable; readonly defer: DeferSet; readonly windows: AttributionWindows;
  readonly health: SeatHealth; readonly liveness: Liveness; readonly shadows: ShadowRecorder; readonly views: ViewBlobs; readonly readSets: ReadSets;
  readonly journal: ApplyJournal; readonly checkpoints: EditCheckpoints; readonly evidence: EvidenceStore; readonly acting: ActingVerbRegistry; readonly activity: HumanActivity;
}

// src/claude/daemon/pipeline.ts — the fixed step order (06 §3.4.2, §3.7, §3.8.4 step 3, §3.12, §3.14, §3.15); Task 5 declares it with every step a no-op stub;
// each concern module registers its own steps (registerStep) when its task lands; no later task reorders STEP_ORDER.
export type StepId = 'seat.register' | 'version.check' | 'health.observe' | 'turn.prompt' | 'turn.expansion' | 'turn.stop' | 'lease.release' | 'evidence.expansion'
  | 'activity.mark' | 'gate.edit' | 'gate.shell' | 'post.edit' | 'post.read' | 'txn.close' | 'checkpoint.trigger' | 'signal.emit' | 'journal.attribution'
  | 'carrier.context' | 'recap.system' | 'stop.decide' | 'transition.deliver' | 'event.deliver';
export const STEP_ORDER: Readonly<Record<HookEvent, readonly StepId[]>>;
//   SessionStart:        seat.register, version.check, health.observe, signal.emit, carrier.context, transition.deliver, event.deliver
//   UserPromptSubmit:    seat.register, turn.prompt, lease.release, signal.emit, activity.mark, carrier.context, recap.system, transition.deliver, event.deliver
//   UserPromptExpansion: seat.register, turn.expansion, evidence.expansion, activity.mark                                  (no output, never blocks)
//   PreToolUse:          seat.register, version.check, health.observe, gate.edit | gate.shell (by gating class), transition.deliver, event.deliver
//   PostToolUse:         seat.register, health.observe, post.edit, post.read, txn.close, lease.release, checkpoint.trigger, signal.emit, journal.attribution, carrier.context, transition.deliver, event.deliver
//   PostToolUseFailure:  seat.register, health.observe, txn.close, lease.release, checkpoint.trigger, signal.emit, event.deliver   (A13-9: the entry ships with Task 5's table, a no-op until Task 13 registers the step;
//                        a Bash/PowerShell TXN closed by its own PostToolUseFailure is trigger (b); an edit-class PostToolUseFailure carries no trigger, so the step is a no-op there)
//   PermissionDenied:    seat.register, health.observe, txn.close, lease.release, signal.emit, event.deliver   (a PermissionDenied close is never a checkpoint)
//   PostToolBatch:       seat.register, lease.release
//   Stop, StopFailure:   seat.register, stop.decide, signal.emit, lease.release, turn.stop, event.deliver
//   SubagentStart:       seat.register, signal.emit, carrier.context
//   SubagentStop, SessionEnd: seat.register, lease.release, signal.emit
//   Notification:        seat.register, health.observe
//   seat.register also renews the seat lease, observes activity and, for every event except UserPromptSubmit, calls TurnOrigin.onOtherHook (fallback (b), R5-36).
//   version.check is a reserved slot that no task registers: the version judgment runs inline in ClaudeServer after the reply (F2 #39, ruling R-F2-7).
export function registerStep(id: StepId, step: HookStep): void;   // throws when id is already registered
/** A5-7 daemon tickers: ClaudeDaemon runs every registered ticker in TICK_ORDER once per second (Δmono, the Scheduler port; one run at a time). Task 5 registers `seats`
 *  (SeatRegistry.tick: pairing windows, the 5 s pid checks, heartbeat loss) and `engineView` (EngineView.observe, A5-8); later tasks register theirs, as with registerStep,
 *  and never edit claudeDaemon.ts except for bindings. */
export type TickerId = 'seats' | 'turns' | 'idle' | 'ring' | 'leases' | 'health' | 'liveness' | 'engineView';
export const TICK_ORDER: readonly TickerId[];   // in the TickerId order above
export function registerTicker(id: TickerId, fn: (ctx: DaemonCtx) => Promise<void> | void): void;   // throws on a duplicate

// src/claude/daemon/engineView.ts — everything Plan 5 reads from the engine, from memory only (no git process, no file read; CCI-12)
export interface EngineView {
  peer(): PeerId; displayName(peer: PeerId): string;
  A(): TreeMap; tipHeight(): number; foldedHeight(): number; fold(): FoldState;
  scopeOf(path: string): { cls: ScopeClass; reason: NotSharedReason | null };                         // D5 scopeOfTracked; D6 admitNew for a new path
  portability(path: string): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string; suggestion: string };       // D7 portable()
  modeOf(path: string): string | null; symlinksEnabled(): boolean;                                   // gate row 7
  pathState(path: string): Promise<'idle' | 'applying' | 'pending' | 'busy'>;                         // Writer.pathState (ENG-38)
  pendingReason(path: string): DeferReason | null;                                                    // meta.pending (R5-30)
  offline(): { offline: boolean; offlineForMs: number }; publishPause(): PublishPause | null; freezeReasons(): readonly FreezeReason[];
  heldRanges(path: string): ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>;                                 // HLD-21: the HeldRangeOverlay while its base equals materializedOid(path) (A12-3), else Plan 3's Engine.heldRanges (unchanged)
  materializedOid(path: string): OidOrAbsent | null;                                                  // A12-2: the last materialized S_disk[p]
  openCsOf(seatId: string, agentId: string | null): string | null;                                    // A6-6 (Task 6; Task 12 uses it unchanged): the open seat CS from Plan 3's ChangeSets.table()
  csPaths(csId: string): readonly string[];                                                           // A6-6: that CsTableEntry's paths
  addOps(drafts: readonly OpDraft[]): void;                                                           // A12-2: the outbox binding SeatRegistry also uses
  untracked(): Promise<readonly string[]>;                                                            // A12-2: Plan 2's untracked listing (a listing command allowed by W1); never on the gate path
  snapshotEntries(): ReadonlyMap<string, { oid: Oid; stat: FsLstat | null }>;                         // A8-8: the newest completed snapshot's private-index entries, from memory (a TXN's S_open)
  registry(): HoldRegistry; lastStatus(): StatusJson | null; loopPeriodMs(): number;              // A5-8: lastStatus = Plan 2's Engine.lastStatusJson()
  ownAnchors(): { lastFetch: Anchor | null; lastPush: Anchor | null; suspendGapMs: number };          // PRO-37 (R5-06)
  checkpointCommands(): readonly string[];                                                            // TeamConfig.checks.checkpoint (R5-23)
  teamLanguage(): string | null;                                                                      // X-53 input (added by the reconciliation pass): the team config's language (glossary §17) from Plan 3's lenient TeamConfig read; null when absent
  peerFoldedAgoMs(peer: PeerId): number | null;   // F05-11: local Δmono since this daemon folded that (peer, epoch)'s newest board commit ({ACT} "last board activity {dur} ago")
  foldRecencyUncertain(): boolean;                // CCI-65: folds made during this daemon's start-up catch-up or the first fetch after an OFFLINE episode (lower bound "≥ {dur}")
  /** A5-8 (F05-11), called by the engineView ticker and the seat.register step: compares the fold's per-writer last_seq (Plan 2 fold step 5, X5-47) with its previous
   *  snapshot and stamps nowMono for every (peer, epoch) whose last_seq grew; peerFoldedAgoMs(peer) = now − the newest stamp of any epoch of that peer, null before one;
   *  foldRecencyUncertain() is true from attach until the first observation after the first completed iteration, and again from an observed publish_pause OFFLINE or AUTH
   *  until the first observation after it has cleared. */
  observe(nowMono: number): void;
}
export function engineView(e: Engine, core: EngineCore): EngineView;

// src/claude/daemon/claudeDaemon.ts — the composition root (06 §3.17; R5-02, R5-19)
/** X-38: Plan 5 owns the mechanics (carriers, caps, slots, metering, delivery, turn origin, nonce, ringing); Plan 6 owns content and decisions and reaches seats only through
 *  these ports, bound in its one file src/coord/adapters/claude.ts — bindClaude(coordinator, ctx: DaemonCtx): Partial<ClaudeBindings> (coordTools, coordHooks, wakeScope,
 *  wakePolicy, digest, slotExtra, dueRequests). X-111: the daemon composes ClaudeBindings = {...plan5Bindings(o), ...bindClaude(coordinator, ctx) (Plan 6), ...Plan 7's
 *  noteScan/flow/wrap bindings} in src/daemon/run.ts (SIM: test/sim/engineAdapter.ts; tests: test/helpers/seatHarness.ts); each plan edits only its own line. */
export interface ClaudeBindings { regions: RegionsPort; wakeScope: WakeScopePort; wakePolicy: WakePolicy; coordTools: CoordToolsPort; coordHooks: CoordHooksPort;
  digest: DigestPort; slotExtra: SlotExtraPort | null; dueRequests: DueRequestsPort; flow: FlowPort; wrap: WrapPort; noteScan: NoteScanPort; revertView: RevertViewPort; }
/** noRegions, plan5WakeScope, neverRing (scriptedRing only in SIM/FakeHost), outboxCoord, noCoordHooks, codeOnlyDigest, null, noDueRequests, noFlowEntries, noWrap,
 *  unavailableScan (canaryScan in test builds), plan3RevertView (R5-05, R5-08…R5-11, R5-37, R5-42, R5-53; X-39, X-40, X-51). */
export function plan5Bindings(o: { testBuild: boolean }): ClaudeBindings;
/** X-40: Plan 6's entry points that have no other Plan 5 port, called by Plan 5 at fixed points (default noCoordHooks: every call a no-op, onK1Deny → null, onK2 → [],
 *  grantPaused → false). The input types keep Plan 6's field lists; Plan 6's Coordinator implements them (src/coord/adapters/claude.ts). suspectCheck is not a member:
 *  the SUSPECT gate half is Plan 5's suspectNow/refreshForSuspect (R5-06), and Plan 6's SuspectApi binds Plan 4's SuspectPort. */
/** V-10 (O-16): renamed from K1DenyInput — the port's input is no longer the same shape as Plan 6's Coordinator K1DenyInput (one name per shape); Plan 6's bindClaude
 *  maps a K1DenyHookInput to its K1DenyInput. */
export interface K1DenyHookInput { seat: string; agent: string; toolUseId: string; turnId: string; path: string; claimId: string; holdId: string; newRecord: boolean;
  holders: ReadonlyArray<{ peer: PeerId; seat: string; claimId: string; sibling: boolean; siblingClaimOpKey: string | null }>;
  hunksInBase: readonly Hunk[]; }   // V-10 (Plan 6's O-16): the denied edit's PredictResult.hunksInBase. wholeFileCause and whitespaceOnly are not port fields (Plan 5 cannot
                                    // compute them: they are Plan 6's adapter helpers, X-24); Plan 6's bindClaude fills them from hunksInBase, its claim view and the live tree
                                    // into its own Coordinator input before composing the deny
export interface K2Input { seat: string; agent: string; toolUseId: string; csId: string; holdId: string; claimIds: readonly string[]; holders: K1DenyHookInput['holders']; foldedAtPre: boolean; }
export interface ReadInput { seat: string; agent: string; path: string; read: { offset?: number; limit?: number; full: boolean; enoent: boolean; size: number }; viewBlob: Oid; lineCount: number; }
export interface CoordHooksPort {
  onK1Deny(i: K1DenyHookInput): Promise<{ items: CarrierItem[]; cid: string; rid: string; creationOpId: string } | null>;   // after the K1 pre-record is written (CCI-18), before the G1 reply
  onK2(i: K2Input): Promise<CarrierItem[]>;                                                                         // after the K2 PIN exists, before the block reply
  onUserPromptSubmit(seat: string, p: { promptId: string; nonces: readonly string[]; midTurn: boolean }): void;     // after TurnOrigin classified the prompt (CCI-37)
  onLeaseLost(seat: string): void;                                                                                  // the seat's lease is no longer valid (R5-35)
  onRead(i: ReadInput): void;                                                                                       // PostToolUse(Read) of a LIVE path (F05)
  grantPaused(seat: string, claimId: string): boolean;                                                              // the G1 GRANT_PAUSE row (13b)
}
export const noCoordHooks: CoordHooksPort;
export interface ClaudeDaemonDeps { repo: LiveRepo; key: Buffer; endpoint: string; pluginVersion: string; ports: DaemonPorts; log: Log; security: SecurityLog; trace: Trace;
  bindings: ClaudeBindings; testMutants?: ReadonlySet<MutantId>;
  hookProto?: number;   // A5-14 (R5-62): default HOOK_PROTO; VersionPolicy({pluginVersion, hookProto}) — tests run a daemon of proto 3
  osLocale: string | null;   // X-53 input (added by the reconciliation pass): the daemon process's OS locale, read once at start by src/daemon/run.ts; with ctx.prefs().language and
                             //   EngineView.teamLanguage() it feeds resolveHumanLang({user, team, osLocale}), whose claudeLang(lh) is the L_c of every daemon-rendered Claude text
  /** A5-12 (R5-55), test-only: these DaemonCtx members replaced by doubles after createClaudeDaemon builds the rest; production passes none. */
  ctxOverrides?: Partial<Pick<DaemonCtx, 'events' | 'leases' | 'health' | 'readSets' | 'views' | 'turns' | 'signals' | 'reads'>>;
  pidAlive: (pid: number) => boolean; }   // A5-11: production process.kill(pid, 0) — true on success or EPERM, false on ESRCH; the tickers use ports.scheduler (DaemonPorts.scheduler)
export interface ClaudeDaemon {
  /** Bound into EngineDeps before the Engine is built. A5-5: claude = noClaude and attribution = humanOnlyAttribution until Task 8 binds DeferSet and hookAttribution; from
   *  Task 8 on every stub DeferSet reads returns its neutral value (R5-55), so no engine iteration reaches a throwing stub. */
  readonly engineDeps: { claude: ClaudeEnginePort; attribution: AttributionPort; lease: LeasePort };
  /** A5-12 (R5-55), in this order: await ctx.shadows.convertLeftovers() (every surviving tmp/deny-*.bin converted before anything else, 06 §3.6.6 step 7); EngineView;
   *  SignalPump; ctx.checkpoints.attachEngine(engine) (Task 13 subscribes onCandidateSettled/onCaptureTaken there); the status observer (→ ctx.transitions.observe,
   *  Task 7's routeHoldCode through EngineCore.onHoldCode once Task 8 binds it); the fold-step and human-CS subscriptions (Task 16). No hook frame is answered before it resolves. */
  attach(engine: Engine, core: EngineCore): Promise<void>;
  listen(): Promise<'serving' | 'reuse' | 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER'>;
  readonly ctx: DaemonCtx;
  txnOpen(): boolean;                                                                                   // X5-27 (Plan 7 LIF-28)
  close(why: 'drain' | 'removal' | 'version'): Promise<void>;
}
export function createClaudeDaemon(d: ClaudeDaemonDeps): ClaudeDaemon;

// src/claude/daemon/server.ts — SEC-21…SEC-23, CCI-09, CCI-41
export class ClaudeServer {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, key: Buffer, endpoint: string);
  /** Binds; on EADDRINUSE runs the handshake as a client: pass → 'reuse'; fail → 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER', a security event and one NT_SECURITY notification. */
  listen(): Promise<'serving' | 'reuse' | 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER'>;
  /** Request kinds other than hook frames (tool, acting, flow, wrap, since_*; Plans 6/7 add theirs); a handler never runs a supplied command, touches a path outside the
   *  main-worktree realpath, or returns a token, the key or an environment value (SEC-23). */
  onRequest(t: string, h: (f: SessionClientFrame, peer: { seatKey: string | null }) => Promise<SessionServerFrame | null>): void;   // R5-58: null for the one-way since_ran and slash_shown (nothing written)
  /** A5-6: writes on that seat's MCP connection (false when none) — the Ringer's send (Task 6). */
  sendToSeat(seatId: string, f: SessionServerFrame): boolean;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}
// A5-6 dispatch of authenticated session frames: hook → the pipeline of STEP_ORDER[event] over a fresh ReplyDraft, then draftToReply(r, d) (a minimal reply: decision deny
//   when d.deny, block when d.block, else none; text = renderClaude of the deny or block code; system null, events [], cursor 0; Task 7 replaces this one call with finishReply,
//   A7-4); printed → ctx.events.printed; MCP hello → seats.fromHello + VersionPolicy; heartbeat → seats.onHeartbeat; seat_end → seats.end(seat, 'mcp_eof'); ring_result →
//   ctx.ring.onResult; any other kind → the onRequest handler, and a valid frame with no handler closes the connection (no code is invented). Hook frames that arrive
//   before attach has resolved are held unanswered until it has (the loader's deadline governs). Each connection's serverHandshake runs with timeoutMs 3,000,
//   HandshakeFailureRate.fail on every failure and onEvent mapped to TraceEvent {kind: 'ipc', peer, op, bytesToUnauthenticated}; listen() calls Ipc.removeStale once on
//   EADDRINUSE before the squatter handshake (A3-5).
// draftToReply(r: HookRequest, d: ReplyDraft): ReplyFrame — A5-6: Task 5's minimal reply, internal to server.ts (A5-6 names the call, not an export; the signature is
//   inferred); superseded at its one call site by finishReply (Task 7, A7-4)

// src/claude/daemon/versions.ts — CCI-52, R2-I3
export class VersionPolicy {
  constructor(own: { pluginVersion: string; hookProto: number });
  /** Newer caller (semver) → 'serve_then_drain' (reply normally, then drain and exit); older caller with hook_proto current or previous → 'serve'; else 'restart_required' (edit-class G1 VERSION_RESTART {v}).
   *  A5-10: semver with prerelease ordering (1.0.0-rc.1 < 1.0.0); "previous" = own hookProto − 1; 'serve_then_drain' makes the server call ClaudeDaemon.close('version')
   *  (→ Engine.drain(null), listener closed) right after that reply is written; a newer hello triggers the same. */
  judge(callerVersion: string, callerProto: number): 'serve' | 'serve_then_drain' | 'restart_required';
}
```

### Seats, leases, turns, signals, rings (Tasks 5, 6)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/seats.ts — 06 §3.11, CCI-31…CCI-33, INV-CCI-SEAT, R5-33, R5-34
export type Wiring = 'FULL' | 'HOOK_ONLY' | 'NO_MCP' | 'NO_HOOKS';
export type SeatFlag = 'DEGRADED' | 'PAUSED' | 'PULL_MODE';
export const DISPLAY_PRECEDENCE: readonly ['NO_HOOKS', 'PAUSED', 'DEGRADED', 'NO_MCP', 'HOOK_ONLY', 'PULL_MODE', 'FULL'];
/** A6-2: each pending wake keeps fallbackEligible (at the `sent` post the seat had no hook in the previous C.WAKE_QUIET_BEFORE_POST_MS and no Stop-less turn open) and
 *  humanPromptSincePost (set by any nonce-less UserPromptSubmit after the post); fallback (b) needs fallbackEligible && !humanPromptSincePost. The seat's posted nonces (the
 *  newest 16) and the prompt_id recorded at each post outlive the 60 s pending-wake expiry, so rule (a) still classifies a later nonce-carrying turn as wake; only the pending
 *  state (fallback (b), slash-expansion classification) expires. */
export interface TurnState { promptId: string | null; origin: 'human' | 'wake' | null; recordedPromptId: string | null;
  pending: ReadonlyArray<{ nonce: string; sentMono: number; eventIds: readonly string[]; fallbackEligible: boolean; humanPromptSincePost: boolean }>;
  lastHumanPromptMono: number | null; wakeTurnStartMono: number | null; }
export interface Seat {
  readonly seatId: string | null;     // 32 lowercase hex (Plan 2 ledger Task 4); minted by the MCP server, or by the daemon after the 3 s pairing window (NO_MCP);
                                      //   A5-2: null only between the seat's creation by its first hook and its binding (≤ 3 s)
  readonly seatKey: string; readonly claudePid: number; readonly number: number;   // "session N" (R5-33)
  readonly aliases: readonly string[]; readonly sessionIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
  readonly createdMono: number;       // A5-2
  wiring: Wiring; flags: Set<SeatFlag>; wiringCause: string | null; activity: SeatActivity;   // A5-2: wiringCause is the cause of the current wiring (e.g. HOOK_ONLY's mcp_lost)
  lastHookMono: number | null; lastHeartbeatMono: number | null; mcpConnected: boolean; ccVersion: string | null;
  pluginVersion: string; hookProto: number; permissionMode: string; turn: TurnState; ended: boolean;
}
export class SeatRegistry {
  /** A5-3 (as amended by R5-55): peer (trace events carry it); notice — the once-per-cause HOOK_ONLY notice, which Task 5 binds to ctx.events.queueSeatContext through a lazy
   *  delegate (Task 7 implements EventStore only); signals — the activity and seat_end signals (SignalPump, Task 6); outbox receives id-less drafts
   *  ({type: 'event', cid: '', kind: 'health', refs: [seat_id], reason_code}); ids are allocated at seq time (Plan 2's OpDraft). */
  constructor(ctx: { clock: Clock; random: Random; trace: Trace; peer: string; outbox: (draft: Extract<OpDraft, { type: 'seat_start' | 'seat_end' | 'event' }>) => void; pidAlive: (pid: number) => boolean;
    notice: (seatId: string, code: string, params: Params) => void; signals: { send(s: CsSignal): void } });
  /** CCI-31: the first hook or hello for a seat_key creates the seat; the first seat_id bound to a seat_key is kept for the process life; a later hello with another id is an alias. */
  fromHook(h: HookHeader): Seat;
  fromHello(f: McpHelloFrame): Seat;
  /** A5-2 (CCI-31, R2-B5): resolves with the MCP hello's id when one arrives first, else mints randomHex(random, 16) at min(seat.createdMono + C.MCP_PAIRING_MS, untilMono)
   *  and sets wiring NO_MCP; the seat.register step awaits it with untilMono = frameArrivalMono + (SessionStart ? 2,500 : 1,000) so the reply still meets the loader deadline;
   *  a hello arriving after the daemon minted becomes an alias. */
  awaitBound(seat: Seat, untilMono: number): Promise<string>;
  /** A5-4: called by the server. A closed MCP connection without a preceding seat_end frame, while the pid lives, makes the seat's next hook set wiring HOOK_ONLY with cause
   *  mcp_lost; a heartbeat older than C.SEAT_HEARTBEAT_VALID_MS on an open connection counts the same. */
  onHeartbeat(seat: Seat): void; onMcpClosed(seat: Seat): void;
  bySeatId(id: string): Seat | null; byKey(key: string): Seat | null;
  /** X5-27 (Plans 6/7). */
  seats(): ReadonlyArray<{ seat_id: string; number: number; activity: SeatActivity; wiring: Wiring; flags: readonly SeatFlag[]; lastHookMono: number | null }>;
  seatActivity(seatId: string): SeatActivity;
  seatWiring(seatId: string): { wiring: Wiring; flags: readonly SeatFlag[]; display: Wiring | SeatFlag };
  /** Each change of the wiring or the flag set queues one health event whose reason_code is the code of the seat's new display state (R5-34, A5-4): FULL → SEAT_FULL,
   *  NO_MCP → SEAT_NO_MCP, HOOK_ONLY → HOOK_ONLY, NO_HOOKS → NOHOOKS, DEGRADED → DEGRADED, PAUSED → PAUSED, PULL_MODE → SEAT_PULL_MODE. */
  setWiring(seat: Seat, w: Wiring, cause: string | null): void; setFlag(seat: Seat, f: SeatFlag, on: boolean): void;
  /** 06 §3.11.2a: MID_TURN / AWAITING_PERMISSION / IDLE / ENDED from hooks only; emits CsSignal activity on change. */
  observeActivity(seat: Seat, ev: HookEvent, p: HookPayload): void;
  /** CCI-32: MCP stdin EOF (seat_end frame) or the claude pid gone (5 s check): sweep leases and TXNs, signal seat_end, publish seat_end and release{reason: seat_end}, hand requests back. */
  end(seat: Seat, why: 'mcp_eof' | 'pid_gone'): Promise<void>;
  /** Pairing window 3 s (daemon mints seat_id, NO_MCP); pid check every 5 s; heartbeat loss with a live pid and hooks arriving → HOOK_ONLY (one notice per cause). */
  tick(): Promise<void>;
  onEnd(cb: (seat: Seat) => Promise<void>): void;
}

// src/claude/daemon/seatLease.ts — CCI-50, R-11, R5-35
export class SeatLeasePort implements LeasePort {
  constructor(seats: SeatRegistry, clock: Clock, onIdle: (entered: boolean) => void);   // entering idle queues the PEER_IDLE health event; X-40: each seat whose own lease
                                                                                        //   stops being valid (R5-35) is reported once to ctx.bindings.coordHooks.onLeaseLost(seatId)
  idleMs(): number;                    // 0 while a seat lease or the human lease is valid, else Δmono since the last one ended
  renewHuman(): void;                  // 60 min from an accepted acting verb (SEC-25 step 7)
  grantHuman(minutes: number): void;   // 1–480 (LIF-41; Plan 7 calls)
}

// src/claude/daemon/turns.ts — CCI-37, glossary §25 F2, O-TURN-ORIGIN, R5-36, R5-39
export class TurnOrigin {
  constructor(seats: SeatRegistry, clock: Clock, trace: Trace);
  onRingInFlight(seat: Seat, nonce: string, eventIds: readonly string[]): void;   // at the ring frame's send (F2 #61, R-F2-17): records the seat's last seen prompt_id; the pending wake is registered in flight
  onRingSent(seat: Seat, nonce: string, eventIds: readonly string[]): boolean;    // on ring_result 'sent' (F2 #61, R-F2-17): true when that nonce was already seen (no new pending wake is created), false otherwise
  onRingFailed(seat: Seat, nonce: string): void;                                  // on 'refused', 'error' or the 5 s wait expiring (F2 #61, R-F2-17): the in-flight entry is cleared
  /** UserPromptSubmit: non-empty nonces → doorbell (confirms delivery of those nonces' events, clears their pending wakes, refreshes nothing, opens nothing, releases nothing);
   *  else a new turn, 'human' (refreshes the attended window). A6-1: delivered lists the prompt's nonces that were posted to this seat; wake is true when the prompt also
   *  starts a wake turn by rule (a) (it carries a posted nonce and its prompt_id differs from the one recorded at that post and from the seat's current one; 06 §3.12.2):
   *  the seat's turn becomes {promptId, origin: 'wake'}, still with no prompt signal, no attended-window refresh, no release. A prompt whose nonces were never posted to this
   *  seat is still a doorbell (never a human prompt) with delivered [] and wake false, and leaves the seat's turn unchanged. */
  onPromptSubmit(seat: Seat, promptId: string | null, nonces: readonly string[]):
    { kind: 'doorbell'; delivered: readonly string[]; wake: boolean } | { kind: 'turn'; origin: 'human' | 'wake'; newTurn: boolean };
  /** Fallback (b): the first non-UserPromptSubmit hook after a post, with no hook in the 60 s before the post and no Stop-less turn open → a wake turn (can only classify as wake). */
  onOtherHook(seat: Seat, promptId: string | null): void;
  /** 06 §3.12.2 slash expansions, SEC-25 step 2: wake while a wake is pending and prompt_id differs from the recorded one, when the expansion carries a cws- nonce, or when the seat's turn is wake. */
  classifyExpansion(seat: Seat, promptId: string | null, nonces: readonly string[]): 'human' | 'wake';
  onStop(seat: Seat): void;                                   // origin cleared at that turn's Stop/StopFailure
  /** A6-3: the per-field turn-origin values of 06 §2 in one place (R1-124): originOf feeds CsSignal.effect.turnOrigin and so `Cowork-CS … origin=prompt|wake`;
   *  requestOrigin feeds request.origin (Task 14); recordOrigin feeds hold-record origin `<seat_id>[/<agent_id>]` (Tasks 11, 12; the wake-scope PIN's origin wake is Plan 6's). */
  originOf(seatId: string): 'prompt' | 'wake';
  requestOrigin(seatId: string): 'claude' | 'wake';
  recordOrigin(seatId: string, agentId: string | null): string;
  expire(): void;                                             // pending wakes 60 s after the post (A6-2: posted nonces and their recorded prompt_ids are kept)
}

// src/claude/daemon/signals.ts — CCI-36, 06 §3.12.1, R5-20, R5-23, R5-24
/** Per-seat ordered delivery into Plan 3's Engine.signal. A6-4 (as amended by ruling R-F2-5, F2 #32 #62): send(s) calls engine.signal(s) synchronously inside send (no queue,
 *  no microtask), attaches a rejection handler that logs log.write('error', 'CS_SIGNAL_REJECTED', {kind: s.kind}) (LOG_ONLY_CODES), and returns a promise that never
 *  rejects and resolves no earlier than Plan 3's queueSignal for s has run — for an effect (which asks for no out-of-cycle iteration) right after Engine.signal's queued
 *  section; per-seat order is the call order. Plan 3 runs queueSignal inside its exclusive chain (loop.ts:610-611: never during start, an iteration or a resolve), so it
 *  has not run when send returns: an effect is in the CS table once the awaited send resolves — at once on an idle engine, after the iteration in flight otherwise (a
 *  signal sent before Plan 3's first successful start is kept and replayed by that start, loop.ts:620-622). Only Task 12's post.edit awaits a send (its effect, before
 *  openCsOf); every other caller drops the promise (R5-24).
 *  nextCandSeq (A6-4, A13-5): the one per-CS cand_seq counter of 06 §2 / §3.8.4 step 1 (X5-12) = 1 + max(the last value it returned for csId in this daemon run, the CS's
 *  CsTableEntry.cap_seq, X5-47); green checkpoints (Task 6), edit candidates (Task 13) and cowork_checkpoint (Task 14) draw from it. */
export class SignalPump { constructor(engine: Engine, log: Log); send(s: CsSignal): Promise<void>; nextCandSeq(csId: string): number; }
/** R5-20: CS paths' content identity within budgetMs; null when over budget or a read stays unstable. A6-5: keeps an in-memory IdentityMemo (per path: the FsLstat and oid
 *  of the newest identity this daemon computed — a post-image, a candidate or a closing capture); per CS path: known → its oid; else lstat equal (sameStat) to the memo → the
 *  memo's oid; else one shortRead + DiskSnapshots.take (route (b) without -w, no object) → its oid, missing → ABSENT, unstable or unreadable → the whole identity null;
 *  elapsed > budgetMs → null; any thrown error (e.g. Task 5's stub before Task 8) → null. */
export function captureIdentity(seatId: string, csPaths: readonly string[], known: ReadonlyMap<string, DiskSnapshot>, ctx: DaemonCtx, budgetMs: number): Promise<Readonly<Record<string, OidOrAbsent>> | null>;
/** HLD-34 fallback. A6-10: tick() sends idle_10min{seat, activity, txnOpen: ctx.leases.openFor(seatId).txns > 0} once per silence: when now − seat.lastHookMono ≥
 *  C.IDLE_CLOSE_MS, and not again until the seat sends another hook. */
export class IdleTimer { constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx); tick(): void; }
/** R5-23: PostToolUse/PostToolUseFailure of Bash|PowerShell whose command starts with a checks.checkpoint entry → checks_run{ok}; ok on PostToolUse also → checkpoint{source: green}.
 *  A6-8: after trimming leading whitespace a command matches an entry c iff it equals c or starts with c followed by a whitespace character (npm test ≠ npm testx); only
 *  Bash and PowerShell calls count (06 §3.12.1). */
export function greenCheck(p: Extract<HookPayload, { event: 'PostToolUse' | 'PostToolUseFailure' | 'PermissionDenied' }>, commands: readonly string[]): { checksRun: boolean | null; green: boolean };
export interface ContextReset { seatId: string; agentId: string | null; why: 'compact' | 'clear' | 'resume' | 'fork' | 'subagent_start' | 'subagent_stop'; }
/** F05-09 (Plan 6 clears its noticed sets). A6-13: emitted per daemon through this module-level registry; seat ids are unique 128-bit values, so a subscriber of one peer in
 *  a multi-peer SIM process filters by its own seats. X-47: this module function is the only form (no ClaudeEvents object); Plan 6's bindClaude subscribes to it. */
export function onContextReset(cb: (r: ContextReset) => void): () => void;

// src/claude/daemon/ring.ts — 06 §3.16.5, CCI-49 (daemon side), R5-37
/** Plan 6 binds (COO-22…COO-25). X-42: Plan 5 posts (Ringer, InboxPoster, mintNonce, NONCE_RE — the only ones; Plan 6 deletes its post.ts, postDoorbell, RingResult,
 *  mintNonce and NONCE_RE) and Plan 6 decides; the optional onRingResult reports each ring's outcome (Plan 6's onDoorbellOutcome), called once per shouldRing answer.
 *  Verify pass V-10 (Plan 6's O-2): the RingDriver mints the nonce before it asks (mintNonce(ctx.random)) and passes it, so Plan 6's text ends with its DB.foot
 *  `ref <nonce>`; Ringer.ring sends exactly that nonce; onRingResult reports it, which is how Plan 6 learns its posted nonces (a UserPromptSubmit nonce it never
 *  posted → its WAKE_FOREIGN_NONCE, from CoordHooksPort.onUserPromptSubmit's nonces). A null answer does not consume the nonce's events (O-5, RingDriver). */
export interface WakePolicy {
  shouldRing(seatId: string, eventIds: readonly string[], nonce: string): { text: string } | null;
  onRingResult?(seatId: string, eventIds: readonly string[], result: PostResult | 'not_rung', nonce: string): void;
}
export const neverRing: WakePolicy;
export function scriptedRing(script: (seatId: string, eventIds: readonly string[], nonce: string) => { text: string } | null): WakePolicy;   // SIM and FakeHost only
export function mintNonce(random: Random): string;   // 'cws-' + 16 lowercase hex (seeded in SIM, CSPRNG binding in production)
export class Ringer {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, send: (seatId: string, f: Extract<SessionServerFrame, { t: 'ring' }>) => boolean);
  /** No ring to a MID_TURN seat; text ≤ 1,500 and never starting with '/'; waits ≤ 5 s for ring_result (missing → error); the ring frame's send marks the pending wake, in flight until ring_result: 'sent' keeps it, 'refused', 'error' or no result in 5 s ends it, and a nonce UPS that arrives before 'sent' confirms it (then 'sent' adds none; 06 §3.12.2 as amended, F2 #61, ruling R-F2-17); events stay in the pull queue until a nonce UPS.
   *  A6-7: a text longer than C.CAPS.DOORBELL or starting with '/' → 'error' with nothing sent; a MID_TURN seat or one without an MCP connection → 'not_rung'.
   *  V-10 (O-2): the nonce is the caller's (the RingDriver's, minted before shouldRing); a text in which NONCE_RE does not find exactly that nonce once → 'error', nothing sent. */
  ring(seatId: string, eventIds: readonly string[], text: string, nonce: string): Promise<'sent' | 'refused' | 'error' | 'not_rung'>;
  onResult(nonce: string, result: 'sent' | 'refused' | 'error'): void;
}
/** A6-7 ringing glue, the `ring` ticker: for each seat with activity IDLE, wiring FULL, no PAUSED/PULL_MODE flag, no pending wake and a current turn that is not
 *  wake-originated, the ids of its EventStore.pending(seatId) events that no post has carried yet go, with a fresh nonce = mintNonce(ctx.random), to
 *  policy.shouldRing(seatId, ids, nonce); a {text} answer → ringer.ring(seatId, ids, text, nonce); with neverRing (production, R5-37) it never rings. V-10 (Plan 6's
 *  O-5): the offer repeats at every tick — a null answer (Plan 6's 10 s coalescing, a cap re-evaluated, 07 §3.12) leaves the ids offerable at the next tick, and so
 *  does 'not_rung'; an id that rode a post of any result (sent, refused, error) is never offered again (it stays for the pull carriers, 07 §3.13 step 4). X-42:
 *  after each ring (or when the seat is not rung) it calls policy.onRingResult?.(seatId, ids, result: PostResult | 'not_rung', nonce) when the policy defines it. */
export class RingDriver { constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, policy: WakePolicy, ringer: Ringer); tick(): Promise<void>; }
```

### Carriers, delivery, transitions, budget, Stop (Task 7)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/events.ts — CCI-41, INV-CCI-DOORBELL, R5-09
export interface SeatEvent { id: string; seatId: string; code: string; params: Params; family: string; mandatory: boolean; slot: 'context' | 'system' | 'both'; createdMono: number;
  state: 'pending' | 'in_batch' | 'delivered'; viaNonce: string | null;
  carriers: readonly CarrierKind[] | null; }   // A7-2: null = any exit-0 carrier; Plan 3's four status-only codes use ['post'], the Stop block ['stop']
export class EventStore {
  constructor(clock: Clock, random: Random, trace: Trace);
  /** One event id per target seat; 'all' = every non-ENDED seat at call time. A doorbell-carried event may also ride a pull carrier; whichever comes first delivers it, once. */
  add(seatId: string | 'all', e: { code: string; params: Params; family: string; mandatory: boolean; slot: 'context' | 'system' | 'both'; carriers?: readonly CarrierKind[] | null }): string[];
  /** A7-1: delivered to the first seat whose exit-0 hook arrives next, once (glossary §25.2 item 9 "else this peer's next seat"). */
  addFirst(e: { code: string; params: Params; family: string; mandatory: boolean; slot: 'context' | 'system' | 'both' }): string;
  queueSeatContext(target: 'all' | string, code: string, params: Params): string[];   // X5-27 (P7 A5-9)
  queueSystemMessage(code: string, params: Params): string[];                       // every live seat's next exit-0 output
  pending(seatId: string): readonly SeatEvent[];
  /** A reply lists its events; they are delivered only on printed{batch_id}. Verify pass V-10 (Plan 6's O-17; 07 COO-20, 07 §6 "loader crashed before flushing",
   *  11 TC-COO-11): a batch whose printed never came is re-queued at the seat's next hook — its events go back to 'pending' with the same ids and are re-sent in
   *  full by that hook's reply (a new batch), delivered on that batch's printed; 06 §3.14.3/CCI-41's "or at the seat's next hook" is read as this re-queue (SE5-22,
   *  in Task 18's spec batch). An event carried as a K1 deny reason is not a SeatEvent and is never re-sent elsewhere (TC-COO-11's second clause).
   *  The one other way an event ends without a printed is A7-3's PULL_MODE suppression (06 §3.16.1: those families are dropped, not delivered late), retired at
   *  compose time exactly as before this note; the full re-send applies only to events a reply actually listed. */
  batch(seatId: string, ids: readonly string[]): string;   // batch_id
  printed(batchId: string): void;
  nextHook(seatId: string): void;   // V-10: re-queues the seat's un-printed batches (never marks them delivered)
  /** Doorbell-carried events are delivered only when a UserPromptSubmit carries that nonce; a sent/refused/error post never removes an event. */
  doorbellSent(nonce: string, ids: readonly string[]): void; nonceSeen(nonce: string): readonly string[];
  cursor(seatId: string): number;
  /** F05-05 (Plan 6 noticed sets). V-10 (Plan 6's O-6): the third argument names the path — carrier = the CarrierKind of the printed batch's reply, or 'doorbell'
   *  when a UserPromptSubmit carrying the nonce delivered the events; Plan 6's ask trace takes `via` from it (O-ASK-ONCE, O-WAKE inputs). */
  onDelivered(cb: (seatId: string, ids: readonly string[], how: { carrier: CarrierKind | 'doorbell'; batchId: string | null; nonce: string | null }) => void): () => void;
}
/** A7-5: the consumer of every Plan 3 hold-code raise (fed by EngineCore.onHoldCode, A8-9): CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE once per CS to its seat (params {n}), INCOMING_WAITING to the
 *  seats whose full-read set holds params.path, CS_GROUP_WAITING / CS_READY_CANCELLED to the CS's seat — each once per raise on ['post']; HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED to the record's
 *  origin seat ({rid}), else addFirst, once per hold id; HOLD_CREATED and other codes are not routed here (R5-09). Returns the event ids added. Assumes X5-42's raise params. */
export function routeHoldCode(r: HoldCodeRaise, ctx: DaemonCtx): string[];

// src/claude/daemon/carriers.ts — CCI-39, CCI-40, glossary §19, §29.6, 06 §3.14.1–§3.14.5
export type CarrierKind = 'session_start' | 'subagent_start' | 'ups' | 'pre_deny' | 'pre_context' | 'post' | 'post_failure' | 'stop' | 'none';
export interface CarrierItem { code: string; params: Params; eventId: string | null; family: string; mandatory: boolean;
  slot: 'context' | 'system' | 'attribution' | 'rules' | 'digest'; tier: number;    // tier = COO's priority rank from DigestPort.rank
  template?: string; }   // R5-60: a template-only item (code '') renders renderClaude(template, params); used for overflow lines (T-MORE, T-STOP.more, T-AHEAD.more)
/** Packs one reply under the caps of 06 §3.14.1 (SessionStart/SubagentStart RULES ≤ 1,000 + digest ≤ 1,500 with every conflict and held line in the first 1,500;
 *  UPS delta ≤ 1,500 and system ≤ 1,050; G1 T1 ≤ 1,050 / other ≤ 800 / T1R ≤ 300; G2/G3 ≤ 800; PostToolUse 1,000 + the 600 attribution slot (ATTRIBUTION first, then a
 *  SlotExtraPort item when both fit); K2 ≤ 1,000; Stop ≤ 1,200); overflow → one template-only item {code: '', template: 'T-MORE', params: {k, where: 'cowork_status'}}
 *  ("and k more: cowork_status"; on stop template 'T-STOP.more' {k}; a CLAIM_AHEAD overflow 'T-AHEAD.more' {k, file}, R5-60), never a message code;
 *  PULL_MODE drops PULL_MODE_SUPPRESSED families; under the host's 2,000-char preview; sanitize truncates placeholders, never whole templates. */
export function composeReply(kind: CarrierKind, items: readonly CarrierItem[], lang: ClaudeLang, o: { pullMode: boolean; deny: ReplyDraft['deny']; block: ReplyDraft['block'] }):
  { decision: CarrierDecision; text: string; system: string | null; eventIds: string[]; overflow: number; chars: number;
    suppressed: string[] };   // A7-3: event ids dropped by PULL_MODE, retired as delivered without charge
/** A7-8: per-carrier caps of 06 §3.14.1. The effective text cap of session_start / subagent_start is the host's 2,000-char preview, not the 2,500 row ("all carriers stay
 *  under the host's 2,000-char preview"; a reading flagged for reconciliation, not a spec change). Packing order there: conflict and held items first, bounded by
 *  min(CAPS.DIGEST, CAPS.PREVIEW − RULES length − 1) with their own MORE, then RULES whole (never cut), then the other digest items to CAPS.PREVIEW. */
export const CARRIER_CAPS: Readonly<Record<CarrierKind, { text: number; system: number }>>;
/** A7-10: thrown by composeReply in test builds (the mutants' test-build switch, R-29) when an item's code starts with UI_FLOW_LAST_; production builds drop such an item
 *  silently, so no edit-check outcome reaches a carrier (INV-CCI-EDITCHECK text clause; D1 final: last_check is shown only in /cowork status --flow and /cowork wrap
 *  replies, whoever ran them — a pull, never a carrier push, X-01, Task 15). */
export class CarrierCodeRefused extends Error { readonly code: string; }
/** A7-4: the one place a hook reply is assembled, called by ClaudeServer after the last step: composeReply over d.items plus the seat's pending store events by rank (honouring
 *  each event's carriers filter), EventStore.batch (→ ReplyFrame.batch_id), suppressed ids retired, BudgetMeter.charge per item family (recap → 'recap', CLAIM_AHEAD* →
 *  'claim_ahead', other context/deny/block text → 'inject', other system → 'system'), one carrier trace event. CarrierKind from the event and outcome: SessionStart →
 *  session_start, SubagentStart → subagent_start, UserPromptSubmit → ups, PreToolUse → pre_deny when d.deny else pre_context, PostToolUse → post, PostToolUseFailure →
 *  post_failure, Stop → stop, any other event → none (no text). */
export function finishReply(r: HookRequest, d: ReplyDraft, ctx: DaemonCtx): ReplyFrame;
/** COO content (Plan 6 binds): RULES, full/delta/subagent digests, carrier priority, EV.* routing (R5-09, R5-11). */
export interface DigestPort {
  rules(seatId: string): CarrierItem[];
  digest(seatId: string, kind: 'full' | 'delta' | 'subagent', o: { supersedes: boolean }): CarrierItem[];   // supersedes → DIGEST_SUPERSEDES first (CCI-40)
  rank(items: readonly CarrierItem[]): CarrierItem[];
}
export const codeOnlyDigest: DigestPort;   // Plan 5 binding: rules → one STANDING_RULES item (07 §3.16.4, R5-60); DIGEST_SUPERSEDES when asked, this seat's own conflict/held items (TurnItems, registry), nothing else; code lines (R5-11)
/** F05 (Plan 6). V-10 (Plan 6's O-7; 06 §3.13 item 7 (e), CCI-58): heldAttribClaimIds = the claim ids whose labels the held-variant ATTRIBUTION placed first in this
 *  reply's slot names ([] when the slot has no held variant); Plan 6 lists none of them and marks their keys noticed with this reply's batch. */
export interface SlotExtraPort { postExtra(seatId: string, agentId: string | null, charsLeft: number, heldAttribClaimIds: readonly string[]): CarrierItem | null; }

// src/claude/daemon/transitions.ts — CCI-44, INV-NOSILENT, R5-09
/** A7-9: the per-seat last-seen value; the baseline is {state: 'ok', freeze_reasons: [], publish_pause: null, offlineRepeatSent: false}. */
export type TransitionTriple = { state: StatusJson['state']; freeze_reasons: readonly FreezeReason[]; publish_pause: PublishPause | null; offlineRepeatSent: boolean };
export class Transitions {
  /** A7-6: heartbeatNow is bound to the same source as EngineDeps.heartbeatMs (machineMonoMs in production, the peer's VirtualClock in SIM, R-35). */
  constructor(heartbeatNow: () => number);
  /** Called with every status.json the engine writes; remembers state, freeze_reasons, publish_pause per seat-last-seen. */
  observe(s: StatusJson, nowMono: number): void;
  /** Once per seat per transition: a systemMessage (human) and an additionalContext line (Claude), codes and typed params only (a NONRETRYABLE pause names the host-reject PIN id,
   *  never the host's text); a heartbeat older than 3 loop periods → DAEMON_DOWN; plus the 06 §3.6.2 10-minute OFFLINE repeat once per seat per episode (params.repeat: true),
   *  whatever the seat's next hook is (A7-6). */
  forSeat(seatId: string): CarrierItem[];
}
/** A7-6, pure: freeze reason added → FROZEN {reason}, removed → UNFROZEN {reason}; pause OFFLINE/AUTH → OFFLINE {offline_for_s, label}, NONRETRYABLE → NONRETRYABLE {hold_id},
 *  VERSION → VERSION_PAUSED {form}, pause cleared → ONLINE; state entering degraded or incident with no freeze/pause change → message_code with message_params; a return to ok →
 *  nothing beyond the component lines. */
export function transitionItems(prev: TransitionTriple, next: StatusJson): CarrierItem[];
// V-10 (Plan 6's O-18), the engine-event split by source: Plan 5's Transitions delivers every line that a change of status.json state, freeze_reasons or
// publish_pause causes (FROZEN/UNFROZEN, OFFLINE/NONRETRYABLE/VERSION_PAUSED/ONLINE, DAEMON_DOWN, and the message_code of an entry into degraded or incident,
// e.g. INCIDENT, a not-converged cause); routeHoldCode delivers exactly its five codes (A7-5); every other engine-raised code (an ENG-57 deferral that leaves
// state ok, BOARD_FULL as the T-BOARD_FULL digest line, an unknown event kind's T-UNKNOWN) is Plan 6's engineEvents, which never re-delivers a code these two
// paths cover. Plan 6 supplies the EV.*/T-* catalog rows of all three paths.

// src/claude/daemon/meter.ts — CCI-46, CCI-61, 06 §3.16.1, R5-43
export type MeterKind = 'inject' | 'system' | 'doorbell' | 'reminder_estimate' | 'claim_ahead' | 'recap' | 'slash';
export interface BudgetUsage { injected: number; reminders: number; reminderChars: number; claimAhead: number; doorbells: number; slashShown: number; pullMode: boolean; windowLeftMs: number; }
export class BudgetMeter {
  constructor(clock: Clock, onPullMode: (seatId: string, on: boolean) => void);   // R5-55: Task 5 binds onPullMode to ctx.seats.setFlag(<seat>, 'PULL_MODE', on)
  touch(seatId: string): void;   // A7-7: anchors the seat-hour window at the seat's first hook (called by event.deliver); a rollover is evaluated lazily on every meter call
  /** Window per seat-hour anchored at the seat's first hook (monotonic). injected (inject + system + recap + claim_ahead + doorbell text) > 20,000, reminder estimate > 15
   *  or > 20,000 chars → PULL_MODE until the window boundary; claim_ahead saturates at 5,000 without ever tripping PULL_MODE; slash is shown, never counted. */
  charge(seatId: string, kind: MeterKind, chars: number): void;                     // X5-27 (Plan 6 charges doorbells and claim-ahead text)
  /** Host-reminder estimate: applied diff chars into a file in the seat's full-read set (one reminder per applied path). */
  reminder(seatId: string, appliedChars: number): void;
  inPullMode(seatId: string): boolean;
  shareLeft(seatId: string, kind: 'claim_ahead'): number;
  usage(seatId: string): BudgetUsage;                                               // Plan 7 /cowork status budget line
  pullModeLeftMs(seatId: string): number;                                           // X-46: Δmono until the seat-hour window that set PULL_MODE ends; 0 when not in PULL_MODE
  cacheCold(seatId: string): boolean;                                               // X-46: 07 §2's cache-window input to the cost estimate (Plan 6's former MeterPort.cacheCold); never gates delivery
}
// X-46: BudgetMeter and MeterKind are the one meter of every plan (Plan 6 deletes its MeterPort and uses these names).

// src/claude/daemon/stop.ts — CCI-42, INV-CCI-ASK-ONCE, 06 §3.15
export interface AskRef { reqId: string; toHolderSeat: string; attempt: number; rid: string; label: string; requester: string; mode: 'go_first' | 'handoff'; }
export class TurnItems {   // R5-39
  /** HLD-23 unacked(seat) and Stop (a) input: denied (SHADOW ids), held (PIN ids), recorded, not-shared (NOT_SHARED paths) items of the current turn, per agent_id. */
  record(seatId: string, agentId: string | null, kind: 'denied' | 'held' | 'recorded' | 'not_shared', id: string): void;
  forTurn(seatId: string): ReadonlyArray<{ agentId: string | null; kind: string; id: string }>;
  unacked(seatId: string): string[];
  endTurn(seatId: string): void;   // a new human or wake turn, Stop/StopFailure (after stop.decide), seat end
}
/** V-10 (Plan 6's O-4): Plan 5 assembles and renders the Stop block — stopDecision's STOP_SUMMARY item goes through composeReply('stop', …) (≤ 1,200, 'T-STOP.more'
 *  overflow) with Plan 6's T-STOP rows of the one Claude registry (src/i18n/claude.ts); Plan 6 supplies only the asks, synchronously, each already entered as its
 *  `@stop` carry in a committed meta.coord (R6-74 (a), kept), and calls nothing at Stop; its composeStop is the reference its content tests compare Plan 5's block with. */
export interface DueRequestsPort { dueRequests(seatId: string): AskRef[]; markAsked(asks: readonly AskRef[]): void; }   // Plan 6 (COO-13, COO-26); markAsked when the block's batch is DELIVERED
export const noDueRequests: DueRequestsPort;                                                                           // [] (R5-10)
/** At most one decision:block per turn; never when stop_hook_active; never in a wake-originated turn; (a) this turn's denied/held/recorded/not-shared items (any agent_id, items of
 *  (a) first, keyed by agent_id) whose ids are not all in ids_in_last_message (null → block whenever such items exist); (b) due asks when background_tasks_running is 0, each
 *  (request identity, attempt) at most once ever; SubagentStop never blocks. */
export function stopDecision(seat: Seat, p: Extract<HookPayload, { event: 'Stop' | 'SubagentStop' }>, items: TurnItems, due: DueRequestsPort, blockedThisTurn: boolean):
  { block: { code: 'STOP_SUMMARY'; params: Params; itemIds: string[]; asks: AskRef[] } | null };
```

### Hook reads, leases, defer set, attribution (Task 8)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/hookRead.ts — CCI-55, glossary §26.1 W1–W3, W6, 01 §3.8 "Hook requests", REG-35, R5-26
export type ShortRead =
  | { kind: 'ok'; bytes: Buffer; stat: FsLstat }
  | { kind: 'unstable'; bytes: Buffer; stat: FsLstat }   // the second read's bytes; the window is approximate
  | { kind: 'missing' } | { kind: 'unreadable' };
/** Open with full sharing, read the whole file in one call, close; lstat before and after; differ → re-read once; still differ → unstable. No handle kept, no git process on the file. */
export function shortRead(fs: Fs, abs: string): Promise<ShortRead>;
export interface DiskSnapshot { readonly path: string; readonly oid: OidOrAbsent; readonly bytes: Buffer | null; readonly spill: string | null; readonly size: number; }   // 04 §2 diskSnapshotId
/** A8-1: bytes in hand before any hash — what the gate holds so no git process runs on its reply path (CCI-12). */
export interface HeldRead { readonly path: string; readonly bytes: Buffer; readonly stat: FsLstat; readonly unstable: boolean; readonly size: number; }
export class DiskSnapshots {
  constructor(repo: GitRepo, fs: Fs, stageDir: StageDir, capBytes: number);
  /** A8-1: synchronous, memory only, counted in the 64 MB cap; spawns no git process. */
  hold(path: string, read: Extract<ShortRead, { kind: 'ok' | 'unstable' }>): HeldRead;
  /** A8-1: oid by route (b) `hash-object --stdin --path=<p>` (hashRouteBStdin, write false) without -w (M41: never an object); cached by (path, SHA-256 of the bytes);
   *  bytes in memory up to 64 MB in total, beyond it spilled under tmp/stage/ (0600, deleted at release); never serialized. */
  settle(h: HeldRead): Promise<DiskSnapshot>;
  /** = settle(hold(path, read)); used by every hook path that may hash before its reply (PostToolUse post-images, TXN-close reads, deny bases), never by the gate. */
  take(path: string, read: ShortRead): Promise<DiskSnapshot>;
  get(path: string, oid: OidOrAbsent): DiskSnapshot | null;
  bytesOf(s: DiskSnapshot): Promise<Buffer>;
  release(s: DiskSnapshot): Promise<void>;   // deletes a spill file
  bytesHeld(): number;
}
/** W2: a 0-byte or > 50 % shrunk post-image relative to its pre-image is used only after a second stable read ≥ 300 ms later. */
export function emptyOrShrunk(pre: DiskSnapshot | null, post: DiskSnapshot): boolean;
/** A8-10 (06 §3.8.1 EOL-only/BOM-only hunks, §3.8.4 trigger (a)): equal after stripping a leading UTF-8 BOM and mapping CRLF and lone CR to LF on both sides; pure, no git;
 *  used by Task 12's post.edit before confirm. */
export function normalizedEqual(a: Buffer, b: Buffer): boolean;

// src/claude/daemon/leases.ts — 06 §3.7.1–§3.7.4, CCI-20…CCI-22, CCI-24, INV-CCI-LEASE, O-LEASE
export type ReleaseWhy = 'post' | 'post_failure' | 'permission_denied' | 'batch' | 'new_turn' | 'stop' | 'stop_failure' | 'session_end' | 'subagent_stop'
  | 'seat_end' | 'backstop' | 'cap' | 'background_return' | 'next_edit_post' | 'synthetic_done';
export interface InflightLease { kind: 'INFLIGHT'; toolUseId: string; seatId: string; agentId: string | null; sessionId: string; path: string; acquiredMono: number;
  foldHeight: number; pre: DiskSnapshot | null; approximate: boolean; synthetic: boolean;   // synthetic: cowork_resolve restore (06 §3.16.3)
  preHeld: HeldRead | null;     // A8-2: the gate passes its HeldRead with pre: null; after the reply it settles it and calls setPre
  view: ClaimView | null;       // X-20: the claim view read at the Pre (K2 "as folded when the Pre was answered"; replaces RegionsPort.claimView's atHeight)
  tentative: PredictResult | null; }   // X-20 (Plan 4 C-5): the predicted TENTATIVE claims of this G2/G3 (06 §3.5.2 row 14) — Plan 5 fence state, discarded at the lease's release
export interface ReadLease { kind: 'READ'; seatId: string; path: string; firstMono: number; expiresMono: number; renewals: number; }
export interface Txn { kind: 'TXN'; toolUseId: string; seatId: string; agentId: string | null; sessionId: string; tool: ShellTool; openMono: number; foldHeight: number;
  sOpen: Readonly<Record<string, OidOrAbsent>>; approximate: boolean; background: boolean; csId: string | null;
  view: ClaimView | null; }   // X-20: the claim view read when the TXN opened (K2 at close against it)
export class LeaseTable {
  constructor(clock: Clock, trace: Trace, reads: DiskSnapshots);
  /** CCI-20: after G2/G3 only (a denied call takes none); keyed by tool_use_id, refcounted per path; pre-image by one short read; never released by the seat's next PreToolUse. */
  acquireInflight(l: Omit<InflightLease, 'kind' | 'acquiredMono'>): InflightLease;
  setPre(toolUseId: string, pre: DiskSnapshot): void;                                                  // A8-2
  /** CCI-21: a full Read (no offset/limit, not truncated) of a LIVE path (A12-8); 120 s, renewed on the seat's hook activity ≤ 3 times, hard cap 300 s; released by the seat's
   *  next edit-class Post on the path. There is no API to lease a ranged read (ranged reads are ReadSets records). */
  acquireRead(seatId: string, path: string): ReadLease;
  renewReads(seatId: string): void;
  releaseRead(seatId: string, path: string, why: ReleaseWhy): ReadLease | null;                         // A8-3
  /** A8-3: every TXN release, with its why (post, post_failure, permission_denied, background_return, new_turn, stop, stop_failure, session_end, seat_end, cap); Task 12 subscribes closeTxn. */
  onTxnClosed(cb: (t: Txn, why: ReleaseWhy) => Promise<void>): () => void;
  /** CCI-22: at PreToolUse(Bash|PowerShell|Monitor) after the advisory checks; S_open = the newest snapshot before the reply; dirty-against-S_open files keep pre-window
   *  attribution (stat of the private index entries bounded at 20 ms, else approximate). */
  openTxn(t: Omit<Txn, 'kind' | 'openMono'>): Txn;
  inflight(toolUseId: string): InflightLease | null; txn(toolUseId: string): Txn | null;
  release(toolUseId: string, why: ReleaseWhy): InflightLease | Txn | null;
  releaseSeat(seatId: string, scope: { sessionId?: string; agentId?: string }, why: ReleaseWhy): ReadonlyArray<InflightLease | Txn | ReadLease>;
  /** Backstops: INFLIGHT 120 s (CCI-24 expiry facts returned), READ 120 s / 300 s, TXN cap 120 s (closed as 'cap'). */
  expire(): { expired: InflightLease[]; capped: Txn[]; reads: ReadLease[] };
  fencedPaths(): ReadonlyMap<string, 'fence' | 'read_lease'>; txnOpen(): boolean; txnOpenSinceMono(): number | null;
  openFor(seatId: string): { inflight: number; txns: number };
  expiredPerSeatHour(seatId: string): number;   // > 5 flags doctor (Plan 7)
}

// src/claude/daemon/defer.ts — CCI-23, ENG-31, 01 §3.11, R5-25 (the ClaudeEnginePort binding)
export type DeferSource = 'fence' | 'read_lease' | 'txn' | 'conflicted_leased' | 'degraded' | 'coalesce_mid_turn' | 'coalesce_pull_mode';
export interface DeferTag { path: string; reason: DeferReason; source: DeferSource; seatNumber: number | null; sinceMono: number; lastApplyIntoReadSetMono: number | null; }
export class DeferSet implements ClaudeEnginePort {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx);
  /** deferred() is recomputed per call from memory (leases, SeatHealth.degradedPaths, ReadSets.fullReadSet, seat activity and lastHookMono, BudgetMeter.inPullMode, the
   *  last-apply instants); it never reads a file; coalesced and DEGRADED paths enter D as read_lease (R5-25). */
  txnOpen(): boolean; deferred(): ReadonlyMap<string, DeferReason>; recheck(paths: readonly string[]): ReadonlyMap<string, DeferReason>; fenced(): ReadonlySet<string>;
  applied(paths: readonly string[], detail?: ReadonlyArray<AppliedPath>): void;
  /** F14 (R5-53): per path its source, seat number and instants, for I3/I4a/I4b; memory only. */
  tags(): ReadonlyMap<string, DeferTag>;
  onTurnBoundary(seatId: string): void;                                                     // A8-4: called by lease.release at a Stop, StopFailure or new-turn UserPromptSubmit (PULL_MODE applies)
  onApplied(cb: (detail: ReadonlyArray<AppliedPath>) => void): () => void;                  // A8-4: fan-out of applied(); Task 12's ApplyJournal subscribes
}

// src/claude/daemon/attribution.ts — 06 §3.8.3, CCI-24, CCI-27, HLD-26, HLD-35, R5-27…R5-29
export interface Attributed { seatId: string; agentId: string | null; csId: string | null; toolUseId: string | null; via: 'inflight' | 'txn' | 'grace'; }
export class AttributionWindows {
  constructor(clock: Clock, leases: LeaseTable);
  onEditPost(seatId: string, path: string): void;                 // opens the 3 s formatter grace; clears an expiry taint on path
  onExpiry(l: InflightLease, writerWroteAfterAcquire: boolean): void;   // CCI-24 taint (path-level, R5-27)
  onTxnClose(t: Txn, why: ReleaseWhy): void;                      // after a cap or a background return: late writes human-class with a display tag (R5-29)
  /** Every overlapping window of every live seat (the stricter result applies to claims and conflicts); null → human-class. */
  attribute(path: string): Attributed[] | null;
  seatTag(path: string): string | null;                           // display-only seat tag of a human-class late write (F04)
  editedSince(seatId: string, sinceMono: number): ReadonlySet<string>;   // A8-6: the seat's attributed paths of the window (Task 9's DEGRADED 30-min edit set)
  /** A12-4: a TXN closed by its own call keeps answering removalAttributed/hookAttributed for the paths closeTxn attributed until the first detection tick after the close
   *  observes them or untilMono (2 loop periods) passes. */
  attributeClosed(t: Txn, paths: readonly string[], untilMono: number): void;
}
/** The AttributionPort binding: removalAttributed and observedEdit/hookAttributed per R5-28; shrinkAttributed keeps Plan 2's R-25 answer (true). */
export function hookAttribution(w: AttributionWindows): AttributionPort;
```

### Seat health and daemon liveness (Task 9)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/health.ts — CCI-33…CCI-35, SEC-28, SEC-29, R2-B8…R2-B11
export class SeatHealth {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, engine: { setFreezeReason(r: 'NOHOOKS', on: boolean, cause: string): Promise<void> });
  /** Pre/Post pairing per edit-class tool_use_id: an unmatched Post/PostFailure → DEGRADED (host_timeout); 3 consecutive matched pairs → recovered (not announced). */
  onPre(seatId: string, toolUseId: string): void; onPost(seatId: string, toolUseId: string): void;
  /** 60 s without hook traffic while the pid lives, the heartbeat continues and the seat is MID_TURN with no open TXN or INFLIGHT lease and not AWAITING_PERMISSION → DEGRADED (silence). */
  tick(): Promise<void>;
  markGateBypassed(seatId: string): void;                        // X5-26 (COO-02 K3 on a claim folded when the edit was gated); X-47: Plan 6's K3 path calls it (no reportDegraded)
  hookLatencyP95Ms(): number | null;                             // X-64: p95 of this daemon's hook handling times over its recent window (Plan 7's doctor/status); null before any hook
  leaseExpiriesPerHour(): number;                                // V-7 (Plan 7 X7-13): LeaseTable lease expiries (not releases) over the last 60 min of Δmono, all seats; 0 before any
  /** The DEGRADED seats' read and edit sets of the last 30 min (R5-25 'degraded'). */
  degradedPaths(): ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  /** NO_HOOKS: (1) a cowork MCP tool call with no hook from that seat_key within 10 s; (2) ≥ 3 unmatched edit Posts in 10 min; (3) static hits (disableAllHooks,
   *  allowManagedHooksOnly with cowork's hooks unobserved, enabledPlugins disabling cowork-sync). Any NO_HOOKS seat → FREEZE NOHOOKS by one transition, the NOHOOKS
   *  systemMessage, status, one OS notification naming the cause; a seat's trigger (1) is lifted by any hook of the seat, trigger (2) only by that seat's next edit-class
   *  PreToolUse (A9-5: the unmatched Posts are themselves hooks; the missing gating hook is what has to return — a reading flagged for reconciliation), trigger (3) when no
   *  static hit remains; the freeze is lifted when every NO_HOOKS seat is lifted or ended. A missing SessionStart never counts. */
  onMcpToolCall(seatId: string): void; onStaticHits(seatId: string, hits: readonly PolicyHit[]): void; onHook(seatId: string): void;
  /** deniedMcpServers / allowManagedMcpServersOnly blocking cowork → the seat's wiring NO_MCP (not a FREEZE); crossSessionInbound hold|refuse, --bare, no messaging env → HOOK_ONLY. */
  policyWiring(seat: Seat, hits: readonly PolicyHit[]): void;
}

// src/claude/daemon/liveness.ts — CCI-50…CCI-53, SEC-23, LIF-36, R5-18, R5-51
export class Liveness {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, d: { pluginEnabled: () => Promise<boolean>; keyHash: () => Promise<string | null>; runtimeExists: () => Promise<boolean>; shutdown: () => Promise<void> });
  /** Every 30 s: cowork-sync enabled in enabledPlugins across the settings layers, ipc.key present with the start-time hash, the runtime copy present; any failure → shutdown (LIF-36). */
  check(): Promise<'ok' | 'shutdown'>;
}
/** A9-1, the production pluginEnabled binding: the highest-precedence of managed, local, project and user settings that names pluginId in enabledPlugins decides; unnamed → enabled;
 *  reads only the enabledPlugins key of those files (SEC-23 capability limits). */
export function pluginEnabledFromSettings(fs: Fs, files: readonly string[], pluginId: string): Promise<boolean>;
export class MarkerBackstop {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, watch: (p: string, cb: () => void) => () => void, shutdown: () => Promise<void>);   // production watch = realWatch (A9-2)
  /** A `live` marker this daemon owns disappears while no stop or shutdown sequence runs → re-create at once with the same content, a security event, a health event
   *  LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED, one NT_LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED notification; <gitdir>/cowork/ itself gone → shutdown instead. */
  start(): void; stop(): void;
  expectRemoval(on: boolean): void;   // set by the drain stopgap (R5-18) and by Plan 7's stop/uninstall (X5-32)
}
/** LIF-36 stopgap (R5-51): own live markers removed first; no further working-tree write or snapshot; one push of captured ready trees through every gate plus release{stop}
 *  (RegionsPort.releaseClaims({peer, seat}, 'stop', 'all'), A9-6, X-22), seat_end per live seat and leave when online (board-only when frozen; no push when offline); exit; one
 *  NT_PLUGIN_REMOVED notification (DAEMON_REMOVED). Every LIF-36 step except meta.stop_mode := removed (A9-3): stop_mode is an 08-lifecycle meta field Plan 2 does not have
 *  (adding it changes meta, LIF-37). X-69: Plan 7 owns LIF-36 — its Task 11 replaces this body with stopSequence(…, mode 'removed') + stop_mode := 'removed' and sets
 *  MarkerBackstop.expectRemoval(true) on stop/uninstall, and its Task 8 deletes the stopgap marker writer; MarkerBackstop's re-creation (CCI-53) stays Plan 5's. */
export function removalShutdown(engine: Engine, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<void>;
/** CCI-51: the previous daemon ended without its final status write and its last heartbeat lies ≤ 5 s after the last SessionEnd its log recorded, with no seat hook logged after
 *  that SessionEnd (its own monotonic clock) → DAEMON_DIED_WITH_HOST (status once; doctor WARN in Plan 7). */
export function diedWithHost(prevLog: ReadonlyArray<{ code: string; mono: number }>, prevStatus: StatusJson | null): boolean;
```
