### The gate, regions, K1, SUSPECT, shell gate, wake scope (Tasks 10, 11)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/gate.ts — 06 §3.5–§3.6, CCI-12…CCI-19, glossary §11 (Task 10: frame, rows 1–8 and 14; Task 11: rows 9–13b)
export type GateOutcome =
  | { g: 'G1'; code: string; params: Params; form: 'T1' | 'T1R' | 'other'; record: string | null }   // record: the SHADOW (tmp) id of a row-13 deny
  | { g: 'G2' | 'G3'; note: CarrierItem | null; lease: InflightLease };   // ≤ 1 note, ≤ 800: in Plan 5 only NOT_SHARED (row 5) or none — both OFFLINE notices of an episode
                                                                         // are Task 7's transition lines (A10-6), the newest pull event on the file is Plan 6's routing
export interface EditGateRequest { seat: Seat; agentId: string | null; sessionId: string; toolName: EditTool; toolUseId: string; path: string; input: EditInput; hookProto: number; pluginVersion: string; }
export const GATE_ROWS: readonly ['VERSION_RESTART', 'CC_TOO_OLD', 'WAKE_TURN', 'STATE_FILE', 'NOT_SHARED', 'PORTABILITY', 'SYMLINK', 'PATH_BUSY',
  'LOCATE', 'MOVED', 'HELDREG', 'SUSPECT', 'FOREIGN_CLAIM', 'GRANT_PAUSE', 'PASS'];   // 06 §3.5.2 rows 1–14 and 13b, first match wins
/** CCI-13 in order: hook_proto (VERSION_RESTART {v}); CC < 2.1.270 (CC_TOO_OLD {have, need}); wake turn → WakeScopePort.gateEdit (WAKE_TURN {mode, path}); target under
 *  <gitdir>/** (STATE_FILE); scope class not LIVE → G2 with NOT_SHARED{reason} (quarantine: 'commit with git'), INFLIGHT lease taken, no claim; a new path failing DAT
 *  portability (PORTABILITY {path, reason, suggestion}); mode 120000 with core.symlinks=false (SYMLINK "edit {target}"); pathState 'busy' (PATH_BUSY); locate and predict
 *  (failure → row 13 with no prediction); MOVED; HELDREG (±1 line of a held range: {path, rid, origin, author, reason}); SUSPECT; FOREIGN CLAIM (K1: CLAIM_HELD_DENY,
 *  T1 on the first deny per cid in the turn, else T1R, escalation clause from the 3rd); GRANT PAUSE {label, requester, rid}; else G3 under publish pause OFFLINE or AUTH, else G2,
 *  taking the INFLIGHT lease and TENTATIVE claims. Under FREEZE the gate keeps evaluating; FREEZE/pause transitions never ride the gate result. Answered from memory. */
export function evaluateEdit(req: EditGateRequest, ctx: DaemonCtx, extend: (ms: number) => boolean): Promise<GateOutcome>;
/** A10-4: rows 10 MOVED, 11 HELDREG, 12 SUSPECT, 13 FOREIGN CLAIM (K1), 13b GRANT PAUSE on row 9's prediction (or a fresh predict after a SUSPECT refresh); null = no region
 *  outcome (Task 10 ships it returning null, Task 11 implements it). Touches on a claim STALE in the seat's claim view and reason <imports> never deny. Under X-20
 *  PredictResult.ownTouches[].granted is a boolean, so 13b's {label, requester, rid} come from the touched claim's ViewClaim in the view read at this Pre: label =
 *  claimLabelText(verifiedLabel(claimId, anchor)), requester and rid from its `grant` (ClaimGrant.grantee, ClaimGrant.req_id); coordHooks.grantPaused (X-40) can add a pause. */
export function regionRows(req: EditGateRequest, p: PredictResult | null, ctx: DaemonCtx, extend: (ms: number) => boolean): Promise<GateOutcome | null>;
/** Doctor's gate canary (Plan 7 X7-21; verify pass V-7 — Plan 5 Task 11 produces it; Plan 7 Task 14 registers the IPC kinds canary_arm/canary_disarm and calls these):
 *  an in-memory synthetic foreign claim on `.cowork-doctor-canary/<nonce>.py` (holder `doctor-<nonce>`, HELD, whole file, canary seat key SHA-256("cowork-doctor-<nonce>")),
 *  expiring 30 s after arming; never folded, published, recorded or shown to Plan 4. An edit-class request on that path is answered by row 13 FOREIGN CLAIM's G1 deny
 *  text before `RegionsPort.predict` is asked (a whole-file foreign claim overlaps without prediction), with no K1 deny-time record, no SHADOW and no TurnItems entry, and
 *  marks the nonce hit. Plan 4 needs no change (the claim view is not consulted for a canary path). */
export function canaryArm(ctx: DaemonCtx, nonce: string): void;
export function canaryDisarm(ctx: DaemonCtx, nonce: string): { hit: boolean };                   // unknown or expired nonce → {hit: false}; disarming twice → {hit: false}

// src/claude/daemon/shellGate.ts — CCI-28, CCI-29 (class e), 06 §3.9, R5-05
/** Advisory: (a) SHELL_GIT, (b) SHELL_FORMATTER, (c) SHELL_INPLACE when a literal target holds a foreign HELD claim (foreignHeldOnPath over RegionsPort.claimViewOf, X-20), (d) SHELL_COWORK_CLI
 *  (acting verbs incl. revert with dist/cli.js or cowork; a remover/mover/creator/writer naming a path under <gitdir>/cowork/ in any spelling; any mention of ipc.key),
 *  (e) WORKTREE_DENY (claude -w/--worktree) → G1 {class, hint}; in a wake-originated turn writesFiles() → G1 WAKE_TURN {mode} in both modes; otherwise the TXN opens (G2). */
export function evaluateShell(req: { seat: Seat; agentId: string | null; sessionId: string; tool: ShellTool; toolUseId: string; command: string; background: boolean },
  ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<GateOutcome | { g: 'G2' | 'G3'; note: CarrierItem | null; txn: Txn }>;

// src/claude/daemon/wakeScope.ts — CCI-38 hook points, R5-05 (Plan 6 binds membership and the PIN). X-41: this is the one wake-scope port (Plan 6's WakeTurnPort,
//   checkEdit/checkShell and wakeCheckShell are deleted names; the shell wake rule is Plan 5's).
export interface WakeScopePort {
  /** Gate row 3 for a wake-originated turn (wake.turnEdits from user.json): 'allow' or a deny with the mode. */
  gateEdit(seatId: string, path: string, p: PredictResult | null, mode: 'allow' | 'deny'): 'allow' | { deny: 'allow' | 'deny' };
  /** Edit-class PostToolUse, TXN close, cowork_resolve restore write in a wake turn: records first (HLD-66 whole-CS PIN guard wake-scope, origin wake) and returns its id.
   *  X-41: each change carries h1 (the post-image's oid, hashed by Plan 5 with hashRouteBStdin(…, write: true) as for its deny pre-records, or 'ABSENT') and localOnly
   *  (true when that hash hit a required-filter failure), so Plan 6 needs no hashing; Plan 6's binding creates the PIN through pinCs('wake-scope', …) (R3-08). */
  backstop(seatId: string, csId: string, changes: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; hunks: readonly Hunk[]; post: DiskSnapshot; h1: Oid | 'ABSENT'; localOnly: boolean }>,
    at: 'post' | 'txn' | 'restore'): Promise<{ holdId: string } | null>;
}
/** A10-5: gateEdit → 'allow' in mode allow and { deny: 'deny' } in mode deny (with wake.turnEdits = deny the wake work set is empty by 06 §3.8.2a, so the deny needs no
 *  Plan 6 membership; membership in allow mode stays Plan 6's); backstop → null (no PIN; X-41: plan5WakeScope creates no PIN). No production wake turn exists
 *  before Plan 6 (neverRing). */
export const plan5WakeScope: WakeScopePort;

// src/claude/daemon/regions.ts — the Plan 4 port (04 §3.1, REG-19, REG-28…REG-35). X-20: Plan 4's API is the contract; the port carries Plan 4's member and field names
//   verbatim, and the types below are structural copies of Plan 4's (src/regions/types.ts, src/claims/types.ts) so Plan 5 compiles before Plan 4 merges; Task 18's
//   type-level test pins Plan 4's exports assignable to them. src/claude/daemon/regions.ts is the only Plan 5 file importing src/regions/** or src/claims/** (Task 18).
export interface Hunk { readonly aLo: number; readonly aHi: number; readonly bLo: number; readonly bHi: number; }
export type Range = readonly [lo: number, hi: number];
export type Ranges = readonly Range[];
export interface DiskSnapshotId { readonly kind: 'disk'; readonly path: string; readonly oid: Oid; }   // 04 §2; the binding registers the bytes with Plan 4's SnapshotStore (B-3)
export type BlobRef = Oid | DiskSnapshotId;
export interface SeatKey { readonly peer: PeerId; readonly seat: string; }   // seat = a seat_id (32 hex) or 'human'
export interface ClaimLabel { readonly kind: string; readonly qualname: string; readonly simple: string; readonly type: string; readonly disc: string; }
export interface ClaimGrant { readonly req_id: string; readonly grantee: { peer: string; epoch: string; seat: string }; readonly cs_id: string; readonly since_height: number; }
export interface ViewClaim { readonly claimId: string; readonly path: string; readonly holder: SeatKey & { readonly epoch: string; readonly agent: string };
  readonly granularity: 'symbol' | 'block' | 'file'; readonly label: ClaimLabel; readonly regionSpec: string; readonly csId: string; readonly source: unknown;   // source: Plan 4's ClaimSource
  readonly order: { readonly tier: 0 | 1; readonly a: number; readonly b: number }; readonly grant: ClaimGrant | null; readonly reservedForReq: string | null; }
export interface ClaimView { readonly tip: Oid; readonly claims: readonly ViewClaim[]; }
export interface TurnContext { readonly origin: 'prompt' | 'wake'; readonly firstConfirmedInTurn: ReadonlySet<string>; }
export interface PredictQuery { readonly seat: string; readonly agent: string; readonly tool: EditTool; readonly input: Json; readonly path: string;
  readonly disk: DiskSnapshotId | 'unstable'; readonly viewBlob: BlobRef | null; readonly turn: TurnContext; }
export interface PredictResult { readonly status: 'located' | 'locate_failed' | 'undecided';
  readonly diskLines: Ranges;   // A10-2 under Plan 4's name: the located ranges in stored-form rows of the current disk bytes (04 §3.1); the HELDREG input
  readonly hunksInBase: readonly Hunk[]; readonly touches: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly reason: string }>;
  readonly moved: { readonly fromLabel: ClaimLabel; readonly toLabel: ClaimLabel | 'TOMBSTONE' } | null; readonly fallbackUsed: boolean;
  readonly ownTouches: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly suspect: boolean; readonly granted: boolean }>; readonly ownContained: boolean; }
export interface Draft { readonly claimId: string; readonly seat: SeatKey; readonly agent: string; readonly csId: string; readonly path: string; readonly srcBlob: BlobRef;
  readonly srcRanges: Ranges; readonly granularity: 'symbol' | 'block' | 'file'; readonly label: ClaimLabel; readonly confirmSeq: number; }
export interface SeatEffect { readonly seat: string; readonly agent: string; readonly csId: string; readonly path: string; readonly pre: BlobRef | 'ABSENT';
  readonly post: DiskSnapshotId | 'unstable' | 'ABSENT'; readonly secondRead?: DiskSnapshotId; readonly turn: TurnContext; readonly viewAtOpen?: ClaimView; }
export interface ConfirmResult { readonly status: 'confirmed' | 'deferred'; readonly hunksInBase: readonly Hunk[];
  readonly touches: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly holder: SeatKey; readonly reason: string }>; readonly drafts: readonly Draft[]; readonly notOwnContained: readonly Hunk[]; }
export type ReleaseReason = 'explicit' | 'answer' | 'save' | 'seat_end' | 'stop' | 'reservation_transfer';   // Plan 4's; Plan 5 uses explicit, seat_end, stop
export interface RegionsPort {
  /** REG-28 (normalized locate), Plan 4's Engine.predict. A10-3 as ruled by the verify pass (V-8, Plan 4 C-4): the gate holds short-read bytes and no git process runs on its
   *  reply path (CCI-12); the binding registers them with SnapshotStore.register(path, blobOidOfBytes(bytes, objectFormat), bytes, owner) — git's blob hash of the raw
   *  bytes computed in process (Task 18, src/claude/daemon/regions.ts), a content key for Plan 4's cache; off the reply path R5-26's hashRouteBStdin oid stays. */
  predict(q: PredictQuery): Promise<PredictResult>;
  /** REG-29, REG-33: CONFIRM + Algorithm CLAIM for one confirmed tool effect (Plan 4's Engine.seatEffect); status 'deferred' → Plan 5 calls it again for the same csId at the
   *  seat's next stable read; touches = the K2 input, notOwnContained = the wake-scope input. */
  seatEffect(e: SeatEffect): Promise<ConfirmResult>;
  /** glossary §3; null before the first fold. Plan 5 reads it at Pre and keeps it with the lease/TXN (K2 "as folded when the Pre was answered"; replaces the deleted atHeight). */
  claimView(): ClaimView | null;
  claimViewOf(reader: SeatKey): readonly ViewClaim[];   // the F05 reader view; Plan 5 derives foreignHeldOnPath from it
  mapRanges(from: BlobRef, to: BlobRef, ranges: Ranges, mode: 'local'): Promise<Ranges | 'TOMBSTONE' | 'WHOLE_FILE'>;   // Plan 4's RegionService.mapRanges, local mode
  verifiedLabel(claimId: string, anchor: Oid): ClaimLabel | null;   // cache only (REG-34); never triggers a parse; rendered with claimLabelText (X-23)
  claimNotes(claimId: string, against?: BlobRef | 'ABSENT'): Promise<ReadonlyArray<{ code: string; params: Params }>>;   // Plan 4's RegionNote[] (cowork_show, deny/hold clauses)
  /** X-22: over Plan 4's one release emitter releaseClaims(claimHostOf(core), holder, reason, target) in src/claims/release.ts; callers seat_end, stop (LIF-36's
   *  release{reason: stop} of removalShutdown, A9-6) and cowork_release (through CoordToolsPort.release); why 'not_own' → NOT_OWN_CLAIM, 'unlanded' → RELEASE_UNLANDED. */
  releaseClaims(holder: SeatKey, reason: ReleaseReason, target: readonly string[] | 'all'): { drafts: readonly OpDraft[]; refused: ReadonlyArray<{ claimId: string; why: 'not_own' | 'unlanded' }> };
}
/** X-23: Plan 4's one label display helper (src/claims/labels.ts): '?' for null; re-exported here from Task 18 on, a structural copy with the same rule before. */
export function claimLabelText(l: ClaimLabel | null): string;
/** Plan 5-local, off the port (X-20; Plan 4 C-5): shell class (c)'s "a literal target holds a foreign HELD claim", computed from claimViewOf(reader). */
export function foreignHeldOnPath(regions: RegionsPort, reader: SeatKey, path: string): boolean;
// Also Plan 5-local (X-20): the predicted TENTATIVE claims of a G2/G3 (06 §3.5.2 row 14; formerly RegionsPort.recordTentative/discardTentative) are fence state of
//   LeaseTable (Task 10), recorded with the INFLIGHT lease and discarded on its release. Deleted names: computeClaims (EMIT is Plan 4's emitStep at publish), atHeight,
//   Prediction, Confirmation, ClaimDraft, GrantRef, Prediction.diskRanges (now PredictResult.diskLines).
/** No claims exist before Plan 4; every value is Plan 4-shaped: predict → {status: 'undecided', diskLines: [], hunksInBase: [], touches: [], moved: null, fallbackUsed: false,
 *  ownTouches: [], ownContained: false}; seatEffect → {status: 'confirmed', hunksInBase: the pre→post hunks, touches: [], drafts: [], notOwnContained: every hunk};
 *  claimView → null; claimViewOf → []; mapRanges(from, to, ranges, 'local') → ranges unchanged (A12-7: nothing to re-anchor, the last maskStep ranges stay, R5-31);
 *  verifiedLabel → null; claimNotes → []; releaseClaims → {drafts: [], refused: every named id with why 'not_own'} ('all' → nothing to release). */
export const noRegions: RegionsPort;
/** Task 18 (A18-4): the production Plan 4 binding — one line per member over Plan 4's Engine.predict, seatEffect, claimView, claimViewOf, verifiedLabel, claimNotes,
 *  RegionService.mapRanges and releaseClaims(claimHostOf(core), …) (X-20, X-22). Plan 5 binds RegionsPort only (X-33). */
export function plan4Regions(core: EngineCore): RegionsPort;

// src/claude/daemon/shadow.ts — CCI-18, CCI-19, HLD-47, REG-30, INV-CCI-RECORD-FIRST, INV-ONE-EVENT
export class ShadowRecorder {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx);
  /** Shadow key (seat_id, claim_id, path), claim_id = the earliest intersected foreign claim; base = current disk bytes at the first deny, post-image = the previous post-image
   *  with this tool_input applied on the normalized view (applyToolInput; chained denials compose); writes one file denyRecordPath(coworkDir, id, seq) = tmp/deny-<id>-<seq>.bin
   *  (A11-1: one canonical-JSON DenyFileMeta line, then the base bytes when base_len is set — first deny only —, then the post bytes; 0600, exclusive create, on the
   *  worktree's volume) and fsyncs (≤ 5 ms); crashpoint('deny.prerecord'); returns before the G1 reply. id = the future hold_id (minted at the first deny of the key, seeded
   *  ulid in SIM); repeat = 1-based count on this key in this turn. */
  prerecord(seat: Seat, agentId: string | null, path: string, claimId: string, claimIds: readonly string[], input: EditInput, disk: ShortRead): Promise<{ id: string; repeat: number }>;
  /** After the reply: both blobs through the clean path (hashRouteBStdin(…, true): hash-object -w --stdin --path=<p>, attr.tree, required filters) → HLD-47 PATCH via
   *  shadowRecord/HoldRegistry.hold (SHADOW, OFF_DISK PARKED, offer pending_leftover, guard claim:<id>, origin <seat_id>[/<agent_id>]); a required-filter failure → guards
   *  [claim:<id>, filter-failure] and no h1, the registry recomputing local_only (HLD-03; A11-4: the one filter-failure creation site in Plan 5); every deny-<id>-*.bin deleted. */
  convert(id: string): Promise<'converted' | 'local_only'>;
  /** Daemon start: every surviving tmp/deny-*.bin is converted before anything else (step 7). */
  convertLeftovers(): Promise<number>;
}
/** A11-1: the deny tmp record's meta line. */
export interface DenyFileMeta { v: 1; id: string; seq: number; seat_id: string; agent_id: string | null; claim_id: string; claim_ids: string[]; path: string; base_len: number | null; post_len: number; }
export function denyRecordPath(coworkDir: string, id: string, seq: number): string;   // <coworkDir>/tmp/deny-<id>-<seq>.bin
/** A11-2, pure: the tool input applied on 06 §3.5.3's normalized view of base (BOM, CRLF, quote folding), mapped back to raw bytes; Write → content; NotebookEdit → the notebook
 *  JSON with that cell's source replaced; an old_string that does not match → null (the gate then answers G2; a write inside the claim anyway is K2's at the Post). */
export function applyToolInput(tool: EditTool, input: EditInput, base: Buffer): Buffer | null;

// src/claude/daemon/suspect.ts — PRO-37 self-guard, CCI-17, R5-06
/** Own last fetch or push older than 60 s by max(Δwall, Δmono), or a suspend gap > 5 s. */
export function suspectNow(engine: EngineView, clock: Clock): boolean;
/** Sends extend{7000} within 100 ms, starts or joins a fetch (Engine.freshFetch(): Promise<void>, A11-3 / X5-07 as ruled by X-77; the landed requestFreshFetch(): void is unchanged), waits ≤ 5 s for a fetch completed after
 *  this request and its fold. */
export function refreshForSuspect(engine: Engine, extend: (ms: number) => boolean, clock: Clock): Promise<'fresh' | 'timeout'>;
```

### PostToolUse, TXN close, attribution notes, edit checkpoints (Tasks 12, 13)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/post.ts — 06 §3.8.1–§3.8.2a, §3.13, CCI-21, CCI-25, CCI-26, CCI-43, F05 prerequisites (R5-08, R5-31, R5-50)
export class ViewBlobs {   // 06 §2 view blob: per (seat, path), the last full or overlapping ranged Read or the last edit-class post-image; never leaves the daemon
  set(seatId: string, path: string, s: DiskSnapshot): void; get(seatId: string, path: string): DiskSnapshot | null; clearSeat(seatId: string): void;
}
export class ReadSets {    // CCI-21, CCI-43 item 1: full reads and ranged reads with their ranges (± 20 lines overlap); memory only
  recordFull(seatId: string, path: string): void; recordRange(seatId: string, path: string, rows: readonly [number, number]): void;
  fullReadSet(seatId: string, sinceMono?: number): ReadonlySet<string>;
  overlaps(seatId: string, path: string, rows: readonly [number, number]): boolean;
  lastApplyInto(seatId: string): number | null; noteApplied(paths: readonly string[]): void;   // F14 I4b instant
}
export interface PostOutcome { block: { code: 'HELD_CS'; params: Params } | null; items: CarrierItem[]; trigger: EditTrigger | null; effects: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string }>; }
/** Edit-class Post matched to an INFLIGHT lease: post-image by one short read (empty-or-shrunk rule), confirmation through RegionsPort.seatEffect (≤ 250 ms p95 in Plan 4's
 *  `fg` pool class, X-21; later results ride the next hook; status 'deferred' → called again at the seat's next stable read), EOL-only/BOM-only hunks dropped, the lease's
 *  TENTATIVE claims `confirmed`, drafts kept by Plan 4 (EMIT is Plan 4's emitStep at publish; no computeClaims, X-20); K2 against the claim view read at the Pre (record first:
 *  PIN over the CS's LIVE paths, local_only on filter failure, no disk write, HELD_CS {rid, cs, label, holder}; repeats join); wake turn → WakeScopePort.backstop (with h1 and
 *  localOnly, X-41); view blob := post-image; held ranges refreshed (R5-31). An unmatched Post has no pre-image: DEGRADED (Task 9), no checkpoint. */
export function handleEditPost(r: HookRequest, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<PostOutcome>;
/** Read: full → READ lease + full-read set; ranged → recorded range (raw offset/limit until R5-50's constants are set); view blob := the read's bytes (one short read); the lease,
 *  view blob and full-read-set entry only for a LIVE path (A12-8: a non-LIVE path receives no apply to defer and carries no claim, CCI-63 — a reading flagged for reconciliation);
 *  READ_REPLACED when ENOENT or 0 bytes on a tracked path the verifying writer replaced ≤ 2 s ago; HOLD_IN_FILE for ACTIVE (ON_DISK) records in the file; late F05 results ride
 *  here (≤ 5 ms main-thread work); for a LIVE path it then calls ctx.bindings.coordHooks.onRead({seat, agent, path, read, viewBlob, lineCount}) (X-40; void, a no-op with
 *  noCoordHooks). A12-12: host defaults to {rows: READ_HOST_DEFAULT_ROWS, offsetBase: READ_OFFSET_BASE} (ESM constants cannot be reassigned, so the
 *  R5-50 test passes its own host); with rows or offsetBase null only read.full is used and ranged reads keep their raw offset/limit. */
export function handleReadPost(r: HookRequest, ctx: DaemonCtx, host?: { rows: number | null; offsetBase: 0 | 1 | null }): Promise<PostOutcome>;
/** A12-3 (R5-31): held ranges refreshed after every PostToolUse on a path, mapped from the materialized S_disk[p] to the post-image (RegionsPort.mapRanges 'local';
 *  TOMBSTONE / WHOLE_FILE → the whole file); EngineView.heldRanges answers them while base equals materializedOid(path), else Plan 3's Engine.heldRanges (signature
 *  unchanged). get returns null when base ≠ materialized (the next materialization replaced the overlay); memory only. */
export class HeldRangeOverlay {
  set(path: string, ranges: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>, base: OidOrAbsent): void;
  get(path: string, materialized: OidOrAbsent | null): ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> | null;
  clear(path: string): void;
}
/** A12-9 (K2, CCI-26, record first): one PIN per (seat CS, claim) over the CS's LIVE paths through Plan 3's pinCs/HoldRegistry.holdOutcome (guard claim:<id>, h1 via
 *  hashRouteBStdin(…, true), offer pending_leftover, origin <seat>[/<agent>]; a filter failure → guard filter-failure, local_only); a repeat hit joins (joined: true, no new op);
 *  returns after the registry CAS and before the reply; null when no hit's claim is in `view` — the claim view read at the Pre or TXN open (InflightLease.view / Txn.view,
 *  X-20; it replaces the former fold-height test: a claim folded after the Pre is K3 at publish, not K2). */
export class K2Detector {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx);
  check(seat: Seat, agentId: string | null, csId: string, view: ClaimView | null, hits: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; claimId: string; holder: string; label: string }>,
    posts: ReadonlyMap<string, DiskSnapshot>): Promise<{ holdId: string; joined: boolean; localOnly: boolean } | null>;
}
/** A12-10 (HLD-22, HLD-23): scanRecords runs from the status observer after each iteration: for every new non-terminal record whose origin names a live seat of this peer,
 *  that is not a K1 SHADOW (its G1 reason named it) and whose id was not marked at its own hook (K2 and wake-scope PIN ids are marked by told.first(id) in post.edit/txn.close),
 *  it queues OWN_CHANGE_HELD {rid, path, reason} once and TurnItems.record(seat, agent, 'recorded', rid); for every PIN whose cs_ids hold a seat's CS and that was not created
 *  at that seat's hook, it queues HOLD_PIN_CONTEXT {path, rid, reason} once per (seat, path, turn). */
export class RecordsTold { first(holdId: string): boolean; firstPinContext(seatId: string, path: string, turnKey: string): boolean; }
export function scanRecords(ctx: DaemonCtx, told: RecordsTold): void;
/** A12-11 (R2-D4): observe() runs from the status observer: for each path holding a HELD claim of a seat of this peer (RegionsPort.claimView) it remembers the last
 *  materialized oid; when EngineView.materializedOid(p) changes and AttributionWindows.attribute(p) is null (human-class), it diffs the two blobs (GitRepo.readBlobs, worker
 *  pool, off the hook path) and queues one HUMAN_EDIT_OWN_CLAIM {label, a, b} per (claim, changed row range) to that claim's seat; with noRegions no path qualifies and no
 *  blob is read (the K5 breach exemption itself is Plan 4's/Plan 6's). */
export class HumanEdits { constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx); observe(): Promise<void>; }
/** A12-5 (F05-08): late SlotExtraPort results ride the seat's next PostToolUse of the same turn; dropped at that turn's Stop. Plan 6's claim-ahead mapping pushes here. */
export class LateSlotQueue { push(seatId: string, agentId: string | null, item: CarrierItem): void; take(seatId: string, agentId: string | null, charsLeft: number): CarrierItem | null; dropTurn(seatId: string): void; }
export function withLateQueue(port: SlotExtraPort | null, q: LateSlotQueue): SlotExtraPort;

// src/claude/daemon/txnClose.ts — 06 §3.7.3 close, §3.8.2–§3.8.4 trigger (b)
export interface TxnCloseResult { attributed: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; post: DiskSnapshot }>; block: PostOutcome['block']; items: CarrierItem[]; trigger: EditTrigger | null; }
/** Close by Post/PostFailure/PermissionDenied/background return/new-turn UPS/Stop/StopFailure/SessionEnd/seat end/120 s cap: snapshot the worktree by short reads of the paths dirty
 *  since S_open, attribute changes first seen in the window to the seat with its open cs_id and tool_use_id, K2, wake-scope backstop; a Bash/PowerShell close by its own call
 *  (Post, PostFailure, background return, cap) that attributed ≥ 1 change to the seat's open CS is edit-checkpoint trigger (b); Monitor, PermissionDenied and turn-boundary closes are not;
 *  a block for a TXN closed by cap or background return rides the seat's next hook. */
export function closeTxn(t: Txn, why: ReleaseWhy, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<TxnCloseResult>;
/** A12-1: trigger (b) of a hookless close (cap, background return), delivered to EditCheckpoints.onTrigger; hook-carried closes set HookRequest.trigger instead. */
export function onCloseTrigger(cb: (t: EditTrigger) => void): () => void;

// src/claude/daemon/attribJournal.ts — CCI-43 items 1, 3, 4 (R5-09)
export interface ApplyRecord { path: string; rows: readonly [number, number]; label: string; name: string; held: boolean; mono: number; }
export class ApplyJournal {
  /** Every apply (from ClaudeEnginePort.applied + the engine's incoming CS writer and display name; labels claimLabelText(RegionsPort.verifiedLabel(claimId, anchor)), '?' until Plan 4, X-23). */
  record(r: ApplyRecord): void;
  /** At most one coalesced ATTRIBUTION {path, lines, label, name, held} per PostToolUse for applies since the seat's previous tool call into files of its read set (full, or ranged ± 20 lines); ≤ 600 with the slot. */
  attributionNote(seatId: string, sinceMono: number, reads: ReadSets): CarrierItem | null;
  replacedWithin(path: string, ms: number): boolean;   // READ_REPLACED (2 s)
}

// src/claude/daemon/editCheckpoints.ts — CCI-56, 06 §3.8.4, glossary §27.1, §29.8, R5-20…R5-22
export type { SkipReason } from '../../holds/lastCheck.js';   // declared in src/holds/lastCheck.ts (src/holds may not import src/claude); re-exported here
export interface EditTrigger { seatId: string; agentId: string | null; csId: string; source: 'edit_post' | 'txn_close'; toolUseId: string; paths: readonly string[]; known: ReadonlyMap<string, DiskSnapshot>; }
export class EditCheckpoints {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx);   // A13-11: built with the rest of DaemonCtx before the Engine exists; the Engine arrives through attachEngine
  /** Before the reply, ≤ 150 ms: skips 1 (CS held whole by a PIN, incl. one created at this hook) and 2 (CS has a SHADOW) record nothing; 3 shrink, 4 slow_read, 6 batch (another
   *  INFLIGHT lease of this seat or another TXN open) record last_check{skipped} in Plan 3's ChangeSets.lastCheck (X-06: Plan 5 writes only skips 3–6 — with record() and the
   *  trigger's own edit_n for a skip decided here, where no cand_seq exists, and with recordFor() for a settled candidate's skip); none in a paused repo; else edit_n += 1,
   *  next cand_seq of the CS, lastCheck.noteCandidate(cs, candSeq, editN), captureIdentity, and the per-CS queue (≤ 1 in flight + 1 waiting, newer replaces waiting) sends
   *  checkpoint{source: 'edit', candSeq, paths, expect}. Emits no code on any outcome. */
  onTrigger(t: EditTrigger, budgetMs: number): Promise<'queued' | { skipped: SkipReason | 'pinned' | 'shadow' | 'paused' }>;
  // A13-10: hookless closes — EditCheckpoints subscribes Task 12's onCloseTrigger when ClaudeDaemon.attach wires it, and calls onTrigger(t, C.CANDIDATE_BOUND_MS) at the
  //   close itself (06 §3.8.4 step 2 "its candidate is taken at the close"); nothing waits for it, and a skip there records exactly as at a hook. Hook-carried triggers
  //   arrive through HookRequest.trigger, read by the checkpoint.trigger step of STEP_ORDER.PostToolUse and (A13-9) PostToolUseFailure.
  /** Engine.onCandidateSettled: 'dropped' with expect mismatch → lastCheck.recordFor(cs, candSeq, {skipped: slow_read}); an A[p] change between capture and result → skip
   *  base_moved (recordFor); pass/fail/incomplete record nothing here (Plan 3's settleCandidate wrote them, X-06); sends the waiting one. */
  settled(csId: string, candSeq: number, outcome: 'pass' | 'fail' | 'incomplete' | 'dropped', why: 'expect' | 'base_moved' | null): void;
  queueState(csId: string): { editN: number; inFlight: number | null; waiting: number | null };   // F14 UI_FLOW_LAST_CHECKING input
  /** A13-11 (R5-55): called by ClaudeDaemon.attach (pre-wired by Task 5; the stub is a no-op); Task 13 subscribes engine.onCandidateSettled → settled and
   *  engine.onCaptureTaken → the awaitCapture waiters here, so Task 13 edits no composition file. */
  attachEngine(engine: Engine): void;
  /** A13-11: resolves true when Engine.onCaptureTaken reports (csId, candSeq) on time, 'late' when reported late or when waitMs passes first (Task 5's stub resolves 'late');
   *  cowork_checkpoint (Task 14) calls it and never names Engine.onCaptureTaken. */
  awaitCapture(csId: string, candSeq: number, waitMs: number): Promise<true | 'late'>;
}

// src/holds/lastCheck.ts — glossary §29.8, 10 §3.5.1. X-05: Plan 3's file (Task 10 amendment, F-P3-1; roadmap row 3), with one instance ChangeSets.lastCheck, memory only
//   (never in CsTable, the cs cache or a state commit; empty after restart). Plan 5 creates and edits nothing here; the block below is the contract Plan 5 consumes (X-05's
//   shape and member set) — a landed difference stops Task 13 for a ruling. Writers (X-06): Plan 3's settleCandidate (fail/conflict/incomplete from verdict.entries?.[0],
//   else failed[0]; more = entries.length − 1; passedFor on a pass), published() (sharedFor), the T0 drop (stale from T0Verdict.findings[0]); Plan 5's editCheckpoints.ts
//   (skips 3–6 and noteCandidate). Readers: Plan 5's flow/wrap replies (X-01), Plan 7 (get, pass; X7-01).
export type LastCheckKind = 'fail' | 'conflict' | 'incomplete' | 'stale' | 'skipped';
export type SkipReason = 'shrink' | 'slow_read' | 'base_moved' | 'batch';   // declared here (src/holds precedes src/claude); editCheckpoints.ts re-exports it
export interface LastCheck { edit_n: number; kind: LastCheckKind; check?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path?: string; line?: number; skip?: SkipReason; more: number; }
export class LastCheckStore {
  record(csId: string, e: LastCheck): void;                                  // keeps the first failed check of the newest edit_n; an older edit_n is ignored
  get(csId: string): LastCheck | null;
  pass(csId: string): { ready: number | null; shared: number | null };       // last_pass
  clearCs(csId: string): void;                                               // CS closed (called inside ChangeSets), or a passing capture with a higher edit_n landed
  // A13-4 (now X-05's member set of Plan 3's store): Plan 3's settle and T0 drop know only cand_seq; edit_n ≠ cand_seq because skipped triggers count (R5-22)
  noteCandidate(csId: string, candSeq: number, editN: number): void;        // called by EditCheckpoints when it sends a candidate
  recordFor(csId: string, candSeq: number, e: Omit<LastCheck, 'edit_n'>): void;   // edit_n from the noted pair; an unknown candSeq records nothing
  passedFor(csId: string, candSeq: number): void;                            // sets last_pass.ready; clears last_check when its edit_n is lower
  sharedFor(csId: string, candSeq: number): void;                            // sets last_pass.shared
}
```

### MCP tools, undo view (Task 14)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/mcpTools.ts — CCI-47, CCI-48, CCI-67, CCI-68, 06 §3.16.3, SEC-27, R5-08, R5-10, R5-41, R5-42
/** X-39: the frame-level result of one MCP tool call (formerly ToolResult; that name is now the CoordToolsPort result below). */
export interface McpToolResult { text: string; structured: Json; }   // never isError; refusals {ok:false, code, text}; ≤ 400 chars except cowork_show ≤ 8,000 (count line on truncation)
/** cowork_status (STATUS_NO_CHANGE within 30 s with an unchanged state digest); cowork_show(id: string) (request display id R+base32c(sha256(hold_id))[0:8] or ULID, C<cid>,
 *  claim_id, cs_id: author, origin, commits, paths, + the undo view for this peer's CS; UNKNOWN_ID; AMBIGUOUS_ID lists both); cowork_request/respond/note/release (validation,
 *  refusal codes, queued under FREEZE/pause, then the matching CoordToolsPort member — release reaching Plan 4's releaseClaims through it, X-22); cowork_checkpoint (checkpoint{source: tool} with expect; {cs_id, paths, captured: true|late});
 *  cowork_resolve(id, "restore") (claudeRestoreRefusal mapping R5-41, the §3.5.2 evaluation as an Edit by this seat, writeLocal inside a synthetic INFLIGHT lease, RESTORE_MARKERS,
 *  wake-scope backstop in a wake turn). No handler saves, stops, pauses, discards, publishes a hold, moves a branch, changes config or answers for another seat.
 *  A14-4 (06 §3.14.4 `{queued: <code>}` row, §3.16.2): the queued value is the first FREEZE reason set, in glossary §8 order (EPOCH, MASS, PAUSED, INCIDENT, CONFIG,
 *  NOHOOKS), else the code of the publish pause that disallows the op (VERSION_PAUSED); INCIDENT is a FREEZE reason, not a pause.
 *  A14-6 (as amended by A13-11): cowork_checkpoint draws its cand_seq from SignalPump.nextCandSeq and calls ctx.checkpoints.awaitCapture(csId, candSeq, C.RING_RESULT_WAIT_MS)
 *  (5,000; the existing IPC liveness bound, no new number): captured true, or 'late' when the identity missed 150 ms, the capture was reported late, or the wait expired. */
export function handleTool(seat: Seat, name: ToolName, args: Json, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<McpToolResult>;
/** Binds the `tool` and `ring_result` request kinds on the server (Task 14; one call in claudeDaemon.ts). */
export function registerMcpRequests(server: ClaudeServer, ctx: DaemonCtx): void;
/** X-39: Plan 5's MCP-tools port (renamed from CoordPort — CoordPort is now Plan 6's engine port in src/engine/coordPort.ts) with exactly Plan 6's CoordTools members and
 *  result; ToolCtx and ToolResult are structural copies of Plan 6's (pinned equal by a type-level test once Plan 6 lands). Plan 5 keeps the schemas, isError (never true)
 *  and the queued mapping (X6-29); handleTool maps a ToolResult onto an McpToolResult. ClaudeBindings.coordTools. */
export type ToolCtx = { seat: string; agent: string; turnOrigin: 'human' | 'wake' | null; lang: ClaudeLang };
export type ToolResult = { ok: true; text: string; queued?: string } | { ok: false; code: string; text: string };   // ≤ 400 chars, cowork_show ≤ 8,000
export interface CoordToolsPort {
  request(c: ToolCtx, a: { rid: string; mode: 'handoff' | 'go_first'; est_minutes?: number; note?: string }): Promise<ToolResult>;
  respond(c: ToolCtx, a: { rid: string; verb: 'accept' | 'decline' | 'later' | 'release'; note?: string }): Promise<ToolResult>;
  note(c: ToolCtx, a: { text: string }): Promise<ToolResult>;
  release(c: ToolCtx, a: { claim_id: string }): Promise<ToolResult>;                    // 'all' allowed; over RegionsPort.releaseClaims (X-22)
  show(c: ToolCtx, a: { id: string | readonly string[] }): Promise<ToolResult>;          // never isError; Plan 5's binding accepts one string (R5-08), Plan 6 widens
  status(c: ToolCtx): Promise<ToolResult>;                                               // never isError
}
/** R5-10 (as renamed by X-39): Plan 5's default binding until Plan 6's bindClaude replaces it — request/respond/note draft exactly the glossary §7 op (request with origin
 *  claude|wake, answer with by: claude, note with its target) with code-only text; release → RegionsPort.releaseClaims with why mapped to NOT_OWN_CLAIM/RELEASE_UNLANDED;
 *  show → Plan 5's cowork_show body (ids, undo view); status → Plan 5's cowork_status body. */
export const outboxCoord: (ctx: DaemonCtx) => CoordToolsPort;
/** X-51: Plan 5's note/status scan port, renamed from SecretScanPort (that name stays Plan 7's engine port) and async; the scanner itself (scanSecrets, scanHit, scanD2) is
 *  Plan 7's alone — Plan 7 binds this port to scanHit; Plan 6 uses it through the bindings and creates no scanner. ClaudeBindings.noteScan. */
export interface NoteScanPort { scan(text: string, kind: 'note' | 'status'): Promise<{ available: boolean; hit: boolean }>; }
export const unavailableScan: NoteScanPort;   // available false → notes NOTE_SCAN_UNAVAILABLE, undo view without diff lines (R5-42); notes are refused until Plan 7 lands (pre-release only, D4)
export const canaryScan: NoteScanPort;        // test builds: hit iff COWORK_CANARY_[A-Za-z0-9_]+

// src/claude/daemon/undoView.ts — CCI-68, FEAT-F10-10, 05 Algorithm REV view mode, glossary §29.7 Revert row
/** X-09: RevertView is declared by Plan 3 Task 17 in src/holds/revertCs.ts (P5's shape below, which is the shape that task declares) and imported here — undoView.ts
 *  declares no local copy; until Task 17 merges, Task 14 compiles against the declaration below as a structural stand-in and replaces it by the import when Task 17 lands. */
import type { RevertView } from '../../holds/revertCs.js';
//   = { kind: 'view' | 'notown' | 'summary_only' | 'unknown' | 'ambiguous'; summary: Json;
//       paths: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; diff?: string; human?: string; skip?: 'MIXED' | 'FILE_CHANGED_LATER' | 'RENAME_PAIR' | 'NOT_LIVE' | 'TEAMMATE_LINE' | 'CHANGED_NOW' }>;
//       kept: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; line: number }>; candidates?: readonly string[] }
export interface RevertViewPort { view(csId: string): Promise<RevertView>; }   // Plan 3's revertView (X5-14, X-09); no object, no file; Plan 6's CoordHost.undo is this port (X-55)
/** A14-2 (as ruled by X-09): delegates in one line to Plan 3 Task 17's revertView(core, csId) (the F10 revert kernel, src/holds/revertCs.ts); only until that task has merged
 *  it answers {kind: 'summary_only', summary, paths: [], kept: []} for every own CS, and CCI-68/TC-F10-12 are met through scriptedRevertView (R5-59). */
export const plan3RevertView: (core: EngineCore) => RevertViewPort;
/** T-SHOW_UNDO.head, per path a -U3 stored-form diff current → reverted, kept/skip/human lines, foot; > 8,000 → whole paths dropped from the end with .trunc; another peer's CS →
 *  .notown; every diff line through NoteScanPort ('status'; hit → [redacted: secret-scan]; unavailable → no diff lines); Plan 4's `bg` pool class (X-21), one at a time; SHOW_NO_CHANGE for the
 *  same (seat, cs_id) within 30 s while TIP and the materialized blobs of its paths are unchanged. */
export function undoView(seatId: string, csId: string, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<string>;
```

### Human-only verbs, D1 and the CLI requests (Task 15)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/evidence.ts — SEC-25, SEC-26, CCI-54, INV-HUMANONLY, R5-13, R5-14, R5-38
export class EvidenceStore {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, key: Buffer | null);
  /** From a human-classified /cowork UserPromptExpansion only (never a wake-classified one, never one carrying a cws- nonce, never a UserPromptSubmit); one per prompt_id. */
  mint(seat: Seat, r: EvidenceRecord): 'minted' | 'duplicate' | 'refused_wake' | 'refused_nonce';
  /** Reads <gitdir>/cowork/tmp/evidence.jsonl: a line whose HMAC-SHA256(ipc.key, "evidence" ‖ canonical line) verifies → minted; an untagged line only for start/doctor while
   *  ipc.key does not exist; a bad tag → discarded + SEC_EVIDENCE_TAG_INVALID; consumed or expired lines are removed from the file. */
  ingestFile(): Promise<void>;
  /** P7 A5-2: an unused record with the same verb and args_sha256 minted ≤ maxAgeMs earlier (Δmono via mono_ns) from a seat of this repo, waiting ≤ waitMs (ingesting the file
   *  every 50 ms); marks it used (single use). */
  await(e: { verb: ActingVerb; argsSha256: string }, o: { maxAgeMs: number; waitMs: number }): Promise<{ ok: true; seatId: string | null } | { ok: false }>;
  /** X-02: the one read-only grant API of every plan (Plan 6's consumeGrant and Plan 7's humanExpansionMark/consumeMark are deleted names): a human-classified
   *  /cowork since expansion records {seat_key, prompt_id, verb, args_sha256, mono_ns}; never evidence for an acting verb; renews no lease. status and wrap mint no grant (X-01). */
  grant(seat: Seat, verb: 'since', argsSha256: string, promptId: string): void;
  /** R5-57: an unused 'since' grant of argsSha256 minted ≤ maxAgeMs before it is matched (Δmono), waiting ≤ waitMs and re-checking every 50 ms like await (the `!` line
   *  can start before the expansion's frame lands, 09 §3.13 step 4, 06 §3.18); true marks it used (single use); its one caller, HumanActivity.sinceRan, passes
   *  {maxAgeMs: C.GRANT_MAX_AGE_MS, waitMs: C.CLI_EVIDENCE_WAIT_MS}. */
  awaitGrant(verb: 'since', argsSha256: string, o: { maxAgeMs: number; waitMs: number }): Promise<boolean>;
  /** V-7 (Plan 7 X7-11, Task 7's onPausedMarkerSeen): true iff a human-classified /cowork <verb> expansion was minted (used or not) within windowMs of atMonoMs, Δmono both
   *  ways; the store keeps every mint's (verb, mono) for ≥ 120 s after minting for this query even once the record is consumed or expired. Read-only: marks nothing used. */
  mintedWithin(verb: ActingVerb, atMonoMs: number, windowMs: number): boolean;
}

// src/claude/daemon/acting.ts — SEC-25 steps 3–7, R5-13
/** X-58: the result widens additively with data (Plan 7's previews, phases and single-use tokens ride in params/data); Plan 5's handlers leave data absent. */
export type ActingHandler = (params: Json, o: { seatId: string | null }) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; code: string; params: Params; data?: WireJson }>;
export class ActingVerbRegistry {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx);
  register(verb: ActingVerb, h: ActingHandler): void;   // Plan 7 registers the verbs other than done and resolve (X5-29); throws on a duplicate verb
  /** Accept iff (a) tty && !ancestry_has_claude, or (b) EvidenceStore.await({verb, args_sha256}, {60 s, 3 s}) ok; then renew the human lease (60 min) once per accepted request
   *  (X-58: this is the only renewal; Plan 7 never calls renewHuman and calls SeatLeasePort.grantHuman(minutes) only for start --human) and run the handler;
   *  else SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN {cmd} (the command to retype).
   *  A15-2 (as amended by R5-60): the evidence gate runs first, then the registry lookup — an unregistered verb (save, revert) without evidence is SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN, with
   *  evidence {ok: false, code: 'RF_UNKNOWN_VERB'} in every build (10 §1.2's registry code; the evidence is consumed). A15-3: cmd is the slash form to retype, built daemon-side from verb and the frame's structured params (/cowork done, /cowork resolve <id> --<verb>); for a
   *  verb without Plan 5 params it is /cowork <verb> (argument text never reaches the daemon, SEC-26). No Plan 5 handler has a preview or confirm step (LIF-02 is Plan 7's). */
  handle(f: Extract<SessionClientFrame, { t: 'acting' }>): Promise<Extract<SessionServerFrame, { t: 'acting_result' }>>;
}
export function registerActingRequests(server: ClaudeServer, ctx: DaemonCtx): void;   // binds the `acting` request kind (Task 15; one call in claudeDaemon.ts)
/** done → Engine.done(); resolve <id> --publish|--discard|--park|--restore → Engine.resolve(id, verb, {humanEvidence: true, actor: 'human'}).
 *  X-59: Plan 7 Task 11 deletes these two r.register lines (Plan 5 reviewer) in the commit that registers its doneHandler/resolveHandler (which call the same Engine.done and
 *  Engine.resolve, plus the R7-40 secret-scan preview), because register throws on a duplicate. */
export function registerPlan5Verbs(r: ActingVerbRegistry, engine: Engine): void;

// src/claude/daemon/flow.ts — 09 §3.11 rev 2.5, FEAT-F14-05, -08, -09, D1 (R5-14, R5-53). X-57: Plan 5 owns and registers the request kinds acting, flow, wrap,
//   since_query, since_ran and slash_shown; Plan 7 registers only its own kinds (status_detail, queue_query, show_query, log_query, doctor_run, doctor_query, canary_arm,
//   canary_disarm, revert_view, uninstall_repo) through its local registerIpcHandler helper over registerFrameKind + ClaudeServer.onRequest (X-56).
/** X-11: Plan 5's daemon half of the F14 inputs (seats, defer tags, the TXN-open instant, edit checks in flight); the engine half is Plan 3 Task 18's
 *  Engine.flowInputs(): EngineFlowInputs (+ ChangeSets.quietLeftMs(), Governor.waitDetail(), hasShadow); lastCheck/lastPass read Plan 3's ChangeSets.lastCheck (X-05). */
export interface ClaudeFlowInputs {
  seats: ReadonlyArray<{ seatId: string; number: number; activity: SeatActivity; pullMode: boolean; fullReadSet: readonly string[]; lastApplyIntoReadSetMono: number | null }>;
  defer: ReadonlyMap<string, DeferTag>; txnOpenSinceMono: number | null;
  editChecks: ReadonlyArray<{ csId: string; seatNumber: number; editN: number; inFlight: number | null; waiting: number | null }>;   // Plan 7's checkingEditN source (X-05)
  lastCheck: (csId: string) => LastCheck | null; lastPass: (csId: string) => { ready: number | null; shared: number | null };
}
/** X-74: Plan 7 binds it as flowEntries({engine: engine.flowInputs(), claude}, selector, {max}) (its stage table and rendering are Plan 7's, X-11); withLastCheck and
 *  admitsLastCheck are deleted names — handleFlow fills last_check from the store (X-01). */
export interface FlowPort { entries(claude: ClaudeFlowInputs, selector: FlowSelector, max: number): { entries: FlowEntryWire[]; omitted: number }; }
export const noFlowEntries: FlowPort;                                                                       // {entries: [], omitted: 0} (R5-53)
export interface WrapPort { rows(caller: { seatKey: string | null }): Promise<Json>; }                     // Plan 7 (F12): its wrapHandler body becomes this binding (X-57); V-7: handleWrap
                                                                                                            // passes the requesting seat (onRequest's peer.seatKey; null from a plain terminal) — wrap's issuing seat;
                                                                                                            // V-16: a Promise, because Plan 7's producer reads git within ATTRIBUTION_BUDGET_MS (handleWrap awaits it)
export const noWrap: WrapPort;
/** From the last iteration's memory, no git process, file read, snapshot or write; one FlowPort.entries(claude, selector, C.FLOW_MAX_ENTRIES) call (≤ 2,000 entries + omitted;
 *  the ≤ 20 ms event-loop block is the port body's bound, measured by Task 15 over syntheticFlow); reply ≤ 1 MiB; a path selector failing validPathSyntax →
 *  refused{code: 'FLOW_PATH_INVALID'} (R5-60). X-01 (no gate, no wait, no evidence or grant check, whoever asked): selector {all: true, detail: 'flow'} → last_check = one
 *  slot per open seat CS that has a ChangeSets.lastCheck record (an array, possibly empty), with last_code/last_params for --json; detail 'section' or a {path} selector →
 *  last_check: null; last_withheld = some entry has stage 'O10' and last_check is null. */
export function handleFlow(f: Extract<SessionClientFrame, { t: 'flow' }>, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<FlowResultFrame>;
/** Read-only; needs and consumes no evidence; renews no lease; no daemon-assembled draft text; rows = await WrapPort.rows({seatKey: peer.seatKey}) (V-7, V-16); last_check always filled as for detail 'flow' (X-01). */
export function handleWrap(f: Extract<SessionClientFrame, { t: 'wrap' }>, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<WrapResultFrame>;
/** R5-58: charges BudgetMeter.charge(seat, 'slash', f.chars) on the seat whose seat_key is f.seat_key (slashShown grows, injected unchanged); an unknown key is
 *  ignored; one-way (resolves null, no reply frame). */
export function handleSlashShown(f: Extract<SessionClientFrame, { t: 'slash_shown' }>, ctx: DaemonCtx): Promise<null>;   // the onRequest handler shape (R5-58)
export function registerFlowRequests(server: ClaudeServer, ctx: DaemonCtx): void;   // binds the `flow`, `wrap` and `slash_shown` request kinds (Task 15; one call in claudeDaemon.ts)
```

### F04 catch-up (Task 16)

```ts
// src/claude/daemon/since.ts — 06 §3.12.2 F04 paragraph, FEAT-F04-01…18, glossary §29.7, 10 §3.9a, §3.12, R5-52
export type ActivityKind = 'ups' | 'expansion' | 'human_cs';
export class HumanActivity {
  constructor(ctx: DaemonCtx, engine: { markSince(m: SinceMarks): void; foldedHeight(): number }, prefs: () => UserPrefs);
  /** Human activity only: a UserPromptSubmit with empty nonces from any seat; a /cowork expansion of any verb not wake-classified; the opening of this peer's human CS unless every
   *  path of its first delta is seat-tagged. A return = activity after ≥ 30 min of this PC's max(Δwall, Δmono) without one, or the first after daemon start: return_h := seen_h,
   *  then seen_h := folded height, one recap pending (a newer return replaces an undelivered one; a return that was itself a /cowork since expansion drops it); else seen_h := fh. */
  onActivity(kind: ActivityKind, seatId: string | null, o: { sinceExpansion: boolean }): void;
  /** The first human-origin UserPromptSubmit of any seat after a return: the recap for reply.system (UI_SINCE_RECAP_HEAD … ≤ 4 rows … UI_SINCE_RECAP_TAIL, ≤ 1,050, cut at line
   *  boundaries keeping the tail; PULL_MODE → head and tail only) and the T-SINCE line (≤ 300, PULL_MODE / since.claudePointer=false / nothing reportable / no room → none);
   *  never in a wake turn; metered in full; nothing when nothing is reportable or since.recap is false (recap) / since.claudePointer is false (line).
   *  A16-3: the recap renders in the human language L_h through 8a's render(code, params, 'systemMessage', L_h) (CHANNEL_CAPS.systemMessage = 1,050); T-SINCE renders in L_c
   *  through renderClaude (R5-11); the recap batch is delivered on printed{batch_id} like every digest (F04 §3 item 4). X-75: this is the one recap composer — Plan 7's
   *  composeSystemMessage (X7-23) is this method on the loader `system` carrier, not a second module. */
  takeRecap(seat: Seat, lang: { human: Lang; claude: ClaudeLang }): { system: string | null; pointer: CarrierItem | null } | null;
  marks(): SinceMarks;   // F04-a's SinceMarks (X-75); Plan 5 writes it through Engine.markSince and the drain's meta-only CAS
  /** FEAT-F04-18: applied only from a tty-mode CLI (tty && !ancestry, a route-(a) request) or, in slash mode, with awaitGrant('since', args_sha256, {60 s, 3 s}) true
   *  (R5-57; the one surviving read-only grant, X-02); otherwise ignored without a refusal; one-way (no reply frame, R5-58). */
  sinceRan(f: Extract<SessionClientFrame, { t: 'since_ran' }>): Promise<void>;
}
/** One running accumulator fed by every folded commit since seen_h (Engine.onFoldStep); at a return it becomes the pending recap's accumulator with from = return_h fixed;
 *  a refold or join restarts it from seen_h in the background (the recap waits for the next human-origin prompt when not ready); canonical-JSON equal to the batch report.
 *  A16-2: it runs only on F04-a's src/since/report.ts — Plan 5 writes no stand-in report; if F04-a has not landed when Task 16 starts, Task 16 stops for a ruling (R5-04). */
export class RecapAccumulator { constructor(core: { sinceInit: typeof sinceInit; sincePush: typeof sincePush; sinceFinish: typeof sinceFinish }, records: () => MyRecord[]);
  push(s: SinceStep): void; restartAt(h: number): void; snapshot(capFrom: number): SinceReport; }
/** since_query: meta.since (heights only), this peer's hold-record metadata (id, paths, state, offer; never a blob), labels only from the verified-label cache, each
 *  rendered claimLabelText(RegionsPort.verifiedLabel(claimId, anchor)) ('?' when null; Plan 4's display helper in src/claims/labels.ts, X-23 — reached through
 *  src/claude/daemon/regions.ts, the one Plan 4 importer, which re-exports it from Task 18 on and holds a structural copy with the same rule before). */
export function handleSinceQuery(ctx: DaemonCtx): SinceResultFrame;
```

```ts
function seatsGenerator(profile: GeneratorProfile, markers: MarkerModel): OpGenerator;   // PROFILES.seats = {name: 'seats', engineWeight: 0.3, steps: 60, weights}

 *  <gitdir>/cowork/log; a rerun of that command reproduces the same failing step. */
function seatsSetup(seed: string, o?: { mutants?: ReadonlySet<MutantId>; peers?: number; seatsPerPeer?: number; steps?: number; crashRate?: number; script?: readonly ScriptStep[] }):
  { tuple: SeedTuple; factory: EngineFactory; options: SimOptions; markers: MarkerModel };
class FakeSeatSim {   // in process (R5-16): runCore with in-memory io, handleTool through the real Ipc and handshake
  hook(event: HookEvent, json: Json): Promise<CoreOutcome & { stdout: string; stderr: string }>; tool(name: ToolName, args: Json): Promise<McpToolResult>;
  typeSlash(verb: string, args: string): Promise<void>;   // UserPromptExpansion, the `!` CLI call, then UserPromptSubmit
  reset(why: 'clear' | 'compact' | 'resume' | 'fork'): Promise<void>; killMcp(): void; killClaude(): void; }

//   SeatLeasePort, hookAttribution, DeferSet — bound into EngineDeps, attach, listen); edits, faults and holds keep alwaysLeased, humanOnlyAttribution and noClaude, so a
//   seatless peer never stops syncing at 120 s inside a Plan 2/3 run. A17-2: every `seats` peer calls SeatLeasePort.grantHuman(480) at its first step (oracleConverge's 60-round
//   quiescence holds; O-NO-ORPHAN is therefore vacuous in random `seats` seeds and non-vacuous only in Task 18's scripted seats-x-orphan-* seeds).
// test/fakehost/inboxStub.ts, scenarios.ts (Task 17, A17-5):
class InboxStub { constructor(o: { mode: 'accept' | 'withhold' | 'refuse' | 'refuse_burst'; deliver: (text: string) => Promise<void> }); readonly socketPath: string; close(): Promise<void>; }
const SCENARIOS: Readonly<Record<'faults' | 'churn' | 'doorbell' | 'verbs' | 'latency' | 'nonlive' | 'plugin_data', (h: FakeHost) => Promise<void>>>;
// tools/ci/spike0-gate.ts (Task 17, A17-3): maps changed paths to TST-27 areas — doorbell and inbox wiring (items c, d): src/ports/inbox.ts, src/claude/client/mcp/doorbell.ts,
//   src/claude/daemon/ring.ts; daemon spawn and lifetime (e): src/claude/client/spawn.ts, src/claude/daemon/{liveness,seatLease}.ts; human-verb evidence and slash-mode detection
//   (g, k): src/entry/hook.ts, src/claude/daemon/{evidence,acting}.ts, src/claude/client/cliClient.ts; hook latency gates (j): src/claude/daemon/gate.ts,
//   src/claude/client/hookCore.ts — and reads the item status table of docs/spikes/SPIKE-0.md (absent → every item INCOMPLETE); Task 18 wires it as a CI status check.
function spike0Gate(changed: readonly string[], reportText: string | null): { pass: true } | { fail: 'SPIKE0_INCOMPLETE'; items: string[] };
// test/sim/oraclesClaude.ts (Task 18): oracleLease, oracleSeat, oracleTurnOrigin, oracleHumanOnly, oracleNonLive, oracleToken, oracleNoOrphan, oracleRecordFirst, oracleAskOnce,
//   oracleIpc, oracleEditCheck — each recomputed from the trace (monoAt), frames captured at the Ipc port, files, env snapshots and the remote with test-side code; none calls the
//   engine or daemon function whose mutant it must catch. A18-2: oracleEditCheck pairs each edit_trigger{outcome: 'candidate', candSeq} with the next edit_check{cs} of the same

//   ClaimsModel}; claude?: true; coord?: true; lifecycle?: true} (declared by Plan 3 Task 16; each plan appends its key); Plan 5 appends claude: with it, the eleven above join
//   Plan 2's six and Plan 3's ten
// test/sim/recordingIpc.ts, test/sim/scriptedDueRequests.ts (Task 18, A18-4):
function recordingIpc(inner: Ipc): Ipc & { frames(): ReadonlyArray<{ conn: number; dir: 'c2s' | 's2c'; bytes: Buffer }>; bytesToUnauthenticated(): number };
function scriptedDueRequests(script: (seatId: string) => AskRef[]): DueRequestsPort;   // makes O-ASK-ONCE non-vacuous in `seats` runs before Plan 6
```

Waves (tasks in one wave touch disjoint files and may run in parallel; every task waits for the waves before it). Every task is gated on R5-01; Task 18 also on Plan 4 merged.

- Wave B: Task 2 ‖ Task 3
- Wave C: Task 4 ‖ Task 5
- Wave D: Task 6 ‖ Task 7
- Wave E: Task 8
- Wave F: Task 9 ‖ Task 10
- Wave G: Task 11 ‖ Task 12
- Wave H: Task 13 ‖ Task 14
- Wave I: Task 15
- Wave J: Task 16
- Wave K: Task 17
- Wave L: Task 18

Task 5 creates every `src/claude/daemon/*.ts` module of §File Structure with its exported names at their §Interfaces signatures as stubs (behaviour throws `Error('Plan 5 Task <n>')` naming the owning task, `register` adds no step), so wave-mates compile against each other; A5-5 (as extended by R5-55): a stub method that Task 5's own code paths, its pre-wired bindings or an earlier-landed module's hot path call returns the neutral value of its type instead — `TurnOrigin.onOtherHook`, `TurnOrigin.expire`, `SignalPump.send`, `Ringer.onResult`, `IdleTimer.tick`, `EventStore.printed`, `EventStore.nextHook`, `SeatHealth.onHook`, `SeatHealth.tick`, `Transitions.observe`, `EditCheckpoints.attachEngine` (void), `EventStore.queueSeatContext`, `EventStore.nonceSeen`, `Transitions.forSeat` (`[]`), `LeaseTable.openFor` (`{inflight: 0, txns: 0}`), `LeaseTable.releaseSeat` (`[]`), `SeatHealth.degradedPaths` (an empty `Map`), `ReadSets.fullReadSet` (an empty `Set`), `ReadSets.overlaps` (`false`), `ReadSets.lastApplyInto` (`null`), `ViewBlobs.get` (`null`), `ShadowRecorder.convertLeftovers` (resolves `0`), `EditCheckpoints.awaitCapture` (resolves `'late'`), `noRegions.releaseClaims` (`{drafts: [], refused: []}`, X-22's shape), `noCoordHooks` (no-op, X-40) and `neverRing.shouldRing` (`null`) — so `DeferSet.deferred()` once Task 8 binds it, Task 10's row 9 (`ViewBlobs.get`), Task 7's `codeOnlyDigest`/`routeHoldCode` (`ReadSets`) and `attach` (`convertLeftovers`) never reach a throwing stub; a task's own tests use test doubles at the §Interfaces signature for a wave-mate's module — through `seatHarness({ctx: …})` (`ClaudeDaemonDeps.ctxOverrides`, A5-12) when the test runs a harness daemon — never the stub. Task 5 also pre-wires the bindings of R5-55 (`SeatRegistry.notice`, `BudgetMeter`'s `onPullMode`, the status observer, and `attach`'s `convertLeftovers` and `attachEngine` calls), so Tasks 7, 11 and 13 edit no composition file. Every task: TDD (failing test first, then code), real git against `file://` bare remotes and the real `Ipc` port (never mocked), SIM tests deterministic by seed, `npm run ci` green before its commit, one commit per green step ending with the Co-Authored-By line.

Test notation (every task): Plan 3 Task 1 Notes apply (`T9`, `T9{2:two}`, `P1`/`P2`, `B0`…`B3`). Seat `S` is the 32-hex seat id `5ea70000000000000000000000000001`, seat `T` is `5ea70000000000000000000000000002`, `V` is `PLUGIN_VERSION` and `N1` is the nonce `cws-0123456789abcdef`; in the test values of Tasks 1–6 only, `K1`/`K2` are 64-hex seat keys (`'a'.repeat(64)`, `'b'.repeat(64)`) — everywhere else K1/K2 are the claim-breach kinds (deny-time record, PostToolUse block). Each task's "Contract additions" were folded into §Interfaces at assembly; the line left in the task names the labels (`A<task>-<n>`), each of which marks its member in §Interfaces (or, for a process rule, the header section that now states it).
