- `"WireJson and CliMode are structurally Plan 2's Json and 8a's OutputMode; the client tier's wire copies match their engine-side shapes"` — type-level mutual assignability both ways (A1-1, A1-2); Plan 2's `StatusJson` assignable to `StatusHeartbeat` and `ClientMutantId` assignable to `MutantId` (A4-14); the wire `SinceMarks` and F04-a's or `src/engine/state.ts`'s mutually assignable when that type exists (else Task 16 adds the check, X5-03); `NotifyParams` and Task 3's `SecParams` structurally 8a's `Params` (X5-45).

*Frames and the phase-keyed schema (R1-301…R1-305, R2-K5, R5-38, R5-45)* — `test/unit/claude.schema.test.ts`
- `"registeredKinds is the handshake set, the glossary §13 / 06 §3.4.3 session set and the rev 2.5 CLI requests"` — handshake `c2s`: `hello`, `cli`; handshake `s2c`: `srv`; session `c2s`: `hook`, `printed`, `hello`, `heartbeat`, `seat_end`, `ring_result`, `tool`, `acting`, `flow`, `wrap`, `since_query`, `since_ran`, `slash_shown` (R5-58); session `s2c`: `reply`, `extend`, `ring`, `tool_result`, `acting_result`, `flow_result`, `wrap_result`, `since_result`, `refused` — nothing else (R2-K5, R1-302, R1-303, R1-305, 09 §3.11 "CLI requests added in rev 2.5").
- `"validateFrame closes on an unknown t, a schema failure, a session frame in the handshake phase and a 1,048,577-byte line"` — `validateFrame('session', 'c2s', {t: 'frobnicate'})` → `null`; a `hook` frame whose `h.seat_key` is the number `5` → `null`; a valid `hook` frame validated in phase `'handshake'` → `null`; the handshake `{t: 'hello', v: 1, n1: <24-char base64>}` validated in phase `'session'` → `null` (the session `hello` is the MCP frame, R5-45, OQ-15 (r2)); `new LineReader().push(Buffer.alloc(1_048_577, 0x61))` throws `FrameTooLarge`, while 1,048,576 bytes followed by `\n` yield exactly one line (SEC-22, R2-K5).
- `"every hook payload shape of 06 §3.4.3 validates, and nothing outside it"` — one valid frame per payload row (SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit, UserPromptExpansion, PreToolUse edit, PreToolUse shell, PostToolUse with and without `read`, PostToolUseFailure, PermissionDenied, PostToolBatch, Stop, SubagentStop, StopFailure, SessionEnd, SubagentStart, Notification) passes; adding `prompt: 'x'` to the UserPromptSubmit payload, `command_args: 'save x'` to the UserPromptExpansion payload, `tool_response: {}` to PostToolUse or `transcript: 'x'` anywhere → `null`; `h.seat_key` must match `^([0-9a-f]{64}|[1-9][0-9]{0,9})$`; `payload.path` with a backslash or a leading `/` → `null` (R1-301, R1-304, A1-11, 09 test 8 frame half, CCI-09).
- `"UserPromptExpansion carries args_sha256 and nonces[] for every verb; the evidence record has exactly the 09 §2 fields"` — a `status --flow` expansion payload `{event, command_name: 'cowork', verb: 'status', command_source: 'plugin', args_sha256: <64 hex>, nonces: [], evidence: null}` passes; the same without `args_sha256` or without `nonces` → `null`; an `evidence` object with an extra `command_name` key → `null`; `evidence.mono_ns` must be a decimal string, `evidence.used` must be `false` (C11 — 09 §2 wins over 06 §3.4.1's field list; C16 — the nonce field; R5-38, SE5-02).
- `"daemon → hook frames: extend ≤ 7,000, reply decisions closed"` — `{t: 'extend', deadline_ms: 7000}` passes, `7001` and `0` → `null`; `reply` with `decision` in `deny|block|context|none` passes, `'allow'` → `null`; `reply.system` may be `null`; `reply.events` must be strings (R1-302, glossary §13 loader protocol).
- `"MCP frames: hello seat_id is 32 hex, ring nonce is cws- + 16 hex, ring text ≤ 1,500, ring_result values sent|refused|error"` — `hello.seat_id` of 31 hex → `null`; `ring.nonce` `cws-0123456789ABCDEF` (upper) → `null`; `ring.text` of 1,501 chars → `null` (a text starting with `/` passes the schema: the MCP server refuses to post it, 06 §3.16.5); `ring_result.result` `'accepted'` → `null` (glossary §25 F1 supersedes §13) (R1-303).
- `"rev 2.5 CLI requests: flow selector, wrap, since_query, since_ran, slash_shown"` — `{t: 'flow', selector: {all: true, detail: 'flow'}}` passes, and with `detail: 'section'`; the same `flow` frame with any of `mode`, `tty`, `ancestry_has_claude`, `verb` or `args_sha256` added → `null`, and `{t: 'wrap'}` likewise passes bare and fails with those keys (closed shapes; X-01: the daemon does not test who asked, 09 §3.11); `since_ran` requires `mode`, `tty`, `ancestry_has_claude`, `verb` and `args_sha256` (X-02); `{all: true}` without `detail`, `detail: 'full'` → `null` (R5-57); `wrap_result` with a `last_withheld` key or `last_check: null` → `null`; `selector: {path: 'src/a.ts'}` passes; `selector: {path: ''}`, `{path: 'a\u0000b'}` and `{all: false}` → `null`; `{t: 'slash_shown', seat_key: <64 hex>, chars: 1234}` passes, `chars: -1` or `1.5` → `null` (R5-58); `flow_result.entries` of 2,001 entries → `null`, of 2,000 → passes with `omitted: 1000`; `since_ran.to` must be a non-negative integer; `wrap_result.rows` any `WireJson`; `acting` with `verb: 'override'` → `null` (R1-305, FEAT-F14-05 cap, 09 §3.11).
- `"registerFrameKind adds a Plans 6/7 kind and refuses a duplicate"` — `registerFrameKind('c2s', 'canary_arm', v => typeof (v as any).nonce === 'string')` then `validateFrame('session', 'c2s', {t: 'canary_arm', nonce: 'x'})` passes; `registerFrameKind('c2s', 'hook', …)` throws; `registeredKinds()` lists `canary_arm` (R5-45, 09 §3.11 "others unchanged").
- `"encodeFrame is canonical and byte-equal to Plan 2's canonicalBytes"` — `test/unit/claude.ndjson.test.ts`: fast-check, 500 runs of `WireJson` values within Plan 2's depth bound: `encodeFrame(v)` equals `Buffer.concat([canonicalBytes(v), Buffer.from('\n')])`; `encodeFrame({b: 1, a: [2, {d: 3, c: 4}]})` is `{"a":[2,{"c":4,"d":3}],"b":1}\n`; `NaN` throws `TypeError`; `LineReader` reassembles a frame split over three `push` calls at arbitrary byte offsets, including inside a UTF-8 multibyte sequence of `"한"` (A1-3).

*Canonical root form (R1-203, glossary §24 default 12)* — `test/unit/claude.canonRoot.test.ts` (fake `RootProbe`) and `test/integration/claude.canonRoot.fs.test.ts` (real files under `makeTempRoot()`, whose path holds a space and non-ASCII characters)
- `"canonRoot: c:\\users\\…\\repo\\src\\a.ts, an 8.3 short name and an NFD spelling equal C:/Users/…/repo; a not-yet-existing file resolves through its nearest ancestor"` — fake probe on `win32` whose `realpathNative` maps `c:\users\me\repo\src\a.ts` and `C:\USERS\ME~1\repo\src\a.ts` to `C:\Users\Me\repo\src\a.ts`: both give `c:/Users/Me/repo/src/a.ts` (lower-case drive letter, `/` separators, on-disk case); `C:\Users\Me\repo\src\new\b.ts` with `src\new` absent resolves `src` and appends → `c:/Users/Me/repo/src/new/b.ts`; on `darwin` the NFD `/Users/me/re\u0301po` gives the NFC `/Users/me/répo`; `sameRoot('c:/Users/Me/repo', 'C:/users/me/REPO', 'win32')` true, on `'linux'` false (R1-203, 06 test 1 unit, glossary §24 default 12).
- `"endpointRoot lower-cases on win32 and darwin only"` — common gitdir `C:\Users\Me\repo\.git` → `c:/users/me/repo/.git` on `win32`; `/Users/Me/repo/.git` → `/users/me/repo/.git` on `darwin`; `/home/Me/repo/.git` unchanged on `linux` (locale-independent: `ı`/`İ` are lowered with `toLowerCase()` of the `en` root locale, never `toLocaleLowerCase`) (SEC-21, glossary §24 default 12).
- `"findRepo walks to the first .git and parses gitdir: and commondir without git"` — a `.git` directory → `{kind: 'dir', root, gitdir}`; a `.git` file `gitdir: ../.git/worktrees/wt1` beside a `commondir` file `../..` → `{kind: 'file', commonGitdir: '<root>/.git', submodule: false}`; `gitdir: ../../.git/modules/sub` → `submodule: true`; a relative `gitdir:` is resolved against the `.git` file's directory; no `.git` up to the filesystem root → `null`; a `.git` file whose target is missing → `{kind: 'file', commonGitdir: null}` (R1-204 parse half, 06 §3.4.1 step 3).
- `"liveState compares canonical forms with the DAT-34 case rule"` — `live` holding `C:/Users/Me/repo\n` and root `c:/users/me/repo` → `'live'` on `win32` and `darwin`, `'nonlive'` on `linux`; `live` absent → `'nonlive'`; `live` naming another worktree → `'nonlive'`; `live` plus `paused` → `'paused'` (R1-205).
- `"the real probe: an 8.3 name, an NFD spelling and a junction or symlink reach one canonical root"` — integration with `realRootProbe`: on `win32` the short name of the temp root (obtained with `cmd /c for %I in ("<dir>") do @echo %~sI`; skipped with a recorded reason when 8.3 generation is off on the volume), a junction (`mklink /J`) and a different letter case all give the same `canonRoot`; on `darwin` an NFD spelling; on `linux` a symlinked parent; every case gives one `endpointRoot` (TC-CCI-16 root half, 09 test 20 spelling half, R1-203).

*Hook input (R1-301, R1-304, F05-01, C11, C16)* — `test/unit/claude.hookInput.test.ts`
- `"buildPayload never carries prompt text, command_args, tool_response content or transcript content; UserPromptSubmit carries only nonces[]"` — every fixture of `test/fixtures/hooks/` with the canary `COWORK_CANARY_P5` planted in `prompt`, `command_args`, `tool_response.stdout`, `tool_response.content`, the last assistant message and every unlisted host field: `JSON.stringify(buildPayload(…))` contains 0 canary occurrences; the UserPromptSubmit payload is exactly `{event: 'UserPromptSubmit', nonces: ['cws-0123456789abcdef']}` for the prompt `please COWORK_CANARY_P5 cws-0123456789abcdef`; SessionStart keeps `transcript_path` (daemon memory only) and no transcript content (R1-301, R1-304, CCI-09, SEC-26).
- `"nonces dedupes in order and matches only cws- plus 16 lowercase hex"` — `'x cws-0123456789abcdef y cws-0123456789abcdef cws-fedcba9876543210 cws-0123456789ABCDEF'` → `['cws-0123456789abcdef', 'cws-fedcba9876543210']` (glossary §25 F2).
- `"normArgs normalizes the shell words of command_args and of argv to one form"` — `'save   "x"  '` → `{verb: 'save', argsNorm: '"x"', argsSha256: sha256hex('"x"')}`; `'  status   --flow '` → `{verb: 'status', argsNorm: '"--flow"'}`; `'done'` → `{argsNorm: '', argsSha256: 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855'}`; `'resolve\t01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV\n--publish'` → `argsNorm` `'"01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV" "--publish"'`; `'save   "fix  the bug"   --sign '` and the array `['save', 'fix  the bug', '--sign']` both → `argsNorm` `'"fix the bug" "--sign"'` and the same `argsSha256`; `"save 'a b'"` and `['save', 'a b']` agree; for 300 fast-check argument lists, the text produced by POSIX-quoting each word and the list itself give one `argsSha256` (09 §2, SEC-25 step 1, X-60: the quoted-argument mismatch SE5-15 flagged is closed).
- `"evidenceFor mints only for a slash_command cowork expansion of an acting verb without a nonce"` — `{expansion_type: 'slash_command', command_name: 'cowork', command_args: 'save "x"', prompt: '/cowork save "x"', prompt_id: 'p1'}` → `{seat_key: K1, prompt_id: 'p1', verb: 'save', args_sha256: sha256hex('"x"'), mono_ns: '123456789', used: false}`; `command_name: 'cowork-sync:cowork'` → minted; `command_name: 'other'`, `expansion_type: 'prompt'`, verb `status`, and a prompt containing `cws-0123456789abcdef` → `null`; `evidenceTag(Buffer.alloc(32, 1), r)` equals `hmacSha256Hex(key, 'evidence' + canonicalJsonLine(r))` (R5-38, C11, 09 §3.13 step 1–2, A1-8).
- `"ReadInfo: full, ranged, truncated and ENOENT reads"` — PostToolUse(Read) `tool_input {file_path, offset: 100, limit: 50}` → `read: {offset: 100, limit: 50, full: false, enoent: false, size: null}` (raw offset, R5-50); no `offset`/`limit` → `full: true`; `tool_response.truncated: true` → `full: false`; `tool_response.enoent: true` → `enoent: true`; `tool_response.file.numLines: 1234` → `size: 1234`; a non-Read PostToolUse → `read: null` (F05-01, CCI-21 "full Read", A1-8 `HOST_FIELDS`).
- `"PostToolUse exit_status, Stop and Notification fields come through HOST_FIELDS"` — `tool_response.exit_code: 0` → `exit_status: 0`; `exitCode: 3` → `3`; absent → `null`; PostToolUse of `Bash` carries `command`, of `Edit` `command: null`; Stop with `last_assistant_message: 'done (see 01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV and src/a.ts) COWORK_CANARY_P5'` → `ids_in_last_message: ['01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV', 'src/a.ts']`, absent → `null`; `background_tasks_running: 2` kept, absent → `0`; Notification `notification_type: 'permission_prompt'` → `type: 'permission_prompt'` (06 §3.4.3 rows, 06 §8 Q6/Q7 defaults, A1-8).
- `"edit-class paths are repo-relative POSIX NFC in the target's on-disk spelling"` — `extra.target` `c:/Users/Me/repo/src/Á.ts` (NFD `A\u0301`) with `repo.root` `c:/users/me/repo` on `win32` → `path: 'src/Á.ts'` (NFC); a target outside the root → `path: null`; `tool_input` edit fields (`old_string`, `new_string`, `replace_all`, `content`, `edits[]`, `notebook.{cell_id, new_source, cell_type, edit_mode}`) are copied and every other `tool_input` key is dropped (R1-301, A1-8).
- `"seatKeyOf hashes the socket name, never the token"` — `seatKeyOf({CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET: '\\\\.\\pipe\\claude-x'}, 4242)` = sha256hex of that string; an empty socket value or none → `'4242'`; a `Proxy` over the env object records every key the function reads: exactly `['CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET']` (it never touches the token's name; L-TOKEN keeps the name out of this test file too) (R1-225 unit, glossary §2, CCI-49).
- `"tokenize splits chains, strips cd and env prefixes, honours git -C and normalizes PowerShell aliases"` — `test/unit/claude.shellTokens.test.ts`: `cd sub && FOO=1 git -C . commit -m x; rm a` (Bash) → two segments `{argv: ['git', 'commit', '-m', 'x'], cwd: 'sub', gitC: '.'}`, `{argv: ['rm', 'a']}`; `echo x > a.txt | sort >> b.txt` → redirects `[{op: '>', target: 'a.txt'}]` and `[{op: '>>', target: 'b.txt'}]`; PowerShell `sc .\a.txt 'x'; ri .git\cowork\live` → argv `['Set-Content', '.\\a.txt', 'x']`, `['Remove-Item', '.git\\cowork\\live']`; quotes and escaped spaces kept as one token; newline-separated commands split (CCI-28 tokenizer half; `classify` and `writesFiles` are Task 10's).

*Plugin data, user.json, text (R5-11, R5-12, R5-47, OQ-10 (r2), OQ-23 (r3))*
- `"resolveDataDir: the variable when set and absolute, else the derived plugin id directory, never another location"` — `test/unit/claude.pluginData.test.ts` over `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts` (X-61; the calls below pass `pluginRoot` explicitly or set `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`): `{CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA: '/d'}` → `/d`; unset with `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` `/home/me/.claude/plugins/cache/test-market/cowork-sync/0.6.0` and home `/home/me` → `/home/me/.claude/plugins/data/cowork-sync-test-market`; the Windows form `C:\Users\Me\.claude\plugins\cache\test.market\cowork-sync\0.6.0` → `C:\Users\Me\.claude\plugins\data\cowork-sync-test-market`; `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/cfg` (absolute) → `/cfg/plugins/data/cowork-sync-test-market`, a relative `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` is ignored; a relative `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` (`d`) is ignored and the derived directory is used; a plugin root of any other shape → `resolveDataDir` returns `null` (fail closed, SEC-20); `pluginDataDir(env, home, platform, root)` equals `resolveDataDir`'s derived answer whenever `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` is unset and the root has the cache shape; `src/viz/cli/dataDir.ts`'s `pluginDataDir` and `resolveDataDir` are the same function objects as `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts`'s (re-export identity, X-61); the module imports only `node:*` (OQ-10 (r2), R5-47, R5-56, A1-12, 06 §8 Q10, 09 §8 Q2, SE5-21, X-61).
- `"readUserJson: missing, bad JSON and mistyped keys give the defaults"` — `null` and `'{bad'` → `{language: null, wakeTurnEdits: 'allow', wakeEnabled: true, wakeMaxPerSeatPerHour: 4, notificationsOs: true, sinceRecap: true, sinceClaudePointer: true, envPassthrough: []}` (X-52); `{"language":"ko","wake":{"turnEdits":"deny"},"since":{"recap":false,"claudePointer":false},"env":{"passthrough":["HTTPS_PROXY","bad name"]}}` → `{language: 'ko', wakeTurnEdits: 'deny', sinceRecap: false, sinceClaudePointer: false, envPassthrough: ['HTTPS_PROXY']}`; the dotted form `{"since.recap": false}` → `sinceRecap: false`; mistyped `{"language": 5, "wake": {"turnEdits": "maybe"}, "since": {"recap": "no"}}` → each key its default (R5-12, OQ-23 (r3), F04-12, A1-10).
- `"loaderLang follows user.json, then the OS locale"` — `('ko', null)` → `'ko'`; `(null, 'ko-KR')` → `'ko'`; `(null, 'ko_KR.UTF-8')` → `'ko'`; `('fr', 'ko-KR')` → `'en'`; `(null, 'de-DE')` → `'en'`; `(null, null)` → `'en'` (glossary §17 loader precedence, A1-10, X-53).
- `"resolveHumanLang is user → team → OS → en and claudeLang maps L_h to en or ko"` — `test/unit/claude.locale.test.ts`: `resolveHumanLang({user: null, team: 'ko', osLocale: 'en-US'}).lang` → `'ko'` (the X-53 divergence case: `loaderLang(null, 'en-US')` → `'en'` for the same machine); `{user: 'en', team: 'ko', …}` → `'en'`; `{user: 'xx', team: null, osLocale: 'ko-KR'}` → `{lang: 'ko', invalid: ['user']}` (a value outside the six counts as absent and is named, I18N-02; V-4); `{user: 'xx', team: 'yy', osLocale: null}` → `{lang: 'en', invalid: ['user', 'team']}`; all null → `{lang: 'en', invalid: []}`; `claudeLang('ko')` → `'ko'`, `claudeLang('en')` → `'en'`, `claudeLang` of every other of the six languages → `'en'` (glossary §17, X-53).
- `"readUserJson reads the wake and notification keys"` — `{"wake":{"enabled":false,"maxPerSeatPerHour":2},"notifications":{"os":false}}` → `wakeEnabled: false`, `wakeMaxPerSeatPerHour: 2`, `notificationsOs: false`; absent → `true`, `4`, `true`; `{"language":"xx"}` → `language: null` (glossary §17 defaults, X-52, I18N-02).
- `"renderClaude renders G4/PAUSED/T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL/T-SINCE equal to 07 in en and ko, and any other id as the code line"` — `test/unit/claude.text.test.ts`: the test reads `docs/spec/07-coordination.md`, extracts the `- en:` and `- ko:` lines of the blocks `**G4**`, `**PAUSED**`, `**T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL**`, `**T-SINCE**`, `**T-SINCE.touch**` and asserts the catalog texts equal them; `renderClaude('G4', {}, 'en')` equals glossary §11's G4 sentence read from `docs/spec/00-glossary.md`; `renderClaude('T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL', {v: '0.6.0'}, 'ko')` equals the ko row with `0.6.0`; `renderClaude('T-SINCE', {from: 1234, s: 2, c: 3, k: 1, touch: ''}, 'en')` = `cowork-sync: since this user's last activity on this PC (#1234): 2 saves and 3 shared change-sets by 1 teammates. The user can see the summary with /cowork since.`; `renderClaude('VERSION_RESTART', {v: '0.6.1'}, 'en')` = `[cowork-sync] VERSION_RESTART v=0.6.1`; `renderClaude('HELDREG', {path: 'a\u202Eb.ts', rid: 'R1a2b3c4d'}, 'en', 'deny')` = `[cowork-sync] HELDREG path=ab.ts rid=R1a2b3c4d` (bidi stripped by `sanitize(…, 'deny')`); `claudeTemplateOf('DAEMON_DOWN')` = `'G4'`; `renderClaude` and `registerClaudeCatalog` are exported by `src/i18n/claude.ts` and by no other module (X-54) (R5-11, R1-211 text half, CCI-45, A1-4, A1-13).
- `"registerClaudeCatalog: identical re-registration is a no-op, a different entry throws"` — registering the seed `G4` again is a no-op; `{G4: {text: 'x'}}` throws; a new id `T_P5_TEST` renders its text afterwards (R5-11; Plan 6 extends the same module in place through this call, X-54).
- `"capLines cuts at line boundaries and keeps the tail when asked"` — `capLines('HEAD\nr1\nr2\nTAIL', 12, true)` = `'HEAD\nr1\nTAIL'`; with 11 → `'HEAD\nTAIL'`; `capLines('a\nbb\nccc', 6, false)` = `'a\nbb'`; `capLines('x'.repeat(20), 10, false)` = `'x'.repeat(9) + '…'` (only the first line is ever cut inside) (F04 recap shape, 06 §3.14.1).
- `"LOADER_TEXTS are the catalog rows G4, PAUSED and T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL in en and ko"` — `LOADER_TEXTS.G4.en` equals glossary §11's text verbatim with no cause slot; `LOADER_TEXTS.HOOK_LOAD_FAIL.ko` keeps the literal `{v}` for the loader to fill; `LOADER_TEXTS.PAUSED.en` = `cowork-sync is paused for this repo by the user; edits are not coordinated.` (R1-211, R5-40, C12, C13, Q-12).

*Ports, trace, engine deps (R2-L1, R2-L4, R5-02)*
- `"Notifier: recordingNotifier() records and counts per class; logNotifier writes one warn line per call"` — `test/unit/notifier.test.ts`: three `raise` calls `NT_SECURITY`, `NT_SECURITY` (with `key: 'r1'`), `NT_NOHOOKS` → `calls` in order (the second with `key: 'r1'`, the others `key: null`) and `countOf('NT_SECURITY') === 2`; `logNotifier(log).raise('NT_SECURITY', 'SEC_IPC_HANDSHAKE_FAILED', {count: 5})` → `log.write('warn', 'SEC_IPC_HANDSHAKE_FAILED', {count: 5, cls: 'NT_SECURITY'})` once; type-level: `Notifier` has exactly the member `raise` (no `notify`), and no `RecordingNotifier` class is exported (R2-L1, 11 §3.2 `Notifier` row, A1-5, X-67).
- `"TraceEvent declares every Plan 5 variant with no duration and no wall time"` — `test/unit/trace.p5.test.ts`: a type-checked test builds one event of each kind `hook`, `lease`, `seat`, `turn`, `ring`, `evidence`, `acting`, `deny_record`, `edit_trigger`, `ipc`, `carrier`; `TraceRecorder.ofKind` returns each; no variant has a key matching `/ms$|Ms$|wall|duration|time/i` (TST-03); `TraceHookEvent` and `HookEvent` are mutually assignable (A1-5).
- `"MutantId widens and EngineDeps.claude defaults to noClaude everywhere"` — `test/unit/engine.deps.p5.test.ts`: `MutantId` accepts `M08`, `M26`, `M27`, `M37`, `M40`, `M41`, `M44`, `M45`, `M47` beside Plan 2's and Plan 3's members; `noClaude.txnOpen()` false, `deferred()` an empty map, `recheck(['a'])` an empty map, `fenced()` an empty set, `applied(['a'])` returns `undefined` and changes nothing; the Plan 2 and Plan 3 suites (`npm test`) pass unchanged with `claude: noClaude` in every fixture (R2-L4, R5-02, X5-01 declaration half).

*Event loop baseline (R5-32, OQ-18 (r3), BL2-21)* — `test/integration/eventloop.baseline.test.ts`

- `realRootProbe` is the only synchronous filesystem reader outside the loader; core, the MCP server and the daemon call `canonRoot(…, realRootProbe, process.platform)`; every other read goes through the async `Fs` port.

- Test: `test/unit/sec.winAcl.test.ts`, `test/unit/sec.ipcKey.test.ts`, `test/unit/sec.endpoint.test.ts`, `test/unit/sec.handshake.test.ts`, `test/unit/sec.securityLog.test.ts`, `test/integration/ipc.real.test.ts`, `test/integration/ipc.win32.test.ts` (windows-latest only), `test/integration/ipc.posix.test.ts` (ubuntu, macos only)

- Consumes: Task 1 `C` (`HANDSHAKE_NONCE_BYTES`, `IPC_KEY_BYTES`, `FRAME_MAX_BYTES`, `HANDSHAKE_FAIL_NOTIFY_AT`, `HANDSHAKE_FAIL_WINDOW_MS`, `SECURITY_NOTIFY_SPACING_MS`, `SOCKET_PATH_MAX_BYTES` — equality tests only, see Notes), `validateFrame`, `registeredKinds`, `LineReader`, `encodeFrame`, `canonRoot`, `endpointRoot`, `realRootProbe`, `resolveDataDir` (X-61), `Notifier`, `recordingNotifier()`, `TraceEvent` (`ipc`); `src/sec/sddl.ts` `parseSddl`, `sddlUserOnly` (X-62); Plan 1 `Fs`, `FsLstat`, `realFs`, `FaultFs`, `Random`, `seededRandom`, `realRandom`, `Clock`, `VirtualClock`, `makeTempRoot`; Plan 2 `Log`, `fileLog`.
- Produces: `IpcConn`, `IpcServer`, `Ipc`, `realIpc`; `KeyDeps`, `KeyCheck`, `readIpcKey`, `createIpcKey`, `keyHash`; `WinAcl`, `createWinAcl`, `realWinAcl`; `endpointName`, `socketDir`; `SecureConn`, `HandshakeFailed`, `clientHandshake`, `serverHandshake`; `SecParams`, `SecurityLog`, `securityLog`, `HandshakeFailureRate`; and A3-1…A3-7 (A3-7: `SecParams`, the local params type of `SecurityLog.event`; with A3-6's local stamp encoding, `src/sec` imports neither `src/claude/wire` nor `src/i18n`).

**Spec:** 09 §3.11 (key, endpoint name, handshake, squatter check, capability limits), §3.19 rows `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL`, `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER`, `SEC_REFUSE_SOCKET_PATH`, `SEC_IPC_HANDSHAKE_FAILED`; SEC-20…SEC-23, INV-IPC; 09 tests 20, 21, 30; 06 §3.4.2 step 2; glossary §6, §20 ("OS notifications ≤ 1 per 30 min per reason class"), §24 default 12; 11 §3.2 `Ipc` row ("the same, real" in SIM), §3.4 O-IPC; TC-CCI-16, TC-SEC-03; ENG-03 (`log/security.jsonl`); rulings R5-45, R5-46, R5-48.

**Tests:**

*Key and ACL (SEC-20, R2-K1, 09 tests 21, 30)*
- `"the key's SDDL verdict is src/sec/sddl.ts's user-only rule"` — `test/unit/sec.winAcl.test.ts` (8a's own `parseSddl`/`sddlUserOnly` tests move with the module, X-62; this test pins only Plan 5's use): `sddlUserOnly(parseSddl('O:S-1-5-21-1111-2222-3333-1001G:S-1-5-21-1111-2222-3333-513D:PAI(A;;FA;;;S-1-5-21-1111-2222-3333-1001)(A;;FA;;;SY)(A;;FA;;;BA)')!, 'S-1-5-21-1111-2222-3333-1001')` → true; adding `(A;OICI;FR;;;WD)` → false; an unparseable SDDL (`parseSddl` → null) → `readIpcKey` refuses `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL` (fail closed); `src/sec/winAcl.ts` exports no SDDL parser (`sddlAllowSids`, `ALLOWED_SYSTEM_SIDS` are absent) and `src/viz/cli/dataDir.ts`'s `parseSddl` is the same function object as `src/sec/sddl.ts`'s (SEC-20, X-62).
- `"createWinAcl runs exactly the three fixed argv and parses a localized whoami"` — a recording `run`: `currentUserSid()` runs `('whoami', ['/user', '/fo', 'csv', '/nh'])` and parses `"desktop-x\홍길동","S-1-5-21-1111-2222-3333-1001"` to the SID; `restrictToUser('C:\\d\\ipc.key', sid)` runs `('icacls', ['C:\\d\\ipc.key', '/inheritance:r', '/grant:r', '*S-1-5-21-1111-2222-3333-1001:F'])`; `readSddl("C:\\d\\it's'; Remove-Item x\\ipc.key")` runs `('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', '(Get-Acl -LiteralPath $env:COWORK_ACL_PATH).Sddl'])` with `env.COWORK_ACL_PATH` equal to that path, which appears in no argv element; a non-zero exit code rejects with `WinAclError` carrying the code only (R5-48, OQ-14 (r2), A3-4).
- `"ipc.key with mode 0644 or an Everyone ACE refuses SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL; a ko-KR Windows account passes the SID check"` — `test/unit/sec.ipcKey.test.ts` with a fake `Fs` and fake `WinAcl`: POSIX key `mode 0o100644` → `{ok: false, code: 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL'}`; `0o100600` with `uid` ≠ `d.uid` → refuse; `0o100600` in a parent of mode `0o40777` → refuse; `0o100600`, own uid, parent `0o40700`, 32 bytes → `{ok: true, key}`; a symlink at the key path → refuse; a 31-byte file → `{ok: false, code: null}`; no file → `{ok: false, code: null}`; win32 SDDL of the ko-KR fixture with `SY` and `BA` → ok; plus `(A;;FR;;;WD)` → refuse (09 test 21, SEC-20, R2-K1).
- `"createIpcKey writes 32 random bytes user-only and verifies them; an existing key is read, never replaced"` — POSIX: `writeFileExclusive(<dir>/ipc.key, 32 bytes from seededRandom('k'), 0o600)` after `mkdirp(<dir>, 0o700)`, then `readIpcKey` → ok with those bytes; win32: `restrictToUser` then `readSddl` called once each; a second `createIpcKey` on the same directory returns the existing key and writes nothing (`FaultFs.calls` has no second write); an existing key whose check refuses throws `IpcKeyRefused` with `code: 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL'` (SEC-20 "created on first start"; X5-28 caller is Plan 7).
- `"a process without CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA reads the key only from the derived directory"` — `readIpcKey(resolveDataDir({CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT: <plugin root of the A1-12 shape>}, home, platform)!, …)` succeeds for a key created there; `FaultFs.calls` lists `lstat`/`readFile` only of `<home>/.claude/plugins/data/cowork-sync-test-market/ipc.key` (and its parent); with a broad ACL there it returns the refusal and no other path is ever read (09 test 30 key half, SEC-20 "MUST NOT fall back", OQ-10 (r2)).
- `"the Windows hook stamp: a matching stamp skips the spawn, a changed key or a failed check deletes it"` — fake `WinAcl` counting calls: a full check with `stamp: 'write'` writes `ipc.key.acl`; `readIpcKey(…, {stamp: 'use'})` then returns ok with 0 `WinAcl` calls; after the key file is rewritten (new `ino`/`mtimeNs`) the next `use` runs the full check (2 calls) and rewrites the stamp; a full check that refuses deletes the stamp, and the next `use` runs the full check again; on POSIX no stamp file is ever written; the stamp's bytes equal `canonicalJsonLine` of the same five-field object (a test-side parity check over 200 random stat tuples: `src/sec` encodes locally and imports nothing from `src/claude/wire`, X5-45) (A3-6).
- `"keyHash is the hex SHA-256 of the key bytes"` — `keyHash(Buffer.alloc(32, 7))` equals `createHash('sha256').update(Buffer.alloc(32, 7)).digest('hex')` (SEC-23 "exists with the same hash"; Task 9 consumes).
- `"real key ACLs on Windows"` — `test/integration/ipc.win32.test.ts`: `createIpcKey` under `makeTempRoot()` with `realWinAcl` → `readIpcKey` ok; the test then runs `icacls <key> /grant *S-1-1-0:R` (test code) → `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL`; the SDDL comparison passes on a runner whose account name is non-ASCII when one is configured (recorded as skipped otherwise) (09 test 21 real half).

*Endpoint and socket directory (SEC-21, R2-K2, 09 test 20, TC-CCI-16)*
- `"two spellings of one repo (drive-letter case, 8.3, NFD) derive one endpoint"` — `test/unit/sec.endpoint.test.ts`: `endpointName(key, endpointRoot('C:\\Users\\Me\\repo\\.git', probe, 'win32'), 'win32', null)` equals the same for `c:/users/me/repo/.git` and for the 8.3 spelling the fake probe resolves; the value is `\\.\pipe\cowork-` + the first 32 hex chars of `HMAC-SHA256(key, "endpoint:" ‖ root)`; on POSIX `<dir>/` + 24 hex + `.sock`; a different key gives a different name (SEC-21, 09 test 20 spelling half, TC-CCI-16 endpoint half).
- `"POSIX socket dir: 0700 owner-only accepted; a symlink swap, a wrong owner and a 101-byte path refuse SEC_REFUSE_SOCKET_PATH"` — fake `Fs`: `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/run/user/1000` → dir `/run/user/1000/cowork-sync` created with mode `0o700` and accepted when `lstat` shows a directory, `uid` 1000, mode `0o40700`; the same path as a symlink → refuse; `uid` 0 → refuse; mode `0o40755` → refuse; an XDG value making the full socket path exactly 100 bytes → accepted, 101 bytes → skipped and `/tmp/cws-1000` chosen; a 95-byte `sockName` (both candidates > 100) → `{ok: false, code: 'SEC_REFUSE_SOCKET_PATH'}`; no XDG → `/tmp/cws-1000` (SEC-21, 09 test 20 POSIX half).
- `"real sockets: one bind per endpoint, a stale socket is removed only when nothing listens"` — `test/integration/ipc.posix.test.ts`: `realIpc.listen(ep)` twice → the second rejects with `code === 'EADDRINUSE'` and the first keeps serving; a child process that bound `ep` and was killed with SIGKILL leaves the socket file: `removeStale(ep)` → `true`, then `listen` succeeds; with a live listener `removeStale(ep)` → `false` and the file stays; a symlink placed at `ep` → `false` (A3-5, SEC-21 "binding it enforces one daemon per clone").
- `"named pipes: one bind per endpoint; a read-only client and a \\\\127.0.0.1\\pipe\\ client receive 0 session bytes"` — `test/integration/ipc.win32.test.ts`: a second `listen` on the same pipe → `EADDRINUSE`; a client that opens the pipe with read-only access and a client connecting through `\\127.0.0.1\pipe\cowork-…` without the key: `ipcCapture` records 0 bytes written to them until they send `hello`, and 0 session bytes ever (the second receives at most the `srv` line after its own `hello`); `removeStale` → `false` (09 test 20 Windows half, TC-SEC-03, INV-IPC).

*Handshake and framing (SEC-22, R2-K3, R5-45, OQ-15 (r2), INV-IPC, O-IPC)*
- `"an unauthenticated client receives 0 bytes and a client sends 0 session bytes to an unauthenticated server"` — `test/unit/sec.handshake.test.ts` over an in-memory `IpcConn` pair with `ipcCapture`: (1) a client that connects and sends nothing → the server writes 0 bytes and, after `timeoutMs` 3,000 on a `VirtualClock`, closes with `onFail('timeout')`; (2) a client that sends `hello` and then a `cli` with a wrong MAC → the server wrote exactly one `srv` line, then closes at once with `onFail('mac')`, `onEvent` reports `('auth_fail', 0)` — `bytesToUnauthenticated` = 0 session bytes; (3) a fake server answering `hello` with a `srv` whose `p` is wrong → `clientHandshake` rejects `HandshakeFailed` (`code` `'SEC_IPC_HANDSHAKE_FAILED'`) and the client wrote only its `hello` line (no `cli`, no session frame) (INV-IPC, O-IPC input, R2-K3).
- `"a matching handshake yields SecureConns that exchange schema-valid frames both ways"` — with key `Buffer.alloc(32, 9)`, endpoint `\\.\pipe\cowork-test`, `seededRandom('h')`: `srv.p` equals `HMAC(key, "srv" ‖ n1 ‖ n2 ‖ endpoint)` (raw nonce bytes, UTF-8 endpoint) and `cli.p` equals `HMAC(key, "cli" ‖ n2 ‖ n1 ‖ endpoint)`; the comparisons go through `timingSafeEqual` (a spy on `node:crypto` records the calls; a MAC of the wrong length is rejected before the call); then a `hook` frame c→s and a `reply` frame s→c arrive intact via `onFrame`; `onEvent` reports `accept` then `auth_ok` (SEC-22, R1-222).
- `"a wrong MAC closes at once with SEC_IPC_HANDSHAKE_FAILED; 5 failures in 60 s give one NT_SECURITY notification, the 6th within 30 min none"` — `HandshakeFailureRate` with `VirtualClock`, `recordingNotifier()` and a recording `SecurityLog`: `fail('mac')` at t = 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 s → 5 security events `SEC_IPC_HANDSHAKE_FAILED` and `countOf('NT_SECURITY') === 1` after the 5th; a 6th at 5 s → 6 events, still 1 notification; 5 more at 31 min → a 2nd notification; 4 failures spread 20 s apart (never 5 within 60 s) → 0 notifications (SEC-22, glossary §20 OS notification row, 09 §6 "Repeated bad handshakes").
- `"frames over 1 MiB and unknown t close the connection"` — after a successful handshake: a session line of 1,048,577 bytes → `close` and `onClose` fire, the oversized line is never delivered; `{t: 'frobnicate'}` → close; a schema-invalid `hook` (missing `h`) → close; a handshake `{t: 'hello', v: 1, n1: …}` sent again after authentication → close (it is a session frame of the wrong shape in the session phase, R5-45, OQ-15 (r2)); a session `hook` frame sent before `cli` → the server closes with `onFail('shape')` and never delivers it (SEC-22, R2-K5 enforcement half).
- `"the handshake survives fragmentation and rejects a v2 hello"` — every handshake and session line delivered one byte per `onData` callback still authenticates and delivers frames; `{t: 'hello', v: 2, n1}` → `onFail('shape')`; a nonce that decodes to 15 bytes → `onFail('shape')` (SEC-22).
- `"the session channel carries no key, nonce or environment value"` — after authentication `SecureConn` exposes only `send`, `onFrame`, `onClose`, `close` (`Object.keys` and prototype names); `HandshakeFailed.message` is the code only; a fast-check run of 200 random handshakes finds neither the key bytes (raw, hex or base64) nor any `process.env` value in the captured byte stream other than the base64 nonces and MACs; `src/sec/{handshake,endpoint,ipcKey}.ts` and `src/ports/ipc.ts` read no `process.env` (the env reaches `socketDir` as a parameter) and load no `child_process` (source scan) (SEC-23 channel half, R2-K4; the handler half — no supplied command, no path outside the main-worktree realpath, no key/token/env in a reply — is pinned by Task 5's `ClaudeServer` tests).
- `"real IPC: realIpc carries an authenticated session on this OS"` — `test/integration/ipc.real.test.ts`: `realIpc.listen` on the endpoint derived from a temp repo and a temp key, `serverHandshake` on accept, `clientHandshake` from `realIpc.connect(ep, 1_000)`; 100 round trips of a 10 KiB frame; `connect` to an endpoint nobody listens on rejects within 1,000 ms; closing the server makes the client's `onClose` fire (R1-222, 11 §3.2 `Ipc` "the same, real").

*Security log (BL2-19, OQ-25 (r3), R5-46)* — `test/unit/sec.securityLog.test.ts`
- `"security.jsonl carries codes only and mirrors to the daemon log"` — `securityLog(fs, clock, '<cowork>', daemonLog).event('SEC_EVIDENCE_TAG_INVALID', {seat_key_prefix: 'ab12cd34'})` → one line in `<cowork>/log/security.jsonl` whose keys are exactly `t`, `level` (`'security'`), `code`, `params`, and one `daemonLog.write('security', 'SEC_EVIDENCE_TAG_INVALID', {seat_key_prefix: 'ab12cd34'})`; a param value that is an object or array → `TypeError`, nothing written; 50 concurrent events → 50 whole lines in call order (Plan 2 `fileLog`'s queue); `SecParams` is a local type (no `src/i18n` import in `src/sec/**`, source scan) and is structurally 8a's `Params` (type-level test, X5-45) (BL2-19, OQ-25 (r3), R5-46, ENG-03, SEC-38 log rule, A3-7).

*Constants parity*
- `"the src/sec IPC numbers equal C"` — `NONCE_BYTES === C.HANDSHAKE_NONCE_BYTES` (16), `KEY_BYTES === C.IPC_KEY_BYTES` (32), `FAIL_NOTIFY_AT === C.HANDSHAKE_FAIL_NOTIFY_AT` (5), `FAIL_WINDOW_MS === C.HANDSHAKE_FAIL_WINDOW_MS` (60,000), `NOTIFY_SPACING_MS === C.SECURITY_NOTIFY_SPACING_MS` (1,800,000), `SOCKET_PATH_MAX_BYTES === C.SOCKET_PATH_MAX_BYTES` (100), `MAX_FRAME_BYTES === C.FRAME_MAX_BYTES` (Notes).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-PORTS and L-NET allow `src/sec/winAcl.ts` and `src/ports/ipc.ts` (Task 1, A1-9); L-GIT-SPAWN still applies inside `winAcl.ts`; L-RANDOM holds (nonces and keys come from the `Random` port).

**Notes:**
- `src/sec/*` declares its own `NONCE_BYTES`, `KEY_BYTES`, `FAIL_NOTIFY_AT`, `FAIL_WINDOW_MS`, `NOTIFY_SPACING_MS`, `SOCKET_PATH_MAX_BYTES`, `MAX_FRAME_BYTES` (it may not import `src/claude/wire`); the parity test keeps them equal to `C`.
- The squatter check, the single-instance bind and the daemon-side byte accounting into the `ipc` trace event are Task 5's (`ClaudeServer.listen`, `onEvent` → `TraceEvent`); the retry every 50 ms until the loader deadline is Task 4's (`openSession`).
- POSIX `socketDir` creates only the final directory component (`mkdirp(dir, 0o700)` of `/tmp/cws-<uid>` or `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/cowork-sync`); it never changes the mode of an existing directory, it refuses it.

### Task 4: Hook loader and core

**Covers:** R1-201…R1-211, R1-221, R1-223, R1-224, R1-225, R1-311, R1-321, R1-407 (loader side), R1-712, R2-A2, R2-A3, R2-I2 (spawn routine, runtime copy), R2-J1 (loader half), R1-104, R1-111, R2-B12 (client read), C13, C15, Q-12; CCI-06…CCI-11, CCI-29, CCI-51, CCI-54 (loader), INV-CCI-FAILCLOSED, INV-CCI-NONLIVE, INV-CCI-NODECISION; 06 tests 1, 2, 3 (unit), 4, 5, 15 (loader), 23; TC-CCI-02, -12 (unit), -19; M08, M26, M27 sites; rulings R5-13 (`ancestry` none here; the loader half of SEC-25 only), R5-19, R5-38, R5-40, R5-44, R5-47, R5-49. **Depends on:** Tasks 1, 2, 3. **Wave:** C (parallel with Task 5; disjoint files — this task's tests talk to `test/helpers/fakeDaemon.ts`, never to Task 5's `ClaudeServer`).

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/entry/hook.ts` (the loader; Notes give its step order), `src/entry/core.ts` (`export { main }` from `hookCore.ts`; Plan 1's `--version` and no-argument behaviour run only when the file is the process entry, `import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1] ?? '').href`)
- Create: `src/claude/client/ipcClient.ts` (`Deadline`, `openSession`, `request`, `wireCodec`), `src/claude/client/hookCore.ts` (`CoreEnv`, `CoreIo`, `CoreDeps`, `CoreOutcome`, `main`, `runCore`, `machineClock`), `src/claude/client/output.ts` (`encodeOutput`; the **M26** site), `src/claude/client/spawn.ts` (`DaemonSpawner`, `realSpawner`, `createSpawner`, `SpawnDeps`, `spawnDaemon`, `copyRuntime`, `headBranch`; the **M27** site), `src/claude/client/worktree.ts` (`AgentDefRoots`, `agentDefRoots`, `worktreeDenied`), `src/claude/client/policy.ts` (`staticPolicyCheck`, `checkInstallScope`, `hostFloorRefusal`, `checkSecondCheckout`, `checkWorktreeShape`), `test/helpers/fakeDaemon.ts` (§Interfaces test helper: authenticated scripted server on a temp endpoint, real `Ipc`, real handshake; `reply(frame)`, `extend(ms)`, `stall(ms)`, `close()`, `frames()`, `connections()`, `printed()`), `test/helpers/loaderRun.ts` (`runLoader(event, json, {cwd, env, dist})` → `{exit, stdout, stderr, elapsedMs}` for `node <dist>/hook.js <Event>` with the JSON on stdin; `loaderRepo(o)` → a temp `git init` repo under `makeTempRoot()` with an optional `live`/`paused` marker in canonical form, a plugin data directory with `createIpcKey`, and the endpoint a `fakeDaemon` should listen on), `test/fixtures/agents/**`, `test/fixtures/settings/**`, `test/hostcheck/plugin-data-probe/` (A4-10)
- Test: `test/unit/claude.output.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.ipcClient.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.spawn.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.worktree.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.policy.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.hookCore.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.faults.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.deadlines.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.protocol.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.markers.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.nonlive.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.paths.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.parity.test.ts`, `test/integration/loader.worktree.test.ts`, `test/integration/core.daemonDown.test.ts` — every `loader.*` integration test first builds the test form of `dist/` into a temp outdir (`node tools/build/build.mjs --outdir <tmp>`) and runs the bundled `hook.js`; `core.daemonDown` runs `runCore` in process (a recording `CoreDeps.spawner`, seat keys from `CoreEnv.env.CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET`), because the bundled `main` always binds `realSpawner`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `C` (`LOADER_DEADLINE_MS` 1,500, `LOADER_DEADLINE_SESSION_START_MS` 3,000, `SUSPECT_DEADLINE_MAX_MS` 7,000, `RETRY_SPACING_MS` 50, `CC_FLOOR`, `NODE_FLOOR_MAJOR`), `HookEvent`, `HookInput`, `parseHookInput`, `buildPayload`, `gatingClass`, `nonces`, `normArgs`, `seatKeyOf`, `evidenceFor`, `evidenceTag`, `canonicalJsonLine`, `encodeFrame`, `LineReader`, `validateFrame`, `canonRoot`, `findRepo`, `liveState`, `endpointRoot`, `LiveRepo`, `RootProbe`, `realRootProbe`, `resolveDataDir`, `pluginIdOf` (`src/data/pluginDataDir.ts`, X-61), `readUserJson`, `loaderLang` (X-53), `renderClaude`, `LOADER_TEXTS`, `HOOK_PROTO`, `ReplyFrame`, `ExtendFrame`, `HookFrame`, `SessionClientFrame`, `SessionServerFrame`, `EvidenceRecord`, `PolicyHit`, `RepoLocation`; Task 2 the hook build split, `__COWORK_LOADER_TEXTS__`, `__COWORK_TEST_BUILD__`, `TOKEN_ENV`, `lintDistLoader`; Task 3 `readIpcKey` (`stamp: 'use'`), `endpointName`, `socketDir`, `clientHandshake`, `FrameCodec`, `SecureConn`, `HandshakeFailed`, `realIpc`, `realWinAcl`, `WinAcl`, and in tests `createIpcKey`, `serverHandshake`; Plan 1 `buildDaemonEnv` (from `src/git/env.ts`, the client tier's one named `src/git` import, R5-03), `Fs`, `realFs`, `FaultFs`, `Clock`, `realClock`, `VirtualClock`, `Random`, `realRandom`, `seededRandom`, `PLUGIN_VERSION`, `makeTempRoot`, `testRunner`; Plan 2 `machineMonoMs` (from `src/ports/clock.ts`); Task 1's wire copies `StatusHeartbeat` and `ClientMutantId` in place of Plan 2's `StatusJson` and `MutantId`, which the client tier may not import even as types (A4-14, L-HOOK-THIN; X5-45 pins the shapes).
- Produces: the loader (`dist/hook.js`); `CoreEnv`, `CoreIo`, `CoreDeps`, `CoreOutcome`, `main`, `runCore`; `Deadline`, `openSession`, `request`; `encodeOutput`; `DaemonSpawner`, `realSpawner`, `SpawnDeps`, `spawnDaemon`, `copyRuntime`, `headBranch`; `AgentDefRoots`, `worktreeDenied`; `staticPolicyCheck`, `checkInstallScope`, `hostFloorRefusal`, `checkSecondCheckout`, `checkWorktreeShape`; test helpers `fakeDaemon`, `runLoader`, `loaderRepo`; and A4-1…A4-12.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces as A4-1 the loader's gating test `argv[2] === 'PreToolUse'`, A4-2 the loader copies and `WT_NOTE`/`WT_SUBMODULE` also on SubagentStart (SE5-18), A4-3 one clock base (`CoreEnv.startNs`, `machineClock`) and `CoreDeps.acl`/`uid`/`probe`, A4-4 `createSpawner`, A4-5 `openSession`'s `env`, `wireCodec`, the `{stamp: 'use'}` key read and the retry rule, A4-6 the `DAEMON_DOWN` notice rule, A4-7 `Agent|EnterWorktree` without IPC, A4-8 detached HEAD, A4-9 the runtime copy's `.tmp-*` protocol, A4-10 the R5-47 host check (`test/hostcheck/plugin-data-probe/`), A4-11 the test-build seams (`COWORK_TEST_MUTANT` M08/M26/M27, `COWORK_TEST_KILL_BEFORE_PRINTED`), A4-12 `agentDefRoots`, A4-13 the data-dir and language read before stdin, A4-14 the wire-local `StatusHeartbeat` and `ClientMutantId` (declared in Task 1's `frames.ts`).


**Spec:** 06 §3.4.1 (steps 1–9 and the last paragraph), §3.4.2, §3.4.4, §3.4.5, §3.6.4, §3.10 (worktrees), §3.17.2, §1.2, §3.2 (install scope, floors), §3.11.6 item 3, §3.14.4 rows `G4_DOWN`, `HOOK_LOAD_FAIL`, `PAUSED_LINE`, `DAEMON_DOWN`, `WORKTREE_DENY`, `WT_NOTE`, CCI-04, CCI-06…CCI-11, CCI-29, CCI-51, CCI-54, INV-CCI-FAILCLOSED, INV-CCI-NONLIVE, INV-CCI-NODECISION; glossary §2, §6, §11, §13, §17, §25 F4/F5/F6, §26.2 items 4 and 6; 07 `G4`, `PAUSED`, `T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL`; 09 §2, §3.4 (DAEMON_ENV), §3.13 step 1, §3.14 item 2, SEC-05, SEC-26, SEC-28/29; 11 TC-CCI-02, -12, -19, §3.9 M08, M26, M27; rulings R5-13, R5-19, R5-38, R5-40, R5-44, R5-47, R5-49.

**Tests:**

*Fail closed (CCI-06, INV-CCI-FAILCLOSED, M08)* — `test/integration/loader.faults.test.ts` (a live, unpaused `loaderRepo`; `fakeDaemon` answering `{decision: 'none'}` where a run gets that far)
- `"loader.faults: core.js syntax error, injected throw, rejected promise and malformed stdin → exit 2 with the load or G4 text for each gating matcher, exit 0 and no bytes for every other event; never exit 1"` — five faults: (a) `dist/core.js` replaced by `export const main = (;`; (b) by `export function main() { throw new Error('x'); }`; (c) by `export function main() { return Promise.reject(new Error('x')); }`; (d) by a `main` that schedules `setTimeout(() => { throw new Error('x'); }, 0)` and awaits 50 ms; (e) the real core with stdin `{"a":`. For PreToolUse with each of `Edit`, `Write`, `MultiEdit`, `NotebookEdit`, `Bash`, `PowerShell`, `Monitor`, `Agent`, `EnterWorktree`: exit 2 and stderr exactly the `T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL` en text with `{v}` = V for (a), exactly `LOADER_TEXTS.G4.en` for (b)–(e); for each of the other 13 events: exit 0 and 0 bytes on stdout and stderr; no run of the 14 × 5 matrix (and its 9-tool PreToolUse expansion) exits 1; with `user.json` `{"language": "ko"}` the ko texts in every cell, malformed stdin (e) included, because the loader reads the data directory and `user.json` before stdin (A4-13) (06 test 2, TC-CCI-19, R1-201, R1-202, R1-210, glossary §25 F6).
- `"M08 is caught: with COWORK_TEST_MUTANT=M08 a gating hook exits 0 on an exception"` — fault (b) for PreToolUse `Edit` under the mutant exits 0 with no output, so the assertion of the previous test fails for that cell (the test runs the cell with and without the mutant and asserts the two outcomes differ) (M08, 11 §3.9, TC-TST-02 form).

*Deadlines and G4 (R1-311, R1-407, CCI-10, CCI-16)* — `test/integration/loader.deadlines.test.ts`
- `"a 30 s stall → G4 within 1.5 s; SessionStart waits 3 s; one extend raises the deadline to 7 s, a second is ignored"` — `fakeDaemon.stall(30_000)` after the handshake: PreToolUse `Edit` exits 2 with stderr `LOADER_TEXTS.G4.en`, elapsed (spawn to exit) in [1,450, 2,200] ms; PreToolUse `Bash` the same; SessionStart exits 0, elapsed in [2,950, 3,700] ms, stdout empty (the daemon answered the handshake, so A4-6's unreachable-daemon notice does not apply); UserPromptSubmit exits 0 with no output in [1,450, 2,200] ms; with `extend{deadline_ms: 7000}` sent 200 ms after the hook frame and then a stall, PreToolUse `Edit` exits 2 in [6,950, 7,700] ms; a second `extend{7000}` at 6,000 ms changes nothing (still ≈ 7,000); `extend{9000}` fails the schema, the client closes and G4 follows at once (exit ≤ 800 ms after the frame) (06 test 8 stall part, 06 test 13 bound, TC-CCI-03 stall half, R1-311, CCI-10, CCI-17 client half).
- `"daemon binary removed: every edit-class and shell PreToolUse exits 2 with the glossary text within 1.5 s; Agent without isolation still passes"` — `status.json` stale, the spawner pointed at a missing `daemon.js`, nothing listens: `Edit`, `Write`, `MultiEdit`, `NotebookEdit`, `Bash`, `PowerShell`, `Monitor` → exit 2, stderr byte-equal to glossary §11's G4 sentence (read from `docs/spec/00-glossary.md`), elapsed ≤ 2,200 ms; `Agent` with `{subagent_type: 'reviewer'}` (no isolation, no definition) → exit 0, no output, 0 connections; `EnterWorktree` → exit 2 `[cowork-sync] WORKTREE_DENY` (06 test 15 loader half, R1-407, INV-CCI-FAILCLOSED, A4-7).
- `"the G4 text has no cause slot: an internal failure and an unreachable daemon print the same bytes"` — stderr of fault (b) above and of the stall case are byte-identical, with `user.json` `en` and `ko` (C13, Q-12, R5-40, SE5-07).

*Protocol (R1-223, CCI-09, 06 test 5)* — `test/integration/loader.protocol.test.ts`
- `"reply then printed{batch_id} on the same connection; killing the loader after the stdout flush and before printed leaves the batch undelivered until the next hook"` — `fakeDaemon.reply({t: 'reply', batch_id: 'b1', decision: 'context', text: 'T', system: 'S', events: ['e1'], cursor: 1})` for SessionStart: stdout is exactly `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"SessionStart","additionalContext":"T"},"systemMessage":"S"}`, exit 0; `fakeDaemon.frames()` on that connection is exactly `[hook, printed{batch_id: 'b1'}]` in that order and the connection carried no other session frame; with `COWORK_TEST_KILL_BEFORE_PRINTED=1` stdout is the same and the frames are `[hook]` only (the "re-sent and delivered at the next hook" half is Task 7's, verify pass V-10) (06 test 5, R1-223, CCI-09).
- `"the loader never waits for the pipe's end event and sends exactly one request"` — `fakeDaemon` keeps the connection open after the reply: the loader exits ≤ 300 ms after the reply frame was written (fakeDaemon timestamps); over 50 runs of mixed events each connection carried exactly one `hook` frame; the hook process leaves no child attached to its stdio (`runLoader` sees the stdout pipe close at exit) (R1-223, 06 §3.4.2 steps 3–5).
- `"UserPromptSubmit sends only nonces[] and nothing waits on it"` — prompt `please COWORK_CANARY_UPS cws-0123456789abcdef`: the frame's payload is exactly `{event: 'UserPromptSubmit', nonces: ['cws-0123456789abcdef']}`, the canary occurs in 0 captured bytes; with the daemon stalling, the hook exits 0 in [1,450, 2,200] ms with no output (R1-209, CCI-54 loader half, SEC-26).

*Markers, paused path and evidence (R1-205, R1-206, R1-208, R2-J1, CCI-08, CCI-54, 06 test 4)* — `test/integration/loader.markers.test.ts`
- `"UserPromptExpansion pause/resume create and remove <gitdir>/cowork/paused with core.js deleted; an acting verb writes one HMAC-tagged evidence.jsonl line with args_sha256 only; a cws- nonce in the prompt writes none"` — with `dist/core.js` deleted: input `{expansion_type: 'slash_command', command_name: 'cowork', command_args: 'pause', prompt: '/cowork pause', prompt_id: 'p1', command_source: 'plugin'}` → `<gitdir>/cowork/paused` exists, exit 0, no output; again → still exists, exit 0; `resume` → removed; `command_name: 'cowork-sync:cowork'` → same effects; `command_name: 'other'` or `expansion_type: 'prompt'` → no change; the acting verb `save "COWORK_CANARY_SUMMARY"` (core deleted, so no IPC) → `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/evidence.jsonl` gains exactly one line whose keys are `seat_key`, `prompt_id`, `verb`, `args_sha256`, `mono_ns`, `used`, `tag`, with `verb: 'save'`, `args_sha256 = sha256hex('"COWORK_CANARY_SUMMARY"')`, `used: false` and `tag = evidenceTag(<ipc.key>, <line without tag>)`; the canary occurs 0 times in the file; the same expansion whose `prompt` contains `cws-0123456789abcdef` → no line (06 test 4, R1-206, R5-38, SEC-26, 09 test 8 loader half).
- `"paused: SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit print exactly the PAUSED line, other events nothing"` — live + `paused`: SessionStart stdout = `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"SessionStart","additionalContext":"cowork-sync is paused for this repo by the user; edits are not coordinated."}}`; UserPromptSubmit the same with `"hookEventName":"UserPromptSubmit"`; with `user.json` `{"language": "ko"}` the ko line `이 저장소의 cowork-sync 는 사용자가 일시 중지했습니다. 편집은 조율되지 않습니다.`; PreToolUse `Edit`, PostToolUse, Stop, SessionEnd, Notification → exit 0, 0 bytes; `fakeDaemon.connections()` is 0 over all of them; an acting-verb expansion in the paused repo still writes its tagged line (06 test 4, TC-CCI-02, R1-208, CCI-08).
- `"evidence rides the UserPromptExpansion request when the daemon answers, and the loader appends it when it does not"` — live + daemon answering `{decision: 'none'}`: the frame's payload is `{event: 'UserPromptExpansion', command_name: 'cowork', verb: 'save', command_source: 'plugin', args_sha256: sha256hex('"x"'), nonces: [], evidence: <the record>}` and `evidence.jsonl` is unchanged (`CoreOutcome.evidenceDelivered` true); live + `fakeDaemon.stall(30_000)` → exit 0 with no output after the deadline and the line appended by the loader; non-live → the line; `/cowork start` in a non-live repo whose plugin data has no `ipc.key` → one **untagged** line (no `tag` key); the read-only `status --flow` → payload `verb: 'status'`, `args_sha256 = sha256hex('--flow')`, `evidence: null`, no line; the loader computes no `ancestry_has_claude` and waits for nothing (the CLI half of SEC-25 steps 3–4 is Task 15's) (R1-206, R2-J1 loader half, R5-13 loader half, SEC-25 step 1, R5-38, SE5-02, 06 test 45 loader half, glossary §25 F5).

*Non-live fast path (R1-207, INV-CCI-NONLIVE, CCI-07)* — `test/integration/loader.nonlive.test.ts`
- `"non-live: zero IPC connections and zero output bytes over ≥ 200 invocations of every event"` — a repo with no `live` marker and a `fakeDaemon` listening on the endpoint the repo would have: 15 invocations of each of the 14 events (210 runs, PreToolUse cycling through the 9 matched tools) → exit 0 each, `fakeDaemon.connections()` 0, stdout and stderr 0 bytes; the UserPromptExpansion `save "x"` runs change `<gitdir>/cowork/` only by appending their evidence lines (a directory snapshot before and after differs in `tmp/evidence.jsonl` only); no `status.json` read is needed (a `FaultFs`-free check: `status.json` absent and no spawn happens) (06 test 3 unit, TC-CCI-12 unit, INV-CCI-NONLIVE; the ≤ 80 ms p95 gate is Task 17's windows-latest leg).

*Target repo (R1-203, R1-204, R2-A3, CCI-07, 06 test 1, TC-CCI-02)* — `test/integration/loader.paths.test.ts`
- `"a .git file: WT_NOTE (WT_SUBMODULE under modules/) only on SessionStart, edit-class PreToolUse and SubagentStart when the common gitdir is live"` — a `git worktree add` worktree of a live repo: SessionStart → stdout additionalContext `[cowork-sync] WT_NOTE`; PreToolUse `Edit` of a file in it → exit 0 with the same additionalContext (`"hookEventName":"PreToolUse"`), not gated, 0 connections; SubagentStart whose input `cwd` is the worktree → `[cowork-sync] WT_NOTE` (A4-2); UserPromptSubmit, PostToolUse and Stop in it → no output; a submodule checkout (`.git` file pointing into `<super>/.git/modules/sub`) of a live superproject → `[cowork-sync] WT_SUBMODULE`; the same shapes whose common gitdir is not live → no output at all (06 test 1, 06 test 23 `claude -w` half, R1-204, R2-A3, A4-2).
- `"the edit path decides the repo, and any spelling of a live root is live"` — two live repos A and B with their own `fakeDaemon`s: PreToolUse `Edit` with `cwd` in A and `file_path` in B → the frame reaches B's daemon, A's sees 0 connections; a relative `file_path` resolves against `cwd`; on `win32` a `file_path` whose every letter is lower-cased (`c:\users\…\src\a.ts`) against the marker's on-disk spelling → live and the frame's `h.repo_root` is the canonical root; the 8.3 spelling (skipped with a recorded reason when 8.3 names are off) → live; on `darwin` an NFD spelling of the temp root → live; a `Write` of `src/new/dir/x.ts` whose directories do not exist → live; a `cwd` with no `.git` up to the filesystem root → exit 0, no output (06 test 1, TC-CCI-02, TC-CCI-16 loader half, R1-203, R1-205).
- `"the loader copies equal Task 1's functions"` — `test/integration/loader.parity.test.ts`: fast-check, 300 spellings of paths inside the temp tree (case flips on win32/darwin, `\` vs `/` on win32, NFD vs NFC, 0–3 trailing components that do not exist): the `h.repo_root` the bundled loader sends equals `findRepo(canonRoot(path, realRootProbe, platform), …).root`; 200 random `(CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET, ppid)` pairs (the ppid is the test process, so only the socket varies; plus unset) → `h.seat_key === seatKeyOf(env, ppid)`; 200 random UserPromptExpansion inputs → the evidence line equals `evidenceFor` + `evidenceTag`; with and without `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA`, over the env × plugin-root table of Task 1's data-directory test, the loader reads `user.json` from exactly `resolveDataDir(…)` of the shared `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts` (the one place a copy is unavoidable, X-61) and renders in `loaderLang(language, locale)` (X-53); the loader's `normArgs` copy gives the same `args_sha256` as Task 1's for 300 fast-check `command_args` with quoted words (X-60) (A4-2, R1-211 language rule, R5-47).

*Worktree refusal (R1-712, R2-A2, CCI-29, 06 test 23)* — `test/integration/loader.worktree.test.ts`
- `"EnterWorktree, Agent isolation:worktree and an Agent definition with isolation: worktree → exit 2 WORKTREE_DENY without IPC; Agent without isolation passes"` — live repo: PreToolUse `EnterWorktree` → exit 2, stderr `[cowork-sync] WORKTREE_DENY`; `Agent` `{isolation: 'worktree'}` → the same; `Agent` `{subagent_type: 'builder'}` with `<repo>/.claude/agents/builder.md` frontmatter `isolation: worktree` → the same, and with the definition only in `<home>/.claude/agents/` or only in an enabled plugin's `agents/` → the same; `Agent` `{subagent_type: 'reviewer'}` whose definition has no `isolation` → exit 0, no output; a definition in a plugin **not** enabled → exit 0; `fakeDaemon.connections()` stays 0 across all of them; in a non-live repo `EnterWorktree` → exit 0 (06 test 23, TC-CCI-15 worktree part, R1-712, R2-A2, A4-7, A4-12).

*Daemon down, spawn, DAEMON_DOWN (R1-221, R1-224, C15)* — `test/integration/core.daemonDown.test.ts`: in-process `runCore` over a live `loaderRepo`, `CoreDeps.spawner` = a recording spawner, an in-memory `CoreIo`; seats `S1`…`S4` are the `CoreEnv.env.CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET` values `s1`, `s2`, `s3`, `s4`, so each seat key is `sha256hex('sN')` and its seen file `seen-<sha256hex('sN')[0:8]>`
- `"status.json liveness: a heartbeat older than 3 loop periods or a missing file spawns the daemon once"` — recording spawner: `status.json` absent → one spawn; `heartbeat_mono_ms = machineMonoMs() − 1_000`, `loop_period_ms: 2_500` → no spawn; `− 7_501` → one spawn; spawn happens before the first connect attempt and at most once per hook process even while retries continue (R1-221, CCI-51 hook path).
- `"a detached HEAD spawns nothing and gating events take G4"` — `<gitdir>/HEAD` = 40 hex, `status.json` stale: 0 spawns; PreToolUse `Edit` → exit 2 G4; SessionStart → exit 0 with A4-6's notice (A4-8, R5-19).
- `"DAEMON_DOWN once per seat after the daemon became unreachable (seen-<8 hex> file)"` — nothing listens, `status.json` stale with `heartbeat_mono_ms: 12345`: seat `S1`'s first SessionStart → stdout `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"SessionStart","additionalContext":<G4 en>},"systemMessage":<G4 en>}` and `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/seen-<sha256hex('s1')[0:8]>` holds `12345`; `S1`'s next UserPromptSubmit → no output; seat `S2` → gets it once; for seat `S3` a Notification hook first → no output and no seen file, then its PostToolUse → the notice; a PostToolUseFailure first for seat `S4` → additionalContext only, no `systemMessage`; after `status.json` shows `heartbeat_mono_ms: 99999` and the daemon is down again, `S1` gets it once more; a pid-keyed seat (no socket variable, `ppid` 4242) uses `seen-<sha256hex('4242')[0:8]>` (R1-224, C15, A4-6, 06 §6 row "Daemon not running / crashed / hung").

*Output encoding (R1-321, CCI-11, INV-CCI-NODECISION, M26, R5-44)* — `test/unit/claude.output.test.ts`
- `"encodeOutput never emits permissionDecision for any event and outcome"` — for each of the 14 events × `decision` ∈ `deny|block|context|none` × `system` ∈ `null|'S'` plus `{g4: true, lang: 'en'|'ko'}`: PreToolUse `deny` → `{exit: 2, stdout: '', stderr: text}`; PreToolUse `context` → exit 0, stdout `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"PreToolUse","additionalContext":text}}` plus a top-level `"systemMessage"` when `system` is set; PreToolUse `none` → exit 0, stdout empty or `{"systemMessage":"S"}`; PostToolUse `block` → `{"decision":"block","reason":text}` (+ `systemMessage`); PostToolUse `context` → `hookEventName` `PostToolUse`; PostToolUseFailure `context` → additionalContext only (a `system` is omitted); SessionStart, SubagentStart, UserPromptSubmit: `context` → additionalContext (+ `systemMessage`), `block` and `deny` → treated as `context`, never `decision`; Stop `block` → `{"decision":"block","reason":text}`, other decisions → exit 0, empty; SubagentStop, StopFailure, SessionEnd, PostToolBatch, PermissionDenied, Notification, UserPromptExpansion → exit 0, stdout and stderr empty whatever the decision; `g4` → exit 2 with `LOADER_TEXTS.G4[lang]` for PreToolUse and exit 0, empty, for every other event; across all cells no stdout or stderr contains `permissionDecision` or `terminalSequence` and every stdout is either empty or one JSON object with no other top-level key than `hookSpecificOutput`, `decision`, `reason`, `systemMessage` (R1-321, CCI-11, INV-CCI-NODECISION, R5-44, glossary §11).
- `"M26 is caught: under COWORK_TEST_MUTANT=M26 a G2 output carries a permission decision"` — the `context` PreToolUse cell under the mutant yields a stdout whose parsed object has a `hookSpecificOutput.permissionDecision` of `"allow"`; the previous test's assertion fails for that cell (M26, A4-11).

*Spawn, runtime copy, branch (R2-I2, CCI-51, M27, R5-19)* — `test/unit/claude.spawn.test.ts`
- `"spawnDaemon passes exactly buildDaemonEnv; messaging and CLAUDE_* variables never reach the child"` — source env `{PATH: '/bin', HTTPS_PROXY: 'http://p:3128', ALL_PROXY: 'socks5://p:1080', GIT_SSH_COMMAND: 'ssh -i k', SSH_AUTH_SOCK: '/s', GIT_INDEX_FILE: '/x', GIT_DIR: '/y', NODE_OPTIONS: '--inspect', ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE: '1', CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET: '/sock', CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA: '/d', [TOKEN_ENV]: 'COWORK_CANARY_TOKEN_1'}`, passthrough `['HTTPS_PROXY']`: the recording spawner's env deep-equals `buildDaemonEnv(source, ['HTTPS_PROXY'])`; it holds no key starting `CLAUDE_`, none of `GIT_INDEX_FILE`, `GIT_DIR`, `NODE_OPTIONS`, `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE`, and no value containing `COWORK_CANARY_TOKEN_1` (09 §3.4, SEC-05, 06 test 43 env half, R2-I2, CCI-49).
- `"M27 is caught: under COWORK_TEST_MUTANT=M27 the canary token reaches the child env"` — the same call under the mutant → the spawner's env contains `COWORK_CANARY_TOKEN_1`, failing the previous assertion (M27, TC-CCI-10 spawn half, A4-11).
- `"spawnDaemon runs the runtime copy when present, else the plugin's dist, with the R5-19 argv"` — `<dataDir>/runtime/V/daemon.js` present → argv `[process.execPath, '<dataDir>/runtime/V/daemon.js', 'run', '--repo', '/r', '--branch', 'main', '--data-dir', '<dataDir>', '--plugin-root', '/p', '--spawned']`; absent → `'/p/dist/daemon.js'` in second place; `createSpawner(spy)` passes `{detached: true, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true, env}` and calls `unref()` once; `spawnDaemon` resolves without waiting for the child (the spy's child never exits) (CCI-51, R2-I2, R5-19, A4-4).
- `"copyRuntime copies daemon.js, worker.js and the grammars through a temp directory and a rename"` — plugin root with `dist/daemon.js`, `dist/worker.js`, `dist/grammars/a.wasm`: first call → `'copied'` and `runtime/V/` holds exactly those three files; second → `'present'`, no write; a plugin root missing `worker.js` → `'failed'` and no `runtime/V/`; a leftover `runtime/.tmp-V-0123456789abcdef/` is removed by the next call; a `FaultFs` rule failing the rename → `'failed'`, `runtime/V/` absent (R2-I2 runtime copy, A4-9, glossary §6 `runtime/<version>/`; the SEC-36 checksum check is Plan 7's, X5-35).
- `"headBranch reads <gitdir>/HEAD as a file"` — `ref: refs/heads/main\n` → `'main'`; `ref: refs/heads/feat/x\n` → `'feat/x'`; 40 hex → `null`; missing → `null`; `FaultFs.calls` lists one `readFile` and no git process runs (R5-19).

*IPC client and core (R1-222 client half, R1-223, R1-311)* — `test/unit/claude.ipcClient.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.hookCore.test.ts`
- `"Deadline: remainingMs counts from loader start and extendTo works once up to 7,000"` — `new Deadline(vclock, 0, 1_500)` at mono 1,000 → `remainingMs()` 500; at 1,500 `expired()` true; `extendTo(7_000)` → true and `remainingMs()` at 1,500 is 5,500; a second `extendTo(7_000)` → false; on a fresh deadline `extendTo(7_001)` → false (CCI-10, CCI-17 client half).
- `"openSession retries every 50 ms until the deadline and never connects without a verified key"` — a `fakeDaemon` that starts listening 400 ms after the call: the session opens at the first attempt after 400 ms (in [400, 500) ms) after ⌊400/50⌋ refused attempts; `readIpcKey` refusing (`SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL`) → `null` at once with 0 connect attempts; a `fakeDaemon` with another key → `HandshakeFailed` on each attempt and `null` at the deadline (R1-222, A4-5, 06 §3.4.2 step 2, 09 test 30 hook half).
- `"runCore sends exactly one hook frame built by buildPayload and maps the reply through encodeOutput"` — in-process `runCore` with a recording `CoreIo` and `fakeDaemon`: one `hook` frame whose `h` holds `seat_key`, `claude_pid` (= `CoreEnv.ppid`), `session_id`, `prompt_id`, `agent_id`, `agent_type`, `permission_mode`, `repo_root`, `plugin_version` V, `hook_proto` `HOOK_PROTO`; the payload equals `buildPayload(input, repo, extra)`; the reply's output equals `encodeOutput(event, reply)`; `io.flush` resolves before `printed` is sent (R1-223, R1-301 sender half).

*Policy and refusal checks (R1-104, R1-111, R2-B12, R5-49)* — `test/unit/claude.policy.test.ts` over a fake `Fs` holding the files of `test/fixtures/settings/`
- `"staticPolicyCheck reports each hit by setting name and file from the documented locations only"` — `platform: 'linux'`: `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json` `{"disableAllHooks": true}` → `{setting: 'disableAllHooks', file: '/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json', scope: 'managed', value: 'true'}`; `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.d/10-x.json` `{"allowManagedHooksOnly": true}` → `scope: 'managed'`; `<home>/.claude/settings.json` `{"enabledPlugins": {"cowork-sync@test-market": false}}` → `{setting: 'enabledPlugins', scope: 'user', value: 'false'}`; `<repo>/.claude/settings.json` `{"crossSessionInbound": "refuse"}` → `scope: 'project', value: 'refuse'`; `<repo>/.claude/settings.local.json` `{"deniedMcpServers": [{"serverName": "cowork"}]}` → `scope: 'local', value: 'set'`; a managed `{"strictKnownMarketplaces": []}` → `value: 'set'`; a managed `{"statusLine": {…}}` → `value: 'set'`; an unparseable file → no hit, no throw; `platform: 'win32'` reads `C:\Program Files\ClaudeCode\managed-settings.json`, `'darwin'` `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`; `FaultFs.calls` lists no path outside those locations and the three settings files (R2-B12, 09 §3.14 item 2, SEC-28, SEC-29, CCI-35 (3) client read).
- `"SessionStart carries static_policy_hits and messaging_present"` — `runCore` for SessionStart with the fixture above → the payload's `static_policy_hits` equals `staticPolicyCheck`'s result and `messaging_present` is `true` iff `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET` is set (R2-B12, 06 §3.4.3 SessionStart row).
- `"checkInstallScope, hostFloorRefusal, checkSecondCheckout and checkWorktreeShape return the 06 §1.2 codes"` — `enabledPlugins` true only in project or local settings → `'REFUSE_PROJECT_SCOPE'`, in user settings → `'ok'`; `hostFloorRefusal({ccVersion: '2.1.269', nodeMajor: 22, fromTerminal: true})` → `{code: 'REFUSE_CC_VERSION', params: {have: '2.1.269', need: '2.1.270'}}`, `'2.1.270'` → `null`, `null` → `null`, `nodeMajor: 18` → `{code: 'REFUSE_NODE_VERSION', params: {have: 18, need: 20}}`; `checkSecondCheckout('/r2', 'main', …)` with `repos.json` `["/r1/.git"]`, `/r1/.git/cowork/live` naming `/r1` and both identities `'abc'` → `{code: 'REFUSE_SECOND_CHECKOUT', params: {first: '/r1'}}`; branch `dev` → `null`; marker absent → `null`; identity `null` → `null`; `checkWorktreeShape({kind: 'file', …})` → `'REFUSE_WORKTREE'`, `{kind: 'dir', …}` → `'ok'` (R1-104, R1-111, CCI-04, R5-49; Plan 7's start/doctor call them, X5-28).

*Token custody at the hook (R1-225, CCI-49 hook side)*
- `"seat_key hashes the socket name; the token variable is never read"` — `runLoader` with `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET='\\\\.\\pipe\\claude-x'` and `env[TOKEN_ENV] = 'COWORK_CANARY_TOKEN_2'` for SessionStart, PreToolUse `Edit`, UserPromptExpansion `save "x"` and PostToolUse: `h.seat_key` = `sha256hex('\\\\.\\pipe\\claude-x')`, `messaging_present: true`; the canary occurs 0 times in every captured IPC byte, stdout, stderr, `<gitdir>/cowork/**` and the plugin data directory; without the socket `h.seat_key` = the test process pid as a decimal string (06 test 38 hook half, TC-CCI-10 hook half, R1-225, L-TOKEN's runtime counterpart).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-LOADER (source half and `lintDistLoader` on the built `hook.js`), L-HOOK-THIN on `src/entry/core.ts`, L-SPAWN, L-PORTS (`spawn.ts` allow-listed), L-PERMISSION, L-TOKEN; L-FS-PORT exempts `src/entry/hook.ts` only (A1-9).

**Notes:**
- Loader steps, in order (each line is one or a few statements of `src/entry/hook.ts`): (1) `startNs = process.hrtime.bigint()`; install `uncaughtException`/`unhandledRejection` handlers mapping to A4-1's rule (gating: `LOADER_TEXTS.G4[L_c]` to stderr, exit 2; else append the pending evidence line, if step 4 produced one and nothing delivered it, and exit 0 silently; `L_c` is `en` only until step 1a has read `user.json`); no argument → exit 0; `--version` → Plan 1 line. (1a, A4-13) Data dir (copy of `resolveDataDir` of `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts`, X-61, with `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` or the loader's own `../`), `user.json` `language`, `L_c` (copy of `loaderLang`, X-53) — none of it needs stdin, so a malformed stdin already renders in `L_c`. (2) Read stdin fully, `JSON.parse` (failure → step 1 mapping). (3) Target path (edit-class PreToolUse: `tool_input.file_path`/`notebook_path` against `cwd`; else `cwd`), canonical root, walk to `.git`: none → exit 0; a file → A4-2's `WT_*` rule; a directory → `liveState`. (4) UserPromptExpansion: markers (`pause` → create `paused` with `{flag: 'wx'}` ignoring `EEXIST`, `resume` → unlink ignoring `ENOENT`), `evidenceFor` (seat key copy, `mono_ns = startNs`). (5) Non-live: append the evidence line if any (tagged when `ipc.key` exists), exit 0. (6) Paused: PAUSED line on SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit, evidence line if any, exit 0. (7)–(8) `await import('./core.js')` in `try`/`catch` → gating: `T-HOOK_LOAD_FAIL` with `{v}` = V, exit 2; else (append the evidence line if any) exit 0. (9) `const o = await core.main(event, json, {root, gitdir}, evidence, coreEnv)`; when `evidence && !o.evidenceDelivered`, append the line; `process.exit(o.exit)`.
- `main` builds `LiveRepo` (`coworkDir = <gitdir>/cowork`, `dataDir`, `endpointRoot` from the common gitdir) and calls `runCore` with `realFs`, `realIpc`, `machineClock`, `realRandom`, `realSpawner`, `realWinAcl`, `process.getuid?.() ?? null` and `realRootProbe`; the `Agent|EnterWorktree` class is answered before any `status.json` read (A4-7).
- `runCore` order: SessionStart only — start `copyRuntime` (A4-9) and compute `staticPolicyCheck`; read `status.json`; spawn when down (A4-8 first); `openSession` (A4-5); one `hook` frame; `request` until a `reply` (an `extend` raises the deadline once); `encodeOutput`; write, `io.flush()`, `printed`; close; await the runtime copy within the remaining deadline. On `null` session, `'timeout'` or a closed connection: gating → `{g4: true}`; non-gating → A4-6.

- `"STEP_ORDER is the fixed per-event order of §Interfaces"` — `STEP_ORDER` deep-equals the 14-row table of the §Interfaces comment, including `checkpoint.trigger` in PostToolUseFailure (A13-9: shipped here, a no-op until Task 13 registers the step), and PreToolUse with `gate.edit` and `gate.shell` both listed (the dispatcher runs the one of the request's gating class); `registerStep('seat.register', f)` twice throws; an unregistered step is a no-op; a step that sets `d.done` stops the later steps of that event; when an earlier step has set `d.deny`, later steps still run but the first deny is kept (06 §3.4.2, §3.7, §3.14).
