- `"version handshake: a newer caller is served then the daemon drains and exits; an older one with an unknown hook_proto gets VERSION_RESTART on edit-class calls only"` — `seatHarness({pluginVersion: '0.6.0', hookProto: 3})` (A5-14): a SessionStart hook frame with `plugin_version: '0.6.1'` gets its reply, then `Engine.drain(null)` is called once and the endpoint stops accepting (a new connect is refused); a fresh `seatHarness({pluginVersion: '0.6.1', hookProto: 3})` receiving frames from a `0.6.0`/proto-3 caller keeps serving and never drains; on the `0.6.0`/proto-3 harness a `0.5.0`/proto-1 caller's SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit, PostToolUse and PreToolUse `Bash` get ordinary replies (no deny) — the edit-class G1 `VERSION_RESTART {v: '0.6.1'}` is gate row 1, asserted in Task 10 — and `close('version')` runs once, inline after the reply, with no `version.check` step registered *(F2 #39, ruling R-F2-7)* (06 test 44, R2-I3, CCI-52).

*Seats (CCI-31…CCI-33, R1-801…R1-806, R2-B1…R2-B8, INV-CCI-SEAT)* — `test/unit/claude.seats.test.ts` (`VirtualClock`, `seededRandom('seats')`, a recording outbox, trace, notice and signals)
- `"seats.continuity: the first seat_id bound to a seat_key is kept; a later hello with another id is an alias; /clear, /compact, in-process /resume and /fork keep seat_id and number"` — `fromHook(h{seat_key: K1, session_id: 's1'})` creates seat #1 with `seatId: null`; `fromHello({seat_id: S, seat_key: K1, …})` at 200 ms binds `S`, wiring `FULL` (messaging present); a second `hello` with `T` → `aliases: [T]`, `seatId` still `S`, `bySeatId(T)` returns the same seat; hooks with `session_id` `s2`, `s3`, `s4` (the `clear`, `compact`, `resume` and `fork` SessionStarts carry their `source`) → the same seat, `number` 1, `sessionIds` = {s1, s2, s3, s4}; the trace has one `seat` `register` event for `K1` and one `alias` (06 test 25 seat half, TC-CCI-09, R1-801, R2-B1, R2-B2, INV-CCI-SEAT).
- `"no hello within 3 s → daemon-minted 32-hex seat_id and NO_MCP, kept for the process life"` — `fromHook(K2)` at 0, `awaitBound(seat, 5_000)`: at 2,999 ms unresolved, at 3,000 ms resolves with a 32-lowercase-hex id equal to `randomHex(seededRandom('seats'), 16)`'s next value, wiring `NO_MCP`, one health event `SEAT_NO_MCP`; a `hello` with `seat_id: S2` at 4,000 ms → alias, `seatId` unchanged, wiring `FULL` (left at MCP `hello`), one health event `SEAT_FULL`; with `untilMono` 1,000 (a 1.5 s hook) the id is minted at 1,000 ms (TC-CCI-09, R2-B5, R1-803, A5-2, glossary §2).
- `"a session touching a second live repo registers there lazily as NO_MCP"` — a second `SeatRegistry` (the other repo's daemon) receiving hooks from `K1` and no `hello` → seat #1 there with a daemon-minted id and `NO_MCP` after 3 s (R2-B4, 06 §3.11.1).
- `"session numbers 1, 2, 3 in registration order, never reused after seat 2 ends"` — `K1`, `K2`, `K3` → numbers 1, 2, 3; `end(seat2, 'mcp_eof')`; `K4` → 4; a `clear` SessionStart on `K1` keeps 1; a new `SeatRegistry` (daemon restart) gives its first seat 1 (R5-33, OQ-03 (r2, r3), R1-802, R2-B3, SE5-04).
- `"MCP stdin EOF → seat_end queued; claude pid killed → seat end within 5 s; SessionEnd reason clear ends nothing"` — `end(seat, 'mcp_eof')`: `ended` true, activity `ENDED`, one outbox draft `{type: 'seat_end', seat_id: S, permission_mode: 'default'}`, `regions.releaseClaims({peer, seat: S}, 'seat_end', 'all')` called once (X-22), `signals.send({kind: 'seat_end', seat: S})` once, every `onEnd` callback once, trace `seat` `end` `why: 'mcp_eof'`; a second `end` is a no-op; `pidAlive(4242)` turning false at 100 ms → the `seats` ticker's 5 s pid check ends the seat at 5,000 ms with `why: 'pid_gone'` (≤ 5,000 ms after the death, checked every `C.CLAUDE_PID_CHECK_MS`); a SessionEnd hook with `reason: 'clear'` → no `end`, seat still bound and numbered (06 test 26, TC-CCI-09, R1-805, R2-B7, CCI-32).
- `"wiring precedence NO_HOOKS > PAUSED > DEGRADED > NO_MCP > HOOK_ONLY > PULL_MODE > FULL; each change queues one health event (SEAT_FULL, SEAT_NO_MCP, …)"` — `seatWiring(S).display` for: `FULL` + {} → `FULL`; `FULL` + {`PULL_MODE`} → `PULL_MODE`; `HOOK_ONLY` + {`PULL_MODE`} → `HOOK_ONLY`; `NO_MCP` + {`PULL_MODE`} → `NO_MCP`; `HOOK_ONLY` + {`DEGRADED`, `PULL_MODE`} → `DEGRADED`; + `PAUSED` → `PAUSED`; `NO_HOOKS` + {`PAUSED`, `DEGRADED`} → `NO_HOOKS`; the sequence `setFlag(PULL_MODE, on)`, `setFlag(DEGRADED, on)`, `setFlag(DEGRADED, off)`, `setWiring(HOOK_ONLY, 'no_messaging')` queues exactly four health drafts with `reason_code` `SEAT_PULL_MODE`, `DEGRADED`, `SEAT_PULL_MODE`, `HOOK_ONLY` and `refs: [S]`; a call that changes nothing queues none; every `reason_code` is in `CCI_CODES` (R5-34, OQ-04 (r2), R1-806, R2-B8, CCI-33, A5-4, SE5-08).
- `"MCP death moves the seat to HOOK_ONLY with one notice per cause; a reconnect hello returns it to FULL with the same seat_id"` — bound `FULL` seat `S`: `onMcpClosed(seat)` with the pid alive, then 5 hooks → wiring `HOOK_ONLY`, `wiringCause` `mcp_lost`, `notice(S, 'HOOK_ONLY', {cause: 'mcp_lost'})` exactly once; a `hello` with `seat_id: S` → `FULL`, `wiringCause` null; another loss → one more notice; a `hello` with `messaging_present: false` → `HOOK_ONLY` cause `no_messaging` and one notice; a heartbeat gap of 45,001 ms on an open connection with hooks arriving → `HOOK_ONLY` cause `mcp_lost` (06 test 27 wiring half, TC-CCI-18 daemon half, R2-B8, CCI-33; the `crossSessionInbound`/`--bare`/policy causes are Task 9's `policyWiring`).
- `"seat activity from hooks only, with one activity signal per change"` — UserPromptSubmit → `MID_TURN`; PreToolUse and PostToolUse → `MID_TURN` (no new signal); Notification `permission_prompt` → `AWAITING_PERMISSION`; the next PostToolUse → `MID_TURN`; Stop with `background_tasks_running: 2` → `MID_TURN`; Stop with `0` → `IDLE`; StopFailure → `IDLE`; Notification `idle_prompt` → `IDLE`; seat end → `ENDED`; `signals.send({kind: 'activity', seat: S, activity})` is called exactly once per change and never for an unchanged activity; `seats()` lists `{seat_id, number, activity, wiring, flags, lastHookMono}` (R1-804, R2-B6, 06 §3.11.2a, X5-27).
- `"O-SEAT (unit): the seat trace is a valid history"` — fast-check, 300 runs of random sequences of hooks from 3 seat keys, `hello`s (with random ids), `mcpDrop`, `mcpEof`, pid deaths, `clear`/`compact`/`resume`/`fork` SessionStarts and virtual time: in the `seat` trace every seat key has exactly one `register`, every seat's `seatId` never changes after its first binding, `alias` events only follow a binding, `end` occurs at most once and nothing but `end` follows it, numbers are strictly increasing in register order, and every `clear`/`compact`/`resume`/`fork` keeps the seat of its key (O-SEAT unit, INV-CCI-SEAT).

*Leases (CCI-50, R2-I1, BL2-01, R5-35, OQ-12 (r3))* — `test/unit/claude.seatLease.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.seats.engine.test.ts`
- `"SeatLeasePort.idleMs is 0 while a heartbeat ≤ 45 s old or a human lease is valid; grantHuman(480) holds 8 h; entering idle queues PEER_IDLE"` — one `FULL` seat with a heartbeat at 0 and no hook: `idleMs()` is 0 at 45,000 and 5,000 at 50,000; a hook at 60,000 → 0 again; a `NO_MCP` seat with `pidAlive` true and no heartbeat → 0 whatever the time; its pid dies → ended at the next 5 s check → `idleMs()` counts from that instant; an ended seat never counts; `renewHuman()` at t → 0 until t + 3,600,000, then Δ; `grantHuman(480)` → 0 until t + 28,800,000; `grantHuman(0)` and `grantHuman(481)` → `RangeError`; `onIdle(true)` is called once when `idleMs()` first becomes > 0 and the registry queues one health draft `PEER_IDLE`; `onIdle(false)` once when a lease is valid again, with no event (R5-35, OQ-12 (r3), R2-I1, BL2-01, SE5-13).
- `"the engine built with ClaudeDaemon.engineDeps.lease stops syncing 2 min after the last seat ended and drains at 10 min"` — `seatHarness({peers: 1, humanLeaseMin: 0})` (R5-62) with one seat; `mcpEof()` and `kill()` at 0; a local edit at 121,000 ms virtual: no capture or apply happens after 120,000 (the trace has no `apply` and no new ready pair), the outbox still flushes the `seat_end` op; at 600,000 the loop drains and the last `status.json` has `state: 'down'`, `message_code: 'IDLE_NO_SESSION'` — Plan 2's lease loop test re-run with the real binding (R2-I1 binding, BL2-01, CCI-50, ENG-56; 06 test 42 in full is Task 9's).

*Ops under FREEZE (BL2-14, R1-803, R5-34)*
- `"seat_start waits in the outbox under FREEZE; seat_end and health go board-only"` — a registered seat queues `{type: 'seat_start', seat_id: S, permission_mode: 'acceptEdits'}` and a health event; with Plan 2's `Outbox` and push states `[frozen]`: `take` returns the health event and not `seat_start`; `end(seat)` adds `seat_end`, which `take` returns while still frozen; after the freeze lifts `seat_start` is taken; the drafts pass `allowedInAll` exactly as Plan 2's PRO-45 table says for their types (BL2-14, R1-803, R5-34, glossary §8, PRO-45).

*Server, bind, squatter (BL2-12, R5-19, SEC-21, TC-CCI-16, X-2.8 bind part)* — `test/integration/claude.server.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.daemonRun.test.ts`
- `"a second daemon on the same clone gets EADDRINUSE, handshakes and exits 0 (reuse); a squatter pipe first → SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER, a security event, one notification, and the MCP side sends nothing"` — `runDaemon` IPC mode on a `setupLiveRepo` clone: the first instance reaches `'serving'`; a second `runDaemon` with the same arguments exits 0 with 0 bytes on stdout and stderr, and `<gitdir>/cowork/daemon.lock` still holds the first instance's pid and instance id byte for byte; a squatter (a raw `realIpc.listen` on the endpoint that answers `hello` with `{"t":"srv","n2":"AA==","p":"AA=="}`) started first: `runDaemon` exits 1 with `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER`, `security.jsonl` gains one `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER` line, `recordingNotifier().countOf('NT_SECURITY') === 1`, no `daemon.lock` was written; the "MCP sends nothing" half is Task 14's (BL2-12, SEC-21, 09 test 20 squatter half, R5-19).
- `"the endpoint is bound before daemon.lock is taken, and a stale POSIX socket does not block a restart"` — with the squatter of the previous test no `daemon.lock` file is ever created (the bind and its squatter handshake come first), and in a reuse run the second instance never touches it (previous test); on POSIX, a daemon child killed with SIGKILL leaves its socket file and the next `runDaemon` removes it (`removeStale` → `true`) and serves (R5-19, A3-5, X-2.8 bind part, R-26).
- `"core and an MCP-like client reach the daemon's endpoint from any spelling of the repo"` — the daemon bound from the canonical spelling; clients deriving the endpoint from `c:\…` lower-cased, the 8.3 spelling, an NFD spelling or a junctioned/symlinked path (each with `realRootProbe` → `endpointRoot` → `endpointName`) all complete the handshake on the same endpoint and no second daemon binds (TC-CCI-16, 06 test 1 endpoint half, SEC-21).
- `"daemon.js run without --data-dir keeps Plan 2/3 behaviour; recover|resume|resolve still refuse while a daemon holds the lock"` — `parseDaemonArgs(['run', '--repo', r, '--branch', 'main'])` has `dataDir: null`, and that run binds no endpoint (no socket file / pipe exists afterwards) and passes Plan 2's daemon tests unchanged; `['run', …, '--spawned']` without `--data-dir` → usage, exit 2; `daemon.js resume` while an IPC daemon runs → Plan 2's lock refusal (OQ-22 (r3), A5-1, X-2.8).
- `"the server answers no request before attach, then routes every frame kind"` — a hook frame sent between `listen` and `attach` gets its reply only after `attach` resolves; `printed` reaches `events.printed`, `heartbeat` `seats.onHeartbeat`, `seat_end` `seats.end`, `ring_result` `ring.onResult` (spies); a valid `acting` frame with no registered handler closes the connection; `sendToSeat(S, ring)` writes on `S`'s MCP connection and returns false for a seat without one; the `ipc` trace shows `accept`, `auth_ok` with `bytesToUnauthenticated` 0 for every connection (A5-6, O-IPC daemon half).
- `"no handler path executes a supplied command, leaves the main worktree or returns a secret"` — with every Plan 5 request kind registered by stubs that echo, a source scan of `src/claude/daemon/server.ts` finds no `child_process`, no `process.env` read and no `ipc.key` path; a frame carrying a 64-byte key-like string in a `WireJson` field is never echoed into a reply by the server itself (the reply comes only from the handler); `ClaudeServer` resolves no path from a frame outside `repo.root` (the only path it resolves is the canonical root at construction) (SEC-23 handler half, R2-K4 via Task 3's channel half).

*Engine view (F05-11, CCI-12)* — `test/unit/claude.engineView.test.ts`
- `"EngineView answers from memory with no git process"` — over `makeEngines({n: 2})` after 3 rounds: 1,000 calls of `A()`, `tipHeight()`, `scopeOf('src/a.ts')`, `portability('NUL.txt')`, `modeOf('src/a.ts')`, `pendingReason('src/a.ts')`, `offline()`, `freezeReasons()`, `heldRanges('src/a.ts')`, `lastStatus()`, `ownAnchors()`, `checkpointCommands()` cause 0 git calls (spy runner) and 0 file reads (`FaultFs.calls` unchanged); `pathState` delegates to `Writer.pathState` (CCI-12, R5-30 input).
- `"peerFoldedAgoMs and foldRecencyUncertain give the {ACT} inputs and their lower bound"` — `peerFoldedAgoMs(P2)` is `null` before P2's first board commit is folded; after a round that folds P2's commit, observed at mono 10,000, it is 15,000 at 25,000; `foldRecencyUncertain()` is `true` from `attach` until the observation after the first completed iteration, then `false`; after an observed `publish_pause: 'OFFLINE'` and its clearing it is `true` until the next observation that follows the first post-OFFLINE fetch, then `false` (F05-11, CCI-65, A5-8).

*Composition root and harness (R5-55, R5-62, A5-12…A5-14)* — `test/unit/claude.composition.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.harness.test.ts`
- `"every stub on a pre-wired or hot path returns its neutral value, and the bindings route through ctx"` — with every Task 5 stub in place: `ctx.health.degradedPaths()` is an empty `Map`, `ctx.readSets.fullReadSet(S)` an empty `Set`, `overlaps` false, `lastApplyInto` null, `ctx.views.get(S, 'a')` null, `ctx.events.queueSeatContext('all', 'X_P5', {})` and `nonceSeen('cws-0123456789abcdef')` `[]`, `ctx.transitions.forSeat(S)` `[]`, `ctx.checkpoints.awaitCapture('S/1', 1, 10)` resolves `'late'`, `ctx.shadows.convertLeftovers()` resolves 0, `attachEngine` and `Transitions.observe` return undefined — none throws; a `SeatRegistry` HOOK_ONLY notice reaches an `events` double's `queueSeatContext(S, 'HOOK_ONLY', {cause})` once; a `meter` charge over 20,000 reaches `SeatRegistry.setFlag(seat, 'PULL_MODE', true)` once (one `SEAT_PULL_MODE` health draft); each `status.json` write the engine makes reaches a `transitions` double's `observe` once; `attach` calls `convertLeftovers` before `attachEngine` and before the first hook frame is answered (spy order) (R5-55, A5-5, A5-12).
- `"ctxOverrides replace exactly the named members"` — `createClaudeDaemon({…, ctxOverrides: {events: E, readSets: R}})`: `ctx.events === E`, `ctx.readSets === R`, every other member is the daemon's own; `seatHarness({ctx: {leases: L}})` passes it through to every peer's daemon; production `src/daemon/run.ts` passes no `ctxOverrides` (source scan) (A5-12).
- `"the fixtures' engineDeps option binds per peer and changes nothing when absent"` — `makeEngines('x', {n: 2, engineDeps: (p) => ({lease: leaseOf(p)})})`: each peer's `EngineDeps.lease` is its own double and `claude`/`attribution` keep `noClaude`/`humanOnlyAttribution`; without the option every peer's deps deep-equal those of a Plan 2/3 fixture built before this task (same port objects by identity: `alwaysLeased`, `humanOnlyAttribution`, `noClaude`), and the Plan 2/3 suites pass unchanged (A5-13).
- `"seatHarness grants a 480-min human lease by default and none with humanLeaseMin: 0"` — default: every peer's `SeatLeasePort.idleMs()` is 0 at 31 virtual minutes with no seat; `humanLeaseMin: 0`: `idleMs()` > 0 from the start; `pluginVersion`/`hookProto` reach `VersionPolicy` (`judge('0.6.0', 3)` on a `{pluginVersion: '0.6.0', hookProto: 3}` harness → `'serve'`) (R5-62, A5-14).

*Continuity end to end (06 test 25 seat half, TC-CCI-09)* — `test/integration/claude.continuity.test.ts`
- `"a seat keeps its id and number across /clear, /compact, in-process /resume and /fork, and a second window is session 2"` — `seatHarness({peers: 1})`: `fakeSeat({seatKey: K1, withMcp: true})` sends `hello`, a SessionStart `startup`, a PreToolUse/PostToolUse pair, then SessionStarts `clear`, `compact`, `resume`, `fork` with new `session_id`s: one `seat` `register` event, `seatId` `S` throughout, number 1; a second `fakeSeat({seatKey: K2})` → number 2; `seats()` lists both; the self-deny half (Plan 4 claims) is Task 18's and the `DIGEST_SUPERSEDES` half Task 7's (06 test 25, TC-CCI-09, R2-B2, Review Focus 4 seat half).

HOOK_PROTO})`; `attach` runs R5-55's order (`convertLeftovers`, EngineView, SignalPump, `attachEngine`, the status observer → `ctx.transitions.observe`); `engineDeps.lease` is the `SeatLeasePort`; `plan5Bindings({testBuild})` returns the stub modules' binding constants (`noRegions`, `plan5WakeScope`, `neverRing`, …) whose owning tasks give them behaviour.

**Tests:**

*Turn origin (CCI-37, R5-36, R1-901…R1-905, R2-C5, R2-C6)* — `test/unit/claude.turns.test.ts` (`VirtualClock`, a `SeatRegistry` with seat `S`, trace recorder)
- `"a nonce-carrying UserPromptSubmit mid-turn releases no lease, closes no TXN, emits no prompt signal, keeps the CS open, does not refresh the attended window, and marks its doorbell's events delivered"` — integration in `test/integration/claude.turnOrigin.test.ts` over `seatHarness({ctx: {leases: <double>, events: <double>}})` (A5-12) with the `LeaseTable` double holding one INFLIGHT lease and one TXN for `S` and the `EventStore` double: human turn `p1` running (UserPromptSubmit `p1` at 0, PreToolUse at 1,000); a ring sent at 2,000 with nonce `N1` for events `[e1, e2]`; at 3,000 a UserPromptSubmit with the same `prompt_id: 'p1'` and prompt `… cws-0123456789abcdef`: result `{kind: 'doorbell', delivered: ['cws-0123456789abcdef'], wake: false}`; `LeaseTable.releaseSeat` not called; the `SignalPump` spy saw no `prompt` signal; Plan 3's `ChangeSets.table()` keeps the seat's CS `open`; `seat.turn.lastHumanPromptMono` is still 0; `EventStore.nonceSeen('cws-0123456789abcdef')` called once; trace `turn` `{op: 'doorbell', humanPromptRefreshed: false, csReset: false, released: 0}` (06 test 30 mid-turn variant, TC-CCI-20 signals half, R1-902, glossary §25 F2, O-TURN-ORIGIN).
- `"M44 is caught: under the mutant the same doorbell refreshes the attended window and emits prompt"` — the previous scenario with `testMutants: {M44}` → `lastHumanPromptMono` 3,000 and one `prompt` signal; the test asserts the outcomes differ (M44, A6-11).
- `"idle seat + sent ring → the next turn with a new prompt_id and the nonce is wake; a ring during a running human turn (same prompt_id) decides nothing"` — seat `IDLE` after Stop of `p1` at 10,000; ring `N1` sent at 80,000 (`recordedPromptId` = `p1`); UserPromptSubmit `p2` carrying `N1` at 80,036 → `{kind: 'doorbell', wake: true}`, `seat.turn` = `{promptId: 'p2', origin: 'wake'}`, `originOf(S)` = `'wake'`, `requestOrigin(S)` = `'wake'`, `recordOrigin(S, 'a1')` = `'5ea70000000000000000000000000001/a1'`; Stop of `p2` → `originOf(S)` = `'prompt'`; race: human turn `p3` running, ring `N2` sent at 200,000, hooks with `prompt_id: 'p3'` before and after (PreToolUse at 200,010, PostToolUse at 200,050) → `seat.turn.origin` stays `'human'`, no `turn` `wake` trace event (06 test 30 origin and race variants, R1-901, R1-903, R1-124, R5-36, A6-1, A6-3).
- `"fallback (b) only classifies as wake and needs 60 s of quiet before the post"` — seat idle with its last hook at 0, ring `N1` sent at 60,000 (`fallbackEligible`): the next hook is a PreToolUse with `prompt_id: 'p9'` at 60,050 (no UserPromptSubmit) → wake turn `p9`; same setup but the last hook at 30,000 (quiet 30 s) → no wake decision, origin unchanged; same setup with a nonce-less UserPromptSubmit `p8` at 60,010 before the PreToolUse → `p8` is a human turn and the PreToolUse decides nothing; a PreToolUse carrying the recorded `prompt_id` → never wake; across 200 fast-check variations (b) never yields `'human'` for any turn it classifies (OQ-01 (r2), R5-36 (b), R1-903, A6-2, SE5-09).
- `"/cowork save typed while a wake is pending with a new prompt_id is wake-classified"` — ring `N1` sent at 0 with recorded `p1`; at 10,000 `classifyExpansion(seat, 'p2', [])` → `'wake'`; the same with `prompt_id: 'p1'` → `'human'`; an expansion carrying `['cws-fedcba9876543210']` → `'wake'` with no pending wake; while `seat.turn.origin === 'wake'` (no Stop yet) → `'wake'`; after the pending wake expired and the wake turn stopped → `'human'`; the `turn.expansion` step sets `r.turn.origin` to the classification (Task 15's evidence step refuses to mint on `'wake'`) (06 test 30 slash variant, R1-904, R2-C6, SEC-25 step 2, 09 test 8 wake half).
- `"a pending wake expires 60 s after the post"` — ring `N1` sent at 0 with recorded `p1`: the `turns` ticker at 59,999 keeps it pending; at 60,000 `expire()` removes it, so `classifyExpansion(seat, 'p2', [])` at 61,000 → `'human'` and fallback (b) no longer applies (a PreToolUse with `prompt_id: 'p3'` at 61,500 decides nothing); a UserPromptSubmit carrying `N1` with `prompt_id: 'p4'` at 70,000 is still a doorbell and, by rule (a), a wake turn: `{kind: 'doorbell', delivered: ['cws-0123456789abcdef'], wake: true}`; a UserPromptSubmit carrying the never-posted `cws-fedcba9876543210` → `{kind: 'doorbell', delivered: [], wake: false}` and the seat's turn unchanged (R1-901, R2-C6, 06 §2 "Pending-wake expiry", A6-1, A6-2).
- `"a nonce UserPromptSubmit that arrives before ring_result 'sent' is that doorbell's wake turn"` *(F2 #61, ruling R-F2-17; the values follow the t6 landed cell, whose title reads "a nonce UPS that arrives while its ring is in flight is the doorbell and the wake turn; the late 'sent' leaves no pending wake (F2 #61, R-F2-17)")* — UPS `p1` at 0, `onStop` at 10,000; `onRingInFlight(seat, N1, ['e1'])` at 80,000 (the in-flight pending wake is registered at the ring frame's send); `onPromptSubmit(seat, 'p2', [N1])` at 80,060 → `{kind: 'doorbell', delivered: [N1], wake: true}`, `seat.turn` `{promptId: 'p2', origin: 'wake'}`, `lastHumanPromptMono` unchanged; `onRingSent(seat, N1, ['e1'])` at 80,105 returns `true` and `seat.turn.pending.length === 0`; `onStop` at 90,000; at 110,000 `classifyExpansion(seat, 'p7', [])` → `'human'` and `onPromptSubmit(seat, 'p8', [])` gives a human turn with `originOf(S) === 'prompt'`; a ring that fails after send clears its in-flight wake, so a later UPS with that nonce is no wake (the t6 cell "a ring that fails after send clears its in-flight wake; a nonce already seen keeps its turn", F2 #61) (06 §3.12.2 as amended, 06 test 30 race variant, glossary §25 F2).
- `"a human UserPromptSubmit opens a human turn and refreshes the attended window"` — UserPromptSubmit `p5` with no nonce at 5,000 → `{kind: 'turn', origin: 'human', newTurn: true}`, `lastHumanPromptMono` 5,000, trace `{op: 'human', humanPromptRefreshed: true, csReset: true}`; a second UserPromptSubmit with the same `p5` → `newTurn: false` (R1-902 complement, R1-905, TC-COO-14 "the next human prompt does both").
- `"O-TURN-ORIGIN (unit): over random sessions a nonce prompt never refreshes the last human prompt, never opens a human turn and never resets the CS mid-turn; a wake turn is exactly a new prompt_id with a nonce, or (b)"` — `test/unit/claude.turns.property.test.ts`, fast-check, 500 runs of random sequences over {ring sent/refused/error/silent, nonce UserPromptSubmit idle or mid-turn with the posted or an unknown nonce, human UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse/PostToolUse with the current or a new `prompt_id`, Stop, StopFailure, `/cowork` expansions, time steps of 1 s–90 s}: the four O-TURN-ORIGIN properties hold on the `turn` trace and the `SignalPump` spy (no `prompt` signal at any nonce prompt; `humanPromptRefreshed` false at every `doorbell`; `released` 0 at every mid-turn `doorbell`; every `wake` event is either a nonce prompt with a new `prompt_id` or a (b) decision whose preconditions held), and under `M44` the property fails (O-TURN-ORIGIN unit, R2-C5, M44).

*Signals (CCI-36, R2-C1…R2-C3, BL3-01, BL3-02, R5-20, R5-23, R5-24, OQ-15 (r3))* — `test/unit/claude.signals.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.signals.engine.test.ts`
- `"Engine.signal is never awaited on the reply path"` — an `Engine` double whose `signal` records the call synchronously and returns a promise that never resolves: a UserPromptSubmit, a Stop and a PostToolUse of `npm test` (a checkpoint command) through the harness each get their reply (the fake seat's `hook` resolves), and `send` returned before its call's promise settled; three `send`s → three `signal` calls in send order, each recorded before `send` returned; a double whose `signal` rejects → one `CS_SIGNAL_REJECTED` log line with `{kind}` and the reply unaffected (R5-24, OQ-15 (r3), A6-4, 06 §3.4.4).
- `"once the awaited SignalPump.send resolves, Plan 3's table shows the effect, also behind an in-flight iteration"` *(F2 #32 #62, ruling R-F2-5; was "after SignalPump.send returns, Plan 3's table already shows the effect")* — real engine via `seatHarness`: `await send({kind: 'effect', seat: S, agent: null, toolUseId: 'tu1', paths: ['src/a.ts'], turnOrigin: 'prompt'})`, then `engine.core.cs.table().cs` holds an open entry for seat `S` with `paths: ['src/a.ts']` (no sleep or settle before the read); with `engine.iterate()` started first and not yet settled, `send` of a second effect on `src/b.ts` returns a pending promise, right after `send` returns the seat's open entry has no `src/b.ts` in its `paths`, the awaited promise resolves only after that iteration has settled, and then `src/b.ts` is there — Task 12's assumption pinned (A6-4 as amended).
- `"checks_run: ok on exit 0 or no exposed status, false on PostToolUseFailure; green only on an ok PostToolUse"` — `checks.checkpoint` = `['npm test', 'pytest -q']`: PostToolUse `Bash` `npm test -- --watch=false` with `exit_status: 0` → `{checksRun: true, green: true}`; `exit_status: 1` → `{checksRun: false, green: false}`; `exit_status: null` → `{checksRun: true, green: true}` (06 §8 Q6 default); PostToolUseFailure of `pytest -q` → `{checksRun: false, green: false}`; PermissionDenied of `npm test` → `{checksRun: null, green: false}`; `npm testx` → `{null, false}`; `Monitor` running `npm test` → `{null, false}`; PowerShell `  npm test` (leading spaces) → matched; through `signal.emit` the ok PostToolUse sends `checks_run{seat: S, ok: true}` before `checkpoint{seat: S, source: 'green', candSeq, paths, expect}`, the failing one only `checks_run{ok: false}`, and with no open CS only `checks_run` (06 test 29 green half, R5-23, R2-C3, BL3-02, A6-8, SE5-06).
- `"cand_seq is one per-CS counter seeded from Plan 3's cap_seq"` — CS `S/1` with `cap_seq: 4` in Plan 3's table: `nextCandSeq('S/1')` → 5, then 6; `nextCandSeq('S/2')` (no entry) → 1; a fresh `SignalPump` (daemon restart) on the same table → 5 again only if Plan 3's `cap_seq` is still 4, else `cap_seq + 1`; a green checkpoint carries the value it drew (A6-4, A13-5, 06 §2 `cand_seq`, X5-12).
- `"cand_seq totally orders one CS's green, edit and tool captures and is independent per CS"` — seat `S` with open CS `S/1` and seat `T` with open CS `T/1`, both at Plan 3 `cap_seq` 0: a green checkpoint of `S` (`npm test`, exit 0) draws 1; an edit candidate of `S` (a Task 13 double calling `nextCandSeq('S/1')`) draws 2; a green checkpoint of `T` draws 1 for `T/1`; a `cowork_checkpoint` of `S` (a Task 14 double) draws 3; the `checkpoint` signals Plan 3 receives for `S/1` carry `candSeq` 1, 2, 3 in send order — no value repeats or decreases within `S/1`; once Plan 3 has recorded the three captures (`cap_seq` 3) a fresh `SignalPump` draws 4 for `S/1` and 2 for `T/1` after `T/1`'s one capture (R1-123, 06 §2 "candidate capture"/`cand_seq`, C4, X5-12, X5-47; added at assembly).
- `"closing signals carry expect computed within 150 ms, else none (late)"` — `captureIdentity` with doubles: a 50-path CS whose 48 paths have memo entries with equal `lstat` and 2 paths changed (each `shortRead` 3 ms) → an `expect` of 50 entries (`ABSENT` for a deleted path) in ≤ 150 ms, and the Stop signal is `{kind: 'stop', seat: S, expect}`; with 50 stat-dirty paths at 5 ms each (250 ms) → `null` and the Stop goes out without `expect`; an `unstable` read → `null`; a `DiskSnapshots` double whose `take` throws (the Task 5 stub case) → `null`, no exception escapes; `known` post-images are used without any read; no double was asked to write an object (`take` is the route (b) form without `-w`) (06 test 29 Stop-capture bound, R5-20 closing/green/tool half, R2-C2, A6-5, SE5-03).
- `"the signal set of 06 §3.12.1, per event"` — a scripted session through the harness: SessionStart → no CS signal; human UserPromptSubmit → `prompt{seat: S, humanOrigin: true, nonce: false}`; nonce UserPromptSubmit → none; PostToolUse `npm test` ok → `checks_run`, `checkpoint{source: 'green'}`; Stop → `stop` (with or without `expect`); StopFailure → `stop_failure`; SubagentStop of `a1` → `subagent_stop{seat: S, agent: 'a1'}`; SessionEnd → `session_end`; `mcpEof` → `seat_end` (from Task 5's `end`, through the real `SignalPump`); Notification and every activity change → `activity` (Task 5's `observeActivity`); no `effect` (Task 12's) and no `checkpoint{source: 'edit'}` (Task 13's) here; every signal is one `CsSignal` member Plan 3 accepts (R2-C1, BL3-01, CCI-36).
- `"idle_10min after 10 min without a hook carries activity and txnOpen"` — seat `MID_TURN` with an open TXN (`LeaseTable` double `openFor` → `{inflight: 0, txns: 1}`) and its last hook at 0: the `idle` ticker at 599,999 sends nothing; at 600,000 one `idle_10min{seat: S, activity: 'MID_TURN', txnOpen: true}`; at 900,000 none again; a hook at 950,000 re-arms it; seat `IDLE` (Esc then silence) → `idle_10min{activity: 'IDLE', txnOpen: false}` at 600,000 after its last hook (HLD-34 decides the close: Plan 3 keeps the first CS open and closes the second, asserted with the real engine) (06 test 29 idle half, R2-C1, A6-10, HLD-34).
- `"CsSignal.expect is accepted by Plan 3 and changes nothing before Task 13"` — `test/unit/holds.signals.expect.test.ts`: `queueSignal(core, {kind: 'stop', seat: S, expect: {'src/a.ts': <oid>}})` returns the same boolean and leaves the same CS table as without `expect`; `writeCsCache` writes no `expect`; a `checkpoint{source: 'green', candSeq: 5, paths, expect}` is accepted; type-level: `expect` is optional on exactly `checkpoint`, `stop`, `stop_failure`, `session_end`, `seat_end`, `subagent_stop` (X5-08 declaration half, R5-20, R5-02).
- `"a CS opened in a wake turn publishes with Cowork-CS … origin=wake, a human turn's with origin=prompt"` — `test/integration/claude.turnOrigin.test.ts`, `seatHarness({peers: 2})` with `scriptedRing` for P1: seat `S` idle, its events pending, the `ring` ticker rings (`fakeSeat.mcpScript(['sent'])`), a UserPromptSubmit `p2` carrying the nonce starts a wake turn; a test-only `effect` signal through `SignalPump` with `turnOrigin: originOf(S)` on `src/a.ts`, then Stop; after `rounds` the live commit carrying `src/a.ts` has a `Cowork-CS` trailer (Plan 2 `parseCsTrailers`) for `S/<n>` with `origin=wake`; a following human turn `p3` with an `effect` on `src/b.ts` and Stop → `origin=prompt`; a hold-record origin for that seat is `recordOrigin(S, null)` = `5ea70000000000000000000000000001` (BL2-15, R1-905, R1-124, 06 test 30 trailer half, TC-COO-14 "CSs with origin=wake").

*Context resets (F05-09)*
- `"onContextReset fires for SessionStart compact|clear|resume|fork and for SubagentStart/SubagentStop of an agent"` — subscriber records: SessionStart `startup` → nothing; `compact`, `clear`, `resume`, `fork` → `{seatId: S, agentId: null, why}` each; SubagentStart of `a1` → `{agentId: 'a1', why: 'subagent_start'}`; SubagentStop of `a1` → `{agentId: 'a1', why: 'subagent_stop'}`; the unsubscribe function stops delivery (F05-09, CCI-59 input, 06 §3.13 item 7 (d), A6-13).

*Doorbell rings (R5-37, R2-H16 daemon side, Q-8, OQ-02 (r2))* — `test/unit/claude.ring.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.ring.test.ts`
- `"mintNonce is cws- plus 16 lowercase hex from the Random port"` — `mintNonce(seededRandom('n'))` matches `^cws-[0-9a-f]{16}$` and equals `'cws-' + randomHex(seededRandom('n'), 8)`; 10,000 draws from `realRandom` are distinct; `NONCE_RE` finds exactly the minted token inside a 1,500-char text ending with it (07 §3.13, R5-37).
- `"ring: no ring to a MID_TURN seat; a text starting with / is never sent; a missing ring_result after 5 s is error; events stay queued after sent until a nonce UPS"` — `Ringer` with a recording `send`: seat `MID_TURN` → `'not_rung'`, 0 frames; text `'/cowork save x'` → `'error'`, 0 frames; a 1,501-char text → `'error'`, 0 frames; a text that does not carry the passed nonce exactly once → `'error'`, 0 frames (V-10, O-2); a valid ring `ring(S, ['e1'], text + '\nref ' + N1, N1)` → one frame `{t: 'ring', event_ids: ['e1'], text, nonce: N1}`; no `onResult` within 5,000 ms (`VirtualClock`) → `'error'`; `onResult(nonce, 'sent')` at 40 ms → `'sent'`, `TurnOrigin.onRingSent(seat, nonce, ['e1'])` and `EventStore.doorbellSent(nonce, ['e1'])` (double) each once, `EventStore`'s events untouched; `'refused'` and `'error'` results → neither call, events untouched; a `ring_result` for an unknown nonce is ignored; trace `ring` events carry `result` `not_rung|error|sent|refused` (06 test 38 daemon half, R2-H16, OQ-02 (r2), CCI-49 daemon side, A6-7).
- `"neverRing never rings and scriptedRing rings on demand"` — with `neverRing` the `ring` ticker over an `IDLE` `FULL` seat with 3 pending events sends 0 frames for 10 virtual minutes; with `scriptedRing((s, ids, nonce) => ({text: 'doorbell for ' + ids.join(',') + '\nref ' + nonce}))` it rings once with the nonce the driver minted and passed (`mintNonce` over the seeded `Random`; the frame's nonce equals the one the policy saw, V-10/O-2); a policy answering null for its first two offers (coalescing) is offered the same ids again at each later tick and rings at the third (V-10/O-5); after a post of any result those ids are never offered again, while after `'not_rung'` they are; and rings no seat that is `MID_TURN`, `HOOK_ONLY`, `PULL_MODE`, has a pending wake or is in a wake turn; a policy with `onRingResult` receives `(seatId, ids, 'sent' | 'refused' | 'error', nonce)` once per ring (scripted `ring_result`s) and `'not_rung'` (with the offer's nonce) when an offered seat could not be rung, and a policy without it is never asked (Q-8, R5-37, A6-7, X-42, R1-905 "a wake turn never causes another doorbell" daemon half).
- `"TC-COO-14 Plan 5 half: a sent doorbell to an idle seat is delivered only by the nonce prompt, which opens a wake turn without refreshing the last human prompt"` — `seatHarness` with `scriptedRing` and `fakeSeat.mcpScript(['sent'])`: after the ring, the harness's `EventStore` double still lists the events pending; a UserPromptSubmit carrying the nonce with a new `prompt_id` → `nonceSeen` called, the turn is wake, `lastHumanPromptMono` unchanged; the next human UserPromptSubmit → human turn, attended window refreshed; the Inbox-stub wire half is Task 17's (TC-COO-14 Plan 5 half, glossary §25 F1, F2).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-RANDOM (nonces through `randomHex`), L-TOKEN (`ring.ts` imports nothing of `src/ports/inbox.ts`: the daemon has no code path that opens an inbox socket), L-HOOK-THIN.

**Notes:**
- Step behaviour: `turn.prompt` (UserPromptSubmit) calls `onPromptSubmit` and sets `r.turn = {doorbell, newTurn, origin}`, then calls `ctx.bindings.coordHooks.onUserPromptSubmit(seatId, {promptId, nonces, midTurn})` once the prompt is classified (X-40; a no-op with `noCoordHooks`); for a doorbell it calls `EventStore.nonceSeen` for each delivered nonce; the ring glue reports each `Ringer.ring` outcome (or `'not_rung'`) to `WakePolicy.onRingResult?` when the policy has it (X-42); its `turn` trace event's `released` is `ctx.leases.openFor(seatId)`'s `inflight + txns` for a new human turn (Task 8's `lease.release` releases exactly those) and 0 otherwise. `turn.expansion` (UserPromptExpansion) sets `r.turn = {doorbell: false, newTurn: false, origin: classifyExpansion(…)}`. `turn.stop` (Stop, StopFailure) calls `onStop` after `stop.decide`. `signal.emit` sends, per event: UserPromptSubmit → `prompt` iff `r.turn.origin === 'human' && r.turn.newTurn && !r.turn.doorbell`; PostToolUse/PostToolUseFailure → `greenCheck`'s `checks_run` and green `checkpoint`; Stop → `stop`, StopFailure → `stop_failure`, SubagentStop → `subagent_stop`, SessionEnd → `session_end` (each closing signal with `expect` from `captureIdentity` over `openCsOf`'s paths when the seat has an open CS, budget `C.CAPTURE_BOUND_MS`); SessionStart and SubagentStart/SubagentStop → `onContextReset` events.
- The capture-at-trigger bound applies to the identity only; the hook reply never waits for `Engine.signal`, Plan 3's 300 ms quiet period or the capture itself (R5-20, R5-24); "captured at the seat's next hook" for a missed bound becomes "sent without `expect` and taken late by Plan 3" (SE5-03).
- Production `WakePolicy` is `neverRing`; `scriptedRing` is bound only by `plan5Bindings({testBuild: true})` overrides in SIM and FakeHost (R5-37).

### Task 7: Carriers, pull delivery, transitions, budget and Stop

**Covers:** R2-E1…R2-E4, R2-E6, R2-E7, BL2-09, R2-F1, R2-G1…R2-G3, R2-D5, R2-C4, BL3-07, F04-07 (meter), F05-04…F05-07, OQ-06 (r3), OQ-21 (r2), OQ-07 (r2); CCI-39…CCI-42, CCI-44, CCI-46, CCI-61, INV-CCI-CAP, INV-CCI-DOORBELL, INV-CCI-ASK-ONCE (Plan 5 half), INV-NOSILENT; 06 tests 31, 32 (frame and (a) logic), 35, 39 (carrier part), 40; TC-CCI-14, TC-CCI-17; rulings R5-09, R5-11, R5-39, R5-43, R5-44, R5-55, R5-60. Depends on: Task 5. Wave: D (parallel with Task 6; Task 6's `turns.ts`, `signals.ts`, `ring.ts` are used through doubles at their §Interfaces signatures, never through the Task 5 stub).

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/claude/daemon/events.ts` (`EventStore`, `routeHoldCode`; replaces the Task 5 stub), `src/claude/daemon/carriers.ts` (`composeReply`, `finishReply`, `CarrierItem`, `CarrierKind`, `DigestPort`, `codeOnlyDigest`, `SlotExtraPort`, `CARRIER_CAPS`; registers the steps `carrier.context` and `event.deliver`), `src/claude/daemon/transitions.ts` (`Transitions`, `transitionItems`; registers `transition.deliver`), `src/claude/daemon/meter.ts` (`BudgetMeter`), `src/claude/daemon/stop.ts` (`stopDecision`, `TurnItems`, `DueRequestsPort`, `noDueRequests`; registers `stop.decide`), `src/claude/daemon/server.ts` (one call site: after the last step of a hook frame the server calls `finishReply(r, d, ctx)` and writes the frame it returns; nothing else in the file changes); no composition file — Task 5 already binds `SeatRegistry.notice` → `EventStore.queueSeatContext`, `BudgetMeter`'s `onPullMode` → `SeatRegistry.setFlag` and the status observer → `Transitions.observe` (R5-55, A5-3 as amended), and Task 6 edits `claudeDaemon.ts` in this wave
- Test: `test/unit/claude.carriers.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.carriers.fuzz.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.events.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.transitions.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.meter.test.ts`, `test/unit/claude.stop.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.delivery.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.transitions.engine.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.budget.test.ts`, `test/integration/claude.nudge.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `C` (`CAPS.*`, `OFFLINE_NOTICE_AGAIN_MS`, `SEAT_HOUR_MS`, `INJECTED_CHARS_PER_SEAT_HOUR`, `REMINDERS_PER_SEAT_HOUR`, `REMINDER_CHARS_PER_SEAT_HOUR`, `CLAIM_AHEAD_SHARE_CHARS`, `NUDGE_AFTER_MS`), `CCI_CODES`, `MANDATORY_FAMILIES`, `PULL_MODE_SUPPRESSED`, `HookEvent`, `HookPayload`, `ReplyFrame`, `CarrierDecision`, `renderClaude`, `capLines`, `ClaudeLang`, `resolveHumanLang`/`claudeLang(lh)` (X-53: L_c = claudeLang(resolveHumanLang({user, team, osLocale}).lang)), `TraceEvent` (`carrier`); Task 5 `DaemonCtx`, `HookRequest`, `ReplyDraft`, `HookStep`, `registerStep`, `STEP_ORDER`, `SeatRegistry` (`seats`, `bySeatId`, `setFlag`), `Seat`, `TurnState`, `EngineView` (`lastStatus`, `loopPeriodMs`, `registry`, `displayName`); Plan 2 `StatusJson`, `FreezeReason`, `PublishPause`, `Clock`, `Random`, `ulid`, `machineMonoMs`, `Trace`; Plan 3 `HoldCodeRaise`, `HoldRegistry` (`nonTerminal`, `get`, `local`), `HOLD_CODES` (no `Engine.unacked`: Plan 3 Task 12a drops the stub, X-13, verify pass V-16); Plan 8a `sanitize`, `Params`, `CHANNEL_CAPS`; test doubles at their §Interfaces signatures for Task 6's `TurnOrigin` and Task 12's `ReadSets` (`fullReadSet`) and `ApplyJournal`.
- Produces: `SeatEvent`, `EventStore` (`add`, `addFirst`, `queueSeatContext`, `queueSystemMessage`, `pending`, `batch`, `printed`, `nextHook`, `doorbellSent`, `nonceSeen`, `cursor`, `onDelivered`), `routeHoldCode`; `CarrierKind`, `CarrierItem`, `CARRIER_CAPS`, `composeReply` (with the additive `suppressed` result), `finishReply`, `DigestPort`, `codeOnlyDigest`, `SlotExtraPort`; `Transitions` (`observe`, `forSeat`), `transitionItems`; `MeterKind`, `BudgetUsage`, `BudgetMeter` (`touch`, `charge`, `reminder`, `inPullMode`, `shareLeft`, `usage`); `AskRef`, `TurnItems`, `DueRequestsPort`, `noDueRequests`, `stopDecision`; the steps `carrier.context`, `transition.deliver`, `event.deliver`, `stop.decide`; trace `carrier` (one per reply).

**Spec:** 06-claude §3.13 items 5–7 (e) and (g), §3.14.1–§3.14.6, §3.15, §3.16.1, CCI-39…CCI-42, CCI-44, CCI-46, CCI-61, INV-CCI-CAP, INV-CCI-DOORBELL, INV-CCI-ASK-ONCE; glossary §13 (`reply`, `printed`), §14 (`status.json`), §19 (Claude-facing caps, systemMessage 1,050, Stop row), §20 (seat-hour budget, reminder budget), §21 INV-NOSILENT, §29.6 (attribution slot, `T-SINCE` 300); 05-holds HLD-22, HLD-23, HLD-43; 07-coordination §3.14 (priorities), §3.16 composition rows `T-STOP.*`, `T-AHEAD.more`; 10-product §3.12 (recap metering); 11-testing TC-CCI-14, TC-CCI-17, O-ASK-ONCE row; rulings R5-09, R5-11, R5-39, R5-43, R5-44.

**Tests:**

*Packing and caps (`composeReply`, unit; synthetic templates registered with `registerClaudeCatalog` under test-only ids `T_FUZZ_*` so lengths are exact, plus test rows for the overflow templates `T-MORE` = `and {k} more: {where}` and `T-STOP.more` = 07's ` and {k} more: cowork_status`; every assertion is on codes, template ids, typed params and lengths)*
- `"composeReply applies each carrier cap of 06 §3.14.1 and the 2,000-char preview"` — one table row per `CarrierKind`, each fed items whose total is the cap + 1 char: `session_start` and `subagent_start` text ≤ 2,000 (the 2,500 row bounded by the 2,000 preview; RULES block ≤ 1,000, digest block ≤ 1,500), `ups` text ≤ 1,500 and `system` ≤ 1,050, `pre_deny` with `deny.form` `T1` ≤ 1,050, `other` ≤ 800, `T1R` ≤ 300, `pre_context` ≤ 800 holding at most one note item, `post` coordination event ≤ 1,000 plus attribution slot ≤ 600, `post` with a `block` (K2) reason ≤ 1,000, `post_failure` ≤ 1,000, `stop` block reason ≤ 1,200; every `system` ≤ 1,050; the returned `chars` equals `text.length + (system?.length ?? 0)` (CCI-39, INV-CCI-CAP, R2-E1).
- `"overflow becomes one template-only item naming the dropped count; dropped events stay pending"` — 12 bystander items of 240 rendered chars (one `\n` between lines) into a `ups` delta (cap 1,500): 6 items take 1,445 chars and fit beside the en overflow line (`and 6 more: cowork_status`, 25 chars, total 1,471), a seventh would pass 1,500; the result carries `{code: '', template: 'T-MORE', params: {k: 6, where: 'cowork_status'}}` last (no message code, R5-60), `eventIds` lists only the 6 included events, `overflow` = 6 and the other 6 events are still `pending` in the store; on `stop` the overflow item is `{code: '', template: 'T-STOP.more', params: {k}}` (R2-E2, CCI-41, R5-60).
- `"a CLAIM_AHEAD overflow points to cowork_show({file}) instead of cowork_status"` — a `SlotExtraPort` double returns a `CLAIM_AHEAD` item for `billing/tax.ts` whose item list does not fit: the overflow item is `{code: '', template: 'T-AHEAD.more', params: {k: 2, file: 'billing/tax.ts'}}` inside the 600 slot (R2-E2, F05-04, R5-60).
- `"no carrier item carries an unregistered code"` — over every result of this file's packing tests and the fuzz run, each item's `code` is `''` (then `template` is set) or a member of `CCI_CODES ∪ ROUTED_CODES`, or a status `message_code` passed through by a transition line (R5-60, glossary §19).
- `"the attribution slot carries ATTRIBUTION first and a SlotExtraPort item only when both fit in 600"` — `ATTRIBUTION` rendered at 361 chars: `postExtra` is called with `charsLeft` 239 and its 300-char item is left out (not queued by Plan 5; F05's waiting rule is Plan 6's); `ATTRIBUTION` at 200 chars: both ride, slot total ≤ 600; no `ATTRIBUTION`: `charsLeft` 600 and `heldAttribClaimIds` `[]`; a held-variant `ATTRIBUTION` naming claim `c1`'s label: `postExtra` receives `heldAttribClaimIds` `['c1']` (V-10, O-7); `slotExtra` bound to `null`: no call (F05-04, R5-08, glossary §29.6).
- `"items are packed in DigestPort.rank order and a mandatory item is never displaced by a non-mandatory one"` — a `post` reply with a `HELD_CS` block reason, an `OWN_CHANGE_HELD` (`wake-scope`, family `own_change_held_wake`) item and 5 bystander items: the block reason and the mandatory item are kept whole; the bystanders fill what is left in rank order (R2-E2, MANDATORY_FAMILIES).
- `"SessionStart and SubagentStart put every conflict and held line within the first 1,500 chars"` — 30 conflict items (family `conflict`) and 10 held items (family `held`) of 45 chars, a 1,000-char RULES item (code `STANDING_RULES`) and 20 other digest items of 45 chars (the overflow lines are `T-MORE` template items): conflict and held items come first, then RULES, then the rest while the text stays ≤ 2,000 (`CAPS.PREVIEW`); the conflict/held block is bounded by min(1,500, 2,000 − RULES length − 1) = 999 here, so it keeps the first 21 conflict items (21 × 46 = 966 chars with their `\n`) and ends with `and 19 more: cowork_status` (26 chars; the 9 remaining conflict and 10 held items stay pending), the last conflict line ends at offset 965 ≤ 1,500, RULES follows whole (never cut), no other digest item fits and the total is 1,993 ≤ 2,000; with 4 conflict and 2 held items instead all 6 come first (276 chars), RULES follows (1,277), then 15 other items and `and 5 more: cowork_status` (total 1,992) (CCI-39, CCI-40, INV-CCI-CAP, 06 test 31).
- `"PULL_MODE drops exactly the PULL_MODE_SUPPRESSED families and shortens a G1 to T1R"` — `pullMode: true`: items with code `ATTRIBUTION`, `CLAIM_AHEAD`, `CLAIM_AHEAD_ENDED`, `SINCE_POINTER`, `CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE` and family `bystander` are absent from the text and listed in the additive `suppressed` result (their event ids, when they came from the store); `HELD_CS`, `OWN_CHANGE_HELD` with reason `wake-scope`, `STOP_SUMMARY` and every G1 stay; a `deny` with form `T1` is rendered with form `T1R` and ≤ 300 chars (CCI-46, CCI-61, F05-07, MANDATORY_FAMILIES).
- `"sanitize truncates placeholders, never whole templates"` — a `HELDREG` deny whose `{reason}` param is 5,000 chars: the rendered reason still starts with the `HELDREG` code line, the param is cut by `sanitize(…, 'deny')`, total ≤ 800 (06 §3.14.1 last paragraph).
- `"teammate free text never reaches a deny or block reason"` — an item of family `note` (a `T-NOTE` block, 280 chars) offered to `pre_deny`, a `post` reply with a `block`, and `stop`: it is not in the text of any of them and stays pending; on `ups` it rides as context, ≤ 280 chars after `sanitize(…, 'claude')` (R2-E6, CCI-39, 06 §3.14.5).
- `"no reply carries an OSC 9 terminal sequence"` — over every `CarrierKind` and every transition item the text and `system` contain no `\u001b]9;` and `ReplyFrame` has no terminal-sequence field (R5-44).
- `"no edit-check family exists on any carrier"` — the carrier family list has no member for edit-check outcomes and `PULL_MODE_SUPPRESSED` ∩ {every `UI_FLOW_LAST_*` code} = ∅; `composeReply` given an item with code `UI_FLOW_LAST_FAIL` throws `CarrierCodeRefused` in test builds (R2-G3, INV-CCI-EDITCHECK text clause, D1).
- `"fuzz (200 claims, 50 requests, 64-char names, 200-char labels, en/ko): every carrier under its cap and the 2,000 preview; every conflict and held line within the first 1,500 chars of SessionStart"` — fast-check, 300 runs: a scripted `DigestPort` emits items for 200 claims and 50 requests with 64-char display names and 200-char labels (worst-case lengths of the 07 caps), each run in `en` and `ko`; every `CarrierKind` result obeys the row of the first test; the six-language `systemMessage` mirror half of 06 test 31 is Plan 7's (X5-37) (06 test 31, INV-CCI-CAP, CCI-39).

*Pull delivery (`EventStore`, unit and integration)*
- `"events are delivered on printed only; an un-printed batch is re-sent in full at the next hook and delivered once"` (PULL_MODE-suppressed ids, retired by A7-3 at compose time, are outside this test) — three events `e1…e3` for seat S; a reply lists them in batch `b1`; `printed('b1')` marks them `delivered` and fires `onDelivered(S, [e1, e2, e3], {carrier: 'post', batchId: 'b1', nonce: null})` once; second scenario: no `printed`, the seat's next hook calls `nextHook(S)` → `e1…e3` are `pending` again (not delivered, no `onDelivered`), that hook's reply lists all three in a new batch `b2`, and `printed('b2')` delivers them once with `batchId: 'b2'`; a third hook delivers nothing again; an event left out by overflow stays `pending` and rides the next carrier; integration over the real `Ipc` port with `fakeSeat`: killing the hook process after the reply line is read and before `printed` leaves the events undelivered, the seat's next hook's output carries the same text again, and they are delivered exactly once (06 test 5 daemon half, CCI-41 as read by verify pass V-10, 07 COO-20, 11 TC-COO-11, R2-E4).
- `"a sent doorbell delivers nothing until a UPS carries its nonce; refused and error remove nothing"` — `doorbellSent('cws-0123456789abcdef', [e1, e2])` leaves both `pending` with `viaNonce` set; a `refused` or `error` result changes nothing; `nonceSeen('cws-0123456789abcdef')` returns `[e1, e2]` and delivers both once; an event already delivered by a pull carrier before the nonce arrives is not delivered twice; `nonceSeen` of an unknown nonce returns `[]` (CCI-41, INV-CCI-DOORBELL, 06 test 38 store half).
- `"cursor advances monotonically per seat"` — `cursor(S)` is 0 before any batch, 3 after a batch of three, 3 after `printed`, 5 after a batch of two more; another seat's cursor is independent; the `reply.cursor` field equals `cursor(S)` after the batch (CCI-41).
- `"queueSeatContext('all') and queueSystemMessage reach every live seat's next exit-0 output once"` — three seats, one `ENDED`: each live seat's next exit-0 reply carries the item once, the ended seat gets none; a G1 (exit 2) reply carries neither and leaves them pending (X5-27, INV-NOSILENT).
- `"addFirst delivers to whichever seat sends the next exit-0 hook, once"` — seat A hooks first: A gets it, B's later hook does not (A7-1; `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED` when the origin seat is gone, glossary §25.2 item 9).
- `"onDelivered fires once per delivered batch with exactly its ids and its path"` — two batches, one printed directly and one printed after its re-send by `nextHook`: two callbacks, ids disjoint and complete, each `how` naming the printed batch and its `CarrierKind`; a doorbell's events delivered by a nonce UserPromptSubmit → one callback with `{carrier: 'doorbell', batchId: null, nonce}` (F05-05; V-10, O-6).
- `"06 test 39 carrier half: in every one of the 20 cells the event reaches Claude at the next hook"` — seat wiring set by a `SeatRegistry` double per cell (permission modes `default`, `acceptEdits`, `auto`, `bypassPermissions`, `plan` × `crossSessionInbound` unset, `accept`, `hold`, `refuse` → wiring `FULL` or `HOOK_ONLY` as Task 9 publishes it): one queued event is in the reply of the seat's next PostToolUse in all 20 cells, with or without a doorbell (06 test 39 carrier part, CCI-41).

*Transitions (`Transitions`, `transitionItems`; unit and integration)*
- `"one systemMessage and one context line per seat per status.json transition; a stale heartbeat renders DAEMON_DOWN"` — statuses observed in order: ok → frozen `[NOHOOKS]` → frozen `[EPOCH, NOHOOKS]` → ok; seat A hooks after each, seat B hooks once at the end, seat C hooks once while both reasons are set: A gets `FROZEN {reason: 'NOHOOKS'}`, then `FROZEN {reason: 'EPOCH'}`, then `UNFROZEN {reason: 'EPOCH'}` and `UNFROZEN {reason: 'NOHOOKS'}`, each as one `slot: 'system'` item and one `slot: 'context'` item; B gets nothing (its last-seen triple equals the current one); C gets both `FROZEN` items at once; a seat's baseline is `{state: 'ok', freeze_reasons: [], publish_pause: null}` (CCI-44, INV-NOSILENT, R2-E7, BL2-09, R5-09).
- `"NONRETRYABLE renders only the host-reject PIN id"` — status `publish_pause: 'NONRETRYABLE'`, `message_code: 'NONRETRYABLE'`, `message_params: {hold_id: '01JAXZ0000000000000000000A', text: 'remote: rejected by policy'}`: the items are `{code: 'NONRETRYABLE', params: {hold_id: '01JAXZ0000000000000000000A'}}` and no item, text or `system` contains `remote:` (06 §3.14.6, glossary §26.2 item 2).
- `"OFFLINE and AUTH carry this PC's offline_for_s; their end renders ONLINE"` — `publish_pause` null → `OFFLINE` with `message_params.offline_for_s` 42 → `{code: 'OFFLINE', params: {offline_for_s: 42, label: 'OFFLINE'}}`; → `AUTH` → label `AUTH`; → null → `{code: 'ONLINE', params: {}}`; `VERSION` → `VERSION_PAUSED {form}` (glossary §25.2 item 4, BL2-09).
- `"the OFFLINE text is repeated once per seat when the episode reaches 10 min"` — an `OFFLINE` episode whose observed `offline_for_s` grows 42 → 599 → 600 → 900: a seat that already got the episode's first pair gets exactly one more `{code: 'OFFLINE', params: {offline_for_s: 600, label: 'OFFLINE', repeat: true}}` pair at its first hook with `offline_for_s ≥ 600`, whatever the hook's tool (a Read, a Bash call, a G2 Edit), and none after; a seat whose first hook of the episode comes at 900 gets one pair only; a new episode resets it (06 §3.6.2, CCI-15, R1-405, glossary §25.2 item 4; the "in every digest" line is Plan 6's `DigestPort`).
- `"state degraded renders the status code; recovery to ok renders nothing"` — ok → degraded with `message_code: 'HOLD_DIVERGED_REGIONS'`, `message_params: {n: 2, age: 61}` → one pair with that code and params; degraded → ok → none; → incident → `INCIDENT` with its `message_params` (BL2-09, R3-61 carry).
- `"a stale heartbeat renders DAEMON_DOWN once per seat until a fresh status is observed"` — `heartbeat_mono_ms` 1,000 and `loop_period_ms` 2,500: at heartbeat-clock 8,500 no item; at 8,501 one `DAEMON_DOWN` pair to each seat at its next hook and none again until a status with a newer heartbeat is observed (CCI-44, 06 §3.14.6).
- `"transition lines never ride a G1"` — a PreToolUse answered G1 while a `FROZEN` transition is pending: the deny text has no `FROZEN`; the next exit-0 reply carries it once (06 §3.5.2 FREEZE paragraph, CCI-44).
- `"06 test 35 (Plan 5 half): credential loss, a held file lock and a stalled loop each reach every seat once"` — integration, `makeEngines({n: 2})` with one `ClaudeDaemon`, two fake seats: renaming the bare remote: the first `OFFLINE` system/context pair appears on each seat's first hook after `status.json` shows the publish pause `OFFLINE`, which happens no later than the 30,000 ms Δmono offline threshold plus one loop period (2,500 ms) after the last successful fetch; each seat gets exactly one such pair (no earlier status change is asserted); a `FaultFs.holdLock` on an incoming path makes the state `degraded` and each seat gets one pair; a `Scheduler` stall of 3 loop periods + 1 ms makes the next hook carry `DAEMON_DOWN`; the OS-notifier-once-per-30-min half is Plan 7's adapter over the `Notifier` port (06 test 35, CCI-44, INV-NOSILENT).
- `"no other engine event is routed by Plan 5"` — engine events `LOST_STATE`, `PHANTOM`, `DEFERRED`, `SAVE_REPORT` raised into status produce no seat item from `Transitions` unless they change `state`, `freeze_reasons` or `publish_pause` (their `EV.*` routing is Plan 6's `DigestPort`, R5-09, OQ-06 (r3), OQ-21 (r2)).

*Budget (`BudgetMeter`; unit with `VirtualClock`, integration with the seat harness)*
- `"over 20,000 injected chars → PULL_MODE: ATTRIBUTION, CLAIM_AHEAD*, SINCE_POINTER and CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE suppressed, G1 still in T1R form, next window restores"` — seat S `touch`ed at mono 0 (its first hook): `charge(S, 'inject', 19,999)` → no flag; `charge(S, 'inject', 2)` → `onPullMode(S, true)` once and `inPullMode(S)` true; a `post` reply then suppresses those families and a G1 renders `T1R`; at mono 3,599,999 still on; at 3,600,000 `inPullMode(S)` false, `onPullMode(S, false)` once, `usage(S).injected` 0 (CCI-46, TC-CCI-14, 06 test 40 meter half, F05-07).
- `"the host-reminder estimate trips PULL_MODE at 16 reminders or above 20,000 chars"` — `reminder(S, 100)` ×15 → off; the 16th → on; in a fresh window one `reminder(S, 20,001)` → on; `reminder(S, 20,000)` alone → off (CCI-46, R2-D5).
- `"claim-ahead text stops at 5,000 without PULL_MODE; slash output is shown, not counted"` — `charge(S, 'claim_ahead', 4,990)` → `shareLeft(S, 'claim_ahead')` 10; `charge(S, 'claim_ahead', 10)` → 0 and `usage(S).injected` 5,000, no PULL_MODE; `charge(S, 'slash', 50,000)` → `usage(S).slashShown` 50,000, `injected` unchanged, no PULL_MODE, including a slash output that carries `last_check` (D1) (CCI-61, F05-06, R2-G1, R2-G2, R2-G3, R5-43).
- `"the F04 recap and every systemMessage mirror are metered in full"` — `finishReply` of a UserPromptSubmit reply whose `system` holds a 1,000-char item of family `recap` and whose context holds a 300-char `SINCE_POINTER` item: `injected` grows by 1,300 (kinds `recap` and `inject`); a 120-char transition `system` item grows it by 120 (kind `system`) (R5-43, OQ-07 (r2), F04-07, CCI-46 rev 2.5).
- `"doorbell text and Stop blocks are counted; idle seats cost nothing"` — `charge(S, 'doorbell', 900)` → `injected` +900 and `doorbells` +1; a 1,100-char Stop block via `finishReply` → +1,100; a seat with no hook and no doorbell for an hour has `usage().injected` 0 (CCI-46).
- `"06 test 40 / TC-CCI-14: scripted churn over 20,000 → PULL_MODE, G1 in T1R, next window restores"` — seat harness: 25 `post` replies of 900 chars each; after the 23rd the seat flag `PULL_MODE` is set through `SeatRegistry.setFlag` (one `SEAT_PULL_MODE` health event queued, R5-34) and later notes are suppressed; a denied edit's reason is `T1R`; advancing the virtual clock to the window boundary clears the flag; the read-set apply half (applies only at Stop/UserPromptSubmit) is Task 8's `coalesce_pull_mode` test and the doorbell half is Task 6's `Ringer`/Plan 6's policy (06 test 40, TC-CCI-14).

*Stop (`stopDecision`, `TurnItems`; unit)*
- `"Stop: one denied Edit + one held CS → exactly one block naming both ids; ids already in the last message → none; stop_hook_active → none; wake turn → none; absent last message → block"` — `TurnItems.record(S, null, 'denied', 'SHD1')`, `record(S, null, 'held', 'PIN1')`: `ids_in_last_message: ['X']` → `{code: 'STOP_SUMMARY', itemIds: ['SHD1', 'PIN1']}` with params `items: [{agent: null, kind: 'denied', id: 'SHD1'}, {agent: null, kind: 'held', id: 'PIN1'}]`; `['SHD1', 'PIN1']` → null; `['SHD1']` → block; `stop_hook_active: true` → null; `seat.turn.origin = 'wake'` → null; `ids_in_last_message: null` → block (06 test 32 frame and (a) logic, CCI-42, R2-F1, 06 §8 Q7 default).
- `"at most one block per turn; SubagentStop never blocks"` — after a block, a second Stop of the same turn with `stop_hook_active: false` (`blockedThisTurn` true) → null; a `SubagentStop` with items → null (CCI-42).
- `"(a) items are keyed by agent_id, the main agent first"` — items recorded by agents `null`, `a2`, `a1`, `null` → `items` order `null`, `null`, `a1`, `a2`, each agent's items in record order (CCI-30 item 2, R2-A1 Stop half).
- `"(b) due asks only with no background task; each (request identity, attempt) once"` — a `DueRequestsPort` double returns `{reqId: 'R1', toHolderSeat: S, attempt: 1, …}`: `background_tasks_running: 2` → no ask (and no block when (a) is empty); 0 → one block with `asks: [{rid, label, requester, mode}]` after the (a) items; when that block's batch is delivered (`printed`) `markAsked([R1@1])` is called once; the double returning R1 attempt 1 again next turn → filtered, no block; attempt 2 → one block; a batch never delivered (no `printed`, no next hook) → `markAsked` not called (INV-CCI-ASK-ONCE Plan 5 half, R2-F1, COO-26).
- `"noDueRequests yields no ask and markAsked is a no-op"` — `noDueRequests.dueRequests(S)` is `[]` (R5-10).
- `"the Stop block rides a batch and its reason is ≤ 1,200"` — 40 items: the reason is ≤ 1,200 and ends with the template-only `T-STOP.more {k}` item; the block is a store event (family `stop_truth`, mandatory, carriers `['stop']`) whose `printed` triggers `markAsked` (CCI-42, INV-CCI-ASK-ONCE).
- `"TurnItems.unacked lists the current turn's ids and endTurn clears them"` — records of kinds `denied`, `held`, `recorded`, `not_shared` across agents `null` and `a1` → `unacked(S)` lists all four ids in record order; `endTurn(S)` → `[]`; seat T's items are untouched; no Plan 5 module names `Engine.unacked` (a source scan; Plan 3 drops the stub, X-13) (R5-39, HLD-23, BL3-06).
- `"stop.decide ends the turn's items unless it blocked"` — a Stop that blocks keeps the items for the continuation; the following Stop (`stop_hook_active: true`) returns null and ends them; a Stop that does not block ends them at once; a new-turn UserPromptSubmit ends them in `carrier.context` (R5-39).

*Nudge and Plan 3's status-only codes (`routeHoldCode`)*
- `"CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE once per CS after 300 s of uncovered edits, never CS_NUDGE"` — `routeHoldCode({code: 'CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE', params: {cs: '<S>/3', n: 4}})` adds one event for seat S (`carriers: ['post']`, family `nudge`, params `{n: 4}`); a second raise for `<S>/3` adds none; a raise for `<peer>/human/7` adds none; `CS_NUDGE` is in neither `CCI_CODES` nor any event; under PULL_MODE it is suppressed (BL3-07, R2-C4, R5-09, TC-CCI-17).
- `"TC-CCI-17 / 06 test 29 nudge half: a CS whose edit checks keep failing gets exactly one nudge at its next PostToolUse after 300 s"` — integration, Plan 3's `timerStep` at 300,000 ms virtual with an `EditCheckPort` double that always fails: one `CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE {n}` on the seat's next PostToolUse reply, none on later ones; with a passing double (the CS covered) none (TC-CCI-17, R2-C4, BL3-07).
- `"INCOMING_WAITING, CS_GROUP_WAITING and CS_READY_CANCELLED reach exactly the seats the code names, once per raise"` — `INCOMING_WAITING {cs: 'P2/7', path: 'src/a.ts', reason: 'locked'}` → one event for each seat whose full-read set (a `ReadSets` double) holds `src/a.ts` (seat A), none for seat B; `CS_GROUP_WAITING {cs: '<B>/2'}` → B; `CS_READY_CANCELLED {cs: '<A>/5'}` → A; each on the `post` carrier; `HOLD_CREATED` is not routed by this function (BL3-07, R5-09).
- `"HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED goes to the record's origin seat, else to the next seat, once"` — `routeHoldCode({code: 'HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED', params: {hold_id: 'H1', reason: 'validator'}})` with record H1's `origin` `<A>/a1` → one event for A with params `{rid: <display id of H1>}`; origin seat ended → `addFirst`; a repeat raise for H1 → none (glossary §25.2 item 9, R2-D2 delivery half).

*Context loss and digests (`carrier.context`, `codeOnlyDigest`)*
- `"SessionStart compact|clear|resume|fork starts the digest with DIGEST_SUPERSEDES"` — for each of the four sources the first digest item of the SessionStart reply is `DIGEST_SUPERSEDES`; `startup` → none; `codeOnlyDigest.digest(S, 'full', {supersedes: true})` returns it first (CCI-40, R2-E3, 06 test 25 digest half).
- `"codeOnlyDigest lists STANDING_RULES as a code line, this seat's own held records and HOLD_IN_FILE for its read files, nothing else"` — `rules(S)` → one item `STANDING_RULES` (07 §3.16.4's registry code, R5-60); with registry records H1 (origin `<S>`, ON_DISK PIN on `src/a.ts`), H2 (origin `<T>`), H3 (ACTIVE PATCH on `src/b.ts`, a file in S's full-read set double): `digest(S, 'full', …)` → `OWN_CHANGE_HELD {rid: <H1>, path: 'src/a.ts', reason}` (family `held`) and `HOLD_IN_FILE {path: 'src/b.ts', rid: <H3>, reason}`; nothing for H2 (R5-11, R2-D5, HLD-22 SessionStart half).
- `"UserPromptSubmit carries the digest delta only when it differs from the last digest this seat saw"` — two new-turn prompts with the same digest: the second reply has no digest item; a changed digest → its delta; a doorbell (nonce) UserPromptSubmit carries no digest (CCI-39, 06 §3.14.1 row 2).
- `"SubagentStart injects RULES and the subagent digest with conflict lines first"` — `digest(S, 'subagent', …)` requested; conflict/held items before RULES; text ≤ 2,000 (CCI-40, R2-A1 item 3).

*Trace*
- `"every reply emits one carrier trace event"` — `{kind: 'carrier', peer, seat, event, chars, metered, codes, eventIds}` with `chars` = rendered length, `metered` = chars charged (slash 0), `codes` in packing order; no duration or wall-time field (TST-03).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** none new.

**Notes:**
- `finishReply` is the one place a hook reply is assembled: `composeReply(kind, d.items + pending store events by rank, lang, {pullMode: meter.inPullMode(seat), deny: d.deny, block: d.block})`, then `events.batch(seat, eventIds)` (the batch id goes into `ReplyFrame.batch_id`), suppressed event ids marked delivered without charge, `meter.charge` per item family (`recap` → `recap`, `CLAIM_AHEAD*` → `claim_ahead`, other context/deny/block text → `inject`, other `system` → `system`), one `carrier` trace event. `CarrierKind` is derived from the event and outcome: SessionStart → `session_start`, SubagentStart → `subagent_start`, UserPromptSubmit → `ups`, PreToolUse with `d.deny` → `pre_deny`, else `pre_context`, PostToolUse → `post`, PostToolUseFailure → `post_failure`, Stop → `stop`, any other event → `none` (no text).
- Step behaviour: `carrier.context` adds RULES + digest items at SessionStart (supersedes for `compact|clear|resume|fork`) and SubagentStart, the digest delta at a new-turn UserPromptSubmit (and calls `TurnItems.endTurn` first when `r.turn.newTurn`), nothing on other events; `transition.deliver` adds `Transitions.forSeat` items (exit-0 carriers only); `event.deliver` calls `meter.touch(seat)` (anchors the seat-hour window at its first hook), `events.nextHook(seat)` (re-queues un-printed batches for a full re-send, V-10), then offers the seat's pending events to the carrier, honouring each event's `carriers` filter; `stop.decide` runs `stopDecision` and, on a block, adds the `STOP_SUMMARY` store event and sets `d.block`.
- `EventStore.add` returns one event id per target seat; `'all'` targets every non-`ENDED` seat at call time. A doorbell-carried event may also ride a pull carrier; whichever comes first delivers it, once.
- The read-set apply rule of `PULL_MODE` (only at Stop or UserPromptSubmit) is a defer-set source (`coalesce_pull_mode`, Task 8); the doorbell rule is the ring path's (Task 6 `Ringer`, Plan 6 `WakePolicy`); this task only owns the flag, the window and the suppression list.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces as A7-1 `EventStore.addFirst`, A7-2 `SeatEvent.carriers` (and `EventStore.add`'s `carriers?`), A7-3 `composeReply(…).suppressed`, A7-4 `finishReply`, A7-5 `routeHoldCode` (Plan 3 raise params: X5-42), A7-6 `new Transitions(heartbeatNow)`, `transitionItems` and the OFFLINE repeat, A7-7 `BudgetMeter.touch`, A7-8 `CARRIER_CAPS` and the SessionStart/SubagentStart packing order (a flagged reading), A7-9 `TransitionTriple` with `offlineRepeatSent`, A7-10 `CarrierCodeRefused`.
