**Tests:**

*Short reads and disk snapshots (`hookRead.ts`)*
- `"shortRead is one read call bracketed by lstat, with one re-read"` — a `FaultFs` spy over a 10-byte file: exactly `lstat`, `readFile`, `lstat` and no open handle after return → `{kind: 'ok'}`; the second `lstat` reporting a changed `(size, mtimeNs)` → one more `readFile` + `lstat`; stable then → `ok` with the second bytes; changed again → `{kind: 'unstable', bytes: <second read's bytes>}`; ENOENT → `missing`; EACCES and EBUSY → `unreadable`; never more than two `readFile` calls (CCI-55, INV-CCI-SHORTREAD, R1-507, R5-26, C21).
- `"an unstable read carries the second read's bytes, unlike Plan 2's stagedRead"` — the same fault script through Plan 2's `stagedRead` returns `{kind: 'unstable'}` without bytes while `shortRead` returns the bytes of read 2 (X-2.2, C21, 06 §3.7.6 "taken from the second read").
- `"hold is memory only; settle hashes by route (b) from stdin without -w"` — `hold('src/a.ts', ok)` spawns no git process (spy runner: 0 calls) and counts its bytes in `bytesHeld()`; `settle(h)` makes exactly one call `hash-object --stdin --path=src/a.ts` (no `-w`, cwd = repo root, `attr.tree=<EPOCH_BASE>` and the required-filter `-c` pairs) and returns `oid` equal to `git hash-object --path=src/a.ts` of a copy of those bytes; `count-objects -v` is unchanged (R5-26, BL2-08, W6, 04 §2).
- `"hashRouteBStdin applies attributes and filters through --path and fails closed"` — a `*.txt filter=counting` fixture: the clean filter runs once per call and the returned oid is of the filtered bytes; a failing required filter → `FilterFailure` and no object; `write: true` stores the object (used by Tasks 11 and 12 for records only) (A8-7, DAT-01, L-OBJ-WRITE).
- `"DiskSnapshots keeps 64 MB in memory and spills the rest under tmp/stage/"` — six holds of 12 MiB each: `bytesHeld()` ≤ 67,108,864 and the sixth is spilled to a file under `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/stage/` (0600); `bytesOf` returns identical bytes; `release` deletes the spill file; nothing is written anywhere else and nothing serializes a snapshot (CCI-20, 06 §3.7.1, R5-26).
- `"emptyOrShrunk is the W2 rule"` — pre 1,000 bytes: post 0 → true, 499 → true, 500 → false, 1,200 → false; no pre-image: post 0 → true, post 10 → false (CCI-55, glossary §26 W2).
- `"normalizedEqual ignores EOL-only and BOM-only differences"` — `﻿a\r\nb\r\n` vs `a\nb\n` → true; `a\nb\n` vs `a\nc\n` → false (06 §3.8.1, §3.8.4 trigger (a)).
- `"hookRead.saves: in-place and atomic-replace saves of the 1.3 MB BOM+CRLF file during 500 Pre/Post pairs and 50 TXNs: no git process names the working file, loose objects unchanged by hook reads, an unstable pre-image is the second read"` — `generateBigBomCrlf` fixture saved every 1–5 ms (virtual) in place and by rename-over by a writer loop; 500 `hold`→`settle` pairs and 50 `openTxn`/release cycles: `gitCallsOf` shows no argv containing the working file's absolute path, `count-objects -v` is unchanged across the run, every `unstable` result's bytes equal the read-2 bytes recorded by the `FaultFs` spy; on windows-latest with `COWORK_SOAK=1` *(F2 #18 #36, ruling R-F2-6)* the cell's soak arm uses real saves (`MoveFileEx` replace through Node `rename`) every 1–5 s for ≥ 3,000 saves per arm against a daemon-off control arm of equal length: daemon-attributable save failures (the daemon arm's minus the control's, glossary §26.1 W3) ≤ 3 (≤ 1 per 1,000) and no `EPERM` while no hook read is in progress; the soak arm runs once in the Plan 5 exit run on the free public runners (Task 18's exit evidence), TST-18's weekly soak stays the release gate, and without `COWORK_SOAK` the default suite runs the cell's in-process arms, whose bounds may be tightened but never loosened (06 test 46 hook half, CCI-55, INV-CCI-SHORTREAD, O-STAGED hook half, glossary §26 W1, W3).
- `"mutant M40 hashes the working file in place; mutant M41 writes an object on a hook read"` — with `COWORK_TEST_MUTANT=M40` the `settle` argv names the working file and the `hookRead.saves` assertion "no git process names the working file" fails; with `M41` `settle` passes `-w` and the loose-object assertion fails; without a mutant both pass (M40, M41 sites; oracle wiring is Task 18).

*Lease table (`leases.ts`; unit with `VirtualClock`, trace through `TraceRecorder`)*
- `"INFLIGHT: keyed by tool_use_id, refcounted per path, never released by a sibling's PreToolUse"` — two leases `tu1`, `tu2` (a parallel batch of two Edits on `src/a.ts`): `fencedPaths()` has `src/a.ts → fence`; a third PreToolUse's acquisition on `src/b.ts` releases nothing; `release('tu1', 'post')` keeps the fence; `release('tu2', 'post')` drops it; INFLIGHT, READ and TXN entries live in this one table and `fencedPaths()` is its only fence view (CCI-20, 06 §3.7.1, INV-CCI-LEASE, R1-501, R1-502).
- `"each release event of 06 §3.7.1 releases exactly its leases"` — `post`, `post_failure`, `permission_denied` (by id), `batch` (every listed id), `new_turn`, `stop`, `stop_failure`, `seat_end` (all of the seat's), `session_end` (scope `{sessionId}`: only leases whose PreToolUse carried that `session_id`), `subagent_stop` (scope `{agentId: 'a1'}`: only `a1`'s); each emits one `lease` trace event with `op: 'release'` and that `why`; a release without a PostToolUse (`permission_denied`, `new_turn`, `stop`, `backstop`) calls `RegionsPort.discardTentative(toolUseId)` on the double (CCI-20, CCI-30 item 1, R1-502).
- `"the backstops: INFLIGHT 120 s, READ 120 s with ≤ 3 renewals and a 300 s hard cap, TXN 120 s"` — INFLIGHT acquired at 0: `expire()` at 119,999 returns nothing, at 120,000 returns it in `expired` (`why: 'backstop'`); READ at 0 renewed by `renewReads` at 10,000, 20,000 and 30,000 (third renewal) → still live at 149,999, expired at 150,000; a fourth `renewReads` at 40,000 is ignored (without the ≤ 3 limit it would live to 160,000) *(F2 #20: the former times 100,000/200,000/280,000/290,000 reached the hard cap at the second renewal, so the limit was not observable)*; a second READ at 0 renewed at 100,000 and 200,000 → still live at 299,999, expired at 300,000 (hard cap: min(0 + 300,000, 200,000 + 120,000)); READ never renewed → expired at 120,000; TXN opened at 0 → `capped` at 120,000 with `why: 'cap'` (CCI-20…CCI-22, INV-CCI-LEASE, O-LEASE unit).
- `"leases.caps: randomly dropped Posts (second hook denies, manual No, deny rule, plan mode, Esc in a slow Write, StopFailure, PermissionDenied in auto mode, a parallel batch of two Edits on one file) → INFLIGHT ≤ 120 s, READ ≤ 300 s, TXN ≤ 120 s, no early release by a sibling's PreToolUse, the incoming change lands within 125 s"` — fast-check, 500 runs over scripted sequences of those cases with 1 s housekeeping ticks: recomputed from the `lease` trace with the recorder's `monoAt`, every acquire has its release with age ≤ its cap; no release has `why` caused by another tool's PreToolUse; integration leg (`makeEngines({n: 2})`, the seat's leases driven directly): a peer change to the fenced path lands on this peer within 125,000 ms of the lease's acquisition (06 test 17, TC-CCI-05, INV-CCI-LEASE, O-LEASE unit).
- `"READ leases: full reads only, released by the seat's next edit-class Post on the path"` — `acquireRead(S, 'src/a.ts')` → `fencedPaths()` has `src/a.ts → read_lease`; `releaseRead(S, 'src/a.ts', 'next_edit_post')` drops it; another seat's edit Post does not; there is no API to lease a ranged read (ranged reads are `ReadSets` records, Task 12) (CCI-21, F05-03, R1-503).
- `"openTxn records S_open, the open fold height and the 20 ms stat bound"` — `openTxn` with `snapshotEntries()` of 200 entries stats them through the `Fs` port and keeps pre-window attribution for the 3 stat-dirty ones; a `FaultFs` slowing `lstat` so the scan passes 20 ms (virtual) → `approximate: true` and the remaining entries unstatted; `txnOpen()` true and `txnOpenSinceMono()` = the open instant until the last TXN closes (CCI-22, 06 §3.7.3, F14-05).
- `"onTxnClosed subscribers see every TXN release with its why"` — closes by `post`, `post_failure`, `permission_denied`, `background_return`, `new_turn`, `stop`, `stop_failure`, `session_end`, `seat_end` and `cap` each fire the subscriber once with the `Txn` and `why` (Task 12 subscribes `closeTxn`) (A8-3, CCI-22).
- `"expiredPerSeatHour counts backstop expiries"` — six INFLIGHT expiries of seat S within one seat-hour → 6 (> 5 flags doctor, Plan 7); one hour later → 0 (CCI-24, 06 §3.7.4).
- `"lease.release follows the per-event rules"` — through the step with `HookRequest` fixtures: a new-turn UserPromptSubmit releases all of the seat's leases and TXNs (`new_turn`) and calls `DeferSet.onTurnBoundary`; a nonce-carrying UserPromptSubmit (`r.turn.doorbell`) releases nothing; an edit-class PostToolUse releases the READ lease of that path; Stop/StopFailure release all; SessionEnd releases only that `session_id`'s; SubagentStop only that agent's; PostToolBatch every listed id; every event that runs the step also calls `renewReads(seat)` (CCI-20, CCI-21, CCI-37 mid-turn rule, R1-502…R1-504).

*Defer set (`defer.ts`; unit with doubles for `SeatHealth`, `ReadSets`, `BudgetMeter`)*
- `"deferred(): fence, read_lease, txn, coalesced and DEGRADED paths with their tags; a MID_TURN seat's read set applied 30 s ago → tag coalesce_mid_turn with last apply instant; PULL_MODE → coalesce_pull_mode"` — INFLIGHT on `a.ts` → `{a.ts: 'fence'}`, tag `{source: 'fence', seatNumber: 1, sinceMono}`; READ on `b.ts` → `read_lease`/`read_lease`; a DEGRADED seat's `degradedPaths()` `{c.ts}` → `read_lease` with source `degraded`; seat 2 `MID_TURN` with a hook 10 s ago and full-read set `{d.ts}` whose last apply into its read set was 30 s ago → `d.ts: 'read_lease'` with source `coalesce_mid_turn`, `seatNumber: 2`, `lastApplyIntoReadSetMono: now − 30,000`; the same seat in `PULL_MODE` → source `coalesce_pull_mode`; `txnOpen()` true adds no per-path entries (the engine skips the worktree) and `tags()` is memory only (T-F14-incoming-deferred (b), (d), (e) daemon side, F14-04, F14-05, R1-506, R5-25, R2-G4, C8, C20, Q-4, OQ-08 (r2), OQ-05 (r3)).
- `"a deferred path's reason is answered from memory"` — for a path fenced before any apply attempt, `tags().get(p).reason` is the lease table's `fence`; after the engine's next apply attempt `meta.pending[p]` holds the same reason and `EngineView.pendingReason(p)` returns it with no git call (spy runner: 0); a `pathState` of `'pending'` is never mapped to a deny (R5-30, C17).
- `"apply coalescing: at most one apply per 2 min into a MID_TURN seat's full-read set"` — seat `MID_TURN` with a hook 5 s ago, `applied(['d.ts'])` at t: `d.ts` deferred until t + 120,000, then not deferred until the next `applied` into that read set; a seat `MID_TURN` by activity but silent for 61 s → not coalescing (its TXN or nothing defers) (06 §3.7.5, glossary §20, OQ-04 (r3), R5-25).
- `"PULL_MODE: applies into the read set only at its Stop or UserPromptSubmit"` — seat in `PULL_MODE` with full-read set `{e.ts}`: deferred; `onTurnBoundary(S)` (its Stop) → not deferred; after an `applied(['e.ts'])` deferred again until the next boundary (06 §3.16.1, 06 test 40 read-set half, TC-CCI-14 apply half).
- `"recheck at P1: a fence or TXN opened after J defers"` — `recheck(['a.ts', 'b.ts'])` with a lease on `b.ts` → `{b.ts: 'fence'}`; with a TXN open → both `txn` (CCI-23, 01 §3.11 P1, BL2-05).
- `"applied fans out with the per-path detail"` — `applied(['a.ts'], [{path: 'a.ts', from: <oid1>, to: <oid2>, writer: 'P2'}])` calls every `onApplied` subscriber once with that detail and records the last-apply instant for each seat whose full-read set holds `a.ts` (A8-4, A8-5; Task 12's `ApplyJournal` subscribes).

*Attribution windows (`attribution.ts`; unit)*
- `"one rule set: INFLIGHT call, TXN window, 3 s formatter grace, else human-class"` — a delta first seen on `a.ts` inside `tu1`'s INFLIGHT window → `[{seatId: S, toolUseId: 'tu1', via: 'inflight', csId}]`; inside TXN `tu2` of S and TXN `tu3` of T at once → both entries (the stricter result applies to claims, Task 12); after `onEditPost(S, 'a.ts')` at t: a delta at t + 2,999 → `via: 'grace'`, at t + 3,001 → `null` (human-class) (CCI-27, 06 §3.8.3, R1-604).
- `"hookAttribution answers per R5-28"` — inside an INFLIGHT or TXN window or the grace: `removalAttributed` true; `shrinkAttributed` true always (R-25 kept); `observedEdit` true for INFLIGHT and grace windows, false inside a TXN window (shell writes are taint-checked as UNOBSERVED); `hookAttributed` true for any seat window; outside every window all false except `shrinkAttributed`; no member takes a blob argument and the port type is Plan 3's unchanged (R5-28, BL2-02, X-2.3, OQ-21 (r3), DEC-02).
- `"the expiry taint is path-level until the seat's next confirmed edit Post on the path"` — `tu1` INFLIGHT on `f.ts` expires at 120,000 after the writer wrote `f.ts` at 60,000 (`writerWroteAfterAcquire` true): `observedEdit('f.ts')` false for every later delta, including one by another tool of S; `onEditPost(S, 'f.ts')` clears it; an expiry with no writer write after acquisition sets no taint (CCI-24, INV-CCI-EXPIRY-TAINT, R1-505, R5-27, C19, Q-11).
- `"late writes are human-class with a display-only seat tag until the seat's next Stop or seat end"` — a TXN closed by `cap` or `background_return`: a later delta on `g.ts` → `attribute('g.ts')` null and `seatTag('g.ts')` = S; after S's next Stop/StopFailure or seat end → `seatTag` null; a delta with no window at all → no tag (R5-29, HLD-35, OQ-28 (r3)).
- `"editedSince lists the seat's attributed paths of the window"` — attributions at 0 (`a.ts`), 600,000 (`b.ts`) and 1,700,000 (`c.ts`): `editedSince(S, now − 1,800,000)` at now 2,000,000 → `{b.ts, c.ts}` (A8-6, input of Task 9's DEGRADED 30-min edit set).
- `"bashEditDiff is never an input"` — a source scan of `attribution.ts`, `leases.ts` and `defer.ts` finds no `bashEditDiff` and no read of `tool_response` (CCI-27).

*Engine wiring (`src/engine/core.ts`, `src/engine/writer.ts`; integration, real git against a `file://` bare remote)*
- `"tickSkip ∪ fenced: a fenced path is never re-read by a detection tick"` — `makeCores({n: 1})` with the real `DeferSet` and an INFLIGHT lease on `src/a.ts`: over 10 ticks the `FaultFs` spy shows no `lstat` or read of `src/a.ts` by the Snapshotter and `TickInput.skip` contains it; after release the next tick reads it (ENG-23, BL2-03, X-2.1).
- `"while a TXN is open the engine takes no snapshot and no apply; fetch and fold continue"` — TXN open: three iterations make ≥ 1 fetch each (P2-NOSLEEP spacing) and fold the peer's new commit, run no detection tick and no materialization (no `snapshot` trace), and write nothing (no `apply` trace); after `txnOpen()` turns false the next iteration applies (06 §3.7.3, ENG-23, BL2-03).
- `"01 test 55: an Edit reported while a Bash TXN is open is captured at the TXN close"` — an `Engine.signal({kind: 'checkpoint', source: 'edit', …})` queued while `txnOpen()` is true starts no capture; the first iteration after the close materializes and captures the bytes written during the TXN (01 test 55 TXN clause, ENG-13 (c)).
- `"the defer set enters D with its reasons and defers whole write groups"` — a peer CS changing `src/a.ts` and `src/b.ts` with `deferred()` = `{src/a.ts: 'fence'}`: the apply writes neither; `meta.pending` = `{src/a.ts: 'fence', src/b.ts: 'closure'}`; publishing of this peer's ready pair in the same iteration is not delayed; after release both apply in one group (CCI-23, ENG-31, INV-GROUP, BL2-04).
- `"a conflict take-TIP write on a leased path defers with conflicted_leased"` — a conflicting peer change to `src/c.ts` while a READ lease on it lives → `meta.pending['src/c.ts'] = 'conflicted_leased'` (01 §3.11 defer table, BL2-04).
- `"T-P5-writer-recheck: a fence acquired between apply.intent and P1 defers the whole group with reason fence and leaves the bytes unchanged"` — an intent of two groups G1 = `{src/a.ts}`, G2 = `{src/b.ts}`; a `FaultFs` hook on G1's P2 link acquires an INFLIGHT lease on `src/b.ts`: G1 `written`, G2 `{outcome: 'deferred', reason: 'fence', written: []}`, `src/b.ts` bytes unchanged, `meta.pending['src/b.ts'] = 'fence'`; the same with a TXN opened during G1 → G2 deferred `txn`; with no hit `recheck` is called exactly once per group, before P1 (T-P5-writer-recheck, 01 §3.11 P1, BL2-05).
- `"applied() is called once per written group with exactly the written paths and their detail"` — a 3-path apply in two groups → two calls; `detail[i].from`/`.to` equal the pre-apply A and the written blob, `writer` the incoming CS's writer when the engine has it, else null (A8-5).
- `"EngineCore.onHoldCode sees every raise with its params"` — `raiseHoldCodes(core, [{code: 'INCOMING_WAITING', params: {cs: 'P2/7', path: 'b.txt', reason: 'locked'}}, {code: 'CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE', params: {cs: '<S>/3', n: 2}}])` → two listener calls in order; `holdCode` and the log behave as before (A8-9, X5-41; Task 7's routing tests consume it).
- `"Read → 6 s → Write under a READ lease while a peer changes the file every 5 s: the Write succeeds and both changes land after the next cycle"` — `makeEngines({n: 2})`; `acquireRead(S, 'src/f.ts')` at 0 on peer A; peer B commits a change to `src/f.ts` at 0, 5,000 and 10,000: none applies on A while the lease lives (`meta.pending` `read_lease`); at 6,000 an INFLIGHT lease and the test's Write (an edit two lines away from B's), then `releaseRead(…, 'next_edit_post')` and `release(tu, 'post')`: after the next cycle A's file holds Claude's line and all three of B's changes, and no lease age exceeds 300,000 (06 test 18, CCI-21).
- `"a 150 s permission prompt while a peer change applies: the expired lease taints the path; the teammate line stays in the live tip and a revert-detector PATCH exists"` — on A: a full read then `acquireInflight(tu, f.ts)` at 0 with the pre-image; B changes line 3 of `f.ts`; the lease defers it until the backstop at 120,000, then it applies; at 150,000 the test writes Claude's Write content built from the old pre-image (which drops B's line) and replays its PostToolUse with no lease: Plan 3's `revertDetector` holds the removal in a `revert` PATCH, `git cat-file -p` of A's next live commit keeps B's line 3 (06 test 19, CCI-24, INV-CCI-EXPIRY-TAINT, R5-27).
- `"a 30-file codemod at 100 ms spacing never yields a live commit with 0 < k < 30 of its files; an apply waits for the TXN close; writes after the cap and from a background process are human-class with a seat tag; a formatter write within 3 s joins the CS"` — TXN open on A; 30 files written 100 ms apart while iterations run every 2.5 s: no live commit of A contains a strict subset of the 30; a B change arriving mid-run applies only after the close; one more write 121 s after the open (TXN capped) and one by a background process after its tool returned → `attribute` null with `seatTag` S; an `onEditPost` followed by a write to the same path 2 s later → `via: 'grace'` (06 test 20, TC-CCI-06, CCI-22, CCI-27).

**Crashpoints:** none new; the writer's existing `apply.intent`, `apply.group.phase1`, `apply.group.mid`, `apply.group.phase2` and `apply.between_groups` are unchanged and the P1 re-check runs after `apply.intent`. **Lint:** L-OBJ-WRITE (the only new `hash-object` argv is `hashRouteBStdin` in `src/data/blobRoutes.ts`); L-FS-PORT (`shortRead` uses the `Fs` port); L-CLOCK (all lease times from the `Clock` port).

**Notes:**
- **No git on the gate's reply path.** CCI-12 forbids a git process before a gate reply, so the INFLIGHT pre-image is taken in two steps: the gate (Task 10) calls `hold(path, read)` (bytes in memory, no git) and passes the `HeldRead` to `acquireInflight` as `preHeld` with `pre: null`; after the reply it calls `settle(h)` and `setPre(toolUseId, s)`. Task 10's latency test (zero git spawns between frame receipt and reply) is the check. Every other hook path (PostToolUse post-images, TXN close reads, deny bases) may call `take` = `settle(hold(…))` before its reply.
- `DeferSet.deferred()` is recomputed per call from memory (leases, `ctx.health.degradedPaths()`, `ctx.readSets.fullReadSet`, seat activity and `lastHookMono`, `meter.inPullMode`, the last-apply instants); it never reads a file. A coalesced or DEGRADED path enters D with the existing reason `read_lease` (R5-25); `meta.pending`'s schema and `parseMeta` are unchanged.
- `conflicted_leased` is chosen by the engine's admission (the `core.ts` edit), not by `DeferSet`: a path the defer set lists as `fence`/`read_lease` whose incoming change is a conflict take-TIP write is recorded with that reason.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces as A8-1 `HeldRead`, `DiskSnapshots.hold`/`settle`/`take`, A8-2 `InflightLease.preHeld`, `LeaseTable.setPre`, A8-3 `LeaseTable.releaseRead`/`onTxnClosed`, A8-4 `DeferSet.onTurnBoundary`/`onApplied`, A8-5 `ClaudeEnginePort.applied(paths, detail?)` and `AppliedPath`, A8-6 `AttributionWindows.editedSince`, A8-7 `hashRouteBStdin` (X5-43), A8-8 `EngineView.snapshotEntries`, A8-9 `EngineCore.onHoldCode` (X5-41), A8-10 `normalizedEqual` (BOM, CRLF and lone CR).

**Tests:**

*DEGRADED (`SeatHealth`, unit with `VirtualClock` and doubles for `ReadSets`, `LeaseTable`)*
- `"health.degraded: a forced host timeout on PreToolUse → DEGRADED once, only that seat's 30-min set deferred, another seat's applies continue, recovery after 3 matched pairs; a 15-min test inside a TXN and 5 min at a permission prompt never DEGRADED"` — seat A: `onPost(A, 'tu-9')` with no `onPre(A, 'tu-9')` → flag `DEGRADED` set once (`SeatRegistry.setFlag`), one event `DEGRADED {cause: 'host_timeout', n: 3}` (context) plus its `system` mirror, `degradedPaths()` = A's full-read set `{r.ts}` (double) ∪ `editedSince(A, now − 1,800,000)` = `{e1.ts, e2.ts}`, each mapped to A; seat B's paths are not in it; a second unmatched Post while DEGRADED adds no event; three consecutive matched `onPre`/`onPost` pairs clear the flag with no event (the cleared flag queues one health event, R5-34); integration leg (`makeEngines({n: 2})`, two seats): a peer change to `r.ts` stays pending `read_lease` (source `degraded`) while a change to B's read file applies; negative leg: seat A `MID_TURN` with an open TXN for 900,000 ms, then `AWAITING_PERMISSION` for 300,000 ms with no hook → never DEGRADED (06 test 28, CCI-34, TC-CCI-13 first half, R1-807, R2-B9).
- `"silence: 60 s without hooks while MID_TURN, pid alive, heartbeat continuing, no TXN or INFLIGHT, not awaiting permission → DEGRADED"` — at 59,999 ms after the last hook no flag; at 60,000 `DEGRADED {cause: 'silence'}`; the same with an INFLIGHT lease open, a TXN open, `AWAITING_PERMISSION`, the MCP heartbeat older than 45,000 ms, or the pid gone → none (CCI-34, 06 §3.11.5).
- `"markGateBypassed makes the seat DEGRADED with cause gate_bypassed"` — one call → `DEGRADED {cause: 'gate_bypassed', n}` once (X5-26, COO-02, X-47).
- `"hookLatencyP95Ms is null before any hook and the p95 of the daemon-side hook handling times after"` — before any hook → `null`; 100 scripted hooks whose daemon handling (frame receipt → reply write, the latency test's measure) is 1…100 ms → `95`; it reads only memory (X-64; Plan 7's doctor and status read it).

*NO_HOOKS and FREEZE `NOHOOKS` (`SeatHealth`, `Engine.setFreezeReason`)*
- `"disableAllHooks + a cowork MCP tool call → FREEZE NOHOOKS within 10 s and one notification; a session opened in a non-live repo then started → no NOHOOKS; ≥ 3 unmatched edit Posts in 10 min → freeze; lifted when every NO_HOOKS seat sends a hook"` — seat P paired by `hello` only: `onMcpToolCall(P)` at t, no hook by t + 10,000 → wiring `NO_HOOKS`, `setFreezeReason('NOHOOKS', true, 'mcp_without_hooks')` once, `recordingNotifier()` one `NT_NOHOOKS` call with `{cause: 'mcp_without_hooks'}`, every live seat gets `NOHOOKS {cause}` once as context and `system`; a hook from P at t + 9,999 instead → nothing; the started-later variant: hello at 0, tool call at 1,000, the UserPromptSubmit hook that follows `/cowork start` at 2,000 → nothing, and no SessionStart ever arriving → nothing; seat Q: unmatched edit Posts at 0, 300,000 and 599,999 → freeze; at 0, 300,000 and 600,001 → none; lift: P sends any hook → still frozen while Q stays NO_HOOKS; Q's next edit-class PreToolUse → `setFreezeReason('NOHOOKS', false, …)` once and one unfreeze event (06 test 27, CCI-35, TC-CCI-13, R1-808, R2-B10, BL2-10).
- `"static hits: disableAllHooks or allowManagedHooksOnly with cowork's hooks unobserved, or enabledPlugins disabling cowork-sync → NO_HOOKS for seats with no hook evidence; a seat whose hook carried the hit stays FULL"` — `onStaticHits(A, [{setting: 'disableAllHooks', file: 'C:\\Program Files\\ClaudeCode\\managed-settings.json', scope: 'managed', value: 'true'}])` from A's SessionStart: A stays `FULL` (its hook proves hooks run) and the hit is kept; seat P known only by `hello` for 10,000 ms → `NO_HOOKS` with cause `static:disableAllHooks` and the freeze; A's next SessionStart reporting `[]` and P ended → lifted; `allowManagedHooksOnly: true` with cowork hooks observed → no effect; `enabledPlugins: {"cowork-sync@<mkt>": false}` in `.claude/settings.local.json` → the same as `disableAllHooks`; only `setting`, `file`, `scope`, `value` are kept per hit (SEC-28, R2-B10, R2-B12 daemon side).
- `"deniedMcpServers or allowManagedMcpServersOnly blocking cowork → NO_MCP, not a FREEZE"` — `policyWiring(A, [{setting: 'deniedMcpServers', value: '["cowork"]', …}])` → wiring `NO_MCP`, no freeze reason, one health event `SEAT_NO_MCP`; `allowManagedMcpServersOnly: true` without cowork in the managed list → `NO_MCP` (SEC-29, R2-B11, 09 §3.14 item 3).
- `"crossSessionInbound refuse → HOOK_ONLY; deniedMcpServers → NO_MCP, not a FREEZE; MCP killed → one HOOK_ONLY notice, gating unchanged"` — `crossSessionInbound: refuse` (or `hold`) → `HOOK_ONLY` with `wiringCause` `cross_session_refuse` (`cross_session_hold`); `messaging_present: false` at SessionStart (no messaging env, `--bare`) → `HOOK_ONLY` cause `no_messaging`; the MCP connection lost while the pid lives and hooks keep arriving (Task 5's wiring) → exactly one `HOOK_ONLY {cause: 'mcp_lost'}` event at the seat's next hook; a second loss with the same cause → none; `/mcp` reconnect (`hello`) → `FULL`; a G2 edit before and after the loss yields the same `gate.edit` outcome (the step is not touched by wiring) (06 test 27, CCI-33, R1-806, R2-B8 notice half, SEC-24 last sentence).
- `"06 test 39 wiring half: every permission mode × crossSessionInbound cell publishes the right seat state"` — 5 modes (`default`, `acceptEdits`, `auto`, `bypassPermissions`, `plan`) × `crossSessionInbound` (unset, `accept`, `hold`, `refuse`): unset/`accept` → `FULL` with health event `SEAT_FULL`, `hold`/`refuse` → `HOOK_ONLY` with `HOOK_ONLY`; the permission mode never changes wiring; `seat_start` carries the mode as `permission_mode` (06 test 39 wiring half, CCI-33).
- `"09 test 18 policy matrix: each setting gives its seat state, NOHOOKS set and lifted, and its health event"` — rows `disableAllHooks`, `allowManagedHooksOnly`, `deniedMcpServers`, `crossSessionInbound: hold`, `crossSessionInbound: refuse`, no messaging env → (`NO_HOOKS` + FREEZE, `NO_HOOKS` + FREEZE when unobserved, `NO_MCP`, `HOOK_ONLY`, `HOOK_ONLY`, `HOOK_ONLY`), each with exactly one health event per change and the freeze lifted when the hit is gone; the `/cowork start` refusal `SEC_NOHOOKS` row is Plan 7's (X5-28) (09 test 18, SEC-28, SEC-29).
- `"setFreezeReason makes one state transition and queues one freeze event; a repeat is a no-op"` — `setFreezeReason('NOHOOKS', true, 'x')`: the state ref advances by one CAS, `meta.freeze_reasons` = `['NOHOOKS']`, the outbox has one `event{kind: 'freeze', reason_code: 'NOHOOKS'}`, `status.json` `state: 'frozen'`, `freeze_reasons: ['NOHOOKS']`; a second identical call → no CAS, no op; `false` → one CAS, `freeze_reasons` `[]`, one `event{kind: 'unfreeze', …}`; while frozen: no code publish, no daemon-initiated working-tree write, fetch and fold continue, the freeze event, `seat_end`, `release` and hold mirrors push board-only, `seat_start` waits in the outbox (XP-01, BL2-10, glossary §8, R-27 pattern).
- `"unreported writes are human-class"` — with seat A `NO_HOOKS`, a change to `h.ts` with no seat window: `hookAttribution` answers `hookAttributed('h.ts')` false and the change opens Plan 3's human CS (SEC-29 second clause, R2-B11).

*Lifetime, liveness and removal (`Liveness`, `removalShutdown`; integration)*
- `"kill every MCP server and stop hooks: no snapshot or write after 2 min, only captured trees published, no process after 10 min, last status.json down + IDLE_NO_SESSION"` — `seatHarness({peers: 1, humanLeaseMin: 0})` (R5-62), one seat with a ready pair captured at t0 − 1 s: every seat ends at t0 (`mcp_eof`), no hook after: from t0 + 120,000 no `snapshot` trace event and no working-tree write (`FaultFs` write spy), the ready pair and pending ops (`seat_end`, `release`) still publish; at t0 + 600,000 `Engine.drain('IDLE_NO_SESSION')` runs, the last `status.json` is `{state: 'down', message_code: 'IDLE_NO_SESSION'}`, `runDaemon` returns, and `<gitdir>/cowork/live` is still present (an idle exit keeps the repo live) (06 test 42 lifetime, TC-CCI-11, CCI-50 exit path, INV-CCI-NO-ORPHAN, R5-35).
- `"plugin disabled or ipc.key deleted → removal shutdown within 30 s plus one drain push; no working-tree mtime change afterwards"` — three runs: `enabledPlugins` set to disable cowork-sync in `~/.claude/settings.json`; `ipc.key` deleted; the runtime copy directory deleted: the first `Liveness.check()` after the change (≤ 30,000 ms) returns `'shutdown'`; `removalShutdown` removes this daemon's `live` marker first (trace order: marker removal before any push), then makes no snapshot or working-tree write (every working file's mtime unchanged until exit), pushes once with the captured ready trees, the `release{reason: 'stop'}` drafts of `RegionsPort.releaseClaims` (none with `noRegions`), `seat_end` per live seat and one `leave` op (board-only when frozen; no push when offline), logs `DAEMON_REMOVED`, makes one `NT_PLUGIN_REMOVED` notification and exits (09 test 23, 06 test 42 disable/key part, TC-CCI-11, CCI-53, R2-I4, R5-51, OQ-13 (r2)).
- `"Liveness reads no token, key bytes or environment value beyond its three checks"` — a canary in `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN` and in `ipc.key`'s bytes: no log, trace or status line contains either; `keyHash` returns only a SHA-256 hex of the key file; `pluginEnabledFromSettings` reads only the `enabledPlugins` key of the documented settings files (SEC-23, 09 §3.11 capability limits).
- `"live marker deleted → back within 30 s (at once on fs.watch), one security event, one LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED health event, one notification; the drain's removal restores nothing"` — `rm <gitdir>/cowork/live` outside any stop: with `fs.watch` delivering the change the marker is re-created within one event-loop turn, without it at the next 30 s check; its content equals the old content (the canonical root); `security.jsonl` gains one `LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED` line, the outbox one `event{kind: 'health', reason_code: 'LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED'}`, the notifier one `NT_LIVE_MARKER_RESTORED {repo}`; a following edit-class PreToolUse reaches the daemon again (the foreign-claim deny half is Task 18's); with `expectRemoval(true)` (the SIGTERM stop of `daemon.js run`) the removal restores nothing; `<gitdir>/cowork/` deleted → `shutdown` instead (06 test 42 marker part, CCI-53, R2-I5).
- `"killed ≤ 5 s after SessionEnd → DAEMON_DIED_WITH_HOST; a crash 60 s later → none"` — `diedWithHost([{code: 'SESSION_END', mono: 100,000}], {state: 'ok', heartbeat_mono_ms: 104,999, …})` → true; heartbeat 105,001 → false; a `SEAT_HOOK` line at 101,000 → false; `prevStatus.state: 'down'` → false; no `SESSION_END` line → false; integration: a spawned daemon whose predecessor's log and `status.json` match the first case writes `message_code: 'DAEMON_DIED_WITH_HOST'` once at start (`{host}` omitted: Plan 5 has no process-name reader); the 60 s case writes nothing (06 test 43 died-with-host part, CCI-51 doctor condition, R2-I2).
- `"daemon.js run --data-dir writes the live marker after startDispatch and removes it only at a SIGTERM stop"` — `runDaemon` with `--data-dir`: after `startDispatch` returns `started` the marker exists with the canonical root of the main worktree; SIGTERM → `expectRemoval(true)`, drain, marker removed, nothing restored; the `IDLE_NO_SESSION` drain and a version drain (`serve_then_drain`) leave it; without `--data-dir` no marker is ever written (R5-18, Q-15, X-2.8).
- `"--spawned writes nothing to stdout or stderr"` — normal run, `reuse` and `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_SQUATTER` refusal: zero bytes on both streams; logs only (R5-19, CCI-51).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-FS-PORT (`node:fs.watch` only inside `realWatch` in `src/ports/fs.ts`); L-PORTS unchanged (no process is spawned by this task).

**Notes:**
- `health.observe` (SessionStart, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, PermissionDenied, Notification): `onHook(seat)` on every event (any hook proves hooks run, CCI-35); SessionStart → `onStaticHits` and `policyWiring` with `static_policy_hits` and `messaging_present`; edit-class PreToolUse → `onPre(seat, tool_use_id)`; edit-class PostToolUse and PostToolUseFailure → `onPost`; SessionEnd → log `SESSION_END {seat, mono}` (mono = `machineMonoMs`, the heartbeat's clock) and the first hook after it `SEAT_HOOK {seat, mono}`. The HOOK_ONLY notice is queued from `Seat.wiringCause` at the seat's next hook and on `SeatHealth.tick()`, once per (seat, cause).
- NO_HOOKS trigger (2) is lifted by the seat's next edit-class PreToolUse (the gating hook is what was missing); trigger (1) by any hook of the seat; trigger (3) when no static hit remains; the freeze is lifted when every NO_HOOKS seat is lifted or ended (06 §3.11.6).
- The `live` marker stopgap is removed only by the SIGTERM/SIGINT stop of the foreground `daemon.js run` (the stand-in for Plan 7's `/cowork stop`) and by `removalShutdown`; an `IDLE_NO_SESSION` drain and a version drain keep it, because glossary §6 removes `live` only at stop, pause or uninstall and the next hook must respawn the daemon (a reading of R5-18's "clean stop or drain", flagged for the controller).

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces as A9-1 `pluginEnabledFromSettings`, A9-2 `realWatch` in `src/ports/fs.ts` (one name with A14-1; the `Fs` interface gains no `watch` member — `MarkerBackstop` takes a `watch` function, tests pass their own; X5-43, X5-46), A9-3 `removalShutdown` without `meta.stop_mode` (R5-51 as amended, X5-32), A9-4 `DaemonArgs.spawned` effects, A9-5 the NO_HOOKS trigger-(2) lift (a flagged reading), A9-6 `RegionsPort.releaseClaims` reason `'stop'` (X5-19).

**Tests:**

*Ordered evaluation, rows 1–8 and 14 (`evaluateEdit`, unit over an `EngineView` double and a `RegionsPort` spy; every outcome asserted as `{g, code, params, form}`)*
- `"one fixture per row 1–8 and 14 with precedence pairs (STATE_FILE before PORTABILITY); NOT_SHARED for every non-LIVE class with quarantine: commit with git; G2 takes an INFLIGHT lease, G1 none"` — row 1 with `ctx.versions = new VersionPolicy({pluginVersion: '0.6.1', hookProto: 3})`: an older caller `{plugin_version: '0.6.0', hook_proto: 1}` → `G1 VERSION_RESTART {v: '0.6.1'}`, `{plugin_version: '0.6.0', hook_proto: 2}` (the previous proto) passes, `{'0.6.0', 3}` passes (A5-10, A5-14); row 2: `ccVersion '2.1.269'` → `G1 CC_TOO_OLD {have: '2.1.269', need: '2.1.270'}`, `'2.1.270'` and `null` pass; row 4: `.git/cowork/live` → `G1 STATE_FILE {path}`, `.GIT/config` → `STATE_FILE` on win32 and darwin, passes row 4 on linux; row 5: `.gitignore` (SAVE_ONLY), `vendor/x.bin` (UNMANAGED), `.claude/settings.json` (QUARANTINED → reason `quarantine: commit with git`), a new `.env` (NOT_ADMITTED), a path of a `local_only` record (LOCAL_ONLY) → `G2` with note `NOT_SHARED {path, reason}`, an INFLIGHT lease, and `predict` never called (P4X-08); row 6: new path `src/aux.ts` → `G1 PORTABILITY {path, reason, suggestion}` with `EngineView.portability`'s values verbatim; row 7: `link` with mode `120000` and `core.symlinks=false` → `G1 SYMLINK {path: 'link', target: 'src/real.ts'}` (target = the short-read bytes, trimmed), with symlinks enabled → passes; row 8: `pathState` `'busy'` → `G1 PATH_BUSY {path}`; row 14: publish pause `OFFLINE` or `AUTH` → `G3`, `NONRETRYABLE` or none → `G2`; pairs: a new `.git/aux` → `STATE_FILE` (row 4 before 6), a non-LIVE new `aux` → `G2 NOT_SHARED` (row 5 before 6), `VERSION_RESTART` before `CC_TOO_OLD`; every G1 creates no lease, every G2/G3 exactly one keyed by `tool_use_id` (06 test 9 rows 1–8 and 14, CCI-13, R1-402, CCI-20 acquisition half).
- `"PATH_BUSY when a write group on the path does not finish within 250 ms"` — integration, `makeEngines({n: 2})`: a peer apply to `src/a.ts` held at `yieldPoint('apply.beforePhase2')` for 300 ms → the Edit's answer is `G1 PATH_BUSY` after ≤ 250 ms of waiting; held for 200 ms → `G2` after the group finished; `'pending'` with `meta.pending['src/a.ts'] = 'locked'` → `G2` and the reason is read from memory (no git call) (BL2-06, ENG-38, R5-30, C17, OQ-27 (r3)).
- `"the gate keeps evaluating under FREEZE and never carries a transition"` — `freezeReasons()` `['NOHOOKS']`: every fixture above gives the same outcome; no G1 text contains `FROZEN` (06 §3.5.2 FREEZE paragraph, R1-402).
- `"an unstable target read gives an INFLIGHT lease marked approximate with the second read's bytes"` — a `FaultFs` script making the target's `lstat` change between the reads of `shortRead` twice (→ `unstable`): the G2's lease has `approximate: true` and `preHeld.bytes` equal to the read-2 bytes recorded by the spy (`preHeld.unstable` true); after the reply `settle` → `setPre` stores the `DiskSnapshot` of those bytes; a stable read → `approximate: false` (06 §3.7.6 "taken from the second read", CCI-55, Review Focus 2).
- `"the gate never answers allow or ask"` — `GateOutcome`'s `g` is exactly `'G1' | 'G2' | 'G3'` (type-level); a G2/G3 draft's decision is `context` or `none` (R1-404, 06 §3.6.1).
- `"G1 reasons stay within their caps and carry no teammate free text"` — each G1 code of rows 1–8 rendered with worst-case params (200-char path, 200-char suggestion) is ≤ 800 after `sanitize(…, 'deny')`; no G1 draft contains an item of family `note` (R1-408, 06 §3.6.5).
- `"locate failure or undecided prediction answers G2"` — with `noRegions` (`predict` → `undecided`) and with a double returning `locate_failed`: `G2`, no deny, no whole-file claim (the lease's TENTATIVE fence state gets the prediction as given) (CCI-14 gate half, R1-403 use).
- `"with noRegions every edit is G2 and no TENTATIVE claim exists; every value is Plan 4-shaped"` — 50 fixtures of edit-class calls on LIVE paths → all `G2`; the leases' TENTATIVE fence state is empty; `noRegions.seatEffect` returns `{status: 'confirmed', touches: [], drafts: [], notOwnContained: <every hunk>}`; `releaseClaims({peer, seat: S}, 'explicit', ['C…'])` → `{drafts: [], refused: [{claimId, why: 'not_own'}]}` (rendered `NOT_OWN_CLAIM`); `claimView()` → null and `claimViewOf(k)` → `[]`; `mapRanges(from, to, ranges, 'local')` returns `ranges` unchanged; `verifiedLabel(id, anchor)` → null and `claimLabelText(null)` → `'?'`; `claimNotes` → `[]`; `foreignHeldOnPath(noRegions, k, p)` → false; type-level: the port has exactly the X-20 members (no `recordTentative`, `discardTentative`, `confirm`, `computeClaims`) (R5-01, P4X-01 stub, X-20, X-22, X-23).

*Wake turns (row 3, CCI-38 hook points)*
- `"row 3: a wake-turn edit consults WakeScopePort with the prediction and the user.json mode"` — `seat.turn.origin = 'wake'`: the frame computes the prediction first and calls `gateEdit(seatId, path, prediction, mode)`; `plan5WakeScope` answers `'allow'` for mode `allow` → evaluation continues; mode `deny` → `{deny: 'deny'}` → `G1 WAKE_TURN {mode: 'deny', path}`; a scripted port answering `{deny: 'allow'}` → `G1 WAKE_TURN {mode: 'allow', path}`; a human-origin turn never calls `gateEdit` (spy) (R1-906 slot, R2-C9 gate half, BL3-11 gate half, CCI-38 hook points, R5-05).

*Latency and the reply path (integration)*
- `"latency: 500 gate requests while six 1.3 MB files are applied: daemon ≤ 15 ms p95, event-loop delay ≤ 20 ms, zero G4"` — a peer applies six `generateBigBomCrlf` files through the real writer while a fake seat sends 500 edit-class PreToolUse frames over the real `Ipc` port: daemon handling time (frame receipt → reply write, measured by the test harness, not traced) p95 ≤ 15 ms, `monitorEventLoopDelay` max ≤ 20 ms, zero G4; runs on every OS, the gate is enforced on windows-latest; the end-to-end ≤ 100 ms half is Task 17's FakeHost leg (06 test 8 daemon half, TC-CCI-03, CCI-12, BL2-21, R5-32).
- `"the gate reply path spawns no git process"` — `spyRunner` over the 500 requests: zero git calls between a frame's receipt and its reply write; each G2's pre-image `settle` (one `hash-object --stdin --path` without `-w`) runs after the reply (Task 8 Notes, CCI-12, R1-401).

*Offline (G3)*
- `"G3 under OFFLINE: OFFLINE text once per episode, again at 10 min; claims stay TENTATIVE"` — integration: the bare remote renamed; after the pause `OFFLINE` is set every Edit answers `G3` and carries no OFFLINE note of its own; the seat's first hook of the episode (here the first G3) carries the `OFFLINE {offline_for_s, label: 'OFFLINE'}` transition pair once and its first hook at `offline_for_s ≥ 600` carries the repeat once (both from Task 7's `Transitions`, through `transition.deliver` on the G3 reply); later G3s carry none; the leases record their TENTATIVE predictions and no claim op is drafted until the remote returns (06 test 14, CCI-15, R1-405, glossary §25.2 item 4; the digest line is Plan 6's `DigestPort`).

*Subagents (06 test 24 subagent part)*
- `"two subagents of one seat editing the same function never deny each other"` — agents `a1` and `a2` of seat S edit `foo()` in `src/f.ts`: both `predict` queries carry `seat = S` (the agent only in `PredictQuery.agent`), never an agent id as the seat; with a `RegionsPort` double that reports a touch only for claims whose `holder.seat ≠ q.seat` and holds `a1`'s confirmed TENTATIVE claim under seat S, both answer `G2` with two INFLIGHT leases (`agentId` `a1`, `a2`); `SubagentStop(a1)` sweeps only `a1`'s lease; the row-13 deny half with a sibling seat runs in Task 11 (06 test 24 subagent part, CCI-30, R1-711, R2-A1).

*Shell gate (`classify`, `writesFiles`, `evaluateShell`)*
- `"every git verb in Bash and PowerShell spellings with cd sub &&, git -C ., env prefixes and ; chains → exit 2 SHELL_GIT; black . → SHELL_FORMATTER; black a.py on an unclaimed file passes; node …/cli.js save and revert → SHELL_COWORK_CLI; node …/cli.js status passes; rm .git/cowork/live, del .git\\cowork\\paused, Remove-Item C:\\…\\.git\\cowork\\live, echo > .git/cowork/paused, touch .git/cowork/x, cat \"$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA/ipc.key\" → SHELL_COWORK_CLI; cat .git/cowork/status.json passes"` — class (a) deny: `git stash`, `git checkout main`, `git checkout -- a.py`, `git restore a.py`, `git switch dev`, `git reset --hard`, `git pull`, `git merge x`, `git rebase main`, `git cherry-pick abc`, `git revert abc`, `git am x.patch`, `git apply x.patch`, `git bisect start`, `git commit -m x`, `git clean -fd`, `git worktree add ../w`, `git update-ref refs/cowork/x HEAD`, `git push origin refs/cowork/x`, each also as `cd sub && …`, `git -C . …`, `GIT_DIR=.git …`, `echo hi; …`, PowerShell `Set-Location sub; …` and `& git.exe …`; pass: `git stash list`, `git stash show`, `git apply --check x.patch`, `git status`, `git log`, `git diff`; class (b) deny: `black .`, `ruff --fix .`, `ruff format .`, `prettier --write .`, `eslint --fix .`, `dotnet format`, `gofmt -w src`, `clang-format -i src/*.c`; pass: `black a.py`, `prettier --write a.ts`; class (d) deny: `node C:/p/dist/cli.js save`, `node "$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT/dist/cli.js" revert s1/4`, `cowork done`, every acting verb of `ACTING_VERBS` the same way, `mv .git/cowork/live /tmp/x`, `cp x .git/cowork/live`, `type %CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA%\ipc.key`, the absolute gitdir spelled in upper case on win32 and darwin; pass: `node …/cli.js status`, `… since`, `… wrap`, `… show x`, `… queue`, `… doctor`, `… log`, `cat .git/cowork/status.json`; class (e) deny: `claude -w`, `claude --worktree x` → `WORKTREE_DENY`; each G1 has `{class, hint}` params (06 test 22, CCI-28, R1-701, TC-CCI-15 shell rows).
- `"class (c) denies only when a literal target holds a foreign HELD claim"` — `sed -i 's/a/b/' src/a.py`, `perl -pi -e 's/a/b/' src/a.py`, `echo x > src/a.py`, `echo x >> src/a.py`, `tee src/a.py`, `python -c "open('src/a.py','w')"`, PowerShell `Set-Content src/a.py x` and `Out-File src/a.py`: with a `RegionsPort` double whose `claimViewOf({peer, seat: S})` lists a foreign HELD claim on `src/a.py` (so `foreignHeldOnPath` is true) → `G1 SHELL_INPLACE {class: 'c', hint}`; none → the TXN opens; with `noRegions` → always passes (CCI-28 class (c), X-20).
- `"in a wake turn every file-writing command → WAKE_TURN in both modes; read-only commands run"` — `seat.turn.origin = 'wake'` with mode `allow` and with `deny`: `echo x > out.txt`, `black .`, `sed -i 's/a/b/' any.py` (no claim) → `G1 WAKE_TURN {mode}`; `ls`, `cat a.py`, `git status`, `npm test` → pass and open a TXN; `node gen.js` passes (an unclassified writer; the backstop at its close is Task 12's) (06 test 30 shell variant, R1-702, R5-05, COO-25).
- `"writesFiles is true exactly for class (b) and any class (c) writer or redirection"` — unit table of the above commands plus `cat a > b` (true), `grep x a.py` (false), `Get-Content a.py` (false), `Add-Content a.py x` (true) (R1-702).
- `"a passing shell call opens the TXN and answers G2, or G3 under OFFLINE/AUTH"` — `npm test` → `openTxn` once with `tool: 'Bash'`, `background: false`, `foldHeight` = the folded height, `view` = `RegionsPort.claimView()` read at this Pre (X-20); `Monitor` and PowerShell the same; `run_in_background: true` → `background: true`; a G1 opens none; every `gate.shell` and `gate.edit` run calls `renewReads(seat)` (CCI-22 open half, 06 §3.9 last paragraph).
- `"class (d) applies only to model tool calls"` — the `!` line of a typed `/cowork save` produces no PreToolUse frame in the seat harness, so nothing is denied; the same command from a model Bash call is denied (CCI-28).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** none new (the advisory list is documented as advisory, CCI-28 docs line, in `commands/cowork.md` of Task 2).

**Notes:**
- `evaluateEdit` order: row 1 (`VersionPolicy.judge` → `restart_required`), row 2, then — only in a wake turn — the row-9 locate is done early so row 3 can pass the prediction, row 3, row 4, row 5, row 6 (only for a path absent from `A`), row 7, row 8 (`await EngineView.pathState`, the one permitted wait), row 9 (`shortRead` of the target → `DiskSnapshots.hold`, then `predict(q)` with `q.viewBlob` = `ctx.views.get(seat, path)` and `q.turn` from the seat's turn origin; the claim view read here is kept with the lease, X-20), `regionRows` (rows 10–13b, Task 11), row 14 (`acquireInflight` with `preHeld`, `pre: null`, `approximate: preHeld.unstable`, and the prediction's TENTATIVE claims as the lease's fence state, X-20); after the reply `settle` → `setPre`. The only file read is the target's short read; the only wait is row 8.
- The G2/G3 note is at most one item within 800 chars, and in Plan 5 it is only `NOT_SHARED` at row 5 (the "newest pull event about the target file" is Plan 6's routing). Both OFFLINE notices of an episode — the first and the 10-minute repeat — are Task 7's transition lines, so a seat that only Reads or runs Bash at the 10-minute mark still gets the repeat (06 §3.6.2).

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces as A10-1 the TENTATIVE claims of a G2/G3 (now Plan 5 fence state on the lease, off the port, X-20), A10-2 the located disk ranges (now Plan 4's `PredictResult.diskLines`, X-20), A10-3 the gate's bytes-without-oid (kept as a flag on `RegionsPort.predict`'s `DiskSnapshotId` query for the Plan 4 reviewer, X5-15), A10-4 `regionRows`, A10-5 `plan5WakeScope`'s deny mode (R5-05 as amended; a flagged reading), A10-6 the G2/G3 note without `OFFLINE` (a flagged reading).

**Tests:**

*Rows 10–13b (`regionRows`, unit with a scripted `RegionsPort`, a `ViewBlobs` double and a held-range source; each outcome asserted as `{g, code, params, form, record}`)*
- `"HELDREG before FOREIGN CLAIM before GRANT PAUSE; a sibling seat's confirmed TENTATIVE claim earlier in local order denies like HELD; STALE and <imports> never deny"` — one prediction carrying a held-range hit, a foreign touch and an own granted touch → `HELDREG`; without the held hit → `CLAIM_HELD_DENY`; with only the granted own touch (`ownTouches[0].granted: true`) → `G1 GRANT_PAUSE {label, requester, rid}` and no record, with `requester`/`rid` taken from that claim's `ViewClaim.grant` (`grantee`, `req_id`) in the view read at the Pre and `label` from `claimLabelText` (X-20, X-23); a touch on seat T's confirmed TENTATIVE claim (T on this daemon, earlier in local order) → `CLAIM_HELD_DENY` like a HELD claim; a touch whose claim is `STALE` in the claim view, or whose `reason` is `<imports>`, is ignored by the gate (G2) even when the double reports it; the holder's own edit under an active grant to another seat → `GRANT_PAUSE`; the `PredictQuery` names the requesting seat (`q.seat = S`, the agent only in `q.agent`), never an agent as the seat; the deny's `label` param is `claimLabelText(verifiedLabel(cid, anchor))` (X-23) (06 test 9 rows 9–13b, CCI-13, R1-402, REG-19, P4X-02, X-20).
- `"MOVED: no view blob → check skipped; same region → pass; the moved.py replay → G1 MOVED"` — `ViewBlobs.get(S, p)` null → `predict` receives `q.viewBlob: null` and no `MOVED` is possible; a view blob with `prediction.moved` null → continues; `moved: {fromLabel: 'parse', toLabel: 'parseAll'}` → `G1 MOVED {path, label: 'parse'}`; `toLabel: 'TOMBSTONE'` → `MOVED` with `label: 'parse'`; the `sweep/host/inses/moved.py` replay with real locate runs in Task 18 (06 test 11, R1-122 MOVED use, R1-402 row 10).
- `"±1 line of an ACTIVE PATCH → HELDREG naming display id, origin, author, reason"` — held ranges `[[5, 7]]` on `src/t.ts` from an ON_DISK PATCH `H1` with guards `[revert]`, origin `<S2>`, `peer_authors: ['P2']`: predicted disk range `[8, 8]` → `G1 HELDREG {path: 'src/t.ts', rid: 'R' + base32c(sha256('H1'))[0:8], origin: '<S2>', author: displayName('P2'), reason: 'revert'}`; `[3, 4]` → `HELDREG`; `[9, 9]` and `[2, 3]` → continue; a PATCH with no guards or an OFF_DISK one → no held range, continue; the held ranges are whatever `EngineView.heldRanges` answers (Task 12's PostToolUse overlay; with `noRegions` the last `maskStep` ranges, R3-62) (06 test 12 HELDREG half, BL3-05, HLD-21, R5-31).
- `"locate failure skips to row 13 with no prediction and answers G2"` — `status: 'locate_failed'` → no `MOVED`, `HELDREG` or K1, `G2`; the gate itself does no locating: it passes one `PredictQuery` (`seat`, `agent`, `tool`, `input`, `path`, `disk` = the target's bytes registered as a `DiskSnapshotId`, `viewBlob`, `turn`) to `predict` exactly once per request (twice only after a SUSPECT refresh) (CCI-14, 06 §3.5.3, C18, P4X-01, X-20).
- `"K1: T1 on the first deny per cid in the turn, T1R after, the escalation clause from the 3rd deny on the shadow key"` — seat S, `turn.promptId 'p1'`, three Edits touching claim `c1` on `src/f.ts`: forms `T1`, `T1R`, `T1R`; params `{cid: 'c1', rid, holder, label, repeat: 1 | 2 | 3, escalate: false | false | true}`; a fourth on another path of `c1` → `T1R` (same cid) with `repeat: 1` for its new key; a new turn `'p2'` → `T1` again; each rendered T1 ≤ 1,050 and T1R ≤ 300 (CCI-19, R1-410, 06 §3.6.5).
- `"the shadow key's claim is the earliest intersected foreign claim; every intersected claim is listed"` — touches on `c3` (board order 7), `c1` (board order 2) and a sibling TENTATIVE `t9` → `claimId: 'c1'`, `claimIds: ['c1', 'c3', 't9']` in board then local order (R1-409 step 1, REG-30).
- `"no deny ever carries raw tool_input"` — the G1 params, the trace and the `deny-*.bin` meta contain no `old_string`, `new_string`, `content` or `edits` key and no prompt text (06 §3.6.6 last paragraph, INV-SECRET frame clause).

*Shadow post-images (`applyToolInput`, unit)*
- `"06 test 10 Plan 5 half: the shadow post-image is the tool input applied on the normalized view, mapped back to raw bytes"` — a BOM+CRLF `T9` file with an Edit whose `old_string` is `'2\n'` (LF) and `new_string` `'two\n'` → BOM and CRLF kept, line 2 `two\r\n`; straight vs curly quotes (`’` on disk, `'` in `old_string`) match; `replace_all: true` across 5 functions changes all 5; a MultiEdit chain of 3 edits applied in order; `Write` → `content` bytes; `NotebookEdit` → the notebook JSON with that cell's source replaced (whole file); an `old_string` that does not match → `null` (no deny: a K1 always has a buildable post-image, so the gate answers G2; if the call then writes inside the foreign claim anyway, the PostToolUse K2 against the claim view recorded at the Pre holds the whole CS, Task 12, and B1 stays the boundary) (06 test 10 Plan 5 half, CCI-14, R1-403).
- `"chained denials compose"` — base `T9`, deny 1 `{2:two}`, deny 2 `{6:six}`, deny 3 `{8:eight}` → the third post-image is `T9{2:two,6:six,8:eight}` and the base stays `T9` (R1-409 step 2).

*Record first (`ShadowRecorder`, integration, real git)*
- `"3 chained denied Edits → one SHADOW whose h1 is all three applied to h0, mirrored as held/<id>/h0|h1/<path>; 6 retries → 1 record, 1 event; the 3rd+ reason carries the escalation clause in T1R ≤ 300"` — `makeEngines({n: 2})`, a scripted foreign HELD claim `c1` on `src/t9.txt`: after the three replies and their conversions the registry has exactly one record: PATCH, guards `[claim:c1]`, `residence: 'OFF_DISK'`, `state: 'PARKED'`, `offer: 'pending_leftover'`, `origin: '<S>'` (`'<S>/a1'` when the Edit came from agent `a1`), `blobs.h0['src/t9.txt']` = `T9`'s blob, `blobs.h1` = `T9{2:two,6:six,8:eight}`'s blob, `displaced_by: null`; after one governed push `git ls-tree -r` of the board tip has `held/<hold_id>/h0/src/t9.txt` and `held/<hold_id>/h1/src/t9.txt`; six more identical retries change no registry head (equal `h1`, HLD-07), draft no further `hold` op beyond one per governed window and no `request` or `event` op; the working file's bytes never change; `TurnItems.unacked(S)` lists the record id once (06 test 16, TC-CCI-08, CCI-18, CCI-19, INV-ONE-EVENT, R1-409, R1-410).
- `"the tmp record is fsynced, 0600 and on the worktree's volume before the G1 reply"` — trace order: `deny_record{phase: 'prerecord'}` precedes the `hook` event of the reply; `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/deny-<id>-1.bin` exists with mode 0600 (POSIX; on win32 the `tmp/` ACL inherited from `<gitdir>/cowork/`), was written by one `writeFileExclusive` with `fsync`, and holds the meta line plus base and post bytes; a repeat writes `deny-<id>-2.bin` with the post bytes only; `convert` deletes every `deny-<id>-*.bin`; the measured fsync time is reported (budget 5 ms, not asserted on shared CI runners) (INV-CCI-RECORD-FIRST, O-RECORD-FIRST unit, CCI-18 step 3).
- `"kill -9 at deny.prerecord and right after the G1 reply, 100 trials each → the record exists after restart and reaches the board after one push"` — trial a: `KilledAt` thrown at `crashpoint('deny.prerecord')`, the daemon instance dropped, a new `ClaudeDaemon` attached over the same repo; trial b: the reply received, `convert` held by a test latch and the instance dropped before it runs; in all 200 trials `convertLeftovers()` runs first in `attach` (Task 5's pre-wired call, R5-55; this test pins the order: before the first hook frame is answered and before the first engine iteration), the SHADOW exists, and after one governed push its `held/` entries are on the remote; seeds vary the edit and the trial point (06 test 16, TC-CCI-08, O-RECORD-FIRST unit, CCI-18 step 7; real-process `kill -9` runs in Task 18's FakeHost matrix).
- `"a broken clean filter → local_only stub, no blob on the remote"` — `*.txt filter=broken` with `filter.broken.clean=false` and `required=true`: `hashRouteBStdin` fails, the record is created with guards `[claim:c1, filter-failure]` and no `h1`, the registry marks it `local_only`, the board's `hold` op is the content-free stub with no `held/` subtree, and no object reachable from any remote ref contains the post-image bytes (canary line `COWORK_CANARY_k1` in the edit) (06 test 16, INV-SECRET, CCI-18 step 5).
- `"a daemon restart mid-turn composes a repeat over the current disk bytes"` — after a restart the in-memory post-image of key `(S, c1, p)` is gone: a further deny writes a new post-image over the current disk bytes and the existing record keeps its first-deny `h0`; its `h1` becomes the new composition (no second record) (R1-410 restart case; this task's Notes).

*SUSPECT (`suspectNow`, `refreshForSuspect`, row 12)*
- `"suspectNow: own last fetch or push older than 60 s by max(Δwall, Δmono), or a suspend gap over 5 s"` — newest of `lastFetch`/`lastPush` 60,000 ms old → false; 60,001 → true; both null → true; Δmono 10,000 with Δwall 61,000 → true; `suspendGapMs` 5,001 with a fresh fetch → true, 5,000 → false (PRO-37 gate half, R5-06, Q-5 (b), C5).
- `"SUSPECT: extend within 100 ms, ≤ 7 s total; claim lost → SUSPECT_LOST; still held → passes; network blocked → G1"` — row 12 applies when `ownTouches` is non-empty and (a touch has `suspect: true` or `suspectNow()`): `extend(7000)` is called before any await (≤ 100 ms after receipt on the `VirtualClock`), a second `extend` returns false; `requestFreshFetch()` resolving at 1,200 ms with the fresh claim view still holding `c5` for S → evaluation continues at row 13 on a fresh `predict` (G2 here); the fresh view showing `c5` STALE or held by T → `G1 SUSPECT_LOST {label: verifiedLabel('c5') ?? '?', dur_s}` with `dur_s` = this PC's own staleness in whole seconds; the fetch not done by 5,000 ms → `SUSPECT_LOST`; total ≤ 7,000 ms from receipt; no other row ever extends (06 test 13, TC-CCI-04, CCI-17, R1-406, BL2-07 (a)).
- `"freshFetch resolves only after a fetch that started after the call, and its fold; requestFreshFetch stays void"` — integration: a fetch in flight when `freshFetch()` is called does not resolve it; the next fetch and its fold do; two concurrent callers share that fetch; with the remote unreachable the promise stays pending (the caller's 5 s timer decides); type-level, Plan 2's landed `requestFreshFetch()` still returns `void` and its callers (Plan 6's `createCoordinator` among them) compile unchanged (X5-07, X-77, ENG-13 (a), BL2-07 (a)).
- `"with noRegions every edit is G2 (no foreign claim exists)"` — `noRegions` bound: 100 edit-class calls with every row-12 precondition except an own touch → none extends, all `G2`, no SHADOW, no `deny.prerecord` hit (R5-01, R5-06).

*Handles and lints*
- `"diskSnapshotId handles never leave the daemon"` — over the shadow tests the captured IPC frames, logs, trace and `status.json` contain no `HeldRead`/`DiskSnapshot` field or byte content; only the `deny-*.bin` file under `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/` holds working-tree-form bytes (P4X-07, 04 §2).
- `"L-CRASHPOINTS: test/crashpoints.json equals the set of call sites"` — the file lists `deny.prerecord` once, next to Plan 2's 14 names, and a source scan finds exactly one `crashpoint('deny.prerecord')` call (L-CRASHPOINTS, 11 §3.6).

**Crashpoints:** adds exactly `deny.prerecord` (06 §3.6.6 step 3: after the fsync, before the G1 reply); `convert`'s registry commit passes Plan 3's existing `registry.cas` call sites. **Lint:** L-CRASHPOINTS; L-OBJ-WRITE (record blobs through `hashRouteBStdin` with `write: true` only, in `convert`, off the reply path).

**Notes:**
- Row order inside `regionRows`: 10, 11, 12, 13, 13b, each on the prediction of row 9 (or of the fresh `predict` after a SUSPECT refresh). Touches the gate itself ignores: a claim `STALE` in the seat's claim view and reason `<imports>` (belt and braces over REG-19; Plan 4's `predict` should already omit them).
- K1 at row 13: `prerecord` (short read of the target as the base on the first deny of the key; post-image = `applyToolInput` over the key's previous post-image, kept in memory per open key; `deny-<id>-<n>.bin` written exclusively and fsynced; `crashpoint('deny.prerecord')`) → `TurnItems.record(S, agent, 'denied', id)` → `ctx.bindings.coordHooks.onK1Deny({seat, agent, toolUseId, turnId, path, claimId, holdId: id, newRecord, holders, wholeFileCause: null, whitespaceOnly: false})` (X-40; after the pre-record, its items composed into the deny reason; `noCoordHooks` → null) → G1 with `record: id`; after the reply `convert(id)`. Row 13b asks `coordHooks.grantPaused(seat, claimId)` (X-40; `noCoordHooks` → false, so the own-grant prediction alone decides before Plan 6). The SHADOW's `hold_id` is the `id` minted at the first `prerecord` (seeded `ulid` in SIM), so the `{rid}` of the reply is final before conversion.
- Turn identity for the T1/T1R and escalation counters is `seat.turn.promptId`; a `null` prompt id (fallback (b) wake turns) uses the wake turn's start instant.
- After a daemon restart the per-key memory (previous post-image, counters) is empty: a further deny on the key of an existing SHADOW composes its post-image over the current disk bytes, keeps the record's first-deny `h0`, replaces its `h1` through `holdOutcome` (one record, never a second), and counts as the first deny of a new turn (`T1`); the record's earlier `h1` stays in the registry chain (HLD-01).

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces as A11-1 `DenyFileMeta`, `denyRecordPath` (`tmp/deny-<id>-<seq>.bin`) and `prerecord`'s `{id, repeat}`, A11-2 `applyToolInput`, A11-3 `Engine.freshFetch(): Promise<void>` beside the landed `requestFreshFetch(): void` (X5-07 as ruled by X-77), A11-4 the `filter-failure` guard of a K1 shadow whose clean filter fails (`ShadowRecorder.convert`).

**Tests:**

*Edit-class PostToolUse (`handleEditPost`; unit with doubles, integration with real git)*
- `"post.emptyOrShrunk: a 0-byte post-image confirms no claim, raises no K2, takes no checkpoint until a second stable read ≥ 300 ms later"` — pre-image 1,000 bytes, the Post's short read 0 bytes: the reply carries no block, `seatEffect` is not called, `PostOutcome.trigger` carries the 0-byte post so Task 13 records `skip: shrink`; at +300 ms (a scheduler timer after the reply) a second read: still 0 bytes and stable → confirmation and K2 run then and their results ride the seat's next hook; the file refilled to 1,000 bytes by then → the refilled bytes are the post-image; 499 bytes behaves like 0, 500 bytes does not; Review Focus 2's atomic-replace and truncate-then-write saves give the same result (06 test 46 0-byte clause, CCI-55, W2).
- `"06 test 10 post-diff half: EOL-only and BOM-only hunks create no claims and no causer events"` — pre `a\r\nb\r\n`, post `a\nb\n` (the host's Edit rewriting EOLs): `normalizedEqual` → `seatEffect` not called, no K2, no trigger, no `effect` signal; a real edit on line 2 of a BOM+CRLF file → `seatEffect({seat: S, agent, csId, path: p, pre, post, turn})` called once with `pre` = the settled pre-image and `post` = a `DiskSnapshotId` whose bytes keep BOM and CRLF (06 test 10 post-diff half, CCI-25, R1-601, X-20).
- `"confirmation tags the hunks with the open cs_id and tool_use_id; the drafts stay Plan 4's"` — after the awaited `SignalPump.send({kind: 'effect', seat: S, agent: null, toolUseId: 'tu1', paths: [p], turnOrigin: 'prompt'})` (F2 #32 #62, ruling R-F2-5), `EngineView.openCsOf(S, null)` returns `<S>/1` at once and `seatEffect` receives it as `csId`; the double's `ConfirmResult.drafts` are kept by Plan 4 (Plan 5 drafts no `claim` op and calls no `computeClaims` — EMIT is Plan 4's `emitStep` at publish, X-20); a `status: 'deferred'` result → `seatEffect` is called again for the same `csId` at the seat's next stable read; a confirmation slower than 250 ms → the reply is sent without it and its K2 result rides the seat's next hook as a mandatory `HELD_CS` event (CCI-25, R1-601, P4X-03 Plan 5 call half, 06 §2 local numbers).
- `"an unmatched Post has no pre-image: nothing is confirmed, held or checkpointed"` — a PostToolUse whose `tool_use_id` has no INFLIGHT lease → no `seatEffect`, no K2, no trigger, no grace window; the change is human-class (Task 9 sets DEGRADED) (06 §3.8.4 trigger (a), CCI-34 input).
- `"the view blob becomes the post-image and the formatter grace opens"` — after a matched Post `ViewBlobs.get(S, p)` is the post `DiskSnapshot` and `AttributionWindows.onEditPost(S, p)` was called once (R1-122, CCI-27, F05-02).
- `"held ranges are refreshed after every PostToolUse on the path"` — held ranges `[[10, 12]]` from the last `maskStep` on `S_disk[p]` = blob X; a Post on p whose post-image inserts 3 lines above line 10: with a `RegionsPort` double whose `mapRanges(X, post, [[10, 12]], 'local')` returns `[[13, 15]]`, `EngineView.heldRanges(p)` answers `[[13, 15]]` until the next materialization replaces the overlay; `TOMBSTONE` or `WHOLE_FILE` → `[[1, <line count>]]`; `noRegions.mapRanges` returns its input, so the `maskStep` ranges stay (R5-31, OQ-14 (r3), BL3-05 refresh, HLD-21).
- `"a NOT_SHARED edit is recorded as a not-shared item of the turn"` — an Edit of `.claude/settings.json` (QUARANTINED; G2 at row 5) → its Post records `TurnItems.record(S, agent, 'not_shared', '.claude/settings.json')`, takes no claim and no K2 (R5-39, CCI-42 items).

*K2 (`K2Detector`, integration with a scripted `RegionsPort` holding foreign claims)*
- `"Bash python -c rewrite of a foreign-held function: the live tip keeps the holder's version; one PIN for the whole CS; the author's bytes equal what Claude wrote; block reason names the record; a second hit joins; a claim folded between Pre and Post → no K2 (K3 at publish)"` — peer B's seat holds `foo()` in `src/f.py` (claim `c1`, folded at height 12); on A a Bash TXN opened at fold height 12 runs `python -c` rewriting `foo()`: at its Post the close attributes the change, `seatEffect`'s touches against the claim view read when the TXN opened (`Txn.view`, X-20) hit `c1` → before the reply one PIN with guard `claim:c1`, `cs_ids: [<S>/1]`, paths = the CS's LIVE paths, `h1` = their post-images through `hashRouteBStdin(…, true)`, `offer: 'pending_leftover'`, `origin: '<S>'`; the reply is `{decision: 'block'}` with `{code: 'HELD_CS', params: {rid, cs: '<S>/1', label, holder}}` ≤ 1,000; A's file bytes equal what the script wrote; after two governed windows `git cat-file -p` of the live code tip shows B's `foo()`; a second hit on `c1` in the same CS joins the record (same id, no new request or notice op); the same rewrite where `c1` is folded at height 13 (after the TXN opened at 12) → no K2, no PIN (B1 at publish is Plan 4's) (06 test 21, TC-CCI-07, CCI-26, R1-602, INV-CCI-RECORD-FIRST K2 half).
- `".envrc edited in the same turn: the PIN lists no .envrc, no .envrc blob on the board or in held/, the author's .envrc unchanged"` — the same seat first edits `.envrc` (G2 `NOT_SHARED`, a low-entropy secret line `COWORK_CANARY_env1`), then the K2 rewrite: the PIN's paths are `[src/f.py]`; after 100 loop cycles `git ls-tree -r` of the board tip and every `held/` entry shows no `.envrc`, no remote object contains `COWORK_CANARY_env1`, and A's `.envrc` bytes are unchanged (06 test 21 variant, 09 test 13 K2 case, INV-SECRET, glossary §9 LIVE-only).
- `"K2 records first: the PIN commit precedes the block reply"` — trace order: the registry `hold` event of the PIN precedes the reply's `hook` event; a `KilledAt` at Plan 3's `registry.cas` after the CAS and before the reply leaves the PIN in the registry after restart (INV-CCI-RECORD-FIRST K2 half).
- `"PostToolUse and TXN close never write the author's working tree"` — over every K2, backstop and shrink case of this task the `FaultFs` write spy records zero writes under the work tree (INV-CCI-NO-REWRITE).
- `"a TXN closed by cap or background return carries its K2 block on the seat's next hook"` — the same rewrite in a TXN capped at 120 s: no hook replies at the close; the seat's next hook carries the `HELD_CS` item once as a mandatory event (CCI-26, 06 §3.8.2 step 4).

*Wake-scope hook point (`WakeScopePort.backstop` call sites)*
- `"a wake-turn TXN close calls WakeScopePort.backstop before the reply; plan5WakeScope creates nothing"` — `seat.turn.origin = 'wake'`: an edit-class Post and a Bash TXN close (`node gen.js` writing `src/out.ts` and `src/in.ts`) each call `backstop(S, <S>/2, changes, 'post' | 'txn')` with only LIVE paths, each change carrying `h1` (its post-image hashed with `hashRouteBStdin(…, true)`) and `localOnly` (false here; true for a required-filter failure, X-41), awaited before the reply; with `plan5WakeScope` it returns null and nothing is held; with a scripted port that PINs → the reply (or, after a K2 block, the next hook) carries `OWN_CHANGE_HELD {rid, path, reason: 'wake-scope'}` (family `own_change_held_wake`, mandatory) and `TurnItems.record(S, null, 'held', rid)`; ignored build output (`dist/x.js`, SAVE_ONLY) is never passed; a human-origin turn never calls `backstop` (R1-603 hook point, BL3-11 hook sites, CCI-38 call sites, R5-05, 09 test 13 wake-scope case, 06 test 30 backstop variant Plan 5 half).

*TXN close (`closeTxn`)*
- `"a TXN close snapshots the dirty paths, attributes them to the seat's open CS and is edit-checkpoint trigger (b) only for an own-call Bash or PowerShell close"` — `S_open` with 200 entries; the command changes 3 tracked files and creates 1 untracked file: `closeTxn` short-reads exactly the 3 stat-dirty paths, takes the untracked list once, sends one `effect` signal with the 4 paths and the TXN's `tool_use_id`, calls `attributeClosed(t, paths, untilMono)`, and returns a trigger `{source: 'txn_close', paths}` for `why` ∈ `post`, `post_failure`, `background_return`, `cap` of a Bash or PowerShell TXN; a `Monitor` close, a `permission_denied` close and a `new_turn`, `stop`, `stop_failure`, `session_end` or `seat_end` close return `trigger: null`; a close that attributed nothing returns `trigger: null` (06 §3.7.3 close, §3.8.4 trigger (b), CCI-22, R1-604 close).
- `"a 30-file codemod in one TXN is one CS: no live commit holds 0 < k < 30 of its files"` — the codemod at 100 ms spacing closes by its Post; the effect carries all 30 paths; over the following governed windows every live commit holds either none or all 30 (TC-CCI-06, CCI-22).

*Reads and in-file notes (`handleReadPost`, `ReadSets`, `ViewBlobs`, `ApplyJournal`)*
- `"a full Read takes a READ lease and joins the full-read set; a ranged Read records its range by raw offset and limit"` — `read: {offset: null, limit: null, full: true, enoent: false, size: 500}` → `acquireRead(S, p)` and `fullReadSet(S)` ∋ p; `{offset: 100, limit: 50, full: false}` → no lease, `recordRange(S, p, [100, 149])`; `overlaps(S, p, [169, 175])` true (169 is 20 rows past 149, `ATTRIB_RANGE_SLACK_LINES`), `[170, 175]` false, `[60, 80]` true, `[60, 79]` false; a non-LIVE path gets no lease (CCI-21, F05-03 record half, R5-50).
- `"every Read of a LIVE path sets the view blob from one short read"` — full or ranged, non-empty, non-ENOENT → `ViewBlobs.get(S, p)` equals `take(p, shortRead(p))`; ENOENT → the view blob is cleared; `clearSeat(S)` drops all of S's (R1-122, F05-02, Q-6, 06 §2).
- `"a Read of a file with an ACTIVE PATCH returns HOLD_IN_FILE; ENOENT within 2 s of a writer replace → READ_REPLACED"` — an ON_DISK PATCH `H1` (`revert`) on `src/t.ts`: the Read's reply carries `HOLD_IN_FILE {path: 'src/t.ts', rid, reason: 'revert'}`; a Read returning `enoent: true` (or `size: 0`) for a tracked path whose apply was journaled 1,999 ms earlier → `READ_REPLACED {path}`; 2,001 ms → none; an untracked path → none (06 test 12 HOLD_IN_FILE, R2-D2, R2-D3, HLD-22, CCI-43).
- `"one coalesced ATTRIBUTION ≤ 600 for applies into a full-read file or a ranged read overlapping ± 20 lines"` — the apply journal gets `{path: 'src/a.ts', rows: [40, 44], label: '?', name: 'Minji', held: false}` from `onApplied` detail (rows from the `from`→`to` blob diff in the worker pool, `name` = `displayName(writer)`, label `claimLabelText(null)` = `?` with `noRegions`, X-23); S fully read `src/a.ts`: S's next PostToolUse (any tool) carries one `ATTRIBUTION {path, lines: '40-44', label: '?', name: 'Minji', held: false}` in the attribution slot ≤ 600; a ranged read `[10, 19]` → none, `[25, 30]` → one; two applies since S's previous tool call → one note for the newest; a `RegionsPort` double reporting the claim still HELD → `held: true`; under PULL_MODE it is suppressed (R2-D1, CCI-43, R2-D6 (c), F05-04 input).
- `"a fold-rejected hold op → HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED exactly once at the creating seat's next hook"` — a K2 PIN of seat S whose `hold` op the fold rejects (a test-written board commit with an invalid path, as Plan 3's test does): Plan 3 raises `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED` once; S's next hook carries `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED {rid}` once; ten more windows add none; with S ended first, the next seat to hook gets it once (06 test 16 mirror clause, glossary §25.2 item 9, R2-D2).
- `"a record created from the seat's own change is told once, and a PIN's paths once per turn"` — Plan 3's revert detector creates a `revert` PATCH with `origin: '<S>'`: `scanRecords` after the iteration queues `OWN_CHANGE_HELD {rid, path, reason: 'revert'}` for S once and `TurnItems.record(S, null, 'recorded', rid)`; a PIN over `<S>/3`'s paths `[a.ts, b.ts]` → `HOLD_PIN_CONTEXT {path, rid, reason}` for each path once per (seat, path, turn) (BL3-06, HLD-22, HLD-23, R2-D2).
- `"a human edit inside an own claim is told to that seat, not raised as K5"` — a `RegionsPort` double gives seat S a HELD claim `foo` on rows 10–20 of `src/f.ts`; a human-class delta on rows 12–13: S's next hook carries `HUMAN_EDIT_OWN_CLAIM {label: 'foo', a: 12, b: 13}` once and no `breach` event is drafted; with `noRegions` nothing (R2-D4, CCI-43).

*F05 prerequisites*
- `"PostToolUse(Read) does ≤ 5 ms of main-thread work and late slot items ride the next PostToolUse"` — 500 Reads of a 1.3 MB file on windows-latest: every synchronous segment of `post.read` (timed in a test build around each segment, excluding awaited I/O and worker jobs) ≤ 5 ms; a `SlotExtraPort` double whose item arrives 300 ms late pushes it into the `LateSlotQueue`, and the seat's next PostToolUse of the same turn carries it; still undelivered at that turn's Stop → dropped (F05-08, CCI-62, R2-D6, R5-08, 06 §3.13 item 7 (e)).
- `"R5-50 host check: until the unranged Read row count and the offset base are recorded, only read.full is used"` — with the default `host` (the constants `READ_HOST_DEFAULT_ROWS` and `READ_OFFSET_BASE`, both null) a ranged Read is recorded with its raw `offset`/`limit` and never converted, and an unranged Read records no range; `handleReadPost(r, ctx, {rows: 2000, offsetBase: 1})` (A12-12; ESM constants cannot be reassigned) makes an unranged Read record `[1, 2000]`, and with `offsetBase: 0` a Read `{offset: 0, limit: 10}` records `[1, 10]` (R5-50, Q-6, F05-01 schema).

*Stop items (integration)*
- `"one K2-held CS and one NOT_SHARED path by a subagent in one turn → exactly one Stop block naming both, keyed by agent_id"` — the main agent's K2 PIN `P1` and subagent `a1`'s Edit of `.claude/settings.json`: the turn's Stop (with `ids_in_last_message: []`) gets one `STOP_SUMMARY` with items `[{agent: null, kind: 'held', id: 'P1'}, {agent: 'a1', kind: 'not_shared', id: '.claude/settings.json'}]`; the denied-Edit + held-CS form of 06 test 32 needs K1 with Plan 4 claims and runs in Task 18 (06 test 24 Stop half, 06 test 32 integration, CCI-42, R2-A1 Stop half, R5-39).

**Crashpoints:** none new; PIN commits pass `registry.cas`. **Lint:** L-OBJ-WRITE (record blobs only through `hashRouteBStdin(…, true)` inside the K2 path); no `hash-object -w` on the Read path.

**Notes:**
- `post.edit` order (06 §3.8.4 step 3, record first): lease lookup → post-image short read (empty-or-shrunk rule) → `normalizedEqual` → `effect` signal (its `send` awaited, F2 #32 #62) → `openCsOf` → `seatEffect` (≤ 250 ms wait, Plan 4's `fg` pool class, X-21) → K2 (PIN, block; once the PIN exists `ctx.bindings.coordHooks.onK2({seat, agent, toolUseId, csId, holdId, claimIds, holders, foldedAtPre})` and its items join the block reason, X-40; `noCoordHooks` → `[]`) → wake-turn `backstop` (with `h1`, `localOnly`, X-41) → view blob, held-range overlay, `onEditPost` → `r.trigger` for Task 13's `checkpoint.trigger`. `txn.close` runs `closeTxn` for the Post/PostFailure/PermissionDenied of a TXN's own `tool_use_id` (releasing it through `LeaseTable.release`); every other TXN close reaches `closeTxn` through `onTxnClosed`, and its K2 block, `OWN_CHANGE_HELD` and trigger (b) go through `EventStore` and `onCloseTrigger`.
- *(F2 #32 #62, ruling R-F2-5; replaces the assumption that `queueSignal` has run when `send` returns)* `SignalPump.send` calls `Engine.signal` synchronously in send order (Task 6) and returns a promise that resolves once Plan 3's `queueSignal` has run; Plan 3 runs it behind the engine's exclusive chain, so `post.edit` awaits the effect's `send` before it reads `openCsOf` and never predicts a CS id. The wait lasts as long as an engine iteration already in flight (fetch, fold, publish); if this task's latency cells show that it threatens `CONFIRM_P95_MS` (250 ms), this task stops for a controller ruling before it changes the step order or the bound.
- `OWN_CHANGE_HELD` is the code Plan 5 selects for "a record created from this seat's own change" (06 §3.14.4 is the complete code list of this chapter); 05 HLD-23 names the same line `HOLD_NOT_SHARED`, which Plan 3 declared for Plan 5 (R3-65). Flagged for the reconciliation pass; Plan 5 never emits `HOLD_NOT_SHARED`. `HOLD_PIN_CONTEXT` (HLD-22) is emitted as written.
- `HOLD_IN_FILE` rides as the PostToolUse coordination event (≤ 1,000); HLD-22's "PostToolUse attribution ≤ 600" reading would move it into the slot after `ATTRIBUTION` (one line in `handleReadPost`), flagged.
- The R5-50 host check is a one-time scripted run against a real Claude Code ≥ 2.1.270 (an unranged Read of a 3,000-line file and a Read with `offset: 1, limit: 1`), recorded in the ledger with the host version; it is not a CI test.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces as A12-1 `HookRequest.trigger` and `onCloseTrigger`, A12-2 `EngineView.addOps`/`materializedOid`/`untracked` (`openCsOf` is Task 6's A6-6, one name), A12-3 `HeldRangeOverlay` (`set`, `get`, `clear`) and `EngineView.heldRanges` consulting it, A12-4 `AttributionWindows.attributeClosed`, A12-5 `LateSlotQueue` and `withLateQueue`, A12-6 `READ_HOST_DEFAULT_ROWS` and `READ_OFFSET_BASE`, A12-7 `noRegions.mapRanges` returning its input, A12-8 the LIVE-only full Read (`LeaseTable.acquireRead`, `handleReadPost`; a flagged reading), A12-9 `K2Detector`, A12-10 `RecordsTold` and `scanRecords`, A12-11 `HumanEdits`, A12-12 `handleReadPost`'s `host` parameter (defaulting to the R5-50 constants).

A13-11's `attachEngine` — subscribing `engine.onCandidateSettled` → `settled` and `engine.onCaptureTaken` → the `awaitCapture` waiters — and `awaitCapture`, `EditTrigger`, the re-export of `SkipReason`; registers the `checkpoint.trigger` step, which Task 5's `STEP_ORDER` already lists for PostToolUse and (A13-9) PostToolUseFailure, reading `HookRequest.trigger` set by Task 12's `post.edit`/`txn.close`; subscribes Task 12's `onCloseTrigger` for the hookless closes (120 s cap, background return), whose candidate is taken at the close — Task 12's `txnClose.ts` is not edited)
