(`LastCheck`, `LastCheckStore`, `ChangeSets.lastCheck` and their writes at HLD-67 step 4 and the T0 drop are Plan 3's, X-05/X-06.)

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces and §File Structure as A13-1 the file list (`src/engine/csStep.ts` besides `src/holds/changesets.ts`), A13-2 `ChangeSets.signal(s, aNow?)` and `verifyCandidate`, A13-3 `ChangeSets.lastCheck` (now Plan 3's, X-05: Plan 5 reads it), A13-4 `LastCheckStore.noteCandidate`/`recordFor`/`passedFor`/`sharedFor` (now members of Plan 3's store, X-05: Plan 5 calls `noteCandidate` and `recordFor`), A13-5 `SignalPump.nextCandSeq` (Task 6's A6-4, one name), A13-6 the four-argument `Engine.onCandidateSettled` and `Engine.onCaptureTaken` (X5-09), A13-7 (deleted by X-06: the T0-drop `stale` write is Plan 3's), A13-8 the trace variant `capture_late`, A13-9 `checkpoint.trigger` registered for `STEP_ORDER.PostToolUseFailure` (the entry itself ships with Task 5's `STEP_ORDER`; a flagged reading), A13-10 hookless closes through `onCloseTrigger`, A13-11 `EditCheckpoints.attachEngine` and `awaitCapture` (R5-55).


**Spec:** 06 §3.8.4 (triggers, skips 1–6, flow steps 1–6), §3.4.4, §3.7.3, §3.7.6 items 3–4, §3.12.1 (capture-at-trigger), §3.14.4 (no code on any outcome), §3.16.1 "Cost impact of edit checkpoints", CCI-22, CCI-56, INV-CCI-EDITCHECK; glossary §26.1 W6, §27.1, §29.8; 01-engine §3.5 (c), §3.8 (a), ENG-13, tests 53–55; 05-holds HLD-34, HLD-39, HLD-40, HLD-48, HLD-50, HLD-67; 04-regions §3.20 (failed entries, X5-18); 10-product §3.5.1 (`last_check`, `last_pass`, `edit_n`); 11-testing O-EDITCHECK, TC-CCI-20, TC-HLD-11, §3.9 M37, M47; rulings R5-07, R5-20, R5-21, R5-22, R5-24, R3-03, R3-41, R3-50.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/lastCheck.contract.test.ts` — the store's own behaviour tests are Plan 3 Task 10's (X-05); this file pins only what Plan 5 relies on and stops the task when it differs.
- `"Plan 3's store has the X-05 shape and one instance per ChangeSets"` — type-level: `LastCheckKind` = `'fail' | 'conflict' | 'incomplete' | 'stale' | 'skipped'`, `SkipReason` = `'shrink' | 'slow_read' | 'base_moved' | 'batch'`, `LastCheck` has exactly `edit_n`, `kind`, `check?`, `path?`, `line?`, `skip?`, `more` (glossary §29.8), and `LastCheckStore` has `record`, `get`, `pass`, `clearCs`, `noteCandidate`, `recordFor`, `passedFor`, `sharedFor`; at run time `makeEngines({n: 1})`'s `core.cs.lastCheck` is one object reused across iterations and is absent from `CsTable`, the `cs/<enc(b)>.json` cache and the state commit (X-05).
- `"noteCandidate and recordFor map cand_seq to edit_n for Plan 5's skips"` — `noteCandidate('s1/4', 7, 5)`; `recordFor('s1/4', 7, {kind: 'skipped', skip: 'slow_read', more: 0})` → `get` = `{edit_n: 5, kind: 'skipped', skip: 'slow_read', more: 0}`; `recordFor('s1/4', 99, …)` (never noted) → no change (A13-4 as members of Plan 3's store, X-05, X-06).

`test/unit/editCheckpoints.test.ts` — `EditCheckpoints` over Task 5 test doubles at the §Interfaces signatures (stub `DaemonCtx` with a recording `SignalPump`, a `VirtualClock`, a scripted `captureIdentity`), CS `s1/4` of seat `S` (32 hex).
- `"skips 1–2 record nothing, skips 3, 4 and 6 record last_check skipped, a paused seat records nothing"` — six triggers on `s1/4` (`edit_n` 1…6): (1) `HoldRegistry.nonTerminal()` holds an ON_DISK PIN with guard `claim:abcdefghij234567` whose `cs_ids` contain `s1/4` → `{skipped: 'pinned'}`, no `last_check`; (2) a non-terminal SHADOW (PATCH, guard `claim:abcdefghij234567`, OFF_DISK PARKED, `cs_ids` ∋ `s1/4`) → `{skipped: 'shadow'}`, none; (3) known post-image 0 bytes against a 1,000-byte pre-image → `{skipped: 'shrink'}`, `last_check` = `{edit_n: 3, kind: 'skipped', skip: 'shrink', more: 0}`; (4) scripted `captureIdentity` returns null → `slow_read`, `{edit_n: 4, …, skip: 'slow_read'}`; (5) `LeaseTable.openFor(S).inflight` = 1 → `batch`, `{edit_n: 5, …, skip: 'batch'}`; (6) `S.flags` has `PAUSED` → `{skipped: 'paused'}`, nothing recorded; `SignalPump.send` called 0 times; trace `edit_trigger` outcomes `pinned, shadow, shrink, slow_read, batch, paused` with `candSeq` null (06 §3.8.4 skips 1–4 and 6, CCI-56, R5-07, R5-22, C6, Q-5 (c)).
- `"a TXN other than the closing one makes a TXN-close trigger skip batch"` — trigger with `source: 'txn_close'` while `LeaseTable.txnOpen()` reports a second open TXN → `batch`; with only the closing TXN (already released) → candidate (06 §3.8.4 skip 6, CCI-22).
- `"a deny of this turn not yet converted (tmp record only) also skips as shadow"` — `TurnItems.forTurn(S)` holds `{agentId: null, kind: 'denied', id: '01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV'}` and the registry no record → `{skipped: 'shadow'}` (C6, R5-07; the K1 record exists before the reply, INV-CCI-RECORD-FIRST, but converts after it).
- `"edit_n counts every trigger, skipped ones included, and cand_seq comes from SignalPump.nextCandSeq"` — triggers: batch, candidate, candidate with a green checkpoint having drawn `cand_seq` 3 in between → `edit_n` 1, 2, 3; candidates carry `candSeq` 2 and 4; `noteCandidate` called with `(s1/4, 2, 2)` and `(s1/4, 4, 3)` (06 §2 per-CS counter, 06 §3.8.4 step 1, R5-22, A13-4, A13-5).
- `"the candidate signal carries candSeq, the CS paths and expect for every CS path"` — `EditTrigger{csId: 's1/4', paths: ['src/a.py', 'src/b.py'], known: {'src/a.py' → oid X}}` with scripted identity `{'src/a.py': X, 'src/b.py': Y}` → exactly one `send({kind: 'checkpoint', seat: S, source: 'edit', candSeq: n, paths: ['src/a.py', 'src/b.py'], expect: {'src/a.py': X, 'src/b.py': Y}})` (06 §3.8.4 step 1, R5-20, X-3.4).
- `"the per-CS queue keeps at most one in flight and one waiting; a newer trigger replaces the waiting one, never the one in flight"` — triggers T1, T2, T3 on `s1/4` with no settle: sends = [T1]; `queueState('s1/4')` = `{editN: 3, inFlight: c(T1), waiting: c(T3)}`; `settled('s1/4', c(T1), 'pass', null)` → sends = [T1, T3] (T2 never sent); a fourth trigger on `s2/1` is sent at once (queues per CS) (R5-21, 06 §3.8.4 step 3, OQ-29 (r3), F14-06).
- `"settled maps the drop reasons and records nothing for pass, fail and incomplete"` — `('dropped', 'expect')` → `last_check{skipped, slow_read}` for that candidate's `edit_n`; `('dropped', 'base_moved')` → `{skipped, base_moved}`; `('dropped', null)` → nothing; `'pass'`, `'fail'`, `'incomplete'` → `EditCheckpoints` writes nothing (Plan 3's settle wrote them, A13-4) (R5-20, R5-22, BL3-04).
- `"attachEngine subscribes the settle and capture callbacks; awaitCapture resolves true, 'late' or 'late' on timeout"` — an `Engine` double with `onCandidateSettled`/`onCaptureTaken` registries: after `attachEngine(e)` each has one listener; `e` firing `('s1/4', 3, 'pass', null)` reaches `settled` once; `awaitCapture('s1/4', 5, 5_000)` resolves `true` when `e` fires `onCaptureTaken('s1/4', 5, false)` at 1,200 ms (VirtualClock), `'late'` when it fires with `late: true`, and `'late'` at 5,000 ms when nothing fires; a capture of another `candSeq` resolves nothing (A13-11, R5-55, A14-6).
- `"onTrigger never awaits Engine.signal"` — the recording pump's engine promise never resolves; `onTrigger` resolves `'queued'` within the scripted identity time (R5-24, INV-CCI-EDITCHECK "no hook reply ever waits for an edit check").
- `"no outcome produces a code, an event, a carrier item, an outbox draft or a log line"` — spies on `EventStore.add`, `ReplyDraft.items`, `Outbox.add`, `Log` for all nine outcomes (`queued`, six skips, `settled` pass/fail) → 0 calls (CCI-56, 06 §3.14.4, R2-G3).

`test/integration/changesets.expect.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2, ports: {editCheck: scriptedEditCheck(…)}})`, signals through `EngineFixture.signal`, edits written with `writeWork`.
- `"an edit candidate whose materialized S_disk equals expect is checked and, passing, becomes the CS's ready pair"` — `checkpoint{source: 'edit', candSeq: 1, paths: ['src/calc.py'], expect: {'src/calc.py': hash(bytes)}}`; the out-of-cycle iteration's ready pair for the CS carries that oid; `onCandidateSettled(cs, 1, 'pass', null)` once (R5-20, X-3.4, 01 #53).
- `"a candidate whose S_disk differs from expect is dropped with why expect and never checked"` — the file rewritten 100 ms after the signal (before the 300 ms quiet ends); `EditCheckPort` spy 0 calls; `onCandidateSettled(cs, 1, 'dropped', 'expect')`; the state commit's ready pairs unchanged (06 §3.8.4 skip 4 "dropped, never taken late", C7, Q-1, OQ-01 (r3)).
- `"an A change on a CS path between the signal and the candidate drops it with why base_moved"` — P1 lands a change to `src/util.py` (a CS path, ≥ 2 lines from the seat's edit) between `queueSignal` and the out-of-cycle iteration; P0 applies it in a regular iteration first → `('dropped', 'base_moved')`, editCheck spy 0 calls (06 §3.8.4 skip 5, A13-2, R5-22).
- `"the 300 ms quiet period stays: the out-of-cycle iteration starts no earlier than 300 ms after the hook-named path's mtime and captures the edited bytes"` — mtime at virtual t, signal at t + 10 ms → the iteration's trace index has `monoAt` ≥ t + 300 ms (01 #55 first clause, C7, R5-20).
- `"a closing, green or tool capture that differs from its expect is captured from S_disk and reported late; one that matches is on time; one without expect is late"` — three signals (`stop`, `checkpoint{source: 'green'}`, `checkpoint{source: 'tool'}`): `onCaptureTaken(cs, n, false)` when equal; disk changed after the signal → the ready pair carries the materialized oid, `onCaptureTaken(cs, n, true)` and one `capture_late` trace event; no `expect` → `late: true` (R5-20, A13-6, A13-8).
- `"M37 site: with M37 a pending edit candidate is journaled as the ready pair without running the edit check"` — rule R1 failing; `setMutants(p0, ['M37'])` → the ready pair holds the failing candidate's R and the editCheck spy saw 0 calls; without M37 the ready pair is unchanged (BL3-09, R3-50, 11 §3.9 M37).
- `"M47 site: with M47 a failing check withdraws the CS's last passing ready pair"` — pass (cand 1) then fail (cand 2): without M47 the ready pair keeps cand 1's oids and publishes at the next window; with `setMutants(p0, ['M47'])` the CS has no ready pair and nothing publishes (BL3-09, R3-50, 11 §3.9 M47, TC-HLD-11 (c)).
- `"Plan 3's settleCandidate writes last_check from verdict.entries for Plan 5's noted edit_n; published() sets shared"` (consumer-side: Plan 5 relies on these Plan 3 writes, X-06, X-08) — the verdict scripted `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [1, 3], entries: [{check: 1, path: 'src/b.ts', line: 42, reason: 'error_node'}, {check: 3, path: 'src/c.ts', line: 7, reason: 'undefined_name'}]}` for a candidate noted `edit_n` 5 → `{edit_n: 5, kind: 'fail', check: 1, path: 'src/b.ts', line: 42, more: 1}`; Plan 3's form without `entries` `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [4]}` → `{edit_n: 5, kind: 'fail', check: 4, more: 0}` (no path, no line); `{verdict: 'incomplete'}` → `kind: 'incomplete'`; an entry whose `reason` is `merge_conflict` → `kind: 'conflict'`; a pass for `edit_n` 6 then a landed publish → `pass` = `{ready: 6, shared: 6}`; `failed` stays an array of check numbers (Plan 5 reads `entries`, never a re-typed `failed`) (X-05, X-06, X-08, BL3-04, 10 §3.5.1).
- `"Plan 3's T0 drop of an open-CS capture at publish records stale for that capture's edit_n"` (consumer-side, X-06, X-07) — a passing candidate (`edit_n` 2) ready; a scripted `T0RegionsPort` failing item 2 at publish while the CS is open, with `findings: [{check: 2, path: 'src/a.py', line: 9}]` → `{edit_n: 2, kind: 'stale', check: 2, path: 'src/a.py', line: 9, more: 0}`, without `findings` → `{edit_n: 2, kind: 'stale', more: 0}`, and nothing published; no Plan 5 file is on that path (BL3-04, glossary §29.8).
- `"no file of this task puts last_check anywhere but memory"` — after a failing check: `cs/<enc(b)>.json`, the state commit tree, `status.json`, `log/*.jsonl` and every pushed object contain neither `last_check` nor `edit_n` nor `src/b.ts:42` (glossary §29.8, INV-CCI-EDITCHECK).

`test/integration/editcheckpoint.seat.test.ts` — `seatHarness` over `makeEngines({n: 2})` with `plan5Bindings({testBuild: true})`, `noRegions`, `editCheck: scriptedEditCheck(R1, R2, R4)`; fake seat `S` on P0 driving PreToolUse/PostToolUse through `runCore`; peer P1 is the observer. Each case is 06 test 47's clause verbatim (Notes: the rules).
- `"(a) a self-contained mid-turn Edit is in the peer's live tip within 40 s with state=checkpoint"` — Edit adds `def added():\n    return 1\n` to `src/calc.py`; no Stop; within 16 rounds of 2,500 ms `git cat-file -p` of the remote live tip shows it, its `Cowork-CS` trailer carries `state=checkpoint`, and P1's disk has it (06 #47 (a), R1-605, BL3-03, TC-HLD-11 (a) SIM part — its p50/p95 latency report is Plan 7's soak).
- `"(b) syntax error then fix → no capture after the first, a capture after the second"` — Edit 1 adds `def broken(:`; Edit 2 fixes it: after Edit 1 the CS's ready pair is unchanged, no hold record, no board op, no event, `BudgetMeter.usage(S).injected` unchanged and no object written by any hook path (the iteration's candidate blobs are 01 §3.8 (a)'s, R5-20/SE5-03); after Edit 2 the fixed content publishes (06 #47 (b), 01 #54, TC-HLD-11 (b)).
- `"(c) an export rename fails check 2 until the last caller Edit; no live commit holds the renamed function with an un-updated caller"` — `a.py` renames `old_fn` → `renamed_fn`, then `b.py`'s caller, then `c.py`'s: checks fail, fail, pass; for every commit of `rev-list` of the remote live ref, `git show <c>:src/a.py`, `b.py`, `c.py` (test-side) never contain both `def renamed_fn(` and `old_fn(` (06 #47 (c), TC-HLD-11 (b)).
- `"(d) passing then failing Edit → the passing capture publishes, the failing content does not"` — the failing line `def broken(:` is in no live commit; the passing content is (06 #47 (d), TC-HLD-11 (c)).
- `"(e) 20 Edits within 2 s on one 1.3 MB file → at most 2 checks in flight or waiting, the newest passing capture published"` — `big_bomcrlf.py` (1.3 MB, BOM + CRLF), 20 Edits 100 ms apart, R1 failing on every 5th: at every trace index `queueState` has `(inFlight ? 1 : 0) + (waiting ? 1 : 0)` ≤ 2; the live tip's blob equals the newest passing candidate's `expect` (06 #47 (e), 01 #55 coalescing clause, R5-21).
- `"(g) sed -i and Set-Content checkpoint at TXN close; Monitor, PermissionDenied and an edit-class PostToolUseFailure do not"` — Bash `sed -i 's/one/uno/' src/a.py` → one `edit_trigger` with `source: 'txn_close'` at its Post; PowerShell `Set-Content src/b.ps1 'x'` → one; a `Monitor` close, a PermissionDenied of a Bash call and a PostToolUseFailure of an Edit → none (06 #47 (g), 06 §3.8.4 trigger (b), TC-CCI-20).
- `"a Bash TXN closed by its own PostToolUseFailure, by the 120 s cap or by a background return checkpoints at the close; a turn-boundary close does not"` — `sh -c "sed -i 's/one/uno/' src/a.py; exit 1"` → its PostToolUseFailure runs `checkpoint.trigger` (A13-9): one `edit_trigger{source: 'txn_close', outcome: 'candidate'}`; a Bash call writing `src/c.py` then sleeping past 120,000 ms (VirtualClock) → at the cap close, with no hook to reply to, one `edit_trigger` through `onCloseTrigger` and the candidate's `expect['src/c.py']` = the close snapshot's oid (A13-10, 06 §3.8.4 step 2); a `run_in_background` call writing `src/d.py` → one at its return; the same write with the TXN closed by the next UserPromptSubmit or by Stop → 0 (the closing capture covers it) (06 §3.7.3 close, CCI-22, R1-605 trigger (b), TC-CCI-20).
- `"(h) a K2 PIN, a wake-scope PIN or a SHADOW → no check; the SHADOW CS publishes only at its close"` — K2: a scripted `RegionsPort` whose `seatEffect` reports a foreign touch on a claim in the lease's `view` (X-20); wake-scope: a test `WakeScopePort` whose `backstop` commits `pinCs('wake-scope', …, 'wake', …)`; SHADOW: a scripted `predict` returning a foreign touch on an earlier Edit of the turn (K1 deny); the editCheck spy sees 0 calls for that CS in each case; the SHADOW CS reaches the live tip only after Stop, as HLD-48 decides (06 #47 (h), R5-07, C6, Q-5 (c)).
- `"(i) an apply to a candidate path between capture and check result drops the candidate (skip base_moved)"` — P1 publishes a change to the edited file's row 60 (≥ 2 rows from the edit) right after the Post; P0 applies it before the out-of-cycle iteration → `last_check` = `{…, kind: 'skipped', skip: 'base_moved'}` and the CS's ready pair unchanged (06 #47 (i), R5-22).
- `"(j) an edit checkpoint in a wake-originated turn inside the wake work set publishes with origin=wake"` — `scriptedRing` rings idle `S`; `fakeSeat` fires UserPromptSubmit with the posted nonce and a new `prompt_id`; `plan5WakeScope` answers `allow` (every path is inside before Plan 6, R5-05); the passing capture's live commit carries `Cowork-CS … origin=wake` (06 #47 (j); Plan 6 re-runs it with the real wake work set under O-WAKETURN).
- `"(k) a CS whose edit checks keep failing still ends as before at Stop"` — three failing Edits then Stop: with `passT0Regions` the Stop capture publishes; with a scripted `T0RegionsPort` failing item 1 → a K7 PIN (HLD-50) and nothing published (06 #47 (k), 01 #54 last clause, TC-HLD-11 (d)).
- `"(l) one parallel batch of two Edits and a Bash codemod → exactly one edit checkpoint, after the last Post or close"` — Pre(E1), Pre(E2), Pre(Bash codemod writing 30 files at 100 ms spacing), Post(E1) → `batch`, Post(E2) → `batch`, Bash Post → one candidate; trace `edit_trigger` outcomes `batch, batch, candidate`; no live commit holds 0 < k < 30 of the codemod's files (06 #47 (l), TC-CCI-06 shape).
- `"(m) a cowork_checkpoint capture after a passing edit capture is newer and is not replaced by a late-finishing check of an earlier candidate"` — edit candidate `cand_seq` 5 with a 2,000 ms stall in `scriptedEditCheck`; then `send({kind: 'checkpoint', source: 'tool', candSeq: nextCandSeq('s1/4') /* 6 */, …})`; when cand 5's check passes after the tool capture, the CS's ready pair stays the tool capture's (06 #47 (m), INV-CCI-EDITCHECK "only when its `cand_seq` is newer").
- `"expect mismatch → candidate dropped and last_check skip slow_read; edit_n counts skipped triggers"` — an editor save 50 ms after Post(E1) → `{edit_n: 1, kind: 'skipped', skip: 'slow_read'}`; the next Edit is `edit_n` 2 (R5-20, R5-22).
- `"TC-CCI-20 checkpoint half: each edit-class tool runs exactly one check; a TXN one at its close; a denied call, a PostToolUseFailure and a doorbell UserPromptSubmit none"` — one Edit, Write, MultiEdit and NotebookEdit → 4 checks; a 30-file Bash codemod → 1 check at close, 0 per write; an exit-2 denied Edit (scripted K1) → 0; an Edit's PostToolUseFailure → 0; a nonce-carrying UserPromptSubmit mid-turn → 0 checks and the CS id unchanged in `inspect().csTable` (TC-CCI-20, R1-605 triggers (a)/(b), BL3-03, O-TURN-ORIGIN CS clause).
- `"no trigger for EOL-only or BOM-only post-images, an unmatched Post or a formatter-grace write"` — CRLF→LF rewrite only, BOM added only, a Post whose `tool_use_id` no gate request saw, a formatter write 1 s after a Post (joins the CS, captured by the next trigger) → 0 `edit_trigger` events (06 §3.8.4 triggers (a), §3.8.3).
- `"subagent calls checkpoint their CS; DEGRADED and PULL_MODE seats still checkpoint and inject nothing"` — an Edit with `agent_id` `a1` → the trigger's `csId` is the CS HLD-34 assigns; `S` forced DEGRADED (Task 9 `SeatHealth` double) and, separately, in PULL_MODE (meter over 20,000) → the checkpoint runs and `BudgetMeter.usage(S)` is unchanged by it (06 §3.8.4, CCI-46, R2-G3).
- `"01 #55 TXN clause: an Edit reported while a Bash TXN is open is captured at the TXN close"` — through `fakeSeat`, not `Engine.signal`: Pre(Bash) opens the TXN; Pre/Post(Edit on `src/a.py`) → `batch`; the Bash Post closes the TXN → one candidate whose `expect['src/a.py']` is the Edit's post-image and whose capture holds it (01 #55, Plan 3 Task 12a's carry to Plan 5).
- `"01 #53: a passing Edit's candidate is journaled in one state update and published in the next governor window before the turn ends; a second passing Edit replaces it and only its content publishes"` — two state-ref updates for two candidates, the second replacing the first's pair; the live tip holds only the second's content (01 #53).
- `"01 #54: a failing Edit leaves no ready pair, record, event or message; an earlier passing capture still publishes; the caller-updating Edit passes and publishes"` (01 #54).
- `"TC-HLD-11 seat half (g): 10 min of continuously failing Edits publish nothing until a check passes or the turn ends, and CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE fires once at 300 s"` — 240 failing Edits at 2.5 s: no live commit of the CS; one `CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE` on the PostToolUse carrier at the first Post after 300 s (TC-HLD-11 (g), TC-CCI-17 shape). TC-HLD-11 (e) is Plan 3's, (a)'s latency report and (f) are Plan 7's soak.
- `"O-EDITCHECK unit form over one scripted run"` — seed `editcheck-unit-0`, 60 Edits on 6 files with R1/R2/R4 failures drawn from the seed: for every live commit whose `Cowork-CS` says `state=checkpoint`, the CS paths' blobs equal the `expect` of an `edit_trigger{outcome: 'candidate'}` whose paired `edit_check` (the next one for that `cs`; one in flight per CS makes the pairing exact) is `pass`; no live commit holds a state that existed only while a TXN was open; every failing check leaves the state ref's ready pairs unchanged (O-EDITCHECK, INV-CCI-EDITCHECK).

`test/integration/editcheckpoint.budget.test.ts` — seatHarness on the real clock (the only Task 13 file that measures time).
- `"(f) ≥ 200 PostToolUse calls with checkpoints on: every candidate capture ≤ 150 ms or dropped, a check stalled 5 s delays no reply"` — 200 Edits over `src/f00.py`…`src/f19.py`, `scriptedEditCheck` stalling every 10th check 5,000 ms: every `edit_trigger` is `candidate` or `slow_read`; each PostToolUse reply is written before its candidate settles; daemon-side reply time p95 ≤ 15 ms excluding the identity wait and max ≤ 165 ms (06 #47 (f), CCI-56, R1-311; the end-to-end ≤ 100 ms p95 half runs in Task 17's FakeHost leg on windows-latest).
- `"the edit check never sits on a reply path and stays below gate requests"` — 500 gate requests during 50 checks stalled 2,000 ms each: daemon gate p95 ≤ 15 ms, event-loop delay ≤ 20 ms; the priority assertion re-runs in Task 18 with Plan 4's pool, whose classes are `fg`/`save`/`bg` (X-21: gate parses, confirmations, edit checks and T0 run as `fg`, undo views as `bg`; Plan 5 adds no class) (OQ-29 (r3), R5-21).

**Crashpoints:** none new. The M37 (`captureStep`) and M47 (`settleCandidate`) switches exist only in test builds (`EngineDeps.testMutants`, R-29). **Lint:** none new; L-HOOK-THIN and L-FS-PORT scan `editCheckpoints.ts` unchanged.

**Notes:**
- `scriptedEditCheck(...rules)` (test helper, an `EditCheckPort`): it reads the merged tree's CS-path blobs test-side and fails **R1** check 1 while any CS path's R contains a line `def broken(:`; **R2** check 2 while a CS path defines `def renamed_fn(` and any CS path of R or any TIP file still contains `old_fn(`; **R4** = `grammarlessEditCheck`'s check-4 size clause; options `stallMs` (per call index) and a call log `{cs, a, r, tip, paths}`. It returns Plan 3's verdict with `entries` (`failed` the check numbers, `entries` `{check, path, line, reason}`, X-08) so the `check`/`path`/`line` mapping is tested now. Task 18 re-runs (b), (c) and (h) with Plan 4's `editCheck` (bound by Plan 4's own `plan4Ports(host)`, X-33).
- Skip predicates, evaluated in this order at the `checkpoint.trigger` step (after `post.edit`, `txn.close` and `lease.release`, so this call's own lease is gone): `paused` (seat flag `PAUSED`), then 1 `pinned` (a record of `HoldRegistry.nonTerminal()` with `kind: 'PIN'`, residence ON_DISK, a non-empty guard set and `cs_ids` containing `csId` — Plan 3's `HeldPin` carries no `cs_ids`, so the registry records are read directly — which includes a K2 or `wake-scope` PIN committed at this same hook, record first), 2 `shadow` (a non-terminal SHADOW whose `cs_ids` contain `csId`, or a `denied` `TurnItems` entry of this seat's current turn from an agent of this CS), 3 `shrink` (`emptyOrShrunk(pre, post)` on the edited path), 6 `batch` (`openFor(seat).inflight > 0`, or `txnOpen()` with any TXN other than a closing one), 4 `slow_read` (`captureIdentity` null). Skips 1–2 and `paused` record nothing; 3, 4, 6 record `{kind: 'skipped', skip}` for this trigger's `edit_n`; 5 is detected only at settle.
- A CS's queue state is dropped when Plan 3 fires `('dropped', null)` for a CS close; `edit_n` is kept per CS id for the daemon's life (memory only, R5-22).

- `"every description carries the board sentence and cowork_show carries F10's clause with id: string"` — each description contains `files a record on the shared cowork board; this is not a message to another session.`; `cowork_show`'s contains `a change-set id of this PC adds an undo diff`; its input schema is `{id: {type: 'string'}}`, required `['id']` (F10-02, R2-H4, R2-H7 (string only), CCI-67, TC-F10-14 schema part).
- `"the live schema stays ≤ 3,000 chars with the 80-char F05 reserve and with F05's clause merged"` — `schemaChars(toolSchemas('live')) + 80 ≤ 3,000`; `toolSchemas('live', {showClause: 'Also takes repo paths or globs (up to 20): other sessions\' held functions in them.', showIdSchema: {oneOf: [{type: 'string'}, {type: 'array', items: {type: 'string'}, maxItems: 20}]}})` ≤ 3,000 (R2-H4, OQ-09 (r2), R5-08, CCI-67, 06 #36, TC-F10-14).
- `"nonlive and too_old advertise only cowork_status within 400 chars"` — `toolSchemas('nonlive')` and `toolSchemas('too_old')` → one schema, `schemaChars` ≤ 400 (R2-H1, R1-104, INV-CCI-NONLIVE MCP half, 06 #36).
- `"argument shapes follow glossary §15"` — `cowork_request`: `mode` enum `go_first|handoff`, `est_minutes` optional integer ≥ 1, `note` optional `maxLength: 280`; `cowork_respond`: `verb` enum `accept|decline|later|release`, `note` ≤ 280; `cowork_note`: `text` ≤ 280; `cowork_release`: string (a claim id or `"all"`); `cowork_resolve`: `id` string and `mode` const `"restore"`; `cowork_status`/`cowork_checkpoint`: no properties (R2-H9…R2-H14, glossary §15).

`test/unit/mcp.doorbell.test.ts`
- `"Doorbell reads the socket and token once, from its own env, into memory"` — a `Proxy` env records reads: `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN` read exactly once, in the constructor; `messagingPresent` true with `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET` set, false without it (CCI-49, SEC-24, INV-CCI-TOKEN, R2-H15).
- `"a text beginning with / is never posted"` — `post('/cowork save x')` → `'error'` and the `InboxPoster` spy saw 0 calls; `post(' /cowork save x')` (leading space) → posted once (R2-H16, 06 #38).
- `"the poster gets only this session's socket"` — every `post` call passes the constructor's socket value; the `ring` frame schema has no socket or token field (R2-H16, SEC-24).

`test/integration/inbox.test.ts` — `realInbox` against `test/helpers/inboxSocket.ts` (real `node:net`, real clock).
- `"auth first, then exactly one user line with no session_id"` — `post(sock, 'COWORK_CANARY_TOKEN_1', 'hello')`: the stub's lines are exactly `{"type":"auth","token":"COWORK_CANARY_TOKEN_1"}` then `{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"hello"}}`; no `session_id` key anywhere (06 #38, R2-H16, TC-COO-14 MCP half).
- `"open 100 ms → sent; closed within 100 ms → refused; no listener → error"` — `open` → `'sent'` after ≥ 100 ms of the caller's clock; `close_after(30)` → `'refused'`; `none` → `'error'` (06 #38, R2-H16, INV-CLOCK: the observation is Δmono of the injected clock).
- `"the post never reads a byte back"` — the stub writes 5 bytes after the user line; the result is still `'sent'` and nothing is parsed (11 §3.2 InboxPoster row).
- `"M45 site: createInbox({frame: 'message'}) writes a message frame, and the auth-then-user assertion fails"` — the stub records `{"type":"message",…}` as the second line, so the "auth first, then exactly one user line" assertion fails for that poster and passes for `realInbox`; in a test build `dist/mcp.js` under `COWORK_TEST_MUTANT=M45` uses it, without the variable `realInbox` (R5-64, 11 §3.9 M45 frame half; the delivered-without-nonce half is Task 7's INV-CCI-DOORBELL test).

`test/integration/mcp.server.test.ts` — `runMcpServer` on `PassThrough` stdio, real `Ipc`, a `seatHarness` daemon, `setupLiveRepo`; VirtualClock except where stated.
- `"06 #36: non-live → one tool ≤ 400; creating the live marker → tools/list_changed and eight tools ≤ 3,000"` — `tools/list` before: `['cowork_status']`; `setupLiveRepo` writes `live` → one `notifications/tools/list_changed` on the `McpDeps.watch` callback (and, with a no-op `watch`, at the 30 s re-check); `tools/list` after: eight (06 #36, R2-H1 step 3, R2-H4).
- `"initialize below 2.1.270 → only cowork_status carrying CC_TOO_OLD, and the daemon gives edit-class calls CC_TOO_OLD"` — `clientInfo.version` `2.1.269`: one tool whose result carries `CC_TOO_OLD {have: '2.1.269', need: '2.1.270'}`; `hello.cc_version` = `'2.1.269'`; the seat's next Edit PreToolUse → G1 `CC_TOO_OLD`; `2.1.270` → eight tools (R1-104, R2-H1 step 2, 06 #36).
- `"hello after the handshake, heartbeats every 15 s, reconnect with the same seat_id"` — `hello{seat_id: /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/, seat_key, claude_pid, cc_version, plugin_version, hook_proto, messaging_present}`; 4 heartbeats in 60 s; the daemon closes its endpoint and rebinds → the server reconnects with backoff and sends `hello` with the same `seat_id` (R2-H1 step 4, CCI-47).
- `"06 #43 MCP half: with the daemon down the MCP server spawns nothing and the seat shows HOOK_ONLY"` — no daemon bound, hooks paused: `McpDeps` has no spawner member; a `child_process` spy over the server's run sees 0 calls; after `/cowork resume` and the next hook (which spawns through `core.js`) the seat's wiring is `HOOK_ONLY` until the server pairs, then `FULL` (06 #43, R2-H1 step 4, L-SPAWN).
- `"stdin EOF → seat_end, then exit within 1 s"` — the daemon receives `seat_end` and `runMcpServer` resolves ≤ 1,000 ms of real time after EOF (R2-H1 step 5, 06 #26 MCP half).
- `"09 #30 MCP half: without CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA the server derives the data dir and pairs; a key with mode 0644 (POSIX) or an Everyone ACE (win32) is never used"` — derived dir `<home>/.claude/plugins/data/<plugin id>` holds `ipc.key` → paired; ACL too broad → no connect, `cowork_status` → `{daemon: 'down', cause: 'SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL'}`, and the daemon marks the seat `NO_MCP` after 3,000 ms (R2-H1 step 1, TC-CCI-18 MCP half, A14-3).
- `"tools/call maps to one tool frame and every result is a non-error result"` — 8 tools × {ok, refusal}: `isError: false`, `content[0].text` ≤ 400 chars except `cowork_show` ≤ 8,000 (CCI-48, R2-H2, A14-5).
- `"daemon down → cowork_status and cowork_show answer {daemon: 'down', cause}, other tools {ok: false, code: 'DAEMON_DOWN'}"` (R2-H2, A14-3).
- `"a ring frame is posted through Doorbell and answered with ring_result"` — daemon `ring{event_ids: ['e1'], text: 'x', nonce: 'cws-0123456789abcdef'}` with the stub `open` → `ring_result{nonce, result: 'sent'}`; with no ring frame nothing is ever posted (R2-H16 MCP side, R5-37).

`test/integration/mcpTools.test.ts` — `handleTool` on `seatHarness` (`makeEngines({n: 2})`), records created through Plan 3's creators; scripted `RegionsPort`/`CoordToolsPort` doubles (at X-20's and X-39's signatures) where claims or requests are needed (before Plan 4/6).
- `"06 #37: every refusal code is a non-error result"` — `cowork_request` on another seat's record → `NOT_OWN_RECORD`; on a `RESOLVED_DISCARD` record → `TERMINAL_RECORD`; on the colliding display id of `test/fixtures/displayIdCollision.ts` → `AMBIGUOUS_ID`; on a `quarantine-review` record → `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY`; on a record spanning claims of two holder seats (scripted `claimView`) → `MULTI_HOLDER`; `cowork_respond` on a request addressed to another seat (a scripted `CoordToolsPort.respond` refusing it) → `NOT_ADDRESSED_TO_SEAT`; `cowork_release` of a foreign claim → `NOT_OWN_CLAIM`, of an own claim with unlanded changes → `RELEASE_UNLANDED`; `cowork_note` with `unavailableScan` → `NOTE_SCAN_UNAVAILABLE`, with `canaryScan` and `COWORK_CANARY_x1` → `NOTE_SECRET_HIT` (both awaited, `NoteScanPort.scan` is async, X-51); `cowork_show('nope')` → `UNKNOWN_ID`: each `{ok: false, code}` with `isError: false`; every `CoordToolsPort` member returns Plan 6's `ToolResult` shape and `handleTool` maps it to an `McpToolResult` (06 #37, R2-H2, R5-10, R5-41, R5-42, X-39).
- `"cowork_request while frozen returns queued with the FREEZE reason and publishes after the lift"` — `Engine.setFreezeReason('NOHOOKS', true, 'test')` → `{ok: true, queued: 'NOHOOKS'}` (the reason that holds the op, 06 §3.14.4), the outbox holds the `request` draft, no push carries it; after `setFreezeReason('NOHOOKS', false, …)` the next governed window lands it on the board; with `meta.freeze_reasons` `['PAUSED', 'NOHOOKS']` (test-set state) → `queued: 'PAUSED'` (glossary §8 order); a regionSpec VERSION pause with no FREEZE → `queued: 'VERSION_PAUSED'` for `request` and `note` (R2-H3, 06 #37, A14-4).
- `"MULTI_HOLDER for both modes and no request op"` — `go_first` and `handoff` on the two-holder record → `MULTI_HOLDER`; the outbox gains no `request` draft (R2-H9, 06 #37).
- `"accept on a go_first request is written as verb grant, on handoff as accept"` — `outboxCoord(ctx).respond(c, {rid, verb: 'accept'})` drafts `answer{req_id, to_holder: {seat}, verb: 'grant', by: 'claude', …}` and `verb: 'accept'` respectively (R2-H10, R5-10).
- `"cowork_note targets the newest request, else the newest HELD claim, else null"` — open request → `target: <req_id>`; none but a HELD claim → `target: <claim_id>`; neither → `target: null`; `note` text > 280 chars refused by the schema, ≤ 280 passed through `sanitize(…, 'claude')` (R2-H11, glossary §24 default 10, 06 #37).
- `"cowork_release drafts release{reason: explicit} only for the seat's own landed claims"` — through `outboxCoord(ctx).release(c, {claim_id})` → `RegionsPort.releaseClaims({peer, seat: S}, 'explicit', ['c1'] | 'all')` (Plan 4's one emitter, X-22): a scripted port with one own landed claim → one `release{reason: 'explicit'}` draft; `why: 'not_own'` → `NOT_OWN_CLAIM`, `why: 'unlanded'` → `RELEASE_UNLANDED`; with `noRegions` `cowork_release('c1')` → `{ok: false, code: 'NOT_OWN_CLAIM'}` and `cowork_release('all')` → `{ok: true}` with nothing released (R2-H12, P4X-03 Plan 5 call half, R5-10, X5-19, X-22, X-39).
- `"cowork_status within 30 s with an unchanged digest → STATUS_NO_CHANGE; otherwise the full view ≤ 400 chars"` — first call: wiring, freeze reasons, publish pause, own claims, requests in/out, held records of this seat, not-shared paths (52 paths → 50 + `and 2 more`), budget use; second call 10 s later → `STATUS_NO_CHANGE`; at 31 s → full (R2-H5, 06 #37).
- `"cowork_show resolves ids first"` — `R` + 8-char display id and the full ULID → the request view; `C<cid>`, a claim id (scripted view) and a cs id of the fixture (`s1/4` form) → their views (a cs id: author, origin, commits, paths); `'src/a.ts'` (a path, not an id) → `UNKNOWN_ID` until Plan 6's path view (R2-H6, F05-10, R2-H7 (string only)).
- `"cowork_checkpoint emits checkpoint{source: tool} with expect and returns cs_id, paths and captured"` — with an `EditCheckpoints` double: `{cs_id, paths, captured: true}` when `awaitCapture(csId, candSeq, 5_000)` resolves `true`; `'late'` when it resolves `'late'`; a scripted identity miss → `captured: 'late'` without waiting; no `checks_run` signal; the source of `mcpTools.ts` names no `onCaptureTaken` (R2-H13, F10-03, R5-20, R5-23, A14-6, A13-11).
- `"cowork_resolve of a record inside a foreign claim returns the K1 refusal and writes nothing; a valid restore writes through writeLocal in a synthetic INFLIGHT lease and joins the seat's CS"` — scripted `predict` (`PredictResult.touches`) with a foreign touch → `{ok: false, code: 'CLAIM_HELD_DENY'}`, the file's mtime and bytes unchanged; an own OFF_DISK PARKED record with guard `conflict` → `Engine.resolve(id, 'restore', {humanEvidence: false, actor: {seat: S, agent: null}})` → disk equals the held blob, trace `lease{lease: 'INFLIGHT', op: 'acquire'}` then `release` with the synthetic key, an `effect` signal naming the path (06 #37, R2-H14, BL2-20, BL3-08).
- `"restore refusals map per R5-41"` — `not_owner` → `NOT_OWN_RECORD`; `binary` → `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY`; `quarantine` → `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY`; `state` on a terminal record → `TERMINAL_RECORD`, on an ON_DISK ACTIVE record → `RESTORE_NOT_ALLOWED`; `ResolveResult.locked` → `PATH_BUSY` (R5-41, OQ-12 (r2), OQ-16 (r3), BL3-08).
- `"a restore in a wake-originated turn passes the wake-scope backstop before the result"` — a test `WakeScopePort` records `backstop(S, cs, changes, 'restore')`, each change with `h1` and `localOnly`, before `handleTool` resolves (R5-05, R2-H14, X-41).
- `"09 #26 MCP half / SEC-27: no tool reaches a branch move, a foreign release, a discard, a config change, a review accept, a scan acknowledge, a quarantined write, save, stop or pause"` — fast-check, 500 runs of random tool names and arguments: spies on `Engine.done`, `Engine.drain`, `Engine.recover`, `Engine.resolve` with any verb but `restore`, `Engine.setFreezeReason`, `Writer.writeLocal` outside a restore, and every config writer → 0 calls; the branch ref and `refs/cowork-local/*/holds` heads unchanged except by restore (SEC-27, 09 #26 MCP half, R2-H4).

`test/integration/undoView.test.ts` — `scriptedRevertView` serving the TC-F10-12 fixture (CS `s1/4` of this peer: `a.ts` in-file text, `n.ts` created, `o.ts` deleted, one kept hunk in `a.ts`, `b.ts` skipped `TEAMMATE_LINE`), `canaryScan`.
- `"TC-F10-12: cowork_show('s1/4') ≤ 8,000 chars with the undo diff and foot"` — CS summary (author, origin, commits, paths), then `T-SHOW_UNDO.head`, a `-U3` stored-form diff current → reverted for `a.ts`, one `T-SHOW_UNDO.kept` line, `T-SHOW_UNDO.skip` with `TEAMMATE_LINE` for `b.ts`, `n.ts` and `o.ts` only as `T-SHOW_UNDO.human` lines (no hunk), `T-SHOW_UNDO.foot` (code lines per R5-11 until Plan 6's templates) (TC-F10-12, CCI-68, F10-01, R2-H8).
- `"a 300-file CS drops whole paths from the end with T-SHOW_UNDO.trunc"` — result ≤ 8,000, no path cut mid-diff (TC-F10-12, CCI-68).
- `"another peer's CS → summary and T-SHOW_UNDO.notown, no diff; unknown → UNKNOWN_ID; ambiguous → AMBIGUOUS_ID with ≤ 5 qualified candidates; an own open, unshared or older-than-14-days CS → summary only"` (R5-41, OQ-17 (r3), TC-F10-12, F10-01).
- `"a canary diff line renders [redacted: secret-scan] and appears in no tool result"` — a line `COWORK_CANARY_<run>` added to `a.ts` by an unpublished human edit (TC-F10-12, CCI-68, R5-42, OQ-10 (r3)).
- `"with unavailableScan the view is the summary without diff lines"` (R5-42, OQ-11 (r2)).
- `"a second call within 30 s with TIP and the materialized blobs unchanged → SHOW_NO_CHANGE; after an apply → the full view"` (CCI-68, TC-F10-12).
- `"the view writes no object and no file"` — loose-object count and the `tmp/stage/` listing identical before and after; `a.ts` edited on disk after the last materializing iteration → one `T-SHOW_UNDO.skip` with `CHANGED_NOW`, no diff for it (CCI-68, F10-04, glossary §26.1 W6).
- `"views run one at a time below gate requests"` — two concurrent `cowork_show` calls run sequentially (second starts after the first ends); 100 gate requests during a 2,000 ms view answer with daemon p95 ≤ 15 ms (CCI-68; re-run in Task 18 with Plan 4's pool, views in its `bg` class, X-21).
- `"applying the view with Edit makes an ordinary seat CS"` — Edits of the shown hunks pass the gate, take an edit checkpoint and publish; on text paths the tree equals the fixture's reverted tree (F10-04, TC-F10-12).
- `"plan3RevertView answers summary_only until Plan 3 Task 17 lands, then delegates"` — for every own CS of the fixture, with no `src/holds/revertCs.ts` present → `summary_only` (R5-59), the `cowork_show` description still carries F10's clause and the gap is recorded for the exit evidence; once Plan 3 Task 17 (the F10 revert kernel, X-09) has merged, `plan3RevertView(core).view(cs)` returns exactly `revertView(core, cs)` and this file's TC-F10-12 cases re-run against it in Task 18 (A14-2, R5-59, X-09).

`test/integration/token.custody.test.ts`

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** none new; Task 2's L-TOKEN, L-SPAWN, L-MCP-SCHEMA and L-HOOK-THIN run on this task's files (`doorbell.ts` is the one file naming the token; `src/claude/client/mcp/**` has no spawn call; the eight schemas measure ≤ 3,000 − 80).

**Notes:** `handleTool` runs in the daemon on behalf of the calling seat; every result string is produced by `renderClaude` (code lines for codes without a seeded row, R5-11) and passed through `sanitize(…, 'claude')`; teammate notes stay inside the delimited block. `cowork_show` and `cowork_status` share the 30 s polling-guard mechanism keyed by `(seat, id)` and `(seat)` with an in-memory digest (F05-10: Plan 6 adds its `(seat, path set)` key on the same mechanism).

(8a's `src/viz/cli/dataDir.ts` is not edited by this task: X-61 deleted the body replacement and the parity test — one module now.)

"CLI requests added in rev 2.5"), §3.13, SEC-22, SEC-23, SEC-25, SEC-26, SEC-38, INV-HUMANONLY, tests 8, 26, 34, 35 (rev 2.5 final text); 10-product §2 (output modes), §3.3, §3.5 `status --flow`, §3.5.1 (inputs, `--json`, "Who sees the latest edit-check result"), FEAT-F14-05, -08, -09, FEAT-F04-18 (the `since` grant); glossary §15, §25 F5, §29.1 D1 (owner decision, final), §29.2, §29.8; 11-testing O-HUMANONLY, TC-SEC-07, TC-CCI-15, F-PEER-7, T-F14-* rows; rulings R5-13, R5-14, R5-36, R5-38, R5-53.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/evidence.test.ts` — `EvidenceStore` with a VirtualClock (`mono_ns` from `machineMonoMs`), a `TurnOrigin` double, a `MemFs` `evidence.jsonl`.
- `"mint only from a human-classified /cowork expansion"` — human-classified `{verb: 'done', args_sha256: sha256(''), prompt_id: 'p1'}` → `'minted'`; the same `prompt_id` again → `'duplicate'`; classified wake → `'refused_wake'`; an expansion carrying `cws-0123456789abcdef` → `'refused_nonce'` (SEC-25 step 2, R1-206, 09 #8, 06 #30 slash variant).
- `"await accepts an unused record ≤ 60 s old with the same verb and args_sha256, once"` — minted at t; `await({verb: 'done', argsSha256: sha256('')}, {maxAgeMs: 60_000, waitMs: 0})` at t + 59,999 → `{ok: true}`; again → `{ok: false}` (single use); a record minted at t awaited at t + 60,001 → `{ok: false}`; `args_sha256` of `01ARZ… --discard` against a record for `01ARZ… --publish` → `{ok: false}` (SEC-25, INV-HUMANONLY, 09 #8).
- `"await waits ≤ 3 s, ingesting evidence.jsonl every 50 ms"` — a tagged line appended at +2,000 ms → `{ok: true}` at ≈ +2,050; at +3,100 → `{ok: false}` after 3,000 ms (06 #45, R5-13, C.CLI_EVIDENCE_WAIT_MS).
- `"ingestFile: a verifying tag mints, a bad tag is discarded with SEC_EVIDENCE_TAG_INVALID, an untagged line only for start/doctor before ipc.key exists, used and expired lines leave the file"` — `HMAC-SHA256(key, "evidence" ‖ canonical line)`; a flipped tag bit → discarded, one security event `SEC_EVIDENCE_TAG_INVALID`; untagged `start` with no `ipc.key` → minted, untagged `save` → discarded; after `await` consumes a line and 60 s pass for another, the file holds neither (R2-J1, TC-SEC-07).
- `"grants: only a human-classified /cowork since expansion records a single-use grant, and it never counts as evidence"` — `grant(S, 'since', sha256(''), 'p2')`; `awaitGrant('since', sha256(''), {maxAgeMs: 60_000, waitMs: 0})` within 60,000 ms → true, again → false; at 60,001 → false; `await({verb: 'done', …})` never matches a grant; a human-classified `/cowork status --flow` or `/cowork wrap` expansion records no grant (the store holds none afterwards; type-level, `grant(S, 'status', …)` fails to compile); `SeatLeasePort.renewHuman` spy 0 calls (X-02, R5-14, OQ-02 (r3), R2-J3).
- `"awaitGrant waits ≤ 3 s for a since grant that lands after the request, re-checking every 50 ms"` — `awaitGrant('since', sha256(''), {maxAgeMs: 60_000, waitMs: 3_000})` called at t; `grant(…)` at t + 2,000 → resolves true at ≈ t + 2,050; a grant at t + 3,100 → resolves false at t + 3,000 and the late grant stays unused for the next matching request (R5-57, 09 §3.13 step 4, 06 §3.18, FEAT-F04-18).
- `"evidence and grants carry args_sha256 only"` — after `/cowork save "<summary>"` (summary `quarterly numbers`): the store's records, every captured frame and `evidence.jsonl` contain 0 occurrences of `quarterly numbers` and of the prompt text (09 #8, SEC-26, CCI-54).
- `"nothing comes from UserPromptSubmit"` — a UserPromptSubmit frame (only `nonces: []`) for a prompt that read `/cowork save x` mints nothing and no `await` resolves from it (R1-209, CCI-54, 09 #8 UPS case).

`test/unit/cliCaller.test.ts`
- `"tty, mode and ancestry"` — `cliCaller('tty', true, {}, walker, ['done'])` → `{mode: 'tty', tty: true, ancestry_has_claude: false}`; `cliCaller('tty', true, {CLAUDECODE: '1'}, …)` → `ancestry_has_claude: true`; `cliCaller('slash', false, {CLAUDECODE: '1'}, …)` → `{mode: 'slash', tty: false}`; `'pipe'` → `{mode: 'pipe', tty: false}`; a `src/entry/cli.ts`-level test: stdout not a TTY with `CLAUDECODE` gives `mode: 'slash'` through 8a's `outputMode`, without `CLAUDECODE` `'pipe'`; a source scan of `src/claude/client/cliClient.ts` finds no `src/viz` import (SEC-25 step 3, 10 §2, A15-6, 8a L-VIZ-IMPORT).
- `"linuxProcWalker finds a claude ancestor within 16 levels"` — a fake `/proc` tree on `MemFs`: `claude` at depth 3 → true; at depth 17 → false; a `node` process whose cmdline names the Claude Code entry point → true; on `win32`/`darwin` the walker is `() => false` and only the env test runs (R5-13, X5-35, SE5-14).
- `"verb and args_sha256 come from argv in normArgs form"` — `['resolve', '01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV', '--publish']` → `verb: 'resolve'`, `args_sha256 = sha256('"01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV" "--publish"')`; `['status', '--flow']` → `sha256('"--flow"')`; `['done']` → `sha256('')`; `['save', 'x']` (from `/cowork save "x"`) equals the loader's `args_sha256` for `command_args` `save "x"` (SEC-25, R5-38, A15-4, X-60).

`test/unit/acting.registry.test.ts`
- `"register throws on a duplicate; the evidence gate runs before the registry"` — `register('done', h)` twice → throws; `acting{verb: 'revert'}` from a fake Bash caller (`tty: false`, no evidence) → `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN`; with a minted `revert` record → `{ok: false, code: 'RF_UNKNOWN_VERB'}` in a test build and in a `--release` build alike, and the record is consumed (R5-13, R5-60, A15-2, 09 #35 revert clause).
- `"SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN names the command to retype"` — `done` → `{cmd: '/cowork done'}`; `resolve {id: '01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV', verb: 'publish'}` → `{cmd: '/cowork resolve 01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV --publish'}` (A15-3, 06 #30 slash variant).
- `"no Plan 5 handler has a preview or confirm step"` — `done` and `resolve` run with no confirm id; the registry exposes no confirm state (LIF-02's preview/confirm is Plan 7's) (R2-J4).
- `"a handler's data rides acting_result unchanged and an accepted request renews the human lease once"` — a test handler for `save` returning `{ok: true, code: 'X', params: {}, data: {preview: [1, 2], token: 't'}}` → `acting_result.data` deep-equals it and `runActing` returns it; Plan 5's `done`/`resolve` results carry no `data`; one accepted request → `renewHuman` spy exactly 1 call, a refused one → 0 (X-58).

`test/integration/acting.test.ts` — `seatHarness` (`makeEngines({n: 2})`), real `Ipc`, `runActing` from the test process; a spy on `Engine.done` and `Engine.resolve`.
- `"acting.races: late evidence found within the wait, single use, 61 s, args mismatch, UPS-only, wrong command_name or expansion_type, TTY with CLAUDECODE"` — the `!` line's `acting{verb: 'done'}` sent at t, the UserPromptExpansion frame lands at t + 2,000 → `{ok: true}` and `Engine.done` called once; a second `acting` with the same args → `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN`; evidence at t, request at t + 61,000 → refused; evidence for `resolve <id> --publish`, request `--discard` → refused; a UserPromptSubmit carrying `/cowork save x` and no expansion → nothing minted, `save` refused; an expansion frame with `command_name: 'other'` or `expansion_type: 'prompt'` is rejected by the schema and mints nothing; `tty: true, ancestry_has_claude: true` → refused (06 #45, 09 #8, TC-SEC-07, R2-J2, O-HUMANONLY unit, Review Focus 5).
- `"/cowork save typed while a doorbell is pending is wake-classified and refused"` — `scriptedRing` sends a ring (pending, recorded `prompt_id` p0); the expansion arrives with `prompt_id` p1 → no mint; `acting{verb: 'save'}` → `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN {cmd: '/cowork save'}` (06 #30 slash variant, R5-36, Review Focus 5).
- `"a model Bash node …/cli.js save run anyway is refused for lack of evidence"` — caller `{mode: 'slash', tty: false, ancestry_has_claude: true}` → `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN`; the branch ref, registry head and claims unchanged (06 #45, TC-CCI-15 daemon half, 09 #26).
- `"/cowork start in a non-live repo leaves an untagged line accepted only for start/doctor"` — no `ipc.key`: the untagged `start` line is ingested and minted once the key exists (the `start` handler itself is Plan 7's; the test registers a recording handler); a tagged line with a bad tag → discarded, `SEC_EVIDENCE_TAG_INVALID` (TC-SEC-07, R2-J1).
- `"done and resolve run their engine entries"` — `done` → `Engine.done()` once; `resolve 01ARZ… --publish|--discard|--park|--restore` → `Engine.resolve(id, verb, {humanEvidence: true, actor: 'human'})` (R5-13, Q-9, C9).
- `"every accepted acting verb renews the 60-min human lease; read-only requests renew nothing"` — with no seat, after an accepted `done` `SeatLeasePort.idleMs()` is 0 for 3,600,000 ms and > 0 after; 20 `flow`/`wrap` requests → `renewHuman` 0 calls (SEC-25 step 7, R5-35, R2-J3).
- `"no cowork process waits for UserPromptSubmit"` — a hook harness delaying UserPromptSubmit by 10,000 ms: `runActing` resolves within 3,000 ms of its request (06 #45, R1-209).

`test/integration/flow.test.ts` — `seatHarness`, a CS `s1/4` of seat 1 whose `ChangeSets.lastCheck` holds `{edit_n: 5, kind: 'fail', check: 1, path: 'src/b.ts', line: 42, more: 0}` and `last_pass {ready: 3, shared: 3}`, `syntheticFlow` bound as `FlowPort`; every assertion is on `FlowResultFrame`/`WrapResultFrame` fields and the trace, never on rendered text (Plan 7 renders `UI_FLOW_LAST_*`).
- `"T-P5-flow-last-acceptance: an all-paths status --flow request carries last_check whoever sent it; plain status and a path selector carry none"` (X-01, D1 final) — `syntheticFlow` returns, among its entries, one `stage: 'O10'` entry for `src/b.ts`. `flow{selector: {all: true, detail: 'flow'}}` sent (1) from a terminal CLI, (2) as the `!` line of a human's `/cowork status --flow` (slash mode), (3) from a fake Claude Bash call with no TTY and `CLAUDECODE` set, (4) the same under a PTY with a fake claude ancestor, (5) in `pipe` mode, (6) twice in a row and (7) with no `/cowork` expansion anywhere → every reply carries `last_check: [{cs: 's1/4', edit_n: 5, kind: 'fail', check: 1, path: 'src/b.ts', line: 42, more: 0, last_code, last_params}]` and `last_withheld: false`; no request waits (each reply is written within one scheduler turn; no `evidence{op: 'grant' | 'consume'}` trace event for `status`); (8) plain status (`detail: 'section'`) → `last_check: null`, `last_withheld: true` (the O10 entry is present), no `params.last_code` anywhere in the reply; (9) `flow{selector: {path: 'src/b.ts'}}` → `last_check: null`, `last_withheld: true`; (10) with the O10 entry removed from `syntheticFlow`, `detail: 'section'` → `last_check: null`, `last_withheld: false`; (11) the `flow` frame carries no `tty`, `ancestry_has_claude`, `mode`, `verb` or `args_sha256` field (schema) (T-P5-flow-last-acceptance, T-F14-claude-fail daemon half, 09 #35, DEC-01, F-D1, R2-K7, Q-2, OQ-02 (r3), OQ-16 (r2), R5-14, R5-57, X-01, Review Focus 5).
- `"wrap carries last_check on every reply"` — `wrap` sent from a terminal, as a slash `!` line and from a fake Claude Bash call → each `wrap_result` has `last_check` equal to case (1) above and `rows` = `noWrap.rows()`; the frame has no `last_withheld` field (R5-14, X-01, X-57, 09 #35).
- `"slash_shown charges the seat's slash meter and nothing else"` — `slash_shown{seat_key: K, chars: 1_234}` for the seat whose key is `K`: `BudgetMeter.usage(S).slashShown` grows by 1,234, `injected` unchanged, no `PULL_MODE`; no reply frame is written on the connection; an unknown `seat_key` → nothing charged, no refusal; a `slash_shown` after a reply carrying `last_check` is charged the same way (R5-58, CCI-46, 10 §3.3).
- `"a path selector failing validPathSyntax is refused FLOW_PATH_INVALID"` — `{path: '../x'}`, `{path: 'C:/x'}` and `{path: 'a\\b'}` → `refused{code: 'FLOW_PATH_INVALID'}` with no entries; `{path: 'src/b.ts'}` → a `flow_result` (09 §3.11, SEC-35, R5-60).
- `"last_check reaches no carrier, mirror, notification, board, status.json, log or file"` — after (1), (2) and (3): carrier outputs, `systemMessage`s, the outbox, `status.json`, `log/*.jsonl` and every file under `<gitdir>/cowork/` contain neither `src/b.ts:42` nor `last_check`; doorbells 0 (R5-14, INV-CCI-EDITCHECK, T-F14-claude-fail spies daemon half).
- `"T-F14-caps-perf IPC half: 3,000 entries → 2,000 and omitted 1,000 inside 1 MiB, no git process, ≤ 20 ms event-loop blocks, nothing changed"` — `syntheticFlow(3_000)` called once with `max = C.FLOW_MAX_ENTRIES` (X-74): `entries.length` 2,000, `omitted` 1,000, encoded frame < 1,048,576 bytes; a spawn spy on the `GitRunner` sees 0 processes during `handleFlow`; `monitorEventLoopDelay` max ≤ 20 ms; refs, registry head, state ref and worktree mtimes identical before and after (T-F14-caps-perf IPC half, 09 #34, R1-305, R2-K6, F14-01, FEAT-F14-05, R5-53, OQ-24 (r3)).
- `"noFlowEntries answers from memory: entries [], omitted 0, tip_height and fetched_age_ms from the last iteration"` — `Fs` read spy 0 calls during the request (R5-53, BL2-17, FEAT-F14-05, X-74).
- `"T-F14-secret daemon half: no canary in any captured frame; hostile path and names only as typed params"` — a file carrying `COWORK_CANARY_<run>` and a syntax error, a path `"\u001b[31m‮/cowork save.ts"`, a teammate display name of 64 chars with ANSI: captured frames hold 0 canary occurrences and no raw `0x1B` byte (the path is a JSON-escaped param); the display name is `sanitize(…, 'terminal')`-clean and ≤ 20 chars (T-F14-secret daemon half, 09 #34, FEAT-F14-08).
- `"T-F14-silent: carrier output is byte-identical whether or not anyone runs status --flow"` — two seeded runs of the same 40-call script, one with a `flow` request after every 5th call: every reply's `text`/`system` identical (T-F14-silent, F14-07, FEAT-F14-09).
- `"ClaudeFlowInputs: seat numbers, activity, PULL_MODE, full-read sets with the last-apply instant, defer tags, TXN-open instant and the edit-check queue"` — two seats → `number` 1 and 2 (`{s}` "Claude session 1/2"); seat 1 `MID_TURN` with `src/r.ts` fully read and applied 30 s ago → `lastApplyIntoReadSetMono` = that instant; an INFLIGHT fence → tag `fence`; an open TXN → `txnOpenSinceMono` set; edit 6 queued behind a running check → `editChecks[0]` = `{csId: 's1/4', seatNumber: 1, editN: 6, inFlight, waiting}` (F14-03, F14-02, R5-33, R5-53, T-F14-incoming-deferred (b), (d), (e) daemon inputs).
- `"09 #35: wrap, since and status --flow need no evidence, consume none and change nothing; revert from Bash is refused, and its confirm needs its own evidence"` — `EvidenceStore` records unchanged; refs, registry, worktree unchanged; `acting{verb: 'revert'}` from a Bash caller → `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN`; `revert … --confirm=<id>` with no fresh evidence → `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN` (09 #35, R2-J3, A15-2).
- `"O-HUMANONLY unit: over the trace of this file's acting cases every accepted acting event has route tty or a consumed human-classified mint ≤ 60 s earlier, each mint consumed at most once"` (O-HUMANONLY unit, INV-HUMANONLY).

`test/integration/cli.dispatch.test.ts` — the bundled `dist/cli.js` as a child process against a `seatHarness` daemon.
- `"done and resolve dispatch to acting frames and print the result code line"` — `node dist/cli.js done` on a pty-less pipe without evidence → stderr carries the `CLI_EN_ROWS` line `[cowork-sync] SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN cmd=/cowork done`, exit 1; with a minted record → exit 0 and `Engine.done` called; run with `CLAUDECODE=1` and a non-TTY stdout (slash mode) the daemon receives one `slash_shown` whose `chars` equals the printed length and whose `seat_key` is `seatKeyOf(env, ppid)` (R5-13, R5-58, A15-7).
- `"every code runActing can print renders without UI_INTERNAL_ERROR"` — for each key of `CLI_EN_ROWS`, `render(code, <typed params>, 'terminal', 'en')` after `registerCatalog('en', CLI_EN_ROWS)` contains the code and no `UI_INTERNAL_ERROR` text; the set of keys equals `SEC_REFUSE_NOT_HUMAN`, `RF_UNKNOWN_VERB`, `DAEMON_DOWN`, `SEC_REFUSE_IPC_ACL` plus the `Engine.done`/`Engine.resolve` result and refusal codes Task 1 re-baselined (A15-7, 8a `render` step 1).
- `"cli.js view and the Plan 5 CLI resolve one data directory"` — the `done`/`resolve` dispatch and 8a's `view` both call `src/data/pluginDataDir.ts`'s `resolveDataDir` (a source scan of `src/entry/cli.ts` and `src/viz/cli/**` finds no other data-directory derivation, and `src/viz/cli/dataDir.ts`'s `resolveDataDir` is the same function object); for a table of env × home inputs (`CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` absolute, relative or unset; `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` absolute, relative or unset; `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` of the cache shape, of another shape or unset with the bundle at `<home>/.claude/plugins/cache/test-market/cowork-sync/0.6.0/dist/cli.js`) the bundled `dist/cli.js done` reads the key from that directory, or refuses when it is null (R5-56, X-61, SE5-21; the 8a-vs-Plan 5 parity test is deleted).
- `"--version, the no-argument no-op, view and since dispatch lines are unchanged"` — `--version` prints `cowork-sync cli <version>`; no argument exits 0 with no output; 8a's `view` and F04-a's `since` lines still dispatch (BL2-13, Plan 1 `build.test.ts` rows, A15-1).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** none new; L-HUMAN-ONLY (Task 2) passes on `acting.ts` (every acting path goes through `ActingVerbRegistry.handle`).

**Notes:** `handleFlow` builds `ClaudeFlowInputs` from memory only (`SeatRegistry.seats()`, `BudgetMeter.inPullMode`, `ReadSets`, `DeferSet.tags()`, `LeaseTable.txnOpenSinceMono()`, `EditCheckpoints.queueState`, Plan 3's `cs.lastCheck`), calls `FlowPort.entries(claude, selector, C.FLOW_MAX_ENTRIES)` once (X-74; the port body, Plan 7's `flowEntries` over Plan 3 Task 18's `Engine.flowInputs()`, keeps the event-loop block ≤ 20 ms), and fills `last_check` from the store for `detail: 'flow'` and `wrap` only, with no evidence, grant or route check and no wait (X-01); `last_check` slots are one per open seat CS (`more` from the store), with `last_code`/`last_params` for `--json`; `last_withheld` is "an O10 entry and no `last_check`". `HumanActivity.sinceRan` (Task 16) is the only caller of `awaitGrant` (X-02). Plan 7 registers only its own request kinds (X-57) and binds `WrapPort.rows(caller)` (the caller's `seatKey` is wrap's issuing seat, V-7); this task also produces `EvidenceStore.mintedWithin(verb, atMonoMs, windowMs)` (V-7, Plan 7 Task 7's paused-marker check) with the test `"mintedWithin is true for a pause expansion minted 59 s before or after the instant, false at 61 s, and stays answerable after the record is consumed"`; its `done`/`resolve` handlers replace `registerPlan5Verbs`' two lines in Plan 7 Task 11 (X-59).

`test/unit/meta.since.test.ts`
- `"01 #6 unit half: meta.since absent reads as no marks; present round-trips with meta.v unchanged; null members accepted"` — over F04-a's landed parse (X-75; this test pins the contract Plan 5's writes rely on and edits no Plan 2 file): `parseMeta` of a Plan 2 meta blob without `since` → `since` undefined; `{seen_h: 40, return_h: null, run_h: null}` round-trips byte-identical through `serializeMeta`/`parseMeta`; `meta.v` unchanged; `seen_h: -1` or `1.5` → `parseMeta` throws (01 #6, BL2-11, R5-52, OQ-11, X-2.4, XP-04).

`test/unit/since.activity.test.ts` — `HumanActivity` over a VirtualClock (wall and mono driven separately), a `markSince` spy and `foldedHeight` script.
- `"human activity is exactly: an empty-nonce UPS, a non-wake /cowork expansion of any verb, a human-CS opening whose first delta is not all seat-tagged"` — each moves `seen_h` to `fh`; a nonce-carrying UPS, a hook of a wake turn, a doorbell and a human-CS opening whose every first-delta path has a seat tag move nothing (R1-907, R2-C7, F04-01, FEAT-F04-04, BL3-13).
- `"the first activity ever sets seen_h and makes no recap pending"` — `marks()` `{seen_h: null, …}` → activity at `fh` 12 → `{seen_h: 12, return_h: null, run_h: null}`, `takeRecap` → null (R5-52, OQ-11).
- `"a return is activity after ≥ 30 min of max(Δwall, Δmono), or the first after daemon start"` — gap 29 min (both clocks) → no return; 31 min → `return_h := seen_h`, `seen_h := fh`, one recap pending; Δmono 10 min with Δwall 50 min (a 40-min suspend gap) → return; the first activity after construction with a persisted `seen_h` → return (F04-03, FEAT-F04-05, FEAT-F04-12, R1-907).
- `"a newer return replaces an undelivered recap; a return that is a /cowork since expansion drops it"` — two returns without a human UPS between → one pending recap with the second `from`; a return by `onActivity('expansion', S, {sinceExpansion: true})` → no pending recap (F04-04, FEAT-F04-05).
- `"run_h moves only by an accepted since_ran"` — `sinceRan({to: 55, mode: 'tty', tty: true, ancestry_has_claude: false, …})` → `run_h` 55; `slash` with a `since` grant → moved, also when the grant lands 2,000 ms after the request (`awaitGrant`, R5-57); `slash` with no grant by 3,000 ms, `pipe`, Bash (`ancestry_has_claude: true`) → unchanged, no refusal (R1-908, R2-C8, FEAT-F04-18, F04-11).
- `"OQ-28 seat-tag window: a background write after a TXN cap is tagged until the seat's next Stop"` — `AttributionWindows.seatTag('build/out.js')` = `S` after a cap close and before `S`'s Stop → the human-CS opening it causes is not activity; the same write after `S`'s Stop → activity (R5-29, OQ-28 (r3), BL3-13).
- `"markSince is called with the marks after every change and never with a wall time"` — the argument is three heights or nulls only (FEAT-F04-03, ENG-05, FEAT-F04-12).

`test/integration/since.loop.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2})`.
- `"01 #6 end of the loop half: only meta.since changed → no state-ref update per loop and exactly one CAS at drain"` — `markSince({seen_h: 3, …})` then 10 idle `rounds`: the state ref's reflog gains 0 entries; `drain('IDLE_NO_SESSION')` → exactly 1 reflog entry whose commit's meta differs from its parent's only in `since`; a transition committed for another reason carries the marks with no extra commit (01 #6, A16-1, BL2-11, F04-08, XP-04).

`test/integration/since.accumulator.test.ts` — always runs (F04-a is a prerequisite of this task, R5-61, A16-2).
- `"T-P5-recap-equals-batch: the accumulator after N loop folds equals sinceReport over the same range, canonical JSON byte-equal"` — 50 board commits folded in 7 loop batches → `RecapAccumulator.snapshot(from)` and `sinceFinish(sinceInit(…) + every SinceStep)` over `(from, tip]` serialize byte-identical (T-P5-recap-equals-batch, F04-09, FEAT-F04-07).
- `"a refold restarts the accumulator from seen_h in the background; the recap waits for the next human prompt when not ready"` — during the restart the prompt's reply carries no recap; the next prompt after it finishes does (F04-09, BL2-18).
- `"the accumulator becomes the pending recap's at a return with from = return_h and a fresh one starts at the new seen_h"` (F04-09, R1-907).

**Crashpoints:** none new (the drain's meta-only CAS passes Plan 2's existing `state` CAS steps). **Lint:** none new.

**Notes:** `HumanActivity` keeps one `Anchor` (`anchorNow`) of the last human activity in memory; the return gap is `ownStaleness(clock, anchor)` (this PC's `max(Δwall, Δmono)`, INV-CLOCK); a daemon start counts the first activity as a return (R5-52). `activity.mark` runs on UserPromptSubmit (empty `nonces` and human origin) and UserPromptExpansion (classified human by `TurnOrigin.classifyExpansion`); `recap.system` runs only on a human-origin UserPromptSubmit and puts the recap into `ReplyFrame.system` and the `T-SINCE` item into the digest delta.

- `"06 #2: core.js syntax error, injected throw, rejected promise and malformed stdin → exit 2 with the load or G4 line for each gating matcher, exit 0 and no bytes for every other event"` (06 #2, TC-CCI-19, M08 site exercised).

- `"TC-CCI-19: every internal failure exits 2, never 1; a gating hook over its deadline answers G4 well inside the 10 s host timeout"` (TC-CCI-19).

- `"Plans 2/3 profiles keep their bindings"` — an `edits` seed with `claude` unset makes the same remote ref oids as before Task 17 (A17-1).
- `"a failing seats seed reproduces from its printed command"` — `seatsSetup('seats-repro-0', {mutants: new Set(['M44'])})` fails `oracleTurnOrigin`'s unit form at a step `k`; the run prints `tsx tools/sim/run-seeds.ts --profile seats --seed seats-repro-0` (with the mutant flag) and writes `test-results/sim/seats-repro-0/` holding the trace, the `recordingIpc` frames, each peer's `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` and `<gitdir>/cowork/log`; running the printed command fails at the same step `k` with a byte-identical trace prefix (R5-63, TST-03).
- `"a failing FakeHost cell reruns alone and keeps its artifacts"` — a cell forced to fail (a test-only expectation flip) leaves `test-results/fakehost/faults/<i>/` with the hook stdin JSON, the loader's stdout, stderr and exit code, the daemon log, `status.json` and the latency samples; `npm run test:fakehost -- --scenario faults --cell <i>` runs exactly that cell (R5-63).

**Crashpoints:** the SIM's crash injection (`crash.rate` 0.02) covers `deny.prerecord` in `seats` runs. **Lint:** none new.

**Notes:** `SeatsOpKind` = `seat_edit | seat_read_full | seat_read_ranged | seat_bash | seat_bash_background | seat_formatter | seat_deny_prone_edit | seat_stop | seat_prompt | seat_doorbell | seat_slash_verb | seat_context_reset | seat_mcp_kill | seat_claude_kill | seat_permission_hold | seat_drop_post` (the header's generator op list); a fake seat in SIM calls `runCore` with in-memory io and `handleTool` through the real `Ipc` and handshake (R5-16); timing is never a SIM decision input. `PROFILES.seats` = `{name: 'seats', engineWeight: 0.3, steps: 60, weights}`; 3 peers, seats per peer drawn 0–3 with peer 0 ≥ 1.

`test/unit/oraclesClaude.test.ts` — each oracle against a hand-built trace/file fixture (TC-TST-02 form: fires on its own plant and no other).
- `"oracleLease fires on an INFLIGHT or TXN key open 120,001 ms or a READ open 300,001 ms, by monoAt"` — plants: INFLIGHT released at +120,001; TXN at +120,001; READ renewed 3 × and released at +300,001; passing twins at 120,000 / 300,000 (O-LEASE, INV-CCI-LEASE, TC-CCI-05).

**CI and exit steps:**
- [ ] `ci.yml`: per leg 100 seeds by X-113's one rule, asserted by `test/unit/ci-config.test.ts` — every merged non-`edits` profile gets its final count (`faults` 15, `holds` 15, `claims` 15, `seats` 15, `coord` 15, `lifecycle` 10) and `edits` takes the remainder, in any merge order (Plan 5 adds the `seats` 15 row; e.g. with Plans 2–5 merged: 40 `edits` + 15 `faults` + 15 `holds` + 15 `claims` + 15 `seats`), with Plan 2's per-Node offsets (200 per OS per PR); one FakeHost leg per OS (`npm run test:fakehost`); the latency gates (06 #8, TC-CCI-03, TC-CCI-12, 06 #47 (f)) fail only on windows-latest; the `SPIKE0_INCOMPLETE` status check (`tools/ci/spike0-gate.ts` over the PR's changed paths) (R5-15, R2-L6).
- [ ] `sim-exit.yml`: + 1,000 `seats` seeds per OS in shards of provisional s = 20, resized from the first green PR run's per-OS p95 seed time to s = min(100, ⌊0.8 × 120 min × 4 ÷ p95⌋) in this task (A17-4, R3-55/R3-58 pattern); its existing `edits` and `holds` runs unchanged.
- [ ] Exit evidence in the ledger: seed ranges, per-OS times, the windows-latest p95 numbers (live gating, non-live), the T-F04-return/-restart/-since-first results, the mutant table (M08…M47 incl. M45); every failing seed or FakeHost cell of the exit run with its rerun command and artifact path (R5-63: no retry — a red seed is reproduced and fixed, never re-rolled); the Task 8 `hookRead.saves` soak arm (`COWORK_SOAK=1`, windows-latest, run once in this exit run; F2 #18 #36, ruling R-F2-6): saves per arm, control-arm and daemon-arm failure counts and any `EPERM` outside a hook read — a red arm fails the exit run like a red seed; the known gaps (R5-59: CCI-68/TC-F10-12 met only through `scriptedRevertView` until Plan 3 Task 17 — the F10 kernel, X-09 — has merged and `plan3RevertView` delegates to its `revertView`; when it has, TC-F10-12 runs against it here).
- [ ] Spec batch: the SE5 items as reconciled (SE5-01 and SE5-12 deleted, SE5-15 closed by X-60, SE5-20 shrunk to `slash_shown` and `FlowSelector.detail`, SE5-21 per X-61), each keeping only sentences rev 2.5 lacks (X-03), applied after owner approval to `docs/spec/**` and the roadmap in one commit, fifth in the batch order Plan 2 → 8a → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 → 7 → 8b (after Plan 4's batch, X-04).

**Crashpoints:** none new; `deny.prerecord` (in `test/crashpoints.json`, the one catalogue, X-116) is exercised by the K1 crash matrix (100 trials). **Lint:** none new.

**Notes:** every oracle recomputes from the trace (`monoAt` for ages), frames captured by `recordingIpc`, files, env snapshots and the bare remote with test-side code; none calls the engine or daemon function whose mutant it must catch (TST-07). O-NO-ORPHAN is vacuous in random `seats` seeds (A17-2) and non-vacuous in `seats-x-orphan-*`; O-ASK-ONCE is non-vacuous through `scriptedDueRequests` until Plan 6 binds `DueRequestsPort`; `oracleAskOnce` is the one O-ASK-ONCE oracle of every plan (Plan 6 imports it, X-50).
